### 1. [The Answer Knows Where to Look: Zooming before MLLMs Answer under Visual-Token Budgets](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32664v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32664

**作者：** Zhensheng Hu、Guanglong Xu、Guanqiang Hu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32664v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32664v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P0

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文研究小视觉 token 预算下 MLLM 如何在回答前选定放大区域：发现答案 token 自中层起已能定位证据，而预答注意力只在顶层、弥散且深度因模型而异，遂引入单个 look token，在单次预填充中以事后方式学习模仿模型自身正确答案的注意力，其隐状态据此在不放大、crop、方形窗、指针自适应窗与 foveated warp 间选择，并由各视图的反事实结果监督。

**相关性简评：** 直接对应「先看再答」中的视觉证据筛选与 LOOK 决策：把预答内部状态当作指针来选择证据获取方式，其中「预答注意力无法直接读出证据位置」的诊断与信息坍缩、显式监督预答注意力反而损害阅读的负结果，以及反事实视图监督下的贡献分配，均值得优先核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** The Answer Knows Where to Look: Zooming before MLLMs Answer under Visual-Token Budgets

**abstract/A1：** Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) read images through visual tokens, and under the small per-image budgets that efficient deployment requires, the fine text of documents, charts and infographics becomes illegible. Zooming in recovers the evidence, but the region must be chosen before the model answers. We find that MLLMs know where the evidence is, but not in a form that can be read before they answer: the answer tokens localize it from the middle layers on, whereas the attention available before answering does so only in the top layers, at a depth that varies across models, and diffusely. Supervising the pre-answer attention to point degrades reading, and the benefit of zooming is governed by each image's information gap. Building on these findings, we introduce ours, which lets an MLLM look where its answer would. A dedicated look token, processed in a single prefill and kept out of the answering pass, learns in hindsight to attend as the model's own correct answers do, extracting this knowledge into a concentrated pointer before any answer exists. Its hidden state then selects how to look, from no zoom to crops, square windows, windows that adapt to the pointer and foveated warps, supervised by the counterfactual outcome of every view. ours needs no box annotations, per-model layer search, external localizers or generation before the answer. On Qwen3-VL at three scales and five text-rich benchmarks, it surpasses the strongest baseline by 4.8-9.1 points at 98 visual tokens per view and attention-guided zooming by 11.1-17.0 points, while running faster than attention-guided and grounding-based zooming.

</details>

<a id="paper-2"></a>

### 2. [Interpreting Physics in Video Encoders using Multi-Object Interaction Videos](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31983v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.31983

**作者：** Ayushi Mehrotra、Sai Vidyaranya Nuthalapati

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31983v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.31983v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文用双物体碰撞视频探测冻结的 V-JEPA2 与 VideoMAE 潜空间是否编码动量传递、恢复系数和摩擦等关系型物理量，并检验这些读数能否跨环境或几何变化迁移。

**相关性简评：** 直接关联 LVR 对内部状态是否保留实体关系与任务必要物理信息的关注；其关系型属性探针、预测器能量偏差和跨环境迁移结果，可供核查 latent 中视觉证据的筛选与稳定性。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Interpreting Physics in Video Encoders using Multi-Object Interaction Videos

**abstract/A1：** Video encoder models are increasingly used as world models for physical AI, where a policy plans by rolling out the latent, so what physics the latent holds bounds what the agent can plan over. Moreover, existing interpretability work probes quantities belonging to a single object; the quantities that make physics relational, such as momentum transfer, restitution, and friction, are defined between bodies and have not been examined. We probe relational physics attributes in two frozen self-supervised video encoders (V-JEPA2 and VideoMAE) under two-body settings and test if they transfer across environments. Our early findings are mixed. Both encoders separate elastic from inelastic collisions reliably. V-JEPA2's own predictor is less likely to predict a collision that creates kinetic energy compared to one that loses it. Momentum-violation detection appears in only one of the two encoders, a kinetic-energy readout survives a change of environment for V-JEPA2 but not for VideoMAE, and neither model holds a friction coefficient that survives a change of geometry.

</details>

<a id="paper-3"></a>

### 3. [MAGIC: Margin-gated Contrastive Debiasing with Evidence Restoration for VLM Test-time Robustness Scaling](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32013v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32013

**作者：** Shunshun Gu、Enze Zhang、Ming Pei、Hang Zhou、Xiao Luo

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32013v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32013v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 研究 LVLM 测试时鲁棒性 scaling，提出 MAGIC：依据关键 token 的决策 margin 门控，选择性启用文本与视觉扰动分支进行对比去偏，并恢复模糊样本中信息量高的损坏视图，再用校准集剪枝无效分支以降低推理成本。

**相关性简评：** 直接涉及视觉证据是否被语言先验旁路、扰动视图筛选及动态计算成本；其 margin 门控与分支剪枝可供核查 LOOK/STOP 的条件和收益，但未涉及 latent 状态生成，证据恢复是否真正改变内部决策仍待阅读验证。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** MAGIC: Margin-gated Contrastive Debiasing with Evidence Restoration for VLM Test-time Robustness Scaling

**abstract/A1：** This paper studies the problem of test-time robustness scaling for large vision-language models (LVLMs), which aims to mitigate language bias during inference. Existing approaches typically adopt test-time scaling techniques such as contrastive decoding to improve inference performance. Despite the progress, the performance of these approaches is far from satisfactory due to intentional evidence removal during perturbations and redundant inference cost. Towards this end, we propose a novel approach named Margin-gated Contrastive Debiasing with Evidence Restoration (MAGIC). The core of our MAGIC is to evaluate the decision margins of LVLMs to selectively activate textual and visual perturbation branches for efficient debiased inference. In particular, our MAGIC first measures the prediction margins of crucial tokens, and applies a gating function to identify low-confidence responses that are susceptible to language biases. For selected samples, we integrate the token logits from multiple textual and image perturbation branches with the original ones for debiased decision. More importantly, we selectively restore the most informative corrupted views with sharp prediction distributions for ambiguous samples after debiasing, enabling evidence recovery for discriminative decision-making. We further utilize a calibration dataset to actively identify and prune ineffective branches during contrastive debiasing for cost reduction. Extensive experiments on benchmark datasets validate the effectiveness of the proposed MAGIC in comparison with extensive baselines.

</details>

<a id="paper-4"></a>

### 4. [SyncPro: Synchronous Reasoning and Responding for Proactive Streaming Video Understanding](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32132v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32132

**作者：** Xueyun Tian、Bingbing Xu、Jiaming Liu、Jinpeng Yu、Yang Shi、Yu Zhao、Ruihua Huang、Yuanzhuo Wang、Huawei Shen

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.CL、cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32132v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32132v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 SyncPro，一个面向主动式流式视频理解的双流框架：Inner 分支持续跟踪证据，Response 分支独立生成用户所需回答，两者共享因果历史并各自激活；作者构建了 189K 双流样本，采用 SFT 加轨迹级强化学习训练，在三个主动式基准上改善了响应时机与回答准确率。

**相关性简评：** 直接对应「何时响应」的停止决策、证据累积与动态计算成本问题；其证据流与回答流分离的生成路径可供核查是否能缓解证据保留与输出目标相耦合，但中间推理仍为文本而非 latent 状态。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** SyncPro: Synchronous Reasoning and Responding for Proactive Streaming Video Understanding

**abstract/A1：** Proactive streaming video understanding requires assistants to track unfolding events and respond when a user's requested condition is met. To make these decisions, assistants must accumulate relevant evidence and reassess response conditions as new observations arrive. Existing work offers complementary capabilities for this process: proactive video assistants learn when to respond, while streaming video reasoning methods generate scheduled intermediate text to organize observations and support subsequent answers. Together, these capabilities provide a foundation for proactive reasoning, but how to coordinate ongoing reasoning with timely responses remains underexplored. In a direct adaptation, fixed reasoning intervals can trigger updates when nothing relevant has changed or postpone useful updates until the next scheduled step. Sharing one generation path also couples two distinct requirements: preserving evidence for future decisions and producing only the response requested by the user. Moreover, responses generated after reasoning must wait for it to finish, even when sufficient evidence is already available. To address these challenges, we introduce SyncPro, a synchronous dual-stream framework with specialized generation paths for evidence tracking and user-facing responses. Its Inner and Response branches share causal history and activate independently, allowing relevant evidence changes to trigger internal updates and responses to proceed concurrently with ongoing reasoning. We construct 189K dual-stream examples and train SyncPro through supervised fine-tuning followed by trajectory-level reinforcement learning. Experiments across three proactive benchmarks show improved response timing and more accurate answers to user requests.

</details>

<a id="paper-5"></a>

### 5. [PEAR: Adaptive Advantage Reshaping for Perception and Exploration in Large Vision-Language Models](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32216v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32216

**作者：** Junxin Wang、Yingfeng Luo、Xiangchen Song、Weiqiao Shan、Runsong Zhao、Xinyu Liu、Shunjie Xing、Yifu Huo、Dingyang Lin、Qiuyu Gu、Yongyu Mu、Chenglong Wang、Changliang Li、Jingbo Zhu、Tong Xiao

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32216v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32216v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 PEAR，一种面向多模态推理的强化学习优势重整形方法：以视觉依赖度为基信号，在高熵位置额外加入与视觉依赖度负相关的探索增益，并映射为 token 级正权重来调整序列级优势。

**相关性简评：** 直接涉及 VLM 推理训练中视觉信息使用与探索的权衡，涉及视觉旁路、贡献分配和高熵 token 的探索价值，可供核查其与 latent 推理训练及视觉证据筛选的联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** PEAR: Adaptive Advantage Reshaping for Perception and Exploration in Large Vision-Language Models

**abstract/A1：** Effective multimodal reasoning requires a model to reliably utilize visual information while sufficiently exploring diverse reasoning paths. To achieve this goal, prior work combines visual dependency and token entropy to reshape sequence-level advantages, but scaling the contribution of token entropy by visual dependency can leave high-entropy tokens with low visual dependency underweighted, despite their important role in reasoning exploration. To address this limitation, we propose PEAR, an adaptive advantage reshaping method for improving perception and exploration in multimodal reasoning. PEAR retains visual dependency as the base signal and adds an entropy-based exploration gain only at high-entropy positions, with lower visual dependency leading to a larger additional gain. The fused signal is then mapped to positive token-level weights that reshape the sequence-level advantage. Experiments on Qwen2.5-VL-3B/7B show that PEAR achieves the highest average performance among evaluated baselines across multimodal reasoning benchmarks, alongside leading results on textual reasoning and general multimodal benchmarks.

</details>

<a id="paper-6"></a>

### 6. [Angular Supervision for Visual Object Grounding in Autoregressive Vision-Language Models](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32220v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32220

**作者：** Parsa Esmaeilkhani、Longin Jan Latecki

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32220v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32220v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出基于视觉 token 嵌入与 unembedding 行余弦相似度的 Angular Relevance 度量，并引入角向对象感知辅助损失，直接监督视觉 token 与物体概念词表方向对齐，以改善自回归 VLM 的定位与指代分割。

**相关性简评：** 直接涉及视觉细节是否保留在内部状态及训练监督如何塑造这种保留；其无需分割头的 token 级读出与角向监督，可供核查 latent 视觉证据读取、视觉旁路和定位贡献分配。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Angular Supervision for Visual Object Grounding in Autoregressive Vision-Language Models

**abstract/A1：** Autoregressive vision-language models (VLMs) struggle to ground generated text in the correct image regions, and existing explainability methods, such as attention maps and Logit Lens, often produce poor spatial explanations due to weak text-to-vision attention and sink tokens. We propose \\textbf{Angular Relevance} ($R\_L$), a simple visualization measure based on cosine similarity between visual token embeddings and unembedding-matrix rows. Because $R\_L$ is invariant to token norm, it produces substantially sharper and more faithful heatmaps than attention maps or Logit Lens on out-of-the-box models. Without any training, $R\_L$ yields competitive referring-expression segmentation (RES), outperforming prior diagnostic maps and CLIP-based training-free methods. To preserve localized object information during cross-modal mixing, we introduce \\textbf{Angular Object-Aware Loss (AOL)}, an auxiliary loss that directly supervises visual token embeddings to align angularly with the vocabulary directions of their corresponding object concepts. Finetuning with AOL alongside the standard next-token prediction objective significantly sharpens object localization in heatmaps, showing that the VLM's own visual tokens can be used directly for grounding and improving RES, without training any segmentation heads and without degrading general VQA performance. We further demonstrate that the learned grounding also transfers to category-level segmentation of object categories not seen during training.

</details>

### 7. [RVG-Bench: A Benchmark for Reasoning-Driven Audio-Visual Temporal Grounding](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32268v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32268

**作者：** Yue Feng、Boshen Xu、Zihao Yue、Xiaokun Liu、Miao Yang、Xin Tao、Pengfei Wan、Qin Jin

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32268v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32268v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 RVG-Bench，一个面向推理驱动的音视频时序定位基准：查询需先推断用户意图才能定位目标片段，涵盖八类推理、1255 个视频与 1890 个查询-区间对，并评测 22 个模型（最佳仅 49.6 mIoU），辅以 oracle-grounding 探测和测试时扩展分析。

**相关性简评：** 其"先理解意图再找视觉证据"的设定与 LVR 中视觉证据获取、THINK/LOOK 组合直接相关；oracle-grounding 与测试时扩展实验可供核查审查过程是否真正使用感知证据、以及推理算力投入的收益边界。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** RVG-Bench: A Benchmark for Reasoning-Driven Audio-Visual Temporal Grounding

**abstract/A1：** Existing temporal video grounding (TVG) benchmarks primarily focus on localizing target video segments according to descriptive text queries of the visual content. However, real-world queries are more diverse and often require the model to first understand the user's intent before localization, such as finding visual evidence, identifying the cause of an event, or recalling a remembered moment, yet such queries remain largely overlooked. To mitigate this gap, we introduce RVG-Bench, a benchmark for reasoning-driven audio-visual temporal grounding, where every query is constructed such that the target segment can only be identified through reasoning about the query's underlying intent. We carefully construct the benchmark and rigorously verify its data quality to ensure models truly comprehend the audio-visual content. RVG-Bench comprises 1,255 videos and 1,890 query-interval pairs across eight reasoning types. We evaluate 22 models and find that the strongest, Qwen3.8-Omni-Flash, achieves only 49.6 mIoU. Through a series of controlled analyses, including oracle-grounding probes and test-time scaling experiments, we systematically examine where and why current models struggle, providing insights for future model development.

**comment/C1：** 42 pages, including appendices. Project page: https://fyrisk.github.io/RVG-Bench/

</details>

### 8. [DMAIC-Bench: From Self-Reflection to Self-Restraint for Agents in Multi-Stage Analytical Tasks](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32305v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32305

**作者：** Shan Peng、Shuaiqi Cheng、Miao Peng、Chenyi Zi、Jia Li

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.CL、cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32305v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32305v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 DMAIC-Bench，用 97 个工业多阶段分析任务及配对反事实变体，检验智能体能否依据中间证据决定继续、改向或停止；结合执行轨迹与专家盲审发现，模型在应停止时仍常继续无效分析。

**相关性简评：** 直接对应 LVR 中「证据是否改变下一步行动」与 STOP 决策的收益和成本，其反事实配对与轨迹评分可供核查如何评价停止行为；需注意其任务为文本化工业分析，迁移到视觉推理尚待验证。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** DMAIC-Bench: From Self-Reflection to Self-Restraint for Agents in Multi-Stage Analytical Tasks

**abstract/A1：** Multi-stage analytical tasks require LLM agents to use intermediate evidence to decide whether to continue, redirect or stop. Self-reflection, in which an agent critiques its output, is widely studied, yet a critique does not show whether evidence changed the next action. We call that capability self-restraint. Existing agent benchmarks largely measure task completion or output quality and leave it untested. DMAIC-Bench evaluates it with 97 seeded tasks across 15 industrial cases in four sectors, built around Lean Six Sigma's explicit statistical prerequisites. Paired counterfactual variants require opposite actions at the same workflow position, distinguishing warranted restraint from indiscriminate stopping. We assess execution traces with a private scorer and final claims through independent expert review. Across 13 models from six providers, execution-level routing accuracy averages 92% on matched GO decisions and 19% on NO-GO decisions, counting any forbidden downstream computation as a route error. Execution traces show agents reporting correct diagnostics before performing analyses that those results disallow. Separately, blinded expert review of the 11 audited agents estimates that reports act on invalid evidence at 47% of reached NO-GO decisions, with an interval from 31% to 61%. Explicit stopping permission helps some models without consistently restoring correct decisions at both GO and NO-GO forks. Sixteen life-sciences tasks in bulk ribonucleic acid sequencing (RNA-seq) and case-control genome-wide association studies (GWAS) show that the same framework can evaluate evidence-dependent decisions beyond industrial workflows. Project code and data are available at: https://github.com/psptpx/DMAIC-Bench-public.

**comment/C1：** 54 pages, 14 figures. Code and data: https://github.com/psptpx/DMAIC-Bench-public

</details>

<a id="paper-9"></a>

### 9. [From Visual Affinity to Learning Credit in Multimodal Reinforcement Learning](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32324v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32324

**作者：** Yuhang Han、Dingning Liu、Yujie Chen、Yaojie Zhang、Jingwei Song、Zequn Yang、Xuanming Shang、Li Ding、Zhijiang Guo、Linfeng Zhang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32324v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32324v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 OVC 插件式框架，在多模态 RLVR 中把视觉关联度量与 token 级信用分配分离，用相对视觉编码、结果条件证据校准与归一化信用分配，使视觉 token 的梯度贡献由结果差异而非单纯视觉亲和度决定。

**相关性简评：** 直接对应「贡献分配与监督」及视觉证据筛选问题：指出视觉亲和度高不等于有用、可能放大负优势更新，可供核查 latent 视觉推理中视觉证据是否被真实使用及训练信号如何归因到视觉状态。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** From Visual Affinity to Learning Credit in Multimodal Reinforcement Learning

**abstract/A1：** Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) improves multimodal reasoning, but uniformly assigning a single response-level advantage to all tokens overlooks the distinct roles of visual observations and subsequent reasoning. Visual affinity alone is insufficient: predictive states may align with both image and prompt-context representations, and upweighting valid visual observations can amplify their negative-advantage updates when later reasoning fails. To address this issue, we propose \\textbf{Outcome-Conditioned Visual Credit (OVC)}, a plug-in framework that separates visual-association measurement from token-level credit assignment and is adaptable to policy-optimization methods with uniform response-level advantage allocation. Relative Visual Encoding constructs a context-relative visual coordinate by contrasting each predictive state's affinity to image and prompt-context representations. Outcome-conditioned Evidence Calibration combines this coordinate with uncertainty-scaled predictive features and calibrates their contributions to outcome-discriminative evidence through mean contrasts between positive- and negative-advantage responses, scaled by regularized within-side variation. Visual association thus informs credit through observed outcome differences rather than directly determining update priority. Normalized Credit Allocation conditions the calibrated evidence on the response's advantage sign to produce positive token weights with unit global mean. These detached weights preserve advantage signs while modulating individual token-gradient contributions. OVC reuses existing rollouts without auxiliary reward models, process annotations, or inference-time modules. Instantiated through GSPO-token, OVC retains sequence-level clipping and consistently improves six-benchmark mean accuracy over GSPO across three models, with gains of 1.86-2.39 percentage points.

</details>

<a id="paper-10"></a>

### 10. [Cycle-of-Thought: Self-Evaluative Iterative Reasoning for Table Visual Question Answering in Document Images](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32423v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32423

**作者：** Somraj Gautam、Gaurav Harit

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI、cs.CL

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32423v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32423v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 Self-RefineVQA，一个无需训练、在推理时进行自我纠正的表格 VQA 框架，让同一 VLM 循环扮演生成者、批评者与修正者，通过 prompt 引导的再评估来减少空间错位与逻辑幻觉。

**相关性简评：** 其「生成—自评—修正」迭代闭环与推理成本随轮次线性增长的复杂度分析，可供核查 THINK/LOOK/STOP 的收益—成本权衡与停止判据；但方法为纯 prompt 层面、无 latent 状态，对视觉证据筛选与内部表示的改动需读原文确认。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Cycle-of-Thought: Self-Evaluative Iterative Reasoning for Table Visual Question Answering in Document Images

**abstract/A1：** Recent advances in Vision-Language Models (VLMs) have significantly improved Visual Question Answering (VQA) performance, yet their reasoning over structured content such as tables remains inconsistent, often plagued by spatial misalignment and logical hallucinations. Existing approaches for improving table VQA typically rely on architectural modifications or expensive supervised fine-tuning (SFT), which impose high barriers to entry due to the need for large-scale, expert-annotated datasets and significant computational resources. In this work, we propose Self-RefineVQA, a training-free, inference-time self-correction framework that improves table VQA performance without additional labeled data or model updates. Inspired by human revision behavior, our method enables a single VLM to iteratively act as a generator, critic, and refiner, forming a closed self-feedback loop that corrects common reasoning errors through prompt-guided re-evaluation. Experiments on tabular VQA benchmarks demonstrate that Self-RefineVQA yields consistent accuracy gains in monolingual and cross-lingual understanding, with a particularly significant boost for small models, enabling lightweight models (e.g., 2B-7B parameters) to rival the performance of significantly larger counterparts. Furthermore, we provide a comparative complexity analysis demonstrating that while inference cost increases linearly, our framework offers superior data efficiency and a lower cumulative cost by eliminating the prohibitive annotation bottleneck of human labeling required for fine-tuning. Our results highlight the potential of prompt-based self-improvement as a scalable, accessible paradigm for reliable table reasoning in VLMs.

</details>

<a id="paper-11"></a>

### 11. [PROVE: Probabilistic Visual Grounding via Patch-Level Evidence for Vision-Language Models](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32440v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32440

**作者：** Mayank Nautiyal、Tianru Zhang、Li Ju、Ekta Vats、Prashant Singh

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32440v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32440v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** PROVE 提出冻结 CLIP 的事后证据适配器，将 patch 特征映射为每个 patch 的 Beta 分布，以正负证据累积建模 patch 是否属于指代对象；单次前向即给出定位图、空间 vacuity 图与图像级缺失分数。

**相关性简评：** 其 patch 级证据累积、vacuity 缺失信号与弃答下的选择性预测，可供核查 LVR 中视觉证据筛选与「何时停止/弃答」的依据；但方法面向定位而非推理期 latent 状态生成，迁移价值需进一步确认。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** PROVE: Probabilistic Visual Grounding via Patch-Level Evidence for Vision-Language Models

**abstract/A1：** Visual grounding with vision-language models such as CLIP typically produces a single deterministic relevance map for an image-query pair, without indicating whether the localisation is reliable or whether the referent is present at all. We introduce \\textbf{PROVE} (\\textbf{Pro}babilistic \\textbf{V}isual Grounding via Patch-Level \\textbf{E}vidence), a post-hoc evidential adapter that maps frozen CLIP patch features to per-patch Beta distributions without modifying the backbone. PROVE casts grounding as per-patch membership: each patch accumulates positive and negative evidence for whether it belongs to the referent, so a query may match zero, one, or many patches. We train PROVE with an evidential objective on soft patch-coverage targets, and define a prior-pull objective that keeps absent-referent examples near an uninformative prior, so that vacuity serves as an absence signal without a dedicated presence classifier. During inference, a single forward pass yields, in closed form, a posterior-mean grounding map, a spatial vacuity map, and an image-level absence score. We evaluate PROVE on referring-expression benchmarks: it is competitive in localisation with state-of-the-art CLIP grounding methods, its uncertainty tracks localisation error and supports selective prediction under abstention, and its image-level score detects when the referent is absent altogether. Our evidential patch-membership modelling offers a simple, drop-in mechanism for probabilistic verification in frozen vision-language grounding systems.

</details>

<a id="paper-12"></a>

### 12. [IGG: A Benchmark for Interactive GUI Grounding under Visibility Constraints](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32536v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32536

**作者：** Kyeongseon Kim、Jiyeon Son、Tae-Hyun Oh

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32536v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32536v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 IGG 基准，覆盖 16 个可控 GUI 环境、601 个任务与七类可见性约束，要求智能体先用滚动、悬停、拖拽、等待等交互使目标显现再点击定位，并通过人类对比与两组干预定位能力缺口。

**相关性简评：** 直接考察「先获取视觉证据再决策」的 LOOK 环节；其干预显示瓶颈在执行揭示交互而非识别已暴露目标，可供核查视觉证据获取成本与 THINK/LOOK/STOP 收益分配的关系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** IGG: A Benchmark for Interactive GUI Grounding under Visibility Constraints

**abstract/A1：** GUI grounding benchmarks ask whether an agent can localize a target that is already visible in a static screenshot. Real interfaces often violate this assumption: a target may be off-screen, appear only after a state change or hover, remain occluded, or become actionable after a delay. This leaves the ability to recover target visibility unevaluated. To measure this reveal step, we introduce Interactive GUI Grounding (IGG), a benchmark in which an agent must first make the target visible through interaction and then click it. IGG contains 601 tasks across 16 controllable GUI environments and seven visibility-constraint sub-types, with a compact action space of scrolling, moving, dragging, waiting, and clicking. In our human study, participants achieve 94.6% accuracy, while GPT-5.5 reaches 79.4% and the strongest evaluated open-source VLM reaches only 31.6%. To identify the source of this gap, we conduct two controlled interventions. Telling Qwen3-VL which interaction to perform raises its accuracy only from 30.8% to 33.9%, whereas performing the interaction for the model raises it to 81.9%. The bottleneck is executing the reveal interaction, not recognizing the target once it is exposed. We further show that this capability is learnable, demonstrating that visibility recovery can be strengthened consistently across model families.

**comment/C1：** Accepted to EMNLP 2026 (Main Conference). 24 pages. Project page: https://igg-bench.github.io/

</details>

<a id="paper-13"></a>

### 13. [DirCast: Live Esports Directing and Commentary with a Single Streaming Video-Language Model](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31734v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.31734

**作者：** Zixian Gao、Lixin Xiu、Penghao Luo、Xiangcheng Cao、Ziqi Cai、Zhen Li、Erwin Wu、Kaipeng Zhang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31734v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.31734v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 DirCast，一个流式视频语言模型，同时承担电竞赛事直播的导播与解说：每秒低分辨率扫描所有选手视角，由「hold-or-switch」策略决定是否切换机位，再对选中视角高分辨率读取后生成解说，并自建 27 场 Dota 2 比赛数据。

**相关性简评：** 其「低分辨率全扫→高分辨率读取选中视角→保持或切换」的流程与仅保留已播内容的上下文过期机制，可作为 LOOK/STOP、视觉证据筛选与动态计算成本界的可核查类比，但属任务级系统，未分析 latent 状态内容。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** DirCast: Live Esports Directing and Commentary with a Single Streaming Video-Language Model

**abstract/A1：** A live esports broadcast needs a director who chooses which camera goes on air and a caster who talks over that choice, every second and without knowing what comes next. Streaming video language models have learned to commentate, but only over a broadcast that a human director has already produced. We study live broadcast production, in which a model receives the raw camera of every player and must both direct and commentate from the past alone. We present DirCast, a single streaming video language model that does both. Every second it scans all cameras at low resolution, decides with a hold-or-switch director whether and where to cut, and reads the chosen camera at high resolution before it speaks. Its context keeps what went on air and what was said and lets the other views expire, which bounds the cost of a stream of any length. We build a dataset of 27 professional Dota 2 matches with a camera for every hero, the human director's choices and the casters' commentary. DirCast directs better than rule-based and zero-shot directors including GPT-5, and its commentary wins 29.2 percent of comparisons against GPT-4o under the Proact-VL protocol, against 19.4 percent for the strongest of eleven streaming commentators that watch the human broadcast.

**comment/C1：** 18 pages, 6 figures

</details>

<a id="paper-14"></a>

### 14. [Agreement rather than normalisation keeps depth reusable in looped language models](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31943v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.31943

**作者：** Ivan Viakhirev、Kirill Borodin、Maxim Abramov、Grach Mkrtchian

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.CL

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31943v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.31943v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文检验 looped language model 的深度可复用性究竟依赖循环步间状态归一化，还是训练与推理所用循环方式的一致性；通过移除 Ouro 权重的 loop-boundary normalisation、从头训练小模型，并重拟合读出层与末层来定位失效来源。

**相关性简评：** 虽非多模态工作，但其对循环深度外推、状态范数增长及训练—推理 loop agreement 的拆解，可供核查 latent 循环推理的状态稳定性与长度泛化；读出层单独重拟合仅部分恢复，也可能提示 latent 内容使用与贡献分配问题。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Agreement rather than normalisation keeps depth reusable in looped language models

**abstract/A1：** Looped language models apply one block of layers repeatedly, and the design pays off only if depth is reusable: loop steps beyond the trained budget must remain usable at inference. We ask whether that rests on normalising the state between loop steps, as the released Ouro models do, or on agreement between the loop a model is trained with and the loop it is run with. On released weights this loop-boundary normalisation can be removed without adding parameters and with the readout path bit-identical, and the operation is an identity at one loop step, which serves as an implementation check. Removing it takes Ouro-1.4B and Ouro-2.6B to zero accuracy by the trained budget on a balanced binary task (chance 0.500). State-norm growth, the failure that prior accounts predict, is not the cause: the norm stays within a factor of 2.021 while the head commits to a single wrong token. Yet in 19M-parameter models trained from scratch on two synthetic tasks, 16 seeds per configuration, training without the normalisation is never worse than training with it and extrapolates better: the two are closest at the trained depth and separate on both sides of it (Cliff's delta up to 0.867 beyond the budget). Both results follow if agreement, and not the normalisation itself, is what keeps depth reusable. The damage sits in the readout together with the last layer of the block: in the models trained from scratch, refitting the head over the frozen modified loop recovers a third of the lost accuracy and plateaus, while refitting the last block layer with it recovers all of it. Finally, comparing looped with stacked depth at an equal inference loop count is confounded by unequal overshoot of the trained budget; at matched overshoot the advantage of looping vanishes on one task and shrinks on the other.

**comment/C1：** Under review at ICLR 2027

</details>

<a id="paper-15"></a>

### 15. [DELA: Learning Controllable Edits for Diffusion Language Models](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32096v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32096

**作者：** Xinsong Feng、Chenan Wang、Leshu Tang、Johnny Liu、Dong Li、Da Li、Haipeng Chen

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32096v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32096v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** DELA 在冻结的扩散语言模型上外接一个可训练的编辑控制器，用 Insert、Delete、Remask、Stop 四种显式动作对变长草稿做可控精修；为兼顾前缀稳定性与 KV-cache 复用，采用「只追加起草 + 全局精修」两阶段解码，并以合成恢复任务、有益编辑挖掘、rollout 偏好与下游反馈分阶段训练控制器。

**相关性简评：** 显式 Stop 与「何时何地编辑」的控制器思路可能为 LVR 的 STOP 决策、草稿式状态修正与动态计算提供可核查线索；其两阶段解码对 KV-cache 与位置结构的处理也涉及 latent 相似度分析中的缓存与深度因素，但该工作仅针对纯文本扩散生成，需人工判断向视觉状态迁移的可行性。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** DELA: Learning Controllable Edits for Diffusion Language Models

**abstract/A1：** Diffusion language models (DLMs) support bidirectional denoising and iterative refinement, but generation remains largely controlled by token-level masking schedules. This leaves draft-level refinement implicit and limits explicit control over when to insert missing content, delete redundant spans, revisit uncertain regions, or stop editing. We introduce Diffusion Editing Language Adapter (DELA), a plug-in framework that enables controllable draft refinement with a frozen DLM. DELA operates on clean, variable-length drafts using four explicit editing actions: Insert, Delete, Remask, and Stop. The controller determines when and where to edit the draft, while the frozen DLM fills masked regions through denoising, separating high-level edit control from token-level generation. A naive single-phase editor would be computationally expensive because arbitrary interior edits can disrupt prefix stability and limit reliable KV-cache reuse. To address this, DELA uses a two-phase decoding procedure: a fast, append-only drafting phase that supports KV-cache reuse, followed by a global refinement phase that enables targeted non-monotonic edits to the full draft. We train the controller using a staged curriculum that begins with learning local edit priors from synthetic recovery tasks and mined actions that improve draft quality. Subsequent stages refine edit selection through rollout-based outcome preferences and alignment with downstream task feedback. Experiments on mathematical reasoning and code generation show that DELA achieves gains of up to 5.5 percentage points over direct diffusion decoding while keeping the backbone frozen. DELA thus provides a modular approach to explicit draft-level control in diffusion language models.

</details>

<a id="paper-16"></a>

### 16. [Putting Transformer Theories to the Test](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32104v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32104

**作者：** Awni Altabaa、Tobias Schnabel、Kiran Tomlinson

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.CL

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32104v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32104v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 将 Star-free、AC⁰、TC⁰、C-RASP、MPC、BAPO、Sensitivity 七种 Transformer 能力理论对齐到统一的任务预测矩阵，并用受控实验考察分布内样本复杂度、长度泛化与预训练语言模型表现是否与理论预测一致。

**相关性简评：** 论文指出的软注意力、内容依赖检索、逐位置有界计算与长度泛化实验范式，可供核查 LVR/VLM 中长度泛化与视觉证据检索的理论预测；但其对象为纯文本 Transformer 能力理论，未见视觉或 latent 推理实验，迁移线索需人工核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Putting Transformer Theories to the Test

**abstract/A1：** Several formal theories have been developed to characterize the capabilities and limitations of the transformer architecture, each employing different abstractions that capture different features of the architecture. Due to the differences in their underlying assumptions and conceptual frameworks, these theories can disagree in their predictions about what tasks transformers find easy or hard. We map out seven theories of transformer capability (Star-free, $\\mathsf{AC}^0$, $\\mathsf{TC}^0$, C-RASP, MPC, BAPO, and Sensitivity) and translate their results into a common matrix of predictions across a suite of tasks covering different computational structures. We provide new theoretical results to complete this matrix. We then evaluate theoretical predictions through controlled experiments measuring in-distribution sample complexity, length generalization, and pretrained language model performance. We find that no single theory explains the observed pattern of successes and failures. When a theory does not capture the transformer behaviors relevant to a task, its predictions break down; for instance, we show that the $k$-hop induction task is predicted hard by C-RASP because it does not model content-dependent attention, but we train small transformers on this task that length-generalize. Similarly, theories assuming hard attention like $\\mathsf{AC}^0$ and BAPO predict binary majority to be hard, for which we also find success in practice. Together, our results point to several specific features that a theory of transformers must capture: soft rather than hard attention, dynamic content-dependent retrieval, bounded computation at each position, an account of learnability and inductive biases, and a combination of upper and lower bounds.

</details>

<a id="paper-17"></a>

### 17. [REACT: Representational Early Analysis and Control of Chain-of-Thought Trajectories](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32113v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32113

**作者：** Hanwen Zhang、Enyi Jiang、Ruike Zhu、Qinan Yu、Sanmi Koyejo

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI、cs.CL

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32113v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32113v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 REACT：由成对的安全/不安全轨迹学习一个安全方向，在 CoT 前几个 token 的隐藏状态上做一次条件激活干预，权重冻结、后续 token 全部重新生成；在三个蒸馏推理模型上逐位置比较前五个干预点，发现有效控制集中在第 2–3 个 token 的早期窗口，而更晚状态虽更能预测风险却几乎无法改变输出。

**相关性简评：** 「单次隐状态编辑 + 完整重生成」可作核查 LVR 中 latent 内容是否真被后续生成使用的因果评估范式；其「风险可读性≠有效控制」的解耦，可能提示干预/动态计算位置的选择应以后续生成影响而非状态可分性来衡量。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** REACT: Representational Early Analysis and Control of Chain-of-Thought Trajectories

**abstract/A1：** Chain-of-thought (CoT) generation offers an opportunity to intervene before a reasoning model completes its answer. But does the point at which risk is easiest to detect also offer the most effective control? We study this question with REACT, a conditional activation intervention that learns a safety direction from paired safe and unsafe trajectories and applies it once to an early CoT hidden state. The model weights remain frozen, and all subsequent tokens are generated without further edits. To study timing, we train directions separately at each of the first five CoT positions and evaluate ungated edits with full post-edit regeneration. Across three distilled reasoning models and three generation seeds, the strongest gains concentrate in a two- to three-token early window; on DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Llama-8B, the effect falls to approximately zero at the fourth and fifth tokens, even though later states can be more predictive of risk. This separates risk readability from effective control: intervention timing must be evaluated through its effect on subsequent generation. On the same 8B model, REACT raises the rate of fully safe CoT-answer outputs on StrongREJECT-273 from 29.06% to 82.05%, while keeping benign refusal and usefulness near baseline. An anonymized implementation is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/REACT\_official2-333B.

**comment/C1：** 26 pages, 2 figures

</details>

<a id="paper-18"></a>

### 18. [MS-TTRL: Breaking the Frequency Conflict in Multiple-Solution Test-Time Reinforcement Learning](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32234v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32234

**作者：** Zhixia Zhang、Binqian Xu、Huaiyang Wang、Yikun Ban、Dianbo Liu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32234v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32234v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文指出多解推理的 test-time RL 中「生成频率」被同时用于正确性估计与多样性保留而相互冲突，提出 MS-TTRL：以 outcome-level 自验证给出与频率无关的正确性估计，频率仅用于保留多样性，并辅以三态结果缓存、候选状态和置信门控的逆概率缩放。

**相关性简评：** 纯文本 TTRL 工作，但「用自验证替代频率投票」以及结果缓存、置信门控可供核查：可能启发多解/多 latent 假设下的正确性估计、信用分配与多样性坍缩缓解，与视觉证据筛选则无直接联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** MS-TTRL: Breaking the Frequency Conflict in Multiple-Solution Test-Time Reinforcement Learning

**abstract/A1：** Scientific, engineering, and mathematical reasoning tasks can admit multiple valid solutions, while exhaustive annotation of the full solution set is often prohibitively costly. Test-time reinforcement learning (TTRL) applies reinforcement learning to unlabeled test instances, offering a label-free approach to multiple-solution reasoning. In this setting, generation frequency serves two opposing objectives: correctness estimation favors frequent outcomes, whereas outcome-level diversity preservation favors less frequent outcomes. We term this tension the \\textbf{Frequency Conflict}. To resolve this conflict, we propose MS-TTRL, which obtains frequency-independent correctness estimates through Outcome-Level Self-Verification while reserving generation frequency solely for diversity preservation. Three-State Outcome Cache and the Candidate State support verification reuse across repeated observations and confidence-aware handling of ambiguous outcomes, while Confidence-Gated Inverse Probability Scaling selectively strengthens the learning signals of rare, reliably verified outcomes. Experiments across two benchmarks, four multiple-solution tasks, and three backbone models, each evaluated with five random seeds, show that MS-TTRL improves Recovery Rate by 19.13 percentage points on average over the best-performing label-free baseline and approaches the performance of the supervised IPS-GRPO.

</details>

<a id="paper-19"></a>

### 19. [Beyond Post-Hoc Rewards: Privileged Reasoning Exploration for Multimodal Embeddings](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32474v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32474

**作者：** Tianyu Yang、Yijun Tian、Lars Benedikt Kaesberg、Jan Philip Wahle、Bela Gipp、Terry Ruas、Daniel Kurzawe

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.CL

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32474v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32474v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 PILOT，将多模态嵌入中的推理视为隐变量，用共享 MLLM 分别实例化仅依赖输入的先验与引入训练期关系证据的后验，并以重要性加权变分目标和先验—后验协同演化，把高价值且先验可达的后验推理轨迹迁移给先验，在 MMEB-V2 上提升检索表现。

**相关性简评：** 虽以检索嵌入为目标，但「推理轨迹作为隐变量＋特权后验引导＋按效用与可达性筛选轨迹」直接可供核查 LVR 的轨迹监督、贡献分配与训练期状态蒸馏线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Beyond Post-Hoc Rewards: Privileged Reasoning Exploration for Multimodal Embeddings

**abstract/A1：** Advances in Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have driven substantial progress in multimodal embeddings. Recent methods exploit generative capabilities by augmenting embedded inputs with self-generated reasoning to produce more informative embeddings. However, conventional retrieval supervision for training embedding models specifies pairwise relevance relations without explicitly guiding the reasoning used for input augmentation. Such reasoning is neither directly observed nor uniquely determined by the input, allowing multiple plausible traces to introduce semantic cues of varying retrieval utility. Existing approaches largely optimize reasoning conditioned on the input alone, limiting the exploration of retrieval-relevant traces. We introduce PILOT, a framework that models reasoning as a latent variable and instantiates its prior and posterior distributions as distinct reasoning modes of a shared MLLM. The prior conditions only on the input, whereas the posterior uses additional training-time relational evidence to guide reasoning exploration. To translate the posterior's broader exploration into inference-time improvements, we derive an importance-weighted variational objective and instantiate it as a prior-posterior co-evolution framework that improves both modes under a shared inference-compatible reward while selectively transferring posterior trajectories to the prior according to their utility and prior reachability. On the 78-task MMEB-V2 benchmark, PILOT outperforms the primary baseline across all 12 task groups at both 2B and 7B parameters, improving overall scores by 3.2 and 3.3 points, respectively, with the largest gains in visual-document retrieval (+6.1 and +6.4 points). Our results show that relational supervision is most valuable not as a post-hoc reward but as a signal for shaping how reasoning is explored in training reasoning-enhanced embedding models.

</details>

<a id="paper-20"></a>

### 20. [Accordion: Elastic-Depth Inference via Fixed-Prefix Layer Looping](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32492v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32492

**作者：** Yudong Zhang、Wei Ding、Kaili Zheng、Yuchen Ding、Zhenyu Zhang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.CL

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32492v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32492v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 Accordion：基于 MoE 的模型通过固定前缀层循环实现五档推理深度预算，最浅档即未循环模型；预训练按 micro-batch 采样单一预算做下一词预测，并用参数组合并与预算匹配的多教师 on-policy 蒸馏在 SFT、RL 后保留各档能力。

**相关性简评：** 其「循环层即额外计算、按预算选择执行路径」及跨预算蒸馏的设定，可能为 LVR 中动态计算、停止/深度决策与 latent 状态训练的监督分配提供核查线索；但工作纯文本、不涉及视觉证据筛选，且未验证 latent 内容是否被实际使用。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Accordion: Elastic-Depth Inference via Fixed-Prefix Layer Looping

**abstract/A1：** Large language models serve applications with diverse inference budgets, but training and maintaining separate models for each budget incurs substantial costs. Recent Looped Transformers enable flexible inference computation through parameter reuse, but maintaining strong performance across different inference budgets remains an open challenge. We introduce Accordion, a mixture-of-experts-based model that supports five inference budgets through fixed-prefix layer looping. Each budget follows a pre-defined execution path, while the minimum budget retains the unlooped model. Joint pretraining samples one budget per micro-batch and optimizes its next-token prediction loss without additional cross-budget consistency objectives. We further preserve these inference budgets through supervised fine-tuning and reinforcement learning, where parameter-group merging and budget-matched multi-teacher on-policy distillation integrate three domain experts into one model. After 3.6T tokens of pretraining, increasing inference depth improves Accordion-6B-A0.5B's five-category macro-average across 37 tasks by 3.01 percentage points, with the middle budget capturing 89% of this gain using half as many additional layer executions. The final model surpasses each domain expert on its corresponding task-and-budget average, while achieving strong instruction-following performance across all budgets and retaining depth-dependent gains in mathematical reasoning and code generation.

</details>

<a id="paper-21"></a>

### 21. [DriveMind: Mind-Evolving Belief Tracking for Closed-Loop Autonomous Driving](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32069v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32069

**作者：** Yan Shi、Kehua Chen、Jiarui Qi、Yihong Tang、Haichen Liao、Yinhai Wang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32069v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32069v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 DriveMind，用 MLLM 驾驶框架同时保留多个场景假设，借鉴粒子滤波逐帧传播、按当前观测打分并重采样，在 Bench2Drive 上尤其改善模糊路口与对向车流场景。

**相关性简评：** 其多假设信念状态的加权更新与证据累积机制，可能为 LVR 中跨帧保留候选解释、避免过早锁定单一 latent 提供表述与评价参考，可供核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** DriveMind: Mind-Evolving Belief Tracking for Closed-Loop Autonomous Driving

**abstract/A1：** Recent multimodal large language models (MLLMs) can perceive a traffic scene, reason about it, and propose a driving action within a single model. However, existing MLLM-based drivers reason from scratch at each frame and commit to a single explanation, discarding earlier hypotheses about the scene. When the scene is ambiguous, the agent locks in on the wrong interpretation and acts on it before evidence accumulates. In this paper, we propose DriveMind, an MLLM-based driving framework that keeps several hypotheses about the scene alive at the same time and updates their weights as new frames arrive. Inspired by particle filtering, DriveMind propagates each hypothesis through a VLM, scores it against the current observation, and resamples to maintain diversity. On Bench2Drive, adding belief tracking to the same driving pipeline improves the Driving Score (DS) from 68.50 to 81.37 and the Success Rate (SR) from 41.36% to 54.09%. The largest gains occur on ambiguous scenarios such as unsignalized intersections (+20.0 DS) and oncoming-traffic interactions (+20.5 DS). Beyond the overall metrics, DriveMind also improves fine-grained interactive driving abilities such as merging, emergency braking, and give-way.

**comment/C1：** Accepted by Neurips 2026

</details>

<a id="paper-22"></a>

### 22. [CEMIR vs. FISER: a comparative study of reasoning-based and enrichment-based approaches to pragmatic intent recognition](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32022v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32022

**作者：** Ziya Hosseynzadeh、Farjad Fallah、Farnoosh Fallah、Soroush Gooran、Mohammad Amanloo、Hossein Sameti

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.RO

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32022v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32022v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文在 HandMeThat v2 上对两种语用意图识别路线做对比诊断：FISER 依赖结构化社会与具身推理，CEMIR 则从文本提示合成叙事与写实图像进行多模态增强，并在 Qwen2.5-Omni-3B 到 GPT-5 四个模型上评测，结果显示两者在任何模型与难度层级上均无统计显著差异，性能主要由底座能力与歧义固有难度决定。

**相关性简评：** 负结果直接触及「视觉旁路是否有用」的边界条件：当任务约束已存在于文本中时，文本衍生的合成视觉证据可能不带来增益，可为 LVR 中视觉证据筛选与信息冗余的判断提供核查线索；但该工作不含 latent 状态生成或内部表示分析。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** CEMIR vs. FISER: a comparative study of reasoning-based and enrichment-based approaches to pragmatic intent recognition

**abstract/A1：** Intent recognition in human-robot interaction requires agents to resolve ambiguous natural language instructions by inferring the speaker's underlying goals. Research has increasingly focused on two notable paradigms for addressing this task: structured symbolic reasoning and multimodal input enrichment. However, it remains unclear whether enrichment-based methods provide benefit in settings where the ambiguity concerns unstated human intent rather than missing observable scene information. In this paper, we present a comparative and diagnostic study benchmarking FISER - a framework utilizing structured social and embodied reasoning - against CEMIR (Context-Enriched Multimodal Intent Recognition), a method that performs multimodal enrichment by synthesizing narratives and photorealistic images from textual prompts. By evaluating both approaches across four Large Language Models (LLMs), ranging from Qwen2.5-Omni-3B to GPT-5, on the HandMeThat v2 benchmark, we analyze their capability to resolve pragmatic ambiguities. Our statistical analysis, employing multiple-comparison correction, reveals no statistically significant difference in performance between FISER and CEMIR across any model or difficulty level. This pattern holds across all sixteen model-by-difficulty comparisons, as well as in a targeted ablation isolating CEMIR's narrative and visual components. Instead, performance is primarily associated with backbone capability and the inherent difficulty of the ambiguity. These findings suggest a boundary condition for text-derived multimodal enrichment: when the relevant task constraints are already available in the textual instruction, synthetic multimodal augmentation may not provide measurable benefit over structured reasoning on the same text.

</details>

<a id="paper-23"></a>

### 23. [Beyond Pattern Matching in 3D MLLMs: Part-Level Functional Reasoning for Generalizable Recognition](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32262v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32262

**作者：** Weihong Li、Hao Jiang、Yaohua Zha、Tao Dai、Shu-Tao Xia

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32262v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32262v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文针对 3D MLLM 仅靠几何模式匹配、难以区分形状相似物体的失败，提出按样本的 Geometric Confusion Score 暴露该问题，并构建 PFIR：基于部件分割生成「几何→功能→身份」功能 CoT 数据，用渐进课程加双目标 GRPO 训练，在严格双 OOD 协议下提升零样本识别且增益集中在几何歧义样本上。

**相关性简评：** 其歧义度分层的失败诊断、部件级几何信息与「功能—身份」推理链条，直接触及 LVR 关心的任务必要视觉细节是否进入推理状态及长度/分布迁移问题；但推理以显式文本 CoT 承载而非 latent，双目标近正交（cos=0.056）的组合现象可供核查贡献分配与训练监督，是否对应 latent 状态留有疑问。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Beyond Pattern Matching in 3D MLLMs: Part-Level Functional Reasoning for Generalizable Recognition

**abstract/A1：** 3D MLLMs recognize objects via pattern matching, which fails when geometrically similar objects require functional reasoning to disambiguate (e.g., dresser vs. wardrobe). Our per-sample Geometric Confusion Score (GCS) exposes this failure: baseline accuracy is 33.7% on ModelNet40's most ambiguous 20% versus 84.0% on its least ambiguous 20%. We identify a function-as-annotation pattern, in which functional language decorates a known identity instead of supporting inference, and hypothesize that reversing this information flow should concentrate improvements on the most ambiguous samples. PFIR (Part-level Functional Inference for Recognition) reverses that flow. Built on PartNeXt part segmentation, its data pipeline produces 507K QA pairs with functional CoTs (geometry $\\to$ function $\\to$ identity), and its progressive curriculum culminates in dual-objective GRPO: strict functional reasoning and descriptive grounding for Objaverse's diverse distribution. The two RL objectives yield nearly orthogonal task vectors ($\\cos = 0.056$), enabling composition via Task Arithmetic. Under a strict dual OOD protocol (Objaverse $\\to$ ModelNet40; XYZRGB $\\to$ XYZ-only), PFIR raises zero-shot accuracy from 60.5% to 66.7%, with gains concentrated $2.6\\times$ more on geometrically ambiguous samples---supporting part-level functional reasoning as an effective route to more generalizable 3D recognition. Code: https://github.com/this233/pfir-3d.

</details>

<a id="paper-24"></a>

### 24. [4DMemory: Benchmarking Long-Horizon Spatiotemporal Memory with Harness-Augmented Agents](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32332v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32332

**作者：** Yanhao Jia、Yi Zhu、Yiran Zhong、Weigao Sun、Feida Zhu、Pengkun Jiao、Sihan Yang、Ming Ma、Steven Hoi、Erik Cambria

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI、cs.CL、cs.MM

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32332v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32332v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 构建约900小时视频、5000余道人工审核问题的 LongVideo 数据集与 4DMemoryBench，并提出整合四类技能、22+ 专用工具模型与模型专属 RAG 的 4DMemory Harness，用于评测13个模型的长时程时空记忆推理。

**相关性简评：** 其长视频证据检索与外部工具编排可供核查「LOOK」式视觉证据获取、检索与缓存在长程语境下对推理的作用，但未涉及内部 latent 状态表示，联系主要在基准与证据筛选层面。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** 4DMemory: Benchmarking Long-Horizon Spatiotemporal Memory with Harness-Augmented Agents

**abstract/A1：** Humans efficiently process multimodal streams spanning hours to years, building persistent, fine-grained spatiotemporal memories that support reasoning about and predicting long-horizon events. In contrast, existing benchmarks focus on short- and medium-context tasks, and most foundation models struggle to preserve fine-grained information over long horizons. To evaluate and improve long-horizon spatiotemporal reasoning, we introduce the LongVideo dataset and the 4DMemoryBench. LongVideo comprises about 900 hours of video and over 5,000 manually reviewed questions covering visual, textual, and spatiotemporal reasoning across diverse context lengths. We further develop the 4DMemory Harness, which integrates four complementary skills, 22+ specialized tool models, and a model-specific retrieval-augmented generation system. Evaluating 13 leading models, we find that standalone foundation models struggle with long-horizon tasks, while the 4DMemory Harness improves all of them by over 50\\%. Compared to previous benchmark, the same models exhibit a performance drop of more than 10\\% on 4DMemoryBench. We hope 4DMemory will facilitate the development of spatiotemporal agents capable of addressing complex, long-horizon tasks.

**comment/C1：** 22 pages, 5 figures

</details>
