### 25. [Search-Based Testing of Modality Sensitivity in Vision Language Models](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32360v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32360

**作者：** Oliver Weißl、Eduardo Breitembach、Andrea Stocco

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.SE

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32360v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32360v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 MoST 黑盒测试框架，用多目标搜索联合调整图像与提示扰动，在四个 VLM 的 ImageNet 和驾驶图像目标定位任务上比较单模态与联合扰动的致错效果，并辅以人类可理解性评估。

**相关性简评：** 该诊断显示分别扰动模态会漏掉联合致错的组合，可供核查 VLM 是否真正依赖视觉证据、何时转而依赖文本线索，与视觉旁路和证据筛选问题相关。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Search-Based Testing of Modality Sensitivity in Vision Language Models

**abstract/A1：** Vision-language models (VLMs) process images and text jointly, yet robustness evaluations often perturb these inputs separately or assess only predefined corruption levels. Such evaluations provide limited evidence of which modality is more susceptible to perturbations and whether smaller changes across both modalities can jointly expose failures. We present MoST, a black-box testing framework that characterizes modality sensitivity by comparing image-only, text-only, and joint perturbations. MoST uses multi-objective search to select and combine perturbations and adjust their severity, seeking prediction degradation while limiting changes to images and prompts. We evaluate MoST on four VLMs performing object localization on ImageNet and driving images. The models exhibit distinct sensitivities: textual perturbations dominate for some, whereas others are susceptible across both modalities. In several configurations, joint perturbations match or exceed the effectiveness of single-modality perturbations with smaller modifications to each input. In a human study, 60-76% of assessments across models judge the perturbed inputs sufficiently understandable to perform the task, and human localization is more accurate than that of the corresponding VLMs. These results show that evaluating modalities separately can overlook failure-inducing combinations and that modality sensitivity provides a more detailed account of VLM robustness than aggregate failure rates alone.

</details>

<a id="paper-26"></a>

### 26. [Tempo-Zero: Recursive Self-Play in Executable Worlds for Video Reasoning](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32433v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32433

**作者：** Jingmin Zhu、Yanyan Zou、Xiuyu Li、James Bailey、Dinh Phung、Tao Yu、Yunyun Hou、Jun Liu、Jun Zhou、Sirui Wang、Nan Duan、Jiachen Liu、Qiuhong Ke

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32433v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32433v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 Tempo-Zero：用可执行程序渲染出带真实答案与时间区间标注的视频，让同一策略兼任出题者与解题者进行执行验证的自博弈，并以解题表现驱动任务难度课程与环境规则演化。

**相关性简评：** 以执行验证替代模型投票伪标签、要求解题者给出带时间戳的证据行，可能与 LVR 的视觉证据筛选、监督信号与课程设计相关，但视频自博弈与推理期 latent 状态的联系仍需核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Tempo-Zero: Recursive Self-Play in Executable Worlds for Video Reasoning

**abstract/A1：** Video reasoning requires understanding how events unfold over time, yet videos with accurate temporal annotations are expensive to collect. Self-play offers an alternative by letting a model generate and solve its own tasks without manual annotation. However, existing video self-play methods train on fixed sets of recorded videos, so they cannot alter the underlying event dynamics and rely on noisy pseudo-labels derived from the model's own outputs, such as majority voting, that risk reinforcing shared model errors. To overcome these limitations, we introduce Tempo-Zero, a self-improvement framework that closes a loop of executable environments, execution-verified self-play, and environment evolution, where executing a program renders a video with the ground-truth option and interval of every question. A single policy plays both Questioner and Solver, proposing questions and answering them with timestamped evidence lines, and is trained on execution-grounded rewards instead of model consensus. The Solver's performance steers a curriculum over tasks and difficulty levels, and the policy selects rule changes that enrich the event dynamics of later rounds. Despite being trained solely on simple programs, without supervised fine-tuning or external video data which prior methods typically require, Tempo-Zero outperforms the base models and recent self-play baselines on average across twelve real-world video benchmarks at different model scales, with particularly strong gains on temporal grounding tasks. Project page: https://alchemist-jo.github.io/tempo-zero/

</details>

<a id="paper-27"></a>

### 27. [PCFBench: How Far Can Large Vision-Language Models Go in Physics-aware Photonic Inverse Design?](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32485v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32485

**作者：** Shengchao Chen、Ting Shu、Sufen Ren

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32485v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32485v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 PCFBench，基于 22 万条 FDTD 仿真构建 26 项任务，评测 VLM 在光子晶体光纤逆向设计中的几何感知、物理理解与设计决策，并考察多智能体流水线的误差传播。

**相关性简评：** 该基准直接诊断 VLM 能否将视觉几何信息转化为物理决策，揭示感知—设计鸿沟与上游错误级联，与 LVR 关注的视觉证据使用及推理状态到决策的映射相关；但其领域专用且未涉及 latent 状态，可供 P2 参考。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** PCFBench: How Far Can Large Vision-Language Models Go in Physics-aware Photonic Inverse Design?

**abstract/A1：** Photonic crystal fibers (PCFs) underpin critical applications in telecommunications, sensing, and high-power laser delivery, yet their design remains a manual, expert-intensive process that couples geometric intuition with deep waveguide physics. Vision-language models (VLMs) offer a promising path toward automating PCF design, but can they truly reason about photonic physics from microscopy images, or do they merely recognize visual patterns? We introduce PCFBench, the first comprehensive benchmark for evaluating VLMs on the perception, reasoning, and decision steps of the photonic inverse-design loop. PCFBench spans 26 tasks organized into six categories of increasing cognitive demand (Geometry Perception, Physics Understanding, Text Generation, Multi-modal Reasoning, Inverse-Design Decision, and Code Generation), built from over 220K FDTD simulation samples across 18 structurally diverse fiber families. We evaluate both individual VLMs on isolated tasks and multi-agent systems (MAS) on the full sequential pipeline from perception to design. Our results reveal a striking perception-design gap: current models can identify fiber geometry with moderate success, yet consistently fail to translate that visual understanding into physics-aware design actions. Moreover, cascading agents through the pipeline exposes significant error propagation, where upstream perception mistakes compound into downstream design failures. These findings suggest that neither model scaling nor naive agent chaining suffices, and that closing the loop demands explicit physical grounding. PCFBench provides the community with a rigorous, multi-dimensional testbed to measure progress toward AI-assisted photonic engineering. The project is available at https://pcfbench.github.io/.

**comment/C1：** Accepted to NeurIPS 2026, 66 pages, 30 figures, 27 tables

</details>

<a id="paper-28"></a>

### 28. [MiD-VLM: Detecting and Grounding Global Semantic Misinformation in Image-Caption Pairs](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32967v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32967

**作者：** Tarunya Prasad、Bharatesh Chakravarthi

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32967v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32967v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 MiD-VLM：将 SigLIP 的图文表示与 DINOv2 的视觉场景特征经投影 MLP 融合，并 LoRA 适配 Qwen2-VL-7B，在 DGM4+ 上同时判别图文对真实性、识别操纵类型并定位误导性 caption token。

**相关性简评：** 研究的「全局语义不一致检测与 token 级定位」可作为视觉证据是否被实际使用、能否筛出关键视觉信息的诊断线索供核查；但其为常规融合加 LoRA 微调，未涉及 latent 推理状态的生成或更新。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** MiD-VLM: Detecting and Grounding Global Semantic Misinformation in Image-Caption Pairs

**abstract/A1：** Semantic misinformation can arise when an authentic image is paired with a caption that changes or distorts its narrative. Because the image itself may contain no visible signs of manipulation, methods designed to detect pixel-level artifacts can miss these misleading image-caption pairs. We study this problem using DGM4+, a dataset designed to evaluate the detection of global scene and semantic inconsistencies. We propose a multimodal model that combines image-text representations from SigLIP with visual scene features from DINOv2 through a projection MLP, and adapts Qwen2-VL-7B using LoRA. The model jointly predicts whether an image-caption pair is authentic, identifies the manipulation type, and grounds the misleading caption tokens. On the DGM4+ validation split, the model achieves an F1 score of $0.9679$ for binary authenticity classification. These results suggest that parameter-efficient adaptation of vision-language models is promising for detecting semantic inconsistencies, while also motivating further work on precise token-level grounding.

</details>

<a id="paper-29"></a>

### 29. [End-to-End Differentiable Diffusion Conditioning for Physics-Informed Optimization](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31721v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.31721

**作者：** Ricardo Luna Gutierrez、Vineet Gundecha、Rahman Ejaz、Varchas Gopalaswamy、Riccardo Betti、Sahand Ghorbanpour、Aarne Lees、Soumyendu Sarkar

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31721v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.31721v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 Differentiable Diffusion Conditioning（D2C）：利用确定性采样使反向去噪链对条件信号可微，在测试时用冻结代理目标与物理约束的梯度直接优化条件变量，把冻结的条件扩散模型转化为端到端梯度优化器，并在激光脉冲设计、数据中心调度与 Design-Bench 上评测。

**相关性简评：** 该方法线索是把损失梯度沿扩散采样链回传到条件或潜变量以实现测试时优化，可供核查如何用外部目标梯度更新 latent 推理状态，但论文本身不涉及视觉或多模态推理。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** End-to-End Differentiable Diffusion Conditioning for Physics-Informed Optimization

**abstract/A1：** Optimizing high-dimensional physical systems under expensive evaluations and complex constraints is a central challenge in science and engineering. In offline model-based optimization (MBO), methods must improve designs using only a fixed dataset and no test-time access to the true objective. However, existing methods typically suffer from conservatism, unconstrained surrogate exploitation, or function as static inference-time samplers that cannot refine candidates against precise physical objectives. In this paper, we propose Differentiable Diffusion Conditioning (D2C) for offline MBO, which leverages the fact that deterministic sampling makes the reverse denoising chain differentiable with respect to the conditioning signal. D2C turns a frozen conditional diffusion model into an end-to-end gradient-based optimizer by directly optimizing conditioning variables at test time via gradients from frozen surrogate objectives and physics-informed penalties backpropagated through the reverse chain. We implement this idea in a composed proposer-evaluator framework and evaluate it on laser pulse optimization for inertial confinement fusion, sustainable data-center workload scheduling, and four Design-Bench tasks. Our method achieves the strongest results on the two real-world tasks and the best average rank across four Design-Bench tasks.

**comment/C1：** Initial version submitted to the NeurIPS 2026 main conference pending publication

</details>

<a id="paper-30"></a>

### 30. [Autoregressive Multimodal Anomaly Detection with Large Language Models for Microservice Systems](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31722v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.31722

**作者：** Yixiang Tang、Fagui Liu、Guoxiang Zhong、Xi Yao、C. L. Philip Chen

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI、cs.SE

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31722v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.31722v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 LLMMAD，把微服务的多模态监控数据嵌入重排为统一序列交由 LLM 自回归建模，并用模态专属重建头保留模态特性、以预测未来状态的目标替代重建以缓解「identity-shortcut」问题。

**相关性简评：** 其「重建目标易退化为复制输入」的 identity-shortcut 分析及改为预测后续状态的做法，可能与 latent 状态信息坍缩、视觉旁路等训练监督问题可比，可供核查；但任务本身是微服务异常检测，不涉及视觉推理。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Autoregressive Multimodal Anomaly Detection with Large Language Models for Microservice Systems

**abstract/A1：** Microservice systems have become a dominant architecture for large-scale cloud applications, enabling rapid deployment and elastic scaling. However, ensuring the reliability and availability of microservice systems critically relies on effective anomaly detection, as failures and performance anomalies can quickly propagate across services and severely impact end-to-end system behavior. Existing multimodal anomaly detection approaches for microservice systems often struggle with two fundamental limitations: (i) the neglect of modality-specific characteristics in favor of cross-modality fusion, and (ii) the "identity-shortcut" problem that affects the feature reconstruction process. To address these issues, we propose LLMMAD, an autoregressive multimodal anomaly detection framework for microservice systems based on Large Language Models (LLMs). Initially, to reconcile cross-modality fusion with modality-specific preservation, LLMMAD embeds and recombines multimodal data into a unified sequence for LLM processing to model inter-modality relationships, while employing modality-wise reconstruction heads to retain modality-specific information. Subsequently, to eliminate the identity-shortcut, we implement a prediction-driven autoregressive objective, under which the model predicts future states rather than copies current inputs. Finally, we conduct extensive experiments on three real-world datasets. The results demonstrate that LLMMAD consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods, achieving F1-score improvements of 3.1%, 1.4%, and 0.9%, respectively, thereby providing a more reliable solution for microservice monitoring.

**comment/C1：** 14 pages, 8 figures, 7 tables

</details>

<a id="paper-31"></a>

### 31. [When and How Does Muon Precondition? The Role of Structured Gradient Noise](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31728v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.31728

**作者：** Xiaoran Chen、Binghui Li、Liwei Wang、Pinyan Lu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31728v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.31728v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文从梯度噪声几何角度分析 Muon 预条件为何有效：证明在等向噪声假设下 Muon 仅等价于学习率缩放，进而在多任务频率不均的联想记忆设定下给出两任务精确平衡阈值并推广到多任务，说明 Muon 可把对任务频率的依赖从线性降为平方根，并讨论 SignSGD 的旋转负迁移与动量抑噪作用。

**相关性简评：** 其「结构化噪声 + fast-slow 动量」的训练机制可供核查是否适用于多任务 LVR 训练中稀有推理技能监督稀疏、频繁与罕见任务失衡导致的梯度可靠性差异，但与原论文视觉推理主线无直接联系，迁移性需人工核实。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** When and How Does Muon Precondition? The Role of Structured Gradient Noise

**abstract/A1：** Muon has become a popular optimizer for large language model training, yet its theoretical foundations remain poorly understood. In this work, we study how gradient noise geometry shapes Muon's preconditioning benefits. We first show, theoretically and empirically, that under the classical isotropic noise assumption, Muon offers no acceleration beyond a rescaling of the learning rate. This motivates us to investigate structured noise arising from mini-batch sampling in associative memory with heterogeneous task frequencies. We establish an exact balancing threshold for two tasks and extend the analysis to multiple tasks, revealing how task coverage and gradient reliability jointly shape learning. Our analysis shows that Muon can soften the dependence on task frequency from linear to square-root, helping balance the learning of frequent and rare tasks. We further identify rotation-induced negative transfer in SignSGD that Muon avoids, and explain how momentum reduces sampling noise while tracking changing gradients. Controlled numerical experiments validate these mechanisms, and a preliminary LLM pretraining study illustrates the practical promise of fast-slow momentum.

**comment/C1：** 62 pages, 11 figures

</details>

<a id="paper-32"></a>

### 32. [ConsMSA: Semantic Distribution Consistency Learning for Multimodal Sentiment Analysis](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31731v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.31731

**作者：** Pan Wang、Lipeng Ke、Huajun Ying、Pritish Mohapatra、Rohan Sarkar、Suresh Lakhani、Sankar Venkataraman、Jingtong Hu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.MM

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31731v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.31731v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文提出 ConsMSA，在多模态情感分析中把模态内与模态间的语义分布一致性量化为可计算得分 I²CS，并据此构造一致性正则、token 重加权与冗余冲突 token 剪枝，在三个基准上验证压缩到 10% token 仍可保持精度。

**相关性简评：** 其以分布一致性得分筛选冗余或冲突 token 的做法，可能为 LVR 中视觉证据筛选与信息坍缩抑制提供可核查的准则线索，但任务是情感分类而非推理，需核查该信号能否指导推理期状态更新。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** ConsMSA: Semantic Distribution Consistency Learning for Multimodal Sentiment Analysis

**abstract/A1：** Multimodal sentiment analysis (MSA) aims to predict human sentiments by integrating signals from different modalities such as text, video, and audio. However, raw multimodal sequences often suffer from semantic inconsistency--exhibiting redundancy or conflicts within and across modalities--which hinders robust understanding and increases computational cost. To this end, we introduce ConsMSA, which explicitly formalizes semantic distribution consistency across both $\\textit{intra}$- and $\\textit{inter}$-modality, providing a consistency-aware mechanism for robust and efficient multimodal sentiment prediction. Specifically, ConsMSA projects multimodal token features into a shared sentiment space to compute an Intra- and Inter-modality Consistency Score ($I^2CS$). By coupling this score with predictive relevance, we formulate consistency-aware importance signals that are utilized: (i) as a consistency regularizer to align latent distributions during training, (ii) to derive semantic-aware weights for adaptive multimodal token reweighting, and (iii) as a practical criterion to prune redundant or conflicting tokens. Extensive experiments on CMU-MOSI, CMU-MOSEI, and CH-SIMS demonstrate that ConsMSA achieves state-of-the-art performance while remaining robust under aggressive token compression--retaining only 10% of tokens yields comparable accuracy. These results establish semantic distribution consistency as a promising foundation for synergizing predictive robustness with computational efficiency.

**comment/C1：** ICML 2026

</details>

<a id="paper-33"></a>

### 33. [Learning to Be Monitored: Causal Bounds and Intervention-Aware Post-Training Recipes for Chain-of-Thought Monitorability](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31733v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.31733

**作者：** Qiang Fu、Xinyue Li、Andre Wibisono、Arman Cohan

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI、stat.ML

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31733v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.31733v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文指出 CoT 监控评估中的 TPR\_mc 会高估「干预真正导致的行为」的检出率，据此建立上界并推导互补下界，再构建 control–intervention 配对数据，用 off-policy 自蒸馏、RL 与 on-policy 自蒸馏三种后训练方法提升 CoT 可监控性。

**相关性简评：** 其干预式因果检出率与下界框架可能可供核查，用于判断 latent 推理内容对答案的因果贡献与轨迹监督分配；但属纯文本 CoT 监控，无视觉或多模态状态，迁移需自行设计。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Learning to Be Monitored: Causal Bounds and Intervention-Aware Post-Training Recipes for Chain-of-Thought Monitorability

**abstract/A1：** Chain-of-thought (CoT) monitoring aims to detect potentially harmful behaviors in large language models (LLMs) from their CoTs. Intervention-based evaluations measure this property by asking whether a monitor can detect the role of an intervention from the CoT when the targeted behavior occurs, typically using minimal-criterion sensitivity ($\\mathsf{TPR\_{mc}}$) proposed by~\\citep{guan2025monitoring}. However, a target behavior observed under intervention is not necessarily caused by it, and we show that this metric can overestimate how often a monitor detects target behaviors actually caused by the intervention. Specifically, we establish $\\mathsf{TPR\_{mc}}$ as an upper bound on this causal detection rate and derive a complementary lower bound, which provides a tighter evaluation framework for CoT monitorability. With this framework, we study whether LLMs can be trained to make their CoTs easier to monitor. We first construct a training dataset of paired control--intervention tasks spanning multiple intervention types. Using the dataset, we design and apply three intervention-specific training methods based on off-policy self-distillation, reinforcement learning, and on-policy self-distillation to train separate variants of each base model. Across various model-monitor configurations, all three methods generally improve CoT monitorability over the corresponding base models under our extended evaluation framework. Our code is available at https://github.com/xinyue-lxy/learning-to-be-monitored.

</details>

<a id="paper-34"></a>

### 34. [Efficient Omni-modal Large Language Model Inference via Per-Query Modality Selection](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31768v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.31768

**作者：** Shuqi Liu、Yuzhou Cao、Luke Ong

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31768v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.31768v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文研究 omni-modal LLM 的按查询模态选择：把「为每个查询提供哪些模态」表述为精度-成本优化问题，用成本敏感学习训练选择策略，并在 WorldSense 上以输入 token、延迟、费用三种成本衡量其收益。

**相关性简评：** 其成本敏感的选择策略与精度-成本权衡可供核查是否能迁移到 LVR 中「提供哪些视觉证据、是否继续 LOOK」的动态决策；但原文只调整输入模态配置，不涉及内部推理状态或 latent 生成。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Efficient Omni-modal Large Language Model Inference via Per-Query Modality Selection

**abstract/A1：** Omni-modal large language models (Omni-LLMs) can jointly process a broad range of modalities, such as text, audio, and video, enabling stronger multimodal understanding and reasoning capabilities, but often at substantial inference cost. We observe that the benefit of additional modalities varies across queries, indicating that a fixed modality configuration can be inefficient. Motivated by this, we study query-wise modality selection, which keeps the underlying Omni-LLM fixed while adaptively selecting which modalities to provide for each query. We formulate the problem as an accuracy-cost optimization problem and develop a cost-sensitive learning approach for modality selection. We evaluate our approach on WorldSense using Qwen2.5-Omni and Qwen3-Omni under three inference-cost measures: input tokens, latency, and monetary cost. Across both models, query-wise modality selection consistently improves the accuracy-cost trade-off and can even outperform the standard setting in which all available modalities are provided to the Omni-LLM. For Qwen2.5-Omni, our cost-sensitive approach matches or exceeds this full-modality accuracy while requiring only approximately 41% of the input tokens, 39% of the latency, and 61% of the monetary cost.

</details>

### 35. [Not All Retained Clients Are Equal: Client Selection for Federated Unlearning](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31786v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.31786

**作者：** Thanh Linh Nguyen、Quoc-Viet Pham

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.DC、cs.ET

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31786v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.31786v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 针对联邦遗忘中「保留客户端并非同等有用」的问题，论文提出 PDF 框架：训练期维护轻量、与目标无关且按关键学习期加权的更新方向与全局对齐历史，收到遗忘请求后据此筛选出全局信息量大但与目标耦合弱的 top-k 客户端做少量轮次重训练式遗忘。

**相关性简评：** 其按路径依赖累积更新历史来排序贡献者、并对训练阶段加权记账的做法，可能为 LVR 中的训练贡献分配与样本/轨迹价值筛选提供核查线索，但与视觉推理状态本身无直接关系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Not All Retained Clients Are Equal: Client Selection for Federated Unlearning

**abstract/A1：** Federated unlearning (FU) aims to remove a designated target client's influence from a trained model without the prohibitive cost of retraining from scratch. Existing methods largely treat unlearning as a post-hoc, stateless operation, overlooking that client influence is path-dependent: contributions made during training, particularly during critical learning periods (CLPs), can have disproportionate and persistent effects. Moreover, knowledge overlap and intermittent client availability make relying on all retained clients inefficient and impractical. We introduce PDF, a path-dependent client-selection framework based on the insight that retained clients are not equally valuable for unlearning. During training, PDF maintains lightweight, target-agnostic, CLP-weighted histories summarizing each client's accumulated update direction and global-model alignment. Upon an unlearning request, these histories identify and rank retained clients that are globally informative yet weakly coupled to the target, enabling retraining-based unlearning with only the top-$k$ clients over a small number of rounds. Experiments on FMNIST, CIFAR-10, and CIFAR-100 under IID and non-IID settings show that PDF achieves effective forgetting and strong retained utility with substantially fewer retained clients. In particular, PDF achieves $0\\%$ forgetting accuracy with retraining-like MIA AUC on non-IID CIFAR-10, improves retained accuracy by up to $6.91$ percentage points over the strongest competing FU baseline on CIFAR-100, and reduces communication by approximately $69\\%$ and computation by $57$--$61\\%$ relative to Retrain.

**comment/C1：** Accepted at NewInML @ NeurIPS 2026

</details>

<a id="paper-36"></a>

### 36. [Phronix: The Capability-Centric Learning Paradigm for Agents](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31789v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.31789

**作者：** Hariharan Devarajan、Neeraj Rajesh、Aniruddha Marathe、Nikoli Dryden、Kin Ng、Michela Taufer

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31789v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.31789v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 Phronix 以能力为中心的 agent 学习范式，用 predictor–validator–corrector 循环从自身执行中获取、验证并精炼可复用能力，存于能力库，用于科学计算性能工程任务。

**相关性简评：** 其「提出—执行验证—修正」闭环与持久能力库，可能为 LVR 中推理状态的证据验证、停止决策与轨迹监督提供可核查线索，但无视觉或多模态成分。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Phronix: The Capability-Centric Learning Paradigm for Agents

**abstract/A1：** Scientific computing increasingly relies on large language model-based agents for performance engineering tasks such as instrumentation, analysis, and workflow porting. However, agent effectiveness depends heavily on the surrounding harness, which typically relies on manually written skills and curated memory. This limits autonomous learning, forcing agents to repeatedly rediscover solutions rather than retain and reuse knowledge from prior executions. We introduce Phronix, a capability-centric learning paradigm that enables agentic harnesses to acquire, validate, and refine reusable capabilities from their own executions. Phronix operates through a predictor-validator-corrector loop: MCP tools and associated skills propose solutions, tool executions validate them, and observed outcomes refine the stored capabilities. It distills every observed interaction into a capability store and researches a failing task rather than abandoning it. First, such learned capabilities solve a recurring task on the first attempt in 93-95% of cases across every model, significantly reducing solution non-determinism and improving reliability. Second, Phronix turns an agent's own execution into 7.6-30.8 durable capabilities per task without user instruction, and creation falls below two once the needed capability exists. Third, warm-start runs consume only 0.38-0.40 million tokens per task, which is three to four times less than a single baseline attempt, while a cold-start costs no more than the baseline. On the 1000 Genome Pegasus workflow, instrumenting a production application for I/O performance analysis, Phronix passes all 11 correctness checks against six for the baseline harness, and also lifts the task solving rates of two cheaper models from 29% and 27% of SWE-bench tasks solved to 91% and 87%, respectively, matching the performance of the most expensive evaluated model.

**comment/C1：** 11 pages plus references

</details>

<a id="paper-37"></a>

### 37. [CuraLoc: Repository-Level Fault Localization via Planned Code Inspection](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31809v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.31809

**作者：** Sangeeta Warrier、Anush Sankaran、Ryen White

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.SE

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31809v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.31809v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 CuraLoc 做仓库级缺陷定位：先用紧凑的行为与结构描述表示候选文件中的符号，再让模型在单次规划中同时选定起始符号、要检查的代码关系并形成可能的修改假设，之后由确定性检索收集源码并据此排序，避免逐步迭代的模型控制式检索。

**相关性简评：** 其「先规划、后取证据」的一次性检查计划可能为 LVR 中 LOOK 步骤的选择与迭代查看成本提供参照，可供核查是否能改造为视觉证据的预先筛选方案；但材料限于代码检索、无 latent 状态，联系仅停留在方法线索层面。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** CuraLoc: Repository-Level Fault Localization via Planned Code Inspection

**abstract/A1：** A bug report describes what went wrong, but rarely where the code should change. Existing repository-level localization methods often let language models iteratively decide what code to inspect next, requiring repeated model calls and substantial token usage. We investigate whether these inspection decisions can instead be planned before source retrieval. In this research, we introduce CuraLoc, which first selects candidate files and represents their symbols using compact behavioral and structural descriptions. In a single planning decision, the model jointly selects starting symbols, chooses code relationships to inspect from each, and forms hypotheses about likely edits. Deterministic retrieval collects the requested source for a final ranking informed by those hypotheses, concentrating model reasoning on choosing and comparing code rather than directing each retrieval step. We demonstrate the efficacy of our proposed approach on the SWE-Bench Lite and Verified datasets. On SWE Bench Verified with GPT-5.2, CuraLoc raises Func@1 from LocAgent's 53.8% to 59.3% while using 90.3% fewer recorded tokens, and also outperforms RepoSearcher (56.2%) and Agentless (39.6%) on Func@1. On SWE-bench Lite, CuraLoc achieves a high 83.8% Func@5 accuracy improving on Agentless by more than 15% in relative accuracy. Additionally, CuraLoc supports different model backbones, with strong localization accuracy demonstrated using both GPT-5.2 and GPT-6 Astra. These results provide evidence that repository-level code inspection can be planned effectively without iterative model-controlled navigation.

</details>

<a id="paper-38"></a>

### 38. [PaperFormalizer: Author-in-the-Loop Autoformalization of Research Papers in Lean](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31810v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.31810

**作者：** Jiyuan Tan、Vasilis Syrgkanis、Jose Blanchet

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.LO

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31810v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.31810v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 PaperFormalizer：在编码智能体中把论文 LaTeX 源码转成语句依赖图，逐条做自然语言审计，再在 Lean/Mathlib 中形式化并证明；遇歧义或需修复时暂停并交由作者接受、拒绝或改向，最终产出修订论文、变更报告及与语句对应的 Lean 代码。

**相关性简评：** 其「分解—审计—形式化验证—人审修订」流程可供核查如何为 latent 推理的中间结论提供可验证监督与停止/改向信号，但论文本身不涉及视觉或多模态推理，迁移需较多改造。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** PaperFormalizer: Author-in-the-Loop Autoformalization of Research Papers in Lean

**abstract/A1：** Even a carefully proofread paper can leave small gaps in its proofs or its stated assumptions. We introduce PaperFormalizer, a Lean-based agentic pipeline that helps authors find and close such gaps in their own papers and turn each paper into a Lean-verified version. Starting from the LaTeX source, PaperFormalizer transcribes the paper into a dependency graph of statements, audits each statement in natural language for faithfulness, ambiguity and correctness, and then formalizes and proves the statements in Lean against Mathlib, optional domain libraries and the author's personal toolkit. Only the author knows what a statement is meant to say and how a formalization gap in a result is best fixed. The pipeline therefore stops and asks the author whenever a statement admits more than one reading, presents every repair, in natural language and TeX symbols, as a proposal that the author can accept, decline or redirect, and leaves to the author every decision that could potentially change what the paper claims. The output is an annotated and revised version of the paper, a report of the changes, and Lean code in which each declaration is linked to the statement it formalizes. The author's toolkit grows as more papers are formalized, and authors can share their verified papers so that researchers who build on them can reuse their Lean code. By making verified papers practical to produce, PaperFormalizer aims to make research that rests on mathematical proofs more robust. PaperFormalizer runs inside a coding agent (Claude Code or Codex) and is available at https://github.com/Jiyuan-Tan/PaperFormalizer. Papers with Lean code shared by users are available at https://paperformalizer.org.

</details>

<a id="paper-39"></a>

### 39. [Don't Forget What You Just Learned: Self-Directed Repair Turns Agent Mistakes Into Lasting Lessons](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31846v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.31846

**作者：** Yuval Kansal、Karen Ullrich、Polina Kirichenko、Ilia Kulikov、Niraj K. Jha、Mohammad Pezeshki、Mark Ibrahim

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31846v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.31846v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 Self-Direction：agent 任务失败后反思自身轨迹、提取教训并即时蒸馏为约 0.02% 参数量的低秩 adapter，存入持久教训库并按需路由复用，在 GAIA、Claw-Eval、Terminal-Bench 2.1 上提升成功率。

**相关性简评：** 把轨迹反思压缩进低秩 adapter 并以相似度路由复用，可供核查 LVR 中推理状态或技能的持久化、检索与跨任务迁移做法，但该工作本身不涉及视觉 latent 推理，迁移线索需人工判断。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Don't Forget What You Just Learned: Self-Directed Repair Turns Agent Mistakes Into Lasting Lessons

**abstract/A1：** Intelligent agents should learn from their mistakes so that experience leads to continuous improvement. Yet today's agents recover from a mistake only temporarily: in-context reflections are easily forgotten across sessions and compete for limited context, while full-parameter fine-tuning is expensive and can interfere with existing capabilities. We introduce Self-Direction, a mechanism for agents to learn from mistakes that combines the benefits of temporary in-context retries and policy updates. When an agent fails a task, it reflects on its own trajectory in order to extract a lesson and immediately distills it into a lightweight low-rank adapter. By keeping the original weights frozen, Self-Direction prevents adapter training from overwriting base-model parameters while training additional parameters equivalent to ~0.02% of the base model's parameter count for each adapter. The agent then saves these adapters in a persistent library of lessons, automatically routing them to solve related problems later. Experiments with agents based on open-weight models from the Qwen family across open-ended research (GAIA), tool-use workflows (Claw-Eval), and terminal tasks (Terminal-Bench 2.1) show that our method improves overall success rates by 8.9 to 17.0 percentage points and performs competitively with frontier systems. We find that Self-Direction reduces repeated mistakes across future encounters of a task, nearly doubling the reliability of an agent's corrections. Learned lessons also transfer: a handful of adapters generalize to dozens of new tasks without additional training. Ultimately, this self-directed repair transforms everyday mistakes into lasting lessons.

</details>

<a id="paper-40"></a>

### 40. [Rethinking Multi-View Fusion for Urban Representation Learning](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31850v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.31850

**作者：** Sean Bin Yang、Zongyi Xu、Jilin Hu、Ying Sun、Kristian Torp、Bin Yang、Torben Bach Pedersen、Christian S. Jensen

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31850v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.31850v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文针对城市多视图表示学习中静态融合与训练目标梯度失衡的问题，提出 PRISM：用稀疏 MoE 路由按区域自适应分配各视图权重，并以 PID 式调节机制缓解梯度冲突。

**相关性简评：** 其按样本/区域动态调节模态权重的路由，以及多目标梯度冲突的调控手段，可能为 LVR 中视觉证据筛选与推理/重构等多损失平衡提供可核查的训练与融合线索，但无视觉推理或 latent 状态内容。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Rethinking Multi-View Fusion for Urban Representation Learning

**abstract/A1：** Urban region representation learning encodes heterogeneous urban signals, such as mobility flows, points of interest, and land-use patterns, into unified embeddings for downstream tasks, including check-in prediction, crime forecasting, and service demand estimation. Existing approaches rely on globally shared fusion and joint optimization, implicitly assuming uniform modality importance and naturally balanced multi-objective training. However, urban data is inherently heterogeneous. Our empirical analysis reveals two structural issues: (i) modality utility varies markedly across regions, causing static fusion to overemphasize signals from certain modalities; and (ii) heterogeneous objectives exhibit persistent gradient imbalance, causing representational bias and unstable training. We present PRISM, a self-adjusting framework for adaptive multi-view learning. PRISM adapts sparse MoE routing to learn region-specific view allocations, while a PID (Proportional Integral Derivative)-inspired regulation mechanism mitigates gradient conflicts during optimization. Experiments on three large-scale urban datasets show that PRISM achieves competitive performance compared with strong multi-view baselines, while providing more stable optimization behavior and more balanced region representations.

**comment/C1：** Accepted by PVLDB 2027

</details>

<a id="paper-41"></a>

### 41. [Cautious Conflict-Free Inverse Gradient Methods](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31855v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.31855

**作者：** Damian Hattler、Qiang Liu、Nils Thuerey

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31855v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.31855v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文针对 PINNs 多目标训练中边界、初值与残差损失之间的梯度冲突，指出 ConFIG 生成的无冲突方向经 Adam 等自适应优化器处理后可能不再无冲突，提出仅三行的 Cautious ConFIG 掩码扩展，并在三个 PDE 基准与玩具系统上考察其性能与稳定性。

**相关性简评：** 与视觉推理主线无直接关联，但其「在权重更新前按各任务梯度方向做谨慎掩码」的多目标冲突处理方式，可供核查是否适用于 LVR 训练中答案损失与视觉证据、latent 对齐损失相互冲突的情形。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Cautious Conflict-Free Inverse Gradient Methods

**abstract/A1：** Partial differential equations (PDEs) are indispensable tools for modeling complex phenomena in physics and engineering. Physics-Informed Neural Networks (PINNs) have emerged as a powerful alternative to traditional numerical solvers by directly incorporating physical constraints into neural network loss functions. Despite their advantages, PINN training requires multi-objective optimization, which often leads to significant gradient conflicts among boundary, initial, and residual loss terms. The Conflict-Free Inverse Gradient (ConFIG) method addresses these conflicts; however, integrating ConFIG with adaptive optimizers such as Adam introduces a critical vulnerability: the conflict-free direction generated by ConFIG is further processed by the optimizer, and the resulting weight update may no longer be conflict-free with respect to the task gradients. To address this issue, Cautious ConFIG is proposed as a concise three-line extension to the ConFIG algorithm that empirically restores the conflict-freeness guarantee prior to the weight update. The method is evaluated on three PINN benchmarks: the 1D Viscous Burgers' equation, the 1D Nonlinear Schrödinger equation, and the 2D Kovasznay flow. Experimental results demonstrate consistent performance improvements over the standard ConFIG baseline. Additionally, the stabilizing effect of cautious masking and its reaction to conflict severity, task number, and optimizer lag are investigated using a simple toy system.

**comment/C1：** Code available at \[https://github.com/damiha/CautiousConFIG\](https://github.com/damiha/CautiousConFIG)

</details>

<a id="paper-42"></a>

### 42. [Forecasting Associations, Not Interventions: A Preregistered Causal Audit of Covariate-Aware Time Series Foundation Models](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31869v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.31869

**作者：** Zong-Han Bai

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31869v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.31869v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文审计协变量感知时间序列基础模型在 what-if 查询中给出的是条件关联还是干预效应，并用理论推导、预注册行为实验、线性探针与干预后训练进行检验。

**相关性简评：** 其探针发现嵌入编码了预测输出未使用的因果信息，且干预后训练可部分修复，可能为 LVR 中「latent 保留但答案未使用」的信息坍缩诊断与监督提供核查线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Forecasting Associations, Not Interventions: A Preregistered Causal Audit of Covariate-Aware Time Series Foundation Models

**abstract/A1：** Covariate-aware time series foundation models (TSFMs) accept a future path for a control variable and return a forecast, inviting what-if queries. The query is interventional; the models are trained on observational data. We determine whether their answers are the conditional slope $\\partial E\[Y\|X\]$ or the interventional effect $\\partial E\[Y\|do(X)\]$, at three levels. Theory: a Bayes-optimal observational predictor answers interventional queries associationally unless the adjustment set is available and used; for in-context least squares we derive the resulting confounding gap -- a model's effect estimate under an observational minus under a randomized context -- in closed form, a parameter-free reference line. Behaviour: on a preregistered grid with exact counterfactual ground truth, Chronos-2, TiRex-2, TabPFN-TS and TimesFM-2.5 all track this line ($R^2$ 0.90-1.00) and respond more confounded than regression itself (slopes 1.3-1.8), on linear and nonlinear structural models. Pairs of models with identical observational laws prove this is not an identifiability limit: when the effect is identifiable, least squares recovers 100% of the true effect difference, Chronos-2 75%, TiRex-2 34%. Representation: linear probes decode the data-generating regime (94-96%) and the true effect ($R^2$ 0.7-0.9) from embeddings that the forecasts ignore. On two physical systems with randomized actuators, pretrained models under-respond by 2-5x and ignore in-window confounding. Minutes of interventional post-training on 3,000 synthetic series cut the gap by up to 83% and transfer outside the training prior. Its forecasting cost is a replay-distribution effect: replaying the model's own pretraining prior cuts it from +47% to +10% MASE, and with low-rank adaptation yields a recipe meeting the preregistered criterion over three seeds (52-61% gap reduction at +1-3% cost) while improving the DoTime benchmark.

**comment/C1：** 21 pages, 11 figures, 12 tables

</details>

### 43. [Effectiveness of Curriculum Learning Depends on Reward Sparsity and Competing Optima](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31884v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.31884

**作者：** John J. Vastola、Ann Huang、Satpreet H. Singh、Samuel J. Gershman、Kanaka Rajan

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31884v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.31884v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文构建开环、分幕式目标拦截的最小策略学习模型，结合 REINFORCE 的理论与数值分析，考察课程学习在稠密/稀疏奖励及竞争最优解下的效果差异。

**相关性简评：** 其「课程通过降低奖励稀疏性并重塑奖励地形以避开坏局部最优」的结论，可能为 latent 推理轨迹的分阶段训练与监督设计提供方法线索，但向 VLM 推理迁移的可行性仍需核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Effectiveness of Curriculum Learning Depends on Reward Sparsity and Competing Optima

**abstract/A1：** While animals and people tend to learn a task more quickly and reliably when they first train on simpler versions of it, curriculum learning's effectiveness in artificial settings varies, and appears substantially greater in reinforcement learning than in supervised learning. In this work, we construct and analyze a minimal model of policy learning to better understand why. We consider an open-loop, episodic target interception task whose parameters---especially how close the agent must be to the target to receive an informative reward signal---can be chosen so that the agent either receives informative rewards frequently ('dense' rewards) or rarely ('sparse' rewards). We mathematically and numerically analyze the dynamics of REINFORCE-based policy learning in both cases. In the dense reward case, curricula have only marginal benefits; in the sparse case, curricula can be required for learning to occur at all. We find that this is especially true when the agent has the conflicting goals of minimizing effort and accumulating high reward, since this can produce distinct reward landscape optima which compete with one another. Our characterization of useful curricula in this setting indicates that they address two issues: they (i) make rewards less sparse to speed up learning, and (ii) shape the reward landscape to steer agents away from bad local optima.

**comment/C1：** Accepted to NeurIPS 2026

</details>

### 44. [Scaling Transformer Energy-based Models For High Quality Image Synthesis](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31889v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.31889

**作者：** Jungsoo Lee、Alexi Gladstone、Yilun Du

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31889v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.31889v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 Transformer Energy-Based Model：在 latent 空间用 transformer 作能量函数，多层能量求和以融合局部纹理到全局语义约束，并用视觉基础模型对齐中间表示、改进采样策略，提升 ImageNet 条件生成质量。

**相关性简评：** 虽属图像生成而非推理研究，但其「能量打分—迭代采样精修—以采样步数换质量」的推理期计算缩放与多层能量聚合，可能为 latent 状态的验证评分、停止判据与计算分配提供可核查线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Scaling Transformer Energy-based Models For High Quality Image Synthesis

**abstract/A1：** Energy-Based Models (EBMs) are a flexible class of generative models that decouple probabilistic modeling from the generation process. This decoupling directly enables compositional generation and inference-time scaling, where sample quality can be refined simply by increasing sampling steps. However, severe challenges in high-dimensional sampling and training instabilities with deep architectures have restricted EBMs to low-dimensional regimes. To resolve these bottlenecks, we introduce the Transformer Energy-Based Model (TEM), a scalable transformer energy function operating entirely within a latent space. On top of this backbone, we extract energies at multiple layers and sum them, composing complementary constraints from local texture to global semantics. Furthermore, two components make this high-quality at scale: Positive Representation Alignment (PRA), which supervises TEM's intermediate representations with a vision foundation model, and advanced sampling strategies that improve sample quality at inference. TEM-XL achieves an FID-50K of 3.82 on class-conditional ImageNet 256$\\times$256 without classifier-free guidance, showing that a contrastive EBM can generate high-fidelity images. On ImageNet 512$\\times$512, TEM achieves an FID-50K of 8.24, demonstrating that the approach scales to higher resolution. We further validate the core promises of EBMs: compositional generation, zero-shot inpainting, and out-of-distribution detection.

</details>

<a id="paper-45"></a>

### 45. [Beyond On-Policy vs. Off-Policy: At What Level Should LLM Agent Distillation Follow the Student?](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31918v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.31918

**作者：** Ya Gao、Zhengtao Zou、Pekka Marttinen

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31918v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.31918v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出分级 agent 蒸馏框架，把 on-policy 程度拆成「诱导交互历史」与「生成当前动作 token 前缀」两级，并通过自蒸馏在三个模型、四个交互环境中比较两级配对、混合比例与训练调度的效果。

**相关性简评：** 纯文本交互 agent、无视觉成分；但两级 on-policiness 的配对、混合比例及「先教师上下文、后全 on-policy」的调度线索，可能可供核查 LVR 中推理轨迹的监督来源与训练课程设计。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Beyond On-Policy vs. Off-Policy: At What Level Should LLM Agent Distillation Follow the Student?

**abstract/A1：** Distilling interactive LLM agents requires choosing between teacher-generated demonstrations and student-generated experience, corresponding broadly to off-policy and on-policy distillation. While both have proven effective, we argue that this distinction is inherently hierarchical: the policy that induces an interaction history need not be the same as the policy that generates the token prefix of the current action. We introduce a hierarchical agent distillation framework that formalizes these two levels of on-policiness and enables explicit control over their pairing, mixture proportions, and training schedule. We study this problem through self-distillation, where a frozen teacher has access to task guidance unavailable to the student, and conduct a three-stage controlled study across three models and four interactive environments. We find that student-induced histories paired with teacher-generated action prefixes generally provide the strongest standalone training signal, whereas following the student at both levels generally performs worst. Nevertheless, fully on-policy data becomes beneficial when incorporated into a mixture with other configurations. Moreover, mixtures with identical teacher/student proportions at each level can yield substantially different performance, showing that how the two levels are paired matters beyond the overall amount of on-policy data. Finally, front-loading fully teacher-generated contexts and delaying fully on-policy contexts improves over a static mixture. These findings provide practical principles for deciding where, how much, and when agent distillation should follow the student.

**comment/C1：** Under review

</details>

<a id="paper-46"></a>

### 46. [Synthetic-to-Real Text-Based Person Anomaly Search via Multi-Model Retrieval Fusion and Cross-Encoder Reranking](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31930v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.31930

**作者：** Eugene Denteh、Blessing Agyei Kyem、Andrews Danyo、Joshua Kofi Asamoah、Richard Dzinyela、Solomon Owusu-Ansah、Dickson G. Asiedu、Armstrong Aboah

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31930v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.31930v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文构建了一个面向合成到真实迁移的文本行人异常检索系统：用双向对比学习、显式负采样、假负例掩蔽和域随机化微调 Qwen3-VL 检索模型，融合六个检查点与 SigLIP2 的四视图图库编码分数，再用 Qwen3-VL-Reranker 对候选重排，并用纯合成数据构建的验证协议选参数。

**相关性简评：** 其「多视图编码 + 多模型分数融合 + top-k 候选交交叉编码器复核」构成分阶段证据筛选流程，或可为 LVR 中视觉证据挑选与候选验证提供工程线索，但本身不含推理期 latent 状态，需核查迁移价值。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Synthetic-to-Real Text-Based Person Anomaly Search via Multi-Model Retrieval Fusion and Cross-Encoder Reranking

**abstract/A1：** Text-based person anomaly search requires a model to retrieve individuals from a large image gallery using natural-language descriptions of both appearance and behavior. This task is challenging because model training relies on synthetic image-text pairs, whereas evaluation is performed on real surveillance images containing domain differences, near-duplicate frames, and many visually similar distractors. We present a multi-model retrieval and reranking framework designed to improve synthetic-to-real transfer. The framework first adapts Qwen3-VL-Embedding-8B to synthetic person descriptions using bidirectional contrastive learning, explicit negative sampling, false-negative masking, and domain randomization. It then combines the normalized scores of six complementary Qwen3-VL checkpoints with those of a frozen SigLIP2 model using four-view gallery encoding. The fused retrieval scores identify the strongest candidates, which are jointly evaluated with the query using Qwen3-VL-Reranker-8B. The cross-encoder and retrieval scores are subsequently blended to produce the final ranking. We also construct a distractor-matched validation protocol entirely from synthetic data to select checkpoint weights, fusion coefficients, candidate depths, and reranking parameters without accessing the official test images. The final system achieves 94.7815% mAP and 90.8493% R@1 under the official AI City Challenge 2026 Track 4 test setting, with a reported eighth-place finish on the public leaderboard.

</details>

<a id="paper-47"></a>

### 47. [LOCI: Localized Continuous Control for Multi-Subject Image Editing](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31950v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.31950

**作者：** Sai Vidyaranya Nuthalapati、Shlok Kumar Mishra

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31950v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.31950v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 LOCI 多主体图像编辑方法：用主体级 attention binding 实现空间隔离，并以 Regional LoRA 把 LoRA 的标量系数替换为与空间布局对齐的 per-token 缩放向量，只在单一强度点训练，推理时缩放区域残差得到连续可调编辑强度。

**相关性简评：** 其「按空间布局对 token 加权、连续调节区域残差」的机制，可能与 LVR 中区域化视觉证据筛选、局部 latent 状态的强度控制存在方法上的可借鉴之处，值得核查；但论文本身属图像编辑，未涉及推理过程或 latent 推理状态。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** LOCI: Localized Continuous Control for Multi-Subject Image Editing

**abstract/A1：** Recent instruction-following image editors such as Flux.1-Kontext enable text-driven editing but lack fine-grained control over where and how much an edit is applied. Existing approaches provide either continuous but global control or spatially localized editing at a fixed strength. To our knowledge, none jointly supports explicit spatial localization, continuous per-region strength, and independent simultaneous control of multiple subjects. We present LOCI (LOcalized Continuous control for multi- subject Image editing). Given a multi-subject image with per-subject regions and instructions, LOCI edits each subject at an independently chosen, continuously adjustable strength, for instance gradually aging one person while adding a beard to another with minimal cross-region leakage. LOCI pairs subject-level attention binding for spatial isolation with a Regional LoRA layer that replaces the LoRA's scalar coefficient with a per-token scaling vector aligned to the spatial layout. A single adapter is trained at one suppression point, and continuous edit strengths follow by rescaling its regional residual at inference. Experiments on multi-subject scenes demonstrate that LOCI achieves strong edit fidelity with significantly reduced cross-region interference compared to baselines, while maintaining continuous control over edit strength

</details>

<a id="paper-48"></a>

### 48. [STEP: Surgical Point Tracking from Sparse Endpoints and Pseudo-trajectories](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31951v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.31951

**作者：** Bing Fan、Anwesa Choudhuri、Zhongpai Gao、Meng Zheng、Benjamin Planche、Terrence Chen、Heng Fan、Ziyan Wu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31951v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.31951v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 STEP 两阶段框架，用冻结教师模型生成候选轨迹，再以时间可逆性与立体一致性筛选出稠密监督，并配合端点锚定损失与往返一致性损失训练因果学生跟踪器，实现仅凭单路 RGB 的手术点跟踪。

**相关性简评：** 用一致性检验筛选伪轨迹、并以稀疏真实端点锚定终点来抑制漂移的做法，可能为 LVR 中推理轨迹的监督质量过滤与漂移约束提供可核查线索，但任务本身是手术视频对应跟踪，与推理期状态生成无直接联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** STEP: Surgical Point Tracking from Sparse Endpoints and Pseudo-trajectories

**abstract/A1：** Surgical point tracking requires persistent correspondence through tissue deformation and occlusion, yet dense trajectory annotations are costly to obtain. We present STEP, a two-stage framework that learns from surgical video and sparse physical endpoints. First, a frozen offline teacher generates candidate trajectories, and temporal reversibility and stereo consistency select dense supervision for a causal student. Second, a robust physical endpoint loss anchors terminal correspondence, while a round-trip loss constrains temporal consistency. A gradient-free prefix exposes training to accumulated drift, while a final differentiable window bounds activation memory. The trained tracker processes a single RGB stream with fixed weights. In STIR-to-VL-SurgPT transfer, temporal-stereo union selection reduces endpoint error by 7.0% relative to unfiltered trajectories. On a post-hoc diagnostic of 29 VL-SurgPT clips excluded from the endpoint-training manifest, STEP reduces endpoint error from 31.52 to 16.80 pixels relative to dense-label fine-tuning. These results support combining curated trajectories with physical observations to train surgical point trackers.

</details>

### 49. [A Mathematical Theory of the Jacobian Lens](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31955v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.31955

**作者：** Mitchell Ostrow、Daoyuan Qian、Toni Liu、Ila Fiete

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI、q-bio.NC

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31955v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.31955v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文为 Jacobian lens 建立数学理论，说明其偏好识别跨层放大、跨 token 位置近似恒定且跨上下文一致的相干方向，并在 Bayes filter 与 toy transformer 上借助 HMM 验证，还提出强调不同时间尺度的 ω-J-lenses。

**相关性简评：** 其对方向跨层传播、位置稳定性与多时间尺度的刻画，可能为核查 LVR 中 latent 工作区表示是否持续保留、深度和位置如何影响状态读取提供表示分析工具。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** A Mathematical Theory of the Jacobian Lens

**abstract/A1：** Recent work from Anthropic introduced a new interpretability tool, called the Jacobian lens (J-lens, Gurnee et al., 2026). This method identified linear directions in the hidden states of frontier language models that had properties reminiscent of working memory-like representations. However, it is unclear what mechanistic properties of such features make them detectable to the J-lens. To this end, we introduce a mathematical theory of the J-lens, and show that it preferentially identifies coherent features -- directions that act like leading, spatially coherent Lyapunov modes. Small perturbations along these directions amplify across layers, remain roughly constant across token positions, and are consistent across different contexts. While we are agnostic to whether these properties are necessarily associated with a \`\`Global Workspace'', the analytically derived preferences of the J-lens nevertheless explain prior empirical applied to both model classes. We validate our analysis on a Bayes filter and a toy transformer, each optimally predicting next tokens emitted from a Hidden Markov Model (HMM). We show how modulating task parameters that change the coherence of features in the Bayes filter correspondingly and predictively modulates the response of the J-lens . We also introduce of a family of J-lenses built to emphasize different temporal scales of information processing in LLMs, which we call $ω$-J-lenses.

**comment/C1：** 27 pages, 19 figures

</details>

### 50. [Spectra: Multi-Scale Implicit Decomposition for Deepfake Detection](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31979v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.31979

**作者：** Dhananjaya Jayasundara、Kartik Narayan、Vishal M. Patel

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31979v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.31979v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 Spectra 深伪检测框架：为每张图像拟合 INR，从重建过程中提取多层级热图，再与语义特征双向融合，以提升跨生成器和跨数据集的泛化性。

**相关性简评：** 其“读取图像专属 INR 内部激活作为重建动态特征”的做法，或可核查为一种探测视觉细节是否保留于内部状态的工具，但论文本身研究的是真伪判别，与 LVR 推理状态、证据获取和停止决策无直接联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Spectra: Multi-Scale Implicit Decomposition for Deepfake Detection

**abstract/A1：** Deepfakes increasingly undermine trust in digital media, yet existing detectors often struggle to generalize across unseen manipulation techniques, compression levels, and dataset shifts. Most approaches rely on RGB appearance, frequency-domain artifacts, or their combination, learning cues present in the final synthesized image. While effective in-distribution, such cues are frequently generator-specific and can diminish as synthesis models evolve. We argue that a complementary perspective can be obtained through implicit neural representations (INRs), which expose how an image is reconstructed rather than merely how it appears. By fitting an INR to an image and analyzing its internal activations, one can reveal reconstruction dynamics that are difficult to observe in the image domain alone and may capture more intrinsic differences between genuine and synthetic content. To this end, we propose Spectra, a deepfake detection framework that combines semantic visual representations with artifact-aware cues derived from INR reconstruction. Spectra extracts multi-level reconstruction heatmaps from image-specific INRs and jointly models them with semantic features through a bidirectional fusion mechanism. This enables direct interaction between image semantics and reconstruction artifacts, allowing the model to identify forgery cues that are less dependent on specific generation pipelines. Extensive experiments on multiple deepfake benchmarks demonstrate improved robustness and cross-dataset generalization, consistently outperforming detectors based solely on RGB appearance or semantic representations. Furthermore, we show that the proposed representation extends beyond deepfake detection to face presentation attack detection, highlighting its potential as a unified framework for facial attack detection.

</details>

<a id="paper-51"></a>

### 51. [Compatible Likelihoods for Flow Matching on Manifolds](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31981v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.31981

**作者：** Lucas Ng、Georgios Batzolis、Mark Girolami

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、stat.ML

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31981v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.31981v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文研究流匹配（flow matching）在流形上的似然目标设计：在 1D 平坦环面上指出已有 Riemannian flow matching 扩展会被整体优化为把样本推离数据的速度场，提出「compatibility」判据来刻画总体最优解是否恢复数据与耦合诱导的边缘速度场，并给出误差界，进而以端点的指数族目标构造既 compatible 又只支撑在可行速度上的新目标。

**相关性简评：** 若 LVR 用 flow matching 或扩散在受约束的 latent 空间（如球面、归一化隐状态）中生成推理状态，其「目标是否恢复正确边缘速度场」的兼容性条件与按速度大小/方向区分权重的损失，可供核查训练目标是否会系统偏离期望的 latent 轨迹分布。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Compatible Likelihoods for Flow Matching on Manifolds

**abstract/A1：** On the simplest non-Euclidean manifold, the 1D flat torus, we exhibit a distribution where a recently proposed extension of Riemannian flow matching is globally optimized by a velocity field that pushes samples away from the data distribution. We trace this failure mode to a property we call compatibility; a likelihood-based flow matching objective is compatible if its population minimizer recovers the marginal velocity field induced by the data and coupling. We give a general condition for compatibility, apply it to existing methods, and bound the worst-case error in terms of a residual component vanishing under compatibility. Next, we note that existing compatible objectives put density on velocities that are infeasible on compact manifolds. We propose an objective built from an exponential family over endpoints, with the conditional velocity as sufficient statistic, which is both compatible and supported only on feasible velocities. This manifests as a reweighting of the flow matching loss which, on constant-curvature manifolds, treats errors in flow speed and direction differently. Empirically, our method recovers the correct velocity field on the test case, outperforms prior methods on high-dimensional product tori, and obtains substantial NLL improvements over prior work on two of four standard geospatial benchmarks on the sphere at moderate runtime overhead. On a large dataset of high-dimensional human poses, our method outperforms prior work according to sample-based metrics.

**comment/C1：** 40th Conference on Neural Information Processing Systems (NeurIPS 2026)

</details>

<a id="paper-52"></a>

### 52. [REDAR: Reinforcement-Driven Adaptive Regularisation for Class Incremental Learning](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31991v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.31991

**作者：** Akila Ayanthi、Darshana Priyasad、Tharindu Fernando、Sridha Sridharan、Clinton Fookes、Peyman Moghadam

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31991v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.31991v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 针对类增量学习中固定正则化系数难以适配不同阶段的问题，提出 REDAR：用轻量 RL 智能体根据当前性能指标与数据统计输出每阶段的蒸馏系数，并在初始阶段构造的伪增量任务上训练策略后直接部署。在 CIFAR-100 与 Tiny-ImageNet 上报告遗忘减少、前向迁移提升，且短伪任务训练的策略可迁移到更长序列。

**相关性简评：** 与视觉推理主线关联较弱，但「由紧凑状态驱动的策略调控标量控制量」以及「短视界策略迁移到长序列」可供核查是否能类比 LVR 中的动态计算分配、停止决策与长度泛化。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** REDAR: Reinforcement-Driven Adaptive Regularisation for Class Incremental Learning

**abstract/A1：** Regularisation-based class-incremental learning (CIL) methods control the stability-plasticity trade-off through fixed coefficients tuned offline, independently of the continual learning objective and blind to phase-specific variations in data volume, task difficulty, and class similarity. We argue this is a fundamental mismatch and propose REDAR: Reinforcement-Driven Adaptive Regularisation for Class Incremental Learning, which treats regularisation strength selection as a sequential decision problem. At each incremental phase, a lightweight reinforcement learning (RL) agent observes a compact state of current performance metrics and incoming data statistics, and outputs the distillation coefficients for that phase. To respect CIL's constraint on historical data access, the policy is trained entirely on pseudo-CIL tasks constructed from the initial phase, then deployed on the incremental sequence without further adaptation. Experiments on CIFAR-100 and Tiny-ImageNet demonstrate that REDAR reduces forgetting and improves forward transfer over fixed and heuristic baselines, and that policies trained on short pseudo-task horizons transfer effectively to longer incremental runs.

**comment/C1：** The 28th International Conference on Digital Image Computing: Techniques and Applications (DICTA 2026)

</details>

### 53. [Can Test-Time Memory Evolution Outperform Test-Time Training for Scientific Discovery?](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32046v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32046

**作者：** Yepeng Liu、Shengmai Chen、Xuandong Zhao、Chengzhi Liu、Dawn Song、Xin Eric Wang、Yuheng Bu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32046v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32046v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 Test-Time Memory Evolution（TTME），在测试阶段通过蒸馏经验持续更新外部记忆来引导后续科学发现，不更新模型权重，并在相同框架下与 test-time training 比较准确率与计算成本。

**相关性简评：** 其「以外部记忆演化替代权重更新」的测试时适应方案，可能为 LVR 中推理状态/工作区的更新与计算成本权衡提供可供核查的线索；但任务为纯文本数学发现，与视觉证据和 latent 表示无直接联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Can Test-Time Memory Evolution Outperform Test-Time Training for Scientific Discovery?

**abstract/A1：** Recent \\emph{test-time training} (TTT) frameworks have enabled open-weight large language models (LLMs) to outperform inference-only frameworks powered by frontier models on scientific discovery tasks. However, TTT introduces additional computational cost for online weight updates during the discovery process, making computational efficiency an important consideration when scaling to larger models. This motivates us to explore an efficient \\emph{test-time adaptation} method for scientific discovery. We introduce \\emph{Test-Time Memory Evolution} (TTME), a plug-and-play test-time adaptation method, as an alternative to TTT. TTME continually updates the external memory by distilling the experience to guide subsequent discovery without updating model weights. To have a clean comparison, we evaluate TTME and TTT under the same discovery framework, replacing only the test-time adaptation method. Our evaluation shows that TTME outperforms TTT on all five mathematical tasks, while reducing computational cost by $82.8$-$84.6\\%$ in FLOPs and $47.7$-$58.4\\%$ in runtime. These results establish TTME as an effective and computationally efficient alternative to TTT in the evaluated discovery settings.

</details>

### 54. [HyScale: Scaling Long-Context Training for Learned Sparse Attention](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32057v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32057

**作者：** Yangsheng Deng、Xinjing Huang、Yulong Wang、Yunzhuo Liu、Jun Hu、Shuyong Tang、Junchi Zeng、Mingji Han、Ray Ying、Key Zhang、Luo Mai

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.DC

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32057v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32057v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 HyScale 长上下文训练系统：用 HyTopK 在不物化全量分数表的情况下在线求出每个 query 的精确 top-k，并用 selection replay 在重计算时复用前向得到的稀疏选择索引，从而缓解 learned sparse attention 中 token 选择的训练瓶颈。

**相关性简评：** 论文把「选择」的输出视为索引集合而非激活并在重算中复用，可能为长上下文 VLM 推理里视觉证据筛选结果的缓存与 LOOK 成本核算提供工程线索；但其本身不涉及推理状态表示或 latent 内容使用，需核查如何迁移。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** HyScale: Scaling Long-Context Training for Learned Sparse Attention

**abstract/A1：** We present HyScale, a long-context training system for learned sparse attention. Learned sparse attention is a promising architecture for million-token contexts, widely adopted in production since DeepSeek-V3.2. Selection, however, grows into the training bottleneck. For each query, it scores every previous token whereas sparse attention reads only $k$; worse, current systems materialize a full score table and repeat selection during recomputation. Our key insight is that the output of selection is not an activation, but rather a set of indices. Building on it, HyScale introduces HyTopK and selection replay. HyTopK finds each query's exact top-$k$ on the fly without a score table, while selection replay reuses forward-pass selection in recomputation. Together with holistic system optimizations tackling end-to-end training challenges, HyScale speeds up GLM-5.1 training on 1M-token sequences by 2.07$\\times$ over Megatron-LM on 512 H20 GPUs. It also speeds up the CSA layers of DeepSeek-V4-Flash and the QSA layers of Qwen3.8-Flash-Next by 2.01$\\times$ and 1.57$\\times$ at 1M tokens on 32 H800 GPUs.

</details>

<a id="paper-55"></a>

### 55. [Concept Acupuncturist: An Efficient and Precise Concept Erasure Method for Diffusion Models via Sparse Autoencoders](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32059v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32059

**作者：** Yiran Xiahou、Siliang Qin、Kai Chen、Shengzhi Zhang、Renyang Liu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI、cs.CR、cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32059v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32059v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 ConceptACP：先把语义概念映射到 U-Net 交叉注意力块并定位最相关的块，再用稀疏自编码器得到的稀疏特征构造修正信号回投到该块输出空间作为监督，从而无需推理期模块即可高效擦除扩散模型中的目标概念。

**相关性简评：** 其「定位概念相关子模块＋用 SAE 稀疏特征做定向干预」的思路，可能为 LVR 中视觉细节的选择性保留或抑制、纠缠概念的解耦与内部状态编辑提供可核查的方法线索；但研究对象是图像生成的遗忘而非多模态推理，需较多核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Concept Acupuncturist: An Efficient and Precise Concept Erasure Method for Diffusion Models via Sparse Autoencoders

**abstract/A1：** Diffusion-based image generation models produce high-quality images but may generate harmful or policy-violating content, motivating machine unlearning (MU) to selectively remove target concepts while preserving general capabilities. However, existing MU approaches often struggle with the distributed and entangled nature of concept representations, leading to inefficient optimization and imprecise edits when applied through large-scale fine-tuning over full diffusion trajectories. To bridge this gap, we propose Concept Acupuncturist (ConceptACP), a novel MU framework that integrates interpretability-guided analysis with inference-free fine-tuning for efficient and precise concept removal. By establishing mappings between semantic concepts and cross-attention blocks in the U-Net denoiser and localizing the most concept-relevant block for targeted optimization, ConceptACP avoids full diffusion trajectory optimization, thereby improving fine-tuning efficiency. To mitigate concept entanglement, we propose a feature-level correction mechanism based on Sparse Autoencoders (SAEs), where SAE-derived sparse, semantically meaningful feature activations are manipulated to construct targeted correction signals, which are then projected back to the output space of the corresponding block and used as supervision for precise concept removal. Consequently, ConceptACP eliminates the need for external inference-time modules while achieving efficient and fine-grained unlearning. Instantiated on SDXL-Turbo, ConceptACP improves the forgetting success rate by 36.34 percentage points in the standard one-step generation setting on average over the best-performing baseline, while preserving image quality and prompt fidelity, and generalizes effectively across other Stable Diffusion backbones. Our code is available at https://github.com/Eran-Xia/ConceptACP.

</details>

<a id="paper-56"></a>

### 56. [Controlled Evaluation of EEG Foundation Models: Random Initialisation, Band-Power and Validation-Selected Probes](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32067v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32067

**作者：** Mingyu Jeon、Suwan Cho、Jae Young Suh

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32067v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32067v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 在统一协议下评测五个公开 EEG 基础模型与一个自研 JEPA 式模型，同时加入三项对照：同一编码器不训练、同数据上拟合的五频带 log-power 特征基线、以及仅依据验证集选层，并覆盖五个基准与四个新增任务族。

**相关性简评：** 其「未训练编码器 + 简单特征基线 + 验证集选层」的对照设计，可供核查 LVR 中 latent 状态是否真正贡献、而非被视觉旁路或基线解释，探针选层偏差的处理也可能迁移到 latent 表征的评测。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Controlled Evaluation of EEG Foundation Models: Random Initialisation, Band-Power and Validation-Selected Probes

**abstract/A1：** EEG foundation models report downstream gains, yet their protocols seldom include three controls. These are the same encoder untrained, spectral features fitted on the same data, and a layer picked without looking at test scores. We therefore evaluate five publicly released EEG foundation models and an in-house JEPA-style model under one protocol with all three controls and several seeds, on five benchmarks and four added task families. The controls change the reading in three ways. First, picking the probed layer by its test score raises the layer-wise peak by an average of +0.034 and reverses the peak-versus-output gap in 9 of 36 cells. Second, with the layer picked on validation, random initialisation matches or beats pretraining for half of the models on sleep and emotion, and no model beats a five-band log-power baseline on emotion or motor imagery. Third, after full fine-tuning only 5 of 30 pairs gain beyond twice their seed spread, and the one clear loss of -0.290 AUROC persists under matched learning rates. From these results we draw four reporting practices, and we release every cell for re-analysis.

</details>

### 57. [Guided Zero-Shot Anomaly Explanation for Anonymized Crowd Video Monitoring: Context, Categorization, and Spatial Localization](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32073v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32073

**作者：** Mulugeta Weldezgina Asres、Lei Jiao、Christian Walter Omlin

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32073v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32073v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 XAVAD，面向匿名化监控视频的零样本异常解释，整合灵活匿名化、异常类型分类、类 Grad-ECLIP 空间热图定位与 VLM 场景上下文描述，并在多个 CCTV/视频数据集上评测准确率与计算成本。

**相关性简评：** 其 prompt 引导的 patch 聚焦（autofocus）与热图定位机制，可能为 LVR 中「LOOK」阶段的视觉证据筛选与计算分配提供方法线索，但论文并未涉及 latent 推理状态，需核查其收益与开销。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Guided Zero-Shot Anomaly Explanation for Anonymized Crowd Video Monitoring: Context, Categorization, and Spatial Localization

**abstract/A1：** Despite the promising development of deep learning for video anomaly detection (VAD), explainability (XAI) of these models remains a challenge. Explainable and privacy-protecting VAD builds transparency and trust that facilitates adoption of computer vision systems, including safety and security monitoring. However, anomaly explainability in privacy-preserving VAD systems is underexplored. In this study, we present the XAVAD approach, a unified descriptive anomaly XAI for an anonymized VAD, that integrates flexible zero-shot anonymization, anomaly type categorization, visual localization, and scene context description. We provide empirical and qualitative assessments of multifaceted explanations integrated with full-body visual anonymization for privacy protection. We adopt a multimodal CLIP embedding for videos, using improved LLM-assisted prompting and pool-scoring mechanisms for anomaly-type identification. We enhance Grad-ECLIP for spatial anomaly heatmap localization, leveraging computation and accuracy through our novel prompt-guided patching autofocus mechanism. We employ VLMs to generate context and reasoning descriptions of detected anomalies through curated prompt engineering. We have evaluated the accuracy and computational cost of XAVAD on several publicly available CCTV and video datasets. Our experiments demonstrate substantial gains of our XAVAD over state-of-the-art benchmark approaches and provide insights into privacy protection trade-offs in XAI for VAD. We further present ablation studies and assessments across diverse configurations and modeling alternatives to demonstrate the efficacy of our proposed methods. Code: https://github.com/muleina/XAVAD}{https://github.com/muleina/XAVAD .

**comment/C1：** 21 pages, 12 figures, 13 tables, preprint

</details>
