### 58. [PRISM: Promoting Sampling Diversity of Large Language Models with Implicit Stochastic Modulation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32101v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32101

**作者：** Qixin Xu、Zihan Zhu、Xiaojiang Liu、Bohan Lyu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32101v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32101v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 PRISM 训练期框架：用子模函数筛选多样化且正确的多教师解答，并将特殊 token 扰动转为随机嵌入调制，使输出多样性内化为模型能力，在写作、数学、代码及 RL rollout 上缓解多样性崩塌。

**相关性简评：** 「以随机嵌入调制向内部表示注入扰动、并构造多样化正确轨迹」的做法，可能可供核查 LVR 中潜推理轨迹的探索多样性、RL rollout 同质化及轨迹监督设计。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** PRISM: Promoting Sampling Diversity of Large Language Models with Implicit Stochastic Modulation

**abstract/A1：** Large language models (LLMs) exhibit mode collapse, where their output distribution concentrates on familiar semantic modes. When diversity disappears, repeated sampling becomes semantic repetition rather than discovery, making rare but stronger solutions unlikely to surface and leaving self-evolution and continual learning difficult to sustain. Most remedies intervene only at inference time, shaping sam- ples without making diversity a model capability. We propose PRISM, a training-time framework that internalizes diversity within the model. PRISM combines (i) submodular curation that pairs each prompt with diverse-yet-correct multi-teacher solutions, and (ii) stochastic embedding modulation, which turns special-token perturbations into implicit signals for sampling distinct outputs. A theoretical analysis establishes the stability of this mechanism. On WritingPrompts and BookMIA, PRISM moves the quality-diversity frontier outward, achieving the best generation quality in all model-benchmark settings while maintaining the strongest or near-strongest semantic diversity across two open-weight model families. It also improves sampling efficiency on math and code benchmarks. Beyond inference, PRISM yields a stronger foundation for reinforcement learning, sustaining diverse rollouts and mitigating the diversity collapse commonly observed during RL training.

</details>

<a id="paper-59"></a>

### 59. [ReMedI: Inference-Time Refinement and Self-Distillation for Controllable Molecular Optimization](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32103v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32103

**作者：** Jia Yi Zhang、Shivanshu Shekhar、Allison McLevige、Tong Zhang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32103v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32103v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 ReMedI：把分子语言模型当作提议分布，在推理时用 Metropolis-Hastings 引导多目标编辑搜索，再把搜索轨迹自蒸馏回基座模型以改善零样本生成。

**相关性简评：** 其「推理时搜索—轨迹蒸馏—再搜索」闭环或可迁移到 LVR 的推理轨迹训练与监督分配：值得核查搜索接受准则如何筛选有效编辑、蒸馏后是否保留搜索带来的状态改进，以及是否可与 latent 状态的提议和验证类比。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** ReMedI: Inference-Time Refinement and Self-Distillation for Controllable Molecular Optimization

**abstract/A1：** In drug development, lead optimization must improve competing properties, such as increasing permeability and reducing toxicity, while limiting scaffold changes that could compromise the lead's potency. RL-based post-training, such as C-MORAL, addresses this problem by fine-tuning molecular language models to directly optimize molecular leads for objectives of interest, which relies on expensive task-specific datasets and faces challenges in achieving multi-objective optimization (MOO). We argue a pre-trained model may already contain useful molecular edits that zero-shot generation cannot efficiently reach. Motivated by the limited search capabilities of zero-shot generation, we develop an efficient Metropolis-Hastings search strategy to better explore the proposal distribution. We propose ReMedI, which uses the molecular language model as a base proposer for inference-time alignment. Given a lead and a set of multi-objective tasks, the model proposes edits conditioned on the current molecule, while Metropolis--Hastings refinement guides sequential edits under an objective for property improvement, preservation, and lead similarity. On both C-MuMOInstruct and SMDD benchmarks, refinement improves frozen base models across three backbones, reaching significantly higher multi-objective optimization success rates compared to baselines. These search traces can then distilled into the base model which improves zero-shot generation and subsequent search using approximately 7.8 times less training molecular leads than that of C-MORAL. ReMedI achieves approximately 2.8 times the strict-success coverage of RL baseline C-MORAL, including on out-of-distribution tasks, with matched lead-relative similarity.

</details>

<a id="paper-60"></a>

### 60. [Who Verifies the Tools? How Warped Formatters Mislead Agents and Their Verifiers](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32110v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32110

**作者：** William Jeffries

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.SE

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32110v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32110v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文报告生产 RAG 系统中工具输出格式化器省略搜索上限、结果条数、标识符命名空间等关键信息，使 agent 给出错误或不全的结论且 verifier 照单接受；作者用「删信息版」与「补齐版」两套格式化措辞做对照消融，统计无支撑声明与 verifier 接受率的变化，并给出一段审计提示词在多个开源 agent 系统中查找同类缺陷。

**相关性简评：** 其「同一计算结果、不同证据呈现方式即改变声明与验证判定」的对照设计，以及验证器在缺失事实时难以否决错误答案的结论，可供核查 VLM 中视觉证据序列化与筛选（如裁切范围、来源元数据丢失）对 latent 推理状态和 verifier 监督可靠性的影响。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Who Verifies the Tools? How Warped Formatters Mislead Agents and Their Verifiers

**abstract/A1：** Agents and verifiers often rely on the text a tool prints, and that text can misrepresent what the tool actually did, even when the tool's computation is correct. We saw this happen in a production RAG research system used by the Pilsudski Institute in New York, where tool output formatters misled an agent into giving wrong or incomplete information to users. Each tool did its job correctly, but its output omitted search limits, result caps, or identifier namespaces. We also recreated an unlabeled estimate caught in code review before release. We studied whether these formatters misled agents and verifiers and how much the repaired wording helps. We fed tool results to agents using two formatter versions: one with the key omissions and one with all missing details clearly stated. That difference alone changed what the agents claimed and what the verifiers accepted. Unsupported agent claims dropped substantially for three of the four failure types. In one test, verifiers went from accepting incorrect answers 90 out of 90 times to rejecting them 90 out of 90 times. A control showed the verifiers could catch the errors once they were given the missing facts and asked to judge correctness. We include the audit prompt we used with Codex and Claude Code in an exploratory demonstration, written with prior knowledge of our own system's defects. When pointed at an old version of our own system, it rediscovered the defects that were still live and surfaced twelve more we had not catalogued. When pointed at OpenClaw and Hermes, two of the best-known open-source agentic systems, it found warped formatters in both. We opened PRs to fix both, and the OpenClaw fix has already been merged. Practitioners can run the same prompt on their own systems today.

**comment/C1：** Accepted as a poster at the Who Verifies the Agents? workshop at NeurIPS 2026 (non-archival). 12 pages, 3 figures. Code and data: https://github.com/williamcodes/who-verifies-the-tools

</details>

<a id="paper-61"></a>

### 61. [MPQ: A Message-Passing View of Post-Training Quantization](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32127v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32127

**作者：** Yanshuo Chen、Chenxi Liu、Reza Shirkavand、Heng Huang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32127v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32127v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文将按行权重量化表述为离散码本上的后验推断，指出 GPTQ、LDLQ 等方法相当于零温度硬判决，进而提出 MPQ：用向量近似消息传递估计分块软后验，再以块坐标下降细化硬判决初始化，在 Llama、Qwen、Mistral 的 2–4 bit 量化上改善困惑度与零样本准确率。

**相关性简评：** 其「推理期保留软后验、仅在输出阶段硬判决」与消息传递迭代更新状态，可供核查是否能类比到 LVR 中 latent 状态的多轮软更新与最终提交，但主题本身是模型压缩，与视觉推理无直接联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** MPQ: A Message-Passing View of Post-Training Quantization

**abstract/A1：** Post-training quantization (PTQ) is a key technique for efficiently deploying large language models, as it compresses weights into low-bit representations without retraining. Existing PTQ solvers, including GPTQ, LDLQ, and block coordinate descent, commit each weight block to a single codeword before its neighbors are considered, leaving near-optimal alternatives unexplored. In this work, we cast row-wise PTQ as posterior inference over discrete codebooks, in which existing solvers correspond to a zero-temperature, hard-decision limit retaining only the top codeword per block. We propose Message-Passing Quantization (MPQ), which preserves soft posterior information across blocks during inference and commits to hard codewords only at the output stage. MPQ first uses vector approximate message passing to compute approximate blockwise posteriors, then refines a hard-decision initialization by exact block coordinate descent. Across 2-bit lattice and 3- and 4-bit scalar quantization on dense language models from Llama, Qwen, and Mistral families, MPQ improves perplexity and zero-shot accuracy over strong hard-decision baselines, and composes favorably with preprocessing techniques such as activation-aware scaling and incoherence processing. Our implementation is available at https://github.com/poseidonchan/MPQ.

**comment/C1：** NeurIPS 2026

</details>

<a id="paper-62"></a>

### 62. [Beyond Data Schedule: Fast LLM Pretraining on Data Mixtures by Loss-Reweighting](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32141v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32141

**作者：** Jiazheng Li、Huaqing Zhang、Jing Xu、Tianle Cai、Jingzhao Zhang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32141v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32141v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文在固定数据曝光量的前提下研究领域加权对预训练知识遗忘与能力权衡的影响，发现损失重加权可在单检查点同时超过分阶段训练的最优知识/推理分数，并提出 LARE：基于运行损失统计、以归一化逆平方根规则自动调整领域权重，在 1.5B 模型上提升五个基准。

**相关性简评：** 该工作本身不涉及视觉或多模态 latent 推理，但其依据损失尺度自动平衡多领域目标的加权机制，可能为 VLM 中视觉损失与语言/推理损失的多目标权衡提供可核查的训练线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Beyond Data Schedule: Fast LLM Pretraining on Data Mixtures by Loss-Reweighting

**abstract/A1：** Large language models often acquire specialized capabilities through successive pretraining stages, but these gains can come at the cost of forgetting earlier knowledge. In this work, we investigate whether such trade-offs can be improved by changing how strongly each domain contributes to learning while keeping its data exposure fixed. We find that, in controlled knowledge--mathematics experiments, loss reweighting produces a single checkpoint that surpasses the best knowledge and reasoning scores attained along either stage trajectory. This reveals an independent control that data-mixture ratios alone do not capture. Building on this finding, we propose LARE (Loss-Adaptive Reweighting under fixed Exposure), which automatically adjusts domain weights from running loss statistics. Motivated by a theoretical analysis of gradient noise, its normalized inverse-square-root rule balances relative loss scales without changing the sampled mixture or requiring additional runs to search for weights. Across knowledge, mathematics, and code, \\Method{} improves all five evaluated benchmarks for a 1.5B model trained on 240B tokens, averaging +7.84 pp over a matched direct mixture, including gains of +17.06 pp on GSM8K and +9.00 pp on MATH. These findings demonstrate that adjusting domain loss scales can improve joint capabilities without additional domain exposure.

</details>

<a id="paper-63"></a>

### 63. [Rethinking LoRA Initialization for Robust Asymmetric Learning Rates](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32142v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32142

**作者：** Disen Liao、Yaoliang Yu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32142v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32142v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文从信号—噪声分解角度分析 LoRA 在非对称学习率（LoRA+）下训练不稳定的来源，指出标准初始化会引入与 η\_B、初始化尺度 β 和秩 r 相关的初始化噪声，并提出按 σ\_A²∝n⁻² 缩放 A 的 initAA 初始化以扩大稳定学习率区间。

**相关性简评：** 其初始化噪声分析与秩/学习率比例的稳定性结论，可能为在 VLM 上用低秩适配器训练 latent 推理模块时的优化稳定性与超参敏感度提供可核查线索，但论文本身不涉及视觉推理或 latent 状态。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Rethinking LoRA Initialization for Robust Asymmetric Learning Rates

**abstract/A1：** Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) is often highly sensitive to learning-rate (LR) choices. Recent empirical studies suggest that, once LRs are carefully tuned, vanilla LoRA can remain competitive with many proposed LoRA variants. This shifts the central practical question from designing ever more variants to reducing the cost and brittleness of LR selection. LoRA+ addresses this issue with asymmetric LRs, using a larger LR for the up-projection matrix $B$ than for the down-projection matrix $A$. However, the ratio $η\_B / η\_A$ itself becomes an additional hyperparameter: large ratios can improve adaptation, but under the standard LoRA initialization they can also cause unstable training and ratio-dependent performance collapse. We identify the source of this brittleness through a signal--noise decomposition of LoRA dynamics. Under standard \\initA, the update contains an initialization-induced noise term whose magnitude can be affected by the LR for $B$, $η\_B$, the initialization scale $β$, and the adapter rank $r$. In practical LLM fine-tuning, this non-vanishing noise can limit the performance gains expected from LoRA+ style asymmetric LRs. To suppress this effect, we propose \\initAA, a simple initialization that scales the variance of $A$ as $σ\_A^2 \\propto n^{-2}$ while keeping $B\_0=0$. Under matched rank and training time for both initializations, this reduces the initialization-noise energy by an additional factor of $1/n$, stabilizing asymmetric LoRA training while preserving zero initial adapter output. Empirically, \\initAA substantially widens the stable LR region across initialization scales, adapter ranks, and LoRA update multipliers. Across tasks and models, \\initAA reduces best-ratio drift, prevents the high-ratio collapse observed with standard \\initA, and makes the width-based guideline $η\_B \\approx n η\_A$ a useful starting point for finite pretrained models.

**comment/C1：** 46 pages, 13 figures, NeurIPS 2026

</details>

<a id="paper-64"></a>

### 64. [Depth-Dependent Safety Geometry in Vision-Language Models: Layer-Wise Localization and Bidirectional Refusal Control](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32182v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32182

**作者：** Muhammad Zeeshan Karamat、Syed Talal Hassan、Christiana Chamon Garcia

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.CL、cs.CR、cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32182v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32182v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出免重训的推理期框架，通过逐层激活差异分析与沿学习到的安全方向的激活引导，在 Qwen3-VL-4B 和 InternVL2.5-4B 上定位并双向操控视觉层的拒答行为，发现安全几何随深度非均匀分布，且表示可分性与因果可控性并不同步。

**相关性简评：** 其「按深度做因果干预、区分表示可分与因果可控」的探测思路可供核查：或可用于判断 LVR 中视觉证据在哪些层被保留、又在哪些层真正影响输出；但研究对象是安全拒答而非多模态推理，迁移需自行验证。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Depth-Dependent Safety Geometry in Vision-Language Models: Layer-Wise Localization and Bidirectional Refusal Control

**abstract/A1：** Vision-Language Models (VLMs) are widely deployed, yet the internal mechanisms behind their safety behavior remain poorly understood. We present a lightweight inference-time framework for localizing and manipulating safety-sensitive representations in frozen VLMs, without retraining or modifying parameters. On Qwen3-VL-4B, layer-wise activation divergence analysis reveals non-uniform safety geometry across depth. Causal interventions show that early vision layers support strong bidirectional refusal control through activation steering along a learned safety direction, with unsafe rejection spanning 6%-96%, whereas later layers respond more to activation magnitude scaling and exhibit a substantial safety-selectivity trade-off. Deeper layers remain well separated along the safety direction yet are largely insensitive to steering, distinguishing representational separability from causal controllability. InternVL2.5-4B shows qualitatively similar depth-dependent activation geometry despite differences in layer location and magnitude. Our results expose a white-box, representation-level refusal-bypass attack surface in VLM vision encoders and motivate inference-time stabilization of safety-sensitive representations.

**comment/C1：** Accepted at the NeurIPS 2026 Workshop

</details>

<a id="paper-65"></a>

### 65. [EVOLVE: Uncertainty-Aware Multivariate Time Series Anomaly Prediction with Adaptive Prototype Memories](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32189v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32189

**作者：** Jianxin Jin、Kai Zhao、Shiyan Hu、Yang Shu、Peng Chen、Meng Wang、Zhongwen Rao、Yijie Li、Jing Wang、Bin Yang、Chenjuan Guo

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32189v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32189v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 EVOLVE 用于无监督多变量时间序列异常预测：用自适应异常原型记忆在变量与上下文层面检索扰动，并采样多个潜在历史状态、经平滑与偏差放大两类解码假设生成多条未来轨迹，以轨迹间分歧估计异常风险。

**相关性简评：** 其「采样多个 latent 历史状态、解码多假设轨迹并以分歧量化不确定性」可能为 LVR 中多 latent 假设的构造与如何用分歧筛选证据提供可供核查的线索，但研究对象是时间序列预测，与视觉推理无直接联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** EVOLVE: Uncertainty-Aware Multivariate Time Series Anomaly Prediction with Adaptive Prototype Memories

**abstract/A1：** Unsupervised multivariate time series anomaly prediction (AP) aims to estimate future anomaly risks from current observations, providing early-warning signals for proactive intervention and maintenance in complex systems. Existing unsupervised AP methods follow a perturbation-based paradigm, where predefined or random perturbations are injected into historical windows to simulate abnormal future evolution. However, these methods share perturbations across different variables and contexts, and learn deterministic history-to-future evolution. In reality, anomaly evolution is variable- and context-dependent: the same perturbation may be amplified into abnormal future dynamics in one variable or context, while being absorbed as normal fluctuation in another. In addition, future evolution is not uniquely determined by a historical window, since similar histories may lead to multiple plausible trajectories, such as gradual degradation or abrupt changes. To address these problems, we propose EVOLVE, a unified framework that models unsupervised AP via variable- and context-conditioned perturbations and uncertainty-aware future reasoning. EVOLVE constructs context-specific anomaly perturbations through an adaptive anomaly prototype memory, which can dynamically update and retrieve relevant perturbations at the variable level. EVOLVE models uncertainty by sampling multiple latent historical states and decoding through complementary smooth and deviation-amplifying evolution hypotheses, and the disagreement among decoded future trajectories is used to estimate future anomaly risk. Extensive experiments on six real-world benchmark datasets demonstrate that EVOLVE consistently outperforms state-of-the-art anomaly prediction baselines.

**comment/C1：** 14 pages. An earlier version of this work was submitted to KDD 2026 on February 9, 2026

</details>

<a id="paper-66"></a>

### 66. [Dual-Adaptive Z-Score PPO: Dynamic Clipping Using Action Confidence and Normalized Advantage Magnitude](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32194v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32194

**作者：** Taha M. Mahmoud、Naima Kaabouch

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32194v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32194v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 研究用样本级自适应裁剪阈值替代 PPO 固定裁剪系数的变体，提出 PPO-DAZ3，以当前策略下所选动作概率和归一化优势幅度共同确定裁剪区间，并在三个经典控制基准上与标准 PPO 比较。

**相关性简评：** 用置信度信号逐样本调节信任域宽度的做法，可能可供核查于 latent 或 VLM 推理的强化学习训练，例如按 token 或轨迹可靠性调整更新幅度；但论文仅在低维控制任务上验证，与视觉推理无直接联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Dual-Adaptive Z-Score PPO: Dynamic Clipping Using Action Confidence and Normalized Advantage Magnitude

**abstract/A1：** Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO) remains a popular on-policy reinforcement learning algorithm owing to its stability and ease of implementation. Its clipping threshold, however, is fixed, so every training sample receives the same trust-region width regardless of how reliable the corresponding policy update is. This work examines adaptive-clipping variants of PPO in which the constant clipping parameter is replaced by sample-dependent thresholds computed from policy and return statistics. The central method, Dual-Adaptive Z-Score PPO (PPO-DAZ3), sets the clipping interval for each sample from two signals: action confidence, given by the probability of the selected action under the current policy, and update confidence, given by the normalized advantage magnitude. The rest of the standard PPO training pipeline is left unchanged. Evaluation on three classic control benchmarks (LunarLander-v3, CartPole-v1, and Acrobot-v1) indicates that adaptive clipping improves learning in some settings but not all. PPO-DAZ3 performs best on LunarLander-v3, the smooth advantage-based variant PPO-AZ3S leads on Acrobot-v1, and standard PPO stays competitive on CartPole-v1, suggesting that adaptive clipping is most beneficial on harder control tasks.

**comment/C1：** 6 pages, 4 figures. This is the author's accepted manuscript of the paper published in the 2026 6th International Conference on Electrical, Computer and Energy Technologies (ICECET 2026), Rome, Italy. The final published version is available via IEEE Xplore at https://doi.org/10.1109/ICECET65726.2026.11633063

</details>

### 67. [DS-BiCA-Net: Pair-Preserving Cross-Temporal Modeling for Remote Sensing Change Captioning](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32200v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32200

**作者：** Licheng Zhang、Naveed Akhtar、Siew-Kei Lam

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32200v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32200v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 DS-BiCA-Net：把图像内上下文建模（窗口化状态空间模块）与跨时相比对（共享投影的双向交叉注意力）拆成独立模块，两幅图像保持双流编码、仅在聚合头融合，并通过受控实验考察算子交互、时间方向与模块顺序。

**相关性简评：** 双流保源身份、模块可单独移除以度量各方向贡献的设计，可能为 LVR 中保留视觉证据身份、规划「贡献分配」与消融诊断提供线索；但该文属遥感变化描述任务，未涉及 latent 推理状态生成或停止决策，需核查可迁移性。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** DS-BiCA-Net: Pair-Preserving Cross-Temporal Modeling for Remote Sensing Change Captioning

**abstract/A1：** Remote sensing image change captioning (RSICC) describes in natural language the semantic changes between two images of the same scene captured at different times. Previous methods commonly merge the pair during repeated processing or combine intra-image and cross-image relations in one operation, weakening source identity and leaving the two temporal directions implicit. Without separate modules for intra-image and cross-temporal processing and a separate path for each direction, the contribution of each part cannot be measured on its own, so it remains unclear which part matters most, and the design of later models has no such evidence to build on. To address these issues, we propose DS-BiCA-Net, which assigns the two kinds of processing to separate modules and keeps the two images as separate streams throughout the encoder. A windowed state-space module models context within each image, and a bidirectional cross-attention module compares the two images along two directed paths that share their projections. The two modules use the same pre-normalisation residual architecture and differ only in their mixers, and the two streams are fused only in the aggregation head, so each module and each direction can be removed and its contribution measured on its own. Controlled experiments on LEVIR-CC and Dubai-CC examine operator interaction, temporal direction and module order, showing that intra-image refinement carries no benefit alone and becomes beneficial only when followed by bidirectional cross-attention, an order that outperforms the reverse at matched parameter count and FLOPs. The bidirectional module also outperforms either temporal direction alone. Our method reaches an S\_m^\* of 82.65 on LEVIR-CC, the highest among the published results compared, and 66.72 on Dubai-CC without geometric augmentation, the second-highest among the published results compared.

</details>

### 68. [Neural Disjunctive Normal Forms for Learning Exact Rank Filters](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32203v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32203

**作者：** Diego Marcondes、Wonder A. L. Alves、Ronaldo F. Hashimoto

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32203v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32203v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文用阈值分解构造 neural disjunctive normal form 来表示 rank filter，给出整数图像下精确性的充分条件，并由平滑阈值方程的根导出光滑代理，借隐式微分得到闭式导数，在 straight-through 框架中前向精确评估、反向用代理，用于去噪与血管分割。

**相关性简评：** 其「前向精确、反向平滑代理」且由平滑参数控制窗口内权重分配的梯度机制，可能可供核查 LVR 中硬性视觉证据筛选（如 top-k patch 选择、阈值门控）的梯度回传与贡献分配问题，但任务场景距离主线较远。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Neural Disjunctive Normal Forms for Learning Exact Rank Filters

**abstract/A1：** Gradient-based learning of morphological operators is hindered by locally sparse derivatives with respect to the structuring elements. We develop a method for learning rank filters that retains exact operator evaluation while distributing the derivatives across the window. Using threshold decomposition, we construct a neural disjunctive normal form representation and establish a sufficient condition for exactness with integer-valued images and structuring elements. We then derive a smooth surrogate from the unique root of a smooth threshold equation. Implicit differentiation gives closed-form derivatives with normalised local weights, whose distribution is controlled by a smoothing parameter. These derivatives are used in a straight-through procedure: the forward pass evaluates the exact rank filter, while the backward pass uses the surrogate. For fixed ranks, the procedure learns real-valued structuring elements of any rank filter, including the median, and extends to compositions, infima, and suprema of rank filters. Experiments on image denoising using MNIST and Fashion-MNIST and retinal vessel segmentation show that, starting from a generic random initialisation, the proposed approach consistently improves performance over training with exact subgradients in every setting. In image denoising, our best network also outperforms smooth morphological networks in all settings. On vessel segmentation, the learned erosions recover structuring elements that fit the vessels, as theoretically expected, whereas most of those trained with exact subgradients remain close to their initialisation.

**comment/C1：** 12 pages, 4 figures, 2 tables

</details>

<a id="paper-69"></a>

### 69. [Watermarking Diffusion Language Models with Learned Foresight and Balanced Bands](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32205v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32205

**作者：** Hyojun Kim、Hyeseon An、Yo-Sub Han

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32205v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32205v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文研究扩散语言模型的绿名单水印，将原先固定的词表绿名单 band 与「预见预测器」改为可学习，并按检测器口径每个不同上下文-token 对只计一次绿 token，在 WaterBench 上于同等失真下提高 z-score 与检测率。

**相关性简评：** 其「预见预测器」学习估计每个候选 token 如何改变邻近位置的预测分布，可能为 LVR 中预测某个候选 latent 步骤对后续推理状态的影响、进而支撑步骤选择与停止决策提供方法线索，可供核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Watermarking Diffusion Language Models with Learned Foresight and Balanced Bands

**abstract/A1：** Green-red watermarks favor tokens from a green list that a secret key derives from the preceding tokens. Diffusion language models, however, often choose a token before its preceding tokens, and the token later shapes its neighbors' green lists. Prior work therefore tilts each choice to raise the expected number of green tokens at its own and its neighbors' positions. Building on this objective, we first learn two components that prior work keeps fixed: vocabulary bands that serve as green lists, separate competing candidates and keep the null distribution exact, and a foresight predictor that estimates how each candidate shifts its neighbors' predictions. Second, we count green tokens as the detector does, once per distinct context-token pair, so repetition earns nothing. On WaterBench with LLaDA-8B-Instruct, each learned component raises the z-score at equal distortion, and their gains are nearly additive. With a strong watermark, ours detects at least 96% of responses at a 1% false-positive rate while matching the LLM-judge score of unwatermarked text. Ours also detects more responses on Dream and as many or more on code and question answering without a significant loss in task accuracy.

**comment/C1：** 31 pages, 7 figures. Code: https://github.com/hyojuun/lfbb

</details>

### 70. [Head Dimension Matters in Language Models for Long-Context Retrieval](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32213v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32213

**作者：** Seiya Tokui、Shoichiro Yamaguchi、Sosuke Kobayashi

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI、cs.CL

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32213v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32213v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文研究固定参数规模下注意力头维度对长上下文检索的影响，发现更宽的头提升长上下文检索并提高「检索头」比例，且冻结注意力投影后仍有增益，提示嵌入与 MLP 等组件也参与适配。

**相关性简评：** 虽为纯文本语言模型研究，但其头维度、检索头比例与参数分配线索可供核查 LVR 中视觉证据检索和注意力结构对 latent 表示空间的影响。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Head Dimension Matters in Language Models for Long-Context Retrieval

**abstract/A1：** Multi-head attention retrieves and combines context representations through query--key interactions and value aggregation, with head dimension constraining the per-head representation space. In long-context processing, it remains unclear whether allocating a fixed parameter budget to fewer, wider heads improves information retrieval. In this paper, we investigate how head dimension affects the performance of long-context retrieval tasks. Our empirical studies reveal that pretraining loss and short-context non-retrieval task performance are not affected much by head dimension, while long-context retrieval performance improves with wider heads at fixed model sizes. We find that models with wider heads have a higher proportion of retrieval heads, associating more parameters with retrieval. Our ablation studies also show that the retrieval performance improves with wider heads even when the projection weights in attention modules are frozen during long-context continual pretraining, suggesting that wider heads not only benefit updates in attention modules but lead to more effective adaptation of components such as embeddings and MLPs.

</details>

### 71. [RxnExBench: Benchmarking Vision-Language Models for Structured Chemical Reaction Extraction from Scientific Literature](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32221v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32221

**作者：** Jiaxing Sun、Junyuan Gao、Ruikang Zhang、Hao Zheng、Lingli Ge、Xiao An、Jiang Wu、Huazheng Zeng、Jiahe Song、Chen Zhu、Haote Yang、Jingchao Wang、Lu Chen、Yuanyuan Cao、Ruijie Zhang、Shengxin Lu、Weijia Li、Lijun Wu、Conghui He

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32221v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32221v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 构建化学文献中信息密集图（Screen Tables、Substrate Scopes）的结构化反应抽取基准 RxnExBench，含 3565 个专家标注反应与 2361 个分子结构，采用 15 种反应角色、26 个核心字段的统一模式，并提供难度分类、标注流程与可分离分子识别与高层推理的 Oracle-MolRec 协议；9 个 VLM 评测显示模式复杂度与推理需求上升时性能明显下降。

**相关性简评：** Oracle-MolRec 将视觉识别与高阶推理分解评测的做法，可供核查细粒度视觉细节保留与「识别 vs 推理」贡献分配的诊断设计，但本身是化学抽取基准而非 LVR 机制研究。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** RxnExBench: Benchmarking Vision-Language Models for Structured Chemical Reaction Extraction from Scientific Literature

**abstract/A1：** Screen Tables and Substrate Scopes are information-dense figures widely used in synthetic chemistry literature, yet automated extraction of structured reaction data from them remains challenging. We introduce RxnExBench, a multimodal benchmark for fine-grained reaction-level evaluation. Built from high-impact chemistry journals, it contains 3,565 expert-annotated reactions and 2,361 molecular structures. The dataset adopts a unified schema with 15 reaction roles and 26 core fields, supporting downstream AI-for-Chemistry tasks such as retrosynthetic planning, yield prediction, and reaction condition recommendation. RxnExBench further provides a reasoning-driven difficulty taxonomy, a modular annotation pipeline, and the Oracle-MolRec protocol, which disentangles molecular recognition from higher-level reasoning. Evaluation of 9 state-of-the-art VLMs shows that performance degrades substantially as schema complexity and reasoning demands increase. Under the Oracle-MolRec setting, results further reveal that accurate molecular recognition alone is insufficient, with multimodal integration and chemistry-aware reasoning remaining the primary bottlenecks. We release all data and code to facilitate future research in chemistry-aware multimodal understanding and AI-for-Chemistry dataset construction. The dataset and code will be released at https://github.com/opendatalab/RxnExBench.

</details>

<a id="paper-72"></a>

### 72. [Correct but Unselectable: The Hidden Interface Tax in Multi-Candidate Reasoning](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32235v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32235

**作者：** Qianli Ma、Zhiqing Tang、Fanshuai Meng、Zhi Yao、Hanshuai Cui、Weijia Jia

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32235v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32235v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文研究小模型「多候选生成—重排序」中的选择瓶颈，提出「隐藏接口税」及覆盖率—转化率分解，区分候选池的原始 oracle 覆盖与真正可被固定选择器消费的可选覆盖，并给出无需重训任一组件、负责修复碎片、对齐答案模式、去除干扰噪声的 CACC 接口层，在 GSM8K、GPQA Diamond、MMLU-Pro 上考察其效果与局限。

**相关性简评：** 虽为纯文本多候选工作，但其「正确候选已存在却无法被选择器读取」的诊断与可选覆盖度量，可供核查 LVR 中多 latent 假设筛选与答案读出接口的同类损失，其方式对齐线索也可能对应 latent 状态与答案模块的兼容性问题。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Correct but Unselectable: The Hidden Interface Tax in Multi-Candidate Reasoning

**abstract/A1：** Small language models deployed under cost, latency, or privacy constraints cannot simply switch to larger models when reasoning fails. Multi-candidate generate-then-rerank offers a natural test-time scaling route. However, more candidates do not always yield higher accuracy. We identify a previously overlooked bottleneck between generation and selection: a correct answer may appear in the candidate pool yet fail to be selected because it is incomplete, unparseable, or misaligned with the answer mode the fixed selector expects. We term this loss the hidden interface tax and formalize a coverage-conversion decomposition that distinguishes raw oracle coverage from selectable coverage, the subset of problems where a correct candidate can actually be consumed by the selector. We propose Compatibility-Aware Candidate Construction (CACC), an interface layer between the candidate source and the fixed selector that repairs fragments, aligns answer modes, and removes selector-confusing noise without retraining either component. On numeric reasoning (GSM8K, competition\_math), CACC raises both oracle coverage and final accuracy. On GPQA Diamond, CACC alone improves final accuracy from 5.6% to 19.2% (+13.6 pp); combined with proposer-side strengthening it reaches 25.8% (+20.2 pp). On MMLU-Pro, CACC exposes a coverage-conversion gap: answer-mode-compatible construction raises oracle coverage, but final accuracy decreases by 0.85 pp under the original selector; adding proposer-side strengthening improves final accuracy.

**comment/C1：** Accepted at NeurIPS 2026

</details>

**相关性简评：** 该文不涉及视觉语言推理，但「启发式预筛 + 扩散动作分布建模」与 GNN 空间结构表示或可为 latent 候选生成、动作剪枝及几何必要信息的内部保留提供可核查线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Spatial Heuristic Strategy based Diffusion Reinforcement Learning for Online 3D Bin Packing

**abstract/A1：** Traditional heuristic methods have accumulated mature experience in core objectives, such as space utilization and layout compactness of 3D bin packing, relying on artificially designed rule-based logic. However, they exhibit obvious limitations in generalization ability and adaptability to dynamic scenarios. Diffusion reinforcement learning, which integrates the spatial search capability of reinforcement learning and the data distribution modeling capability of diffusion learning, has achieved certain progress in action distribution modeling and decision compliance constraints. Nevertheless, there remain issues including insufficient perception of the spatial topological structure in 3D bin packing and limited layout optimization capability. To address these problems, we propose a diffusion reinforcement learning method integrated with spatial heuristic strategies, which combines classical bin packing heuristic rules with a diffusion reinforcement learning framework. Specifically, it compresses the action space through heuristic action pre-screening, introduces a graph neural network to model the spatial structure, and designs a composite reward function incorporating flatness to suppress spatial fragmentation, thereby realizing the organic integration of prior knowledge and adaptive optimization. Experimental results demonstrate that the pro

</details>

### 74. [Anti-coherence Regularized Absolute Scaling for Out-of-Distribution Detection](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32255v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32255

**作者：** Donghwan Kim、Youngwoong Jin、Hyunsoo Yoon

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32255v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32255v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文修正了事后 OOD 检测方法 SCALE 在存在负激活时的尺度比失效问题，改用绝对值激活计算比例，并提出 Anti-coherence 正则，按样本 max-logit 贡献中符号不一致的比例惩罚缩放因子，以抑制不可靠证据。

**相关性简评：** 「按符号一致性判断证据是否可靠」与对绝对值激活的重塑，可能为 LVR 中的视觉证据筛选与贡献分配提供评价线索，但原文面向分类器 OOD 检测，未涉及多模态推理状态，需要核查其可迁移性。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Anti-coherence Regularized Absolute Scaling for Out-of-Distribution Detection

**abstract/A1：** Out-of-distribution (OOD) detection is recognized as a critical problem in AI safety. SCALE is an effective post-hoc OOD detection method in the activation-shaping family that requires no training data and rescales penultimate activations using a percentile-based magnitude ratio. However, this ratio is ill-defined when negative activations are present, since positive and negative contributions cancel within the underlying sum, causing SCALE to degenerate on backbones such as ViT. We address this with an Absolute Fix that computes the ratio over absolute-valued activations rather than discarding negative activations through ReLU. Building on this corrected scale, we propose Anti-coherence regularization, which penalizes the scaling factor by the fraction of a sample's max-logit contributions to the max logit that are sign-mismatched and thereby suppresses unreliable evidence for the predicted class. Despite requiring no access to training data or training labels, our method ties or outperforms activation-shaping methods that rely on training data in terms of AUROC and FPR@95 on CIFAR-10 and ImageNet under the OpenOOD v1.5 protocol.

**comment/C1：** 29 pages, 6 figures

</details>

### 75. [Representation Learning via Composite Subspace Flow Matching](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32256v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32256

**作者：** José Afonso、Mário Figueiredo

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32256v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32256v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出复合子空间流匹配（CSFM），将自监督表示学习表述为对谱、颜色、光照等子空间确定性退化课程的最小方差反演，并用硬时间风格截断约束轨迹方差，使容量受限编码器忽略瞬时风格、保留持续内容，在 CIFAR/STL-10 上兼顾判别迁移与生成合成。

**相关性简评：** 不属于多模态推理主线，但其「分阶段退化课程＋轨迹方差上界」迫编码器区分持续内容与瞬变风格，可能为 latent 状态中视觉细节的保留与无关变化的筛选提供可核查线索，流匹配潜空间轨迹与插值也可对照 latent 状态生成与几何结构。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Representation Learning via Composite Subspace Flow Matching

**abstract/A1：** Distilling semantic meaning from raw image data remains a challenge in machine learning. Current self-supervised paradigms face a trade-off: joint-embedding architectures produce relevant features by aggressively discarding intra-class variance, irreversibly spatial and reconstruction. Conversely, generative models preserve spatial details but struggle to isolate semantics, as their optimization is dominated by pixel variations. To bridge this divide, we propose composite subspace flow matching (CSFM). Rather than corrupting data with unstructured isotropic noise, CSFM formulates representation learning as the variance-minimizing inversion of a deterministic degradation curriculum across spectral, color, and illumination subspaces. By bounding trajectory variance with hard temporal style truncation, a capacity-constrained encoder is driven to ignore transient style artifacts and encode persistent content. Empirical evaluations across CIFAR-10, CIFAR-100, and STL-10 show that CSFM yields competitive discriminative transferability while preserving generative synthesis across both convolutional and vision transformer architectures. By avoiding the collapse pathologies of non-generative methods, CSFM forms a smooth latent space that outperforms standard optimal transport flows on linear probing and few-shot classification, while enabling coherent synthesis and trajectory interpolation. These results confirm that steering the physical dynamics of generative flows offers a scalable paradigm for learning representations that are simultaneously linearly separable and generatively invertible.

**comment/C1：** 27 pages, 4 figures

</details>

### 76. [Trial by Trace: Benchmarking AI Agent Harnesses for Reliability and Failure Attribution](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32285v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32285

**作者：** Zhiyong Cao、Hengjie Zhu、Yongxin Wang、Xuchen Li、Jintao Li、Shuxia Liu、Dunqiang Liu、Bo Wang、Kairan Zhang、Cong Lin、JiangDi Yu、Siyuan Liu、Sijia Yao、Yi-Liang Wei、Lingxu Ma、Aijia Wang、Anxin Chen、Zixian Wei、Xiang Ruan、Ruizhi Xu、Ruqian Shi、Baihua Xiao、Sicheng Zhou

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32285v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32285v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 TraceTrial-Bench，将 agent harness 可靠性拆为七个组件，构建 95 个专家级任务，配合轨迹追踪、多层评估与多源证据采集，对 4 种 harness、9 个模型共 3420 次运行做评测和运行级故障归因。

**相关性简评：** 其「显式机制链」故障归因与多源证据采集的做法可能可供核查，用于把 LVR 的失败拆分到视觉编码、latent 状态更新与答案读出等环节；但论文本身不涉及视觉推理或 latent 状态生成。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Trial by Trace: Benchmarking AI Agent Harnesses for Reliability and Failure Attribution

**abstract/A1：** LLM agents are increasingly used to solve real-world problems, and their effectiveness depends on both the underlying models and the harnesses that support their operation. However, most existing benchmarks focus on model capabilities and overall agent performance, with limited attention to the evaluation of agent harnesses. To address this gap, we introduce TraceTrial-Bench, which organizes harness reliability into seven components covering context, execution, governance, lifecycle, observability, tools, and verification. We construct 95 expert-level, mechanism-oriented tasks with executable or state-based ground truth to support semantically equivalent comparisons while preserving the native behavior of each harness. We design a Trajectory Tracing (TT) module to capture agent execution traces and introduce a two-layer evaluation framework that separately assesses agent performance and harness reliability. In addition, we design a Multi-source Evidence Acquisition (MEA) module to provide evidence from the agent context, harness, and execution environment for LLM-based evaluation, and develop a Failure Attribution (FA) module that uses explicit mechanism chains to support attribution at the run level. Across 4 harnesses, 9 models, and 95 tasks, our evaluation of 3,420 runs yields macro-averaged harness scores ranging from 59.8% to 72.0%, while 31.1% of runs achieve task success despite failing at least one harness criterion. TraceTrial-Bench provides an auditable and reproducible foundation for comparing agent harnesses, diagnosing their failures, and studying how harness design interacts with model behavior.

</details>

<a id="paper-77"></a>

### 77. [Complementing DINO Features with Image Structure for Part Discovery](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32293v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32293

**作者：** Samyak Rawlekar、Nikhil Chowdary Paleti、Amey Gupta、Narendra Ahuja

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI、cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32293v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32293v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文诊断出 DINO 特征在跨部件边界的 patch 上熵更高、部件分配置信度更低，据此提出无需训练的 Part-DINO：用低层图像分割得到的边界划分区域，在区域内池化 DINO 特征后再聚类以发现部件。

**相关性简评：** 其「patch 网格混合像素导致特征边界模糊、语义特征缺少几何信息」的诊断，可供核查视觉细节在内部表示中被稀释的机制；用低层结构补充语义特征的做法或可类比 LVR 中的视觉旁路，但论文本身不涉及推理过程或 latent 状态生成，迁移需自行判断。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Complementing DINO Features with Image Structure for Part Discovery

**abstract/A1：** Self-supervised Vision Transformers (ViTs) like DINO encode rich semantics, enabling training-free segmentation of objects from the background and discovery of their parts via feature clustering. This paper focuses on part discovery: partitioning a foreground object into semantic parts. Existing approaches produce imprecise part boundaries. We trace this to the rigid ViT patch grid used in training DINO, where patches straddling boundaries contain pixels from multiple parts. While DINO's invariance-based training could absorb this pixel-level mixing into clean features, experiments with over 26K foreground patches show it does not: boundary-overlapping patches produce features with higher entropy and lower assignment confidence, blurring predicted part boundaries. Rather than make the massive effort of retraining DINO over geometrically coherent regions, an easier approach is to take boundaries directly from a boundary detector, which supplies the geometric information DINO's features lack. Motivated by this, we introduce Part-DINO, a training-free framework that combines boundaries from low-level image segmentation with DINO's semantic features. We use the boundaries to partition the image into regions, pool DINO features within each region, and group them to discover parts. Without any training, Part-DINO produces structurally coherent part assignments, outperforming training-free baselines across four standard benchmarks (CUB, CelebA, PartImageNet-OOD, PartImageNet-Seg) and remaining competitive with weakly-supervised and unsupervised methods.

</details>

<a id="paper-78"></a>

### 78. [Lifelong Context Adaptation through Bidirectional Memory-Skill Learning](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32295v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32295

**作者：** Tianyi Xu、Huazheng Wang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32295v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32295v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 MemCryst：在冻结语言模型上，让记忆中的重复片段驱动技能创建与修订，技能引导的交互又回写为新记忆，形成记忆—技能双向学习，并用任务结果调整已检索记录的未来检索影响力，在十类任务上评测。

**相关性简评：** 其「不重写内容、仅按任务结果调整检索影响力」的做法，可供核查是否能类比 LVR 中的证据筛选、贡献分配与轨迹监督；但研究的是纯文本智能体记忆，未涉及 latent 状态或视觉推理。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Lifelong Context Adaptation through Bidirectional Memory-Skill Learning

**abstract/A1：** Lifelong LLM agents must learn from an expanding history of interactions while making each decision within a bounded context window. Memory makes past experience available, but the agent may still need to infer how to solve each new task from the retrieved records. Skills provide reusable procedures, yet may omit the task-specific facts needed to apply them. Because retrieved memory and skills shape the agent's behavior, they also influence the experience available for future learning. We introduce MemCryst, a framework for lifelong context adaptation through bidirectional learning of memory and skills with a frozen language model. Recurring episodes stored in memory support skill creation and revision, and the interactions guided by those skills supply new memory for subsequent learning. Facts retrieved from memory and learned skills jointly form the agent's context, while task outcomes adjust the future retrieval influence of the records actually served without rewriting their content. Across ten settings spanning embodied control, long-document reading, conversation, mathematics, and code, MemCryst leads seven reported comparisons. On ALFWorld, joint serving achieves 63.17% success, compared with 59.83% for memory-only and 50.50% for skill-only serving. Code is available at https://github.com/tianyi0216/Memcryst.git

**comment/C1：** 42 pages, including references and appendices. 18 figures, 13 tables

</details>

### 79. [Solvent: Long-Horizon Reasoning for Multi-Step Retrosynthesis](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32308v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32308

**作者：** Yifei Wang、Botian Wang、YiFei Wang、Jiangtao Feng、Xinbo Zhang、Yawen Ouyang、Yixin Wu、Lihao Wang、Xiaoqing Zheng、Xuanjing Huang、Hao Zhou

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32308v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32308v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 Solvent，一个 8B 模型，用两轮后训练课程把长时程 LLM 推理用于多步逆合成，使模型交替进行合成规划、外部可获得性核验与路线修订，并在四个基准上取得 61.86%。

**相关性简评：** 纯文本、非视觉任务，但「规划—外部核验—路线修订」的交错循环与两轮后训练课程，可能为 LVR 中的证据获取、回溯式停止决策与轨迹训练提供可核查线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Solvent: Long-Horizon Reasoning for Multi-Step Retrosynthesis

**abstract/A1：** Multi-step retrosynthesis requires recursively transforming a target molecule through successive reaction steps into purchasable building blocks, and recent work has begun to draw on large language model (LLM) reasoning to tackle the task. In its latest evolution, LLM reasoning has entered the long-horizon regime: models pursue objectives across interdependent steps, interact with environments, and adapt to feedback. This shift has markedly advanced LLM capabilities, as illustrated by breakthroughs in AI-driven mathematical research. At this frontier, we introduce Solvent, an 8B LLM that successfully extends long-horizon reasoning to multi-step retrosynthesis. Through a two-round post-training curriculum, Solvent learns to interleave iterative synthesis planning, external availability verification, and adaptive route revision. Experiments show that, following a $2.1\\times$ performance boost from RL, Solvent scores 61.86% across four benchmarks, surpassing mid-scale language models and prior search-based retrosynthesis systems, and rivaling frontier trillion-parameter LLMs.

**comment/C1：** 49 pages, 18 figures. Code: https://github.com/alden199/Solvent ; Model: https://huggingface.co/Alden19910/Solvent

</details>

### 80. [GeoLEON-CLIP: Graph-Anchored Multimodal Contrastive Learning for Geographic Region Representation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32311v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32311

**作者：** Radoslaw Malek、Szymon Soltysiak、Aleksandra Kawala-Sterniuk、Piotr Szymanski

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32311v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32311v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 GeoLEON-CLIP：以冻结的 OpenStreetMap 图嵌入作为视觉与语言之间的语义锚点，用可训练残差投影头连接三个冻结编码器并做对称三模态对比训练；并提出链式跨模态召回 R@A@B，比较经图模态中介的检索与直接检索。

**相关性简评：** 以固定中间模态作锚、并在等候选预算下比较「中介路径」与「直达路径」的做法，可能可供核查 LVR 中 latent 中间状态是否真正承载信息、以及如何区分视觉旁路与真实贡献；但其锚定是否仅属表示对齐而非推理使用仍需验证。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** GeoLEON-CLIP: Graph-Anchored Multimodal Contrastive Learning for Geographic Region Representation

**abstract/A1：** Geographic regions are multimodal: satellite imagery captures visual morphology, text conveys human-interpretable semantics, and map structures describe urban context. Existing geospatial contrastive methods typically align image-text or image-location pairs, underusing structured map data. We introduce GeoLEON-CLIP, a graph-anchored contrastive framework that aligns three views of each H3-indexed region: an OpenStreetMap-derived graph embedding, a satellite image crop, and a textual description. Frozen pretrained LEON graph embeddings provide a semantic anchor between vision and language. Frozen graph, image, and text encoders are connected by trainable residual projection heads and trained with a symmetric tri-modal contrastive objective. Experiments show strong graph-image and graph-text alignment; graph-image retrieval achieves the best cross-modal performance in a mixed-resolution pool. To evaluate the anchoring hypothesis, we propose chained cross-modal recall $\\mathrm{R}@A@B$, which routes retrieval through the graph modality and compares it with direct retrieval under a matched candidate budget. At $\\mathrm{R}@1@1$, image$\\rightarrow$graph$\\rightarrow$text and text$\\rightarrow$graph$\\rightarrow$image outperform direct image-text retrieval by 4.8 and 4.4 points, respectively, despite using a single intermediate cell. Downstream probes show task-dependent gains, with the largest improvements on Airbnb and Beijing housing. These results indicate that OpenStreetMap graph structure can anchor multimodal representations combining maps, imagery, and language.

**comment/C1：** Accepted at the 9th International Workshop on AI for Geographic Knowledge Discovery (GeoAI 2026), co-located with the 34th ACM SIGSPATIAL International Conference on Advances in Geographic Information Systems (SIGSPATIAL 2026)

</details>

<a id="paper-81"></a>

### 81. [SKG in Practice: OOD Generalization Cases Across FourDomains](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32314v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32314

**作者：** Jian An、Chaoshi Wu、Xinpan Luo

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32314v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32314v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文给出 SKG 推理框架的工程实现与四个领域完整案例（耗散力学系统、检测 sim-to-real、序列建模、分子动力学），报告 OOD 泛化对比结果，并整理预验证、任务不变量构造、三阶段与残差训练及配套定理。

**相关性简评：** 「状态相关度量」与「任务不变量构造」可能为核查 LVR 内部状态是否保留任务必要信息提供方法线索，sine→square 外推也可供核查长度与模式泛化，但全文无视觉语言推理或 latent 生成，需较多核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** SKG in Practice: OOD Generalization Cases Across FourDomains

**abstract/A1：** This paper presents the engineering implementation methods of the SKG inference framework and provides complete case studies across four domains. Case 1, 2D Dissipative Mechanical System: OOD MSE is 1.69 to 4.12 times lower than the best neural baseline; the state-dependent metric yields a 10^8-order improvement under controlled conditions. Case 2, Target Detection Sim-to-Real: Real-machine mAP increases from 0.2564 to 0.4964 (+24.00 points, single-run experiment, 32 images, point estimate), and AP75 increases from 0.1736 to 0.5765 (+40.29 points), with no real-machine retraining required. Case 3, Sequence Modeling sine to square: OOD MSE is 0.0296 versus Transformer 0.0641 (mean of 5 seeds, p=0.024). Case 4, Molecular Dynamics MD17: ID@100 ADE is 0.0285 versus ESTAG 0.1467; OOD@100 is 0.0316 versus 0.1411 (single seed). For each case, we present the objective, applied SKG theory, parameter settings, baseline comparison protocol, data results, and sources of advantage. Following the cases, we provide the complete methodology: pre-validation, task invariant construction, three-stage training, residual training, and the four theorems and one proposition supporting these methods. This paper not only demonstrates effective configurations of SKG across four domains but also honestly reports the variance of single-run experiments, limitations of single-seed results, negative updates caused by parameterization mismatch, and practical experience of using engineering thresholds as diagnostic signals rather than hard constraints.

</details>

<a id="paper-82"></a>

### 82. [Organizing Temporal Evidence in Hyperbolic Space for Video Moment Retrieval](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32317v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32317

**作者：** Mengnan Liu、Sanping Zhou、Kun Xia、Jiaxuan Lin、Gang Hua、Le Wang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32317v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32317v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 HTEO，把查询相关的时序记忆映射到 Poincaré 球，用径向证据组织与正交角度精化区分目标区间、边界与干扰上下文，再投影回切空间并以轻量残差记忆更新注入 Transformer 解码器，服务于视频时刻检索。

**相关性简评：** 其查询条件化的证据组织与残差记忆注入方式，可能为 LVR 中的视觉证据筛选和 latent 状态更新提供几何化线索，可供核查；但工作面向检索定位而非 latent 推理生成，联系需验证。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Organizing Temporal Evidence in Hyperbolic Space for Video Moment Retrieval

**abstract/A1：** Video moment retrieval aims to localize a query-relevant temporal segment in an untrimmed video. Existing methods typically perform video-text alignment in Euclidean space, where representations are embedded in a flat geometry. However, accurate moment localization requires organizing query-relevant evidence across multiple grounding states, where target regions, boundary transitions, and contextual distractors exhibit distinct temporal structures and relevance patterns. Such Euclidean representations lack an explicit geometry to jointly capture these relationships, motivating us to explore structured temporal evidence organization from a geometric perspective. Specifically, we propose Hyperbolic Temporal Evidence Organization (HTEO), a geometric framework that organizes query-aware temporal memory in hyperbolic space. HTEO maps temporal representations into a Poincaré ball and organizes temporal evidence through Radial Evidence Organization (REO) and Orthogonal Angular Refinement (OAR). REO adjusts temporal tokens' radial positions to encode the structural differences among temporal evidence, while OAR enhances query-conditioned semantic alignment through orthogonal rotation. The organized representation is then projected back to the Euclidean tangent space and injected into the decoder through a lightweight residual memory update, enabling hyperbolic geometric cues to be incorporated into standard Transformer decoders while preserving the original cross-modal memory. Furthermore, we introduce radial structure regularization and angular relevance supervision to explicitly guide temporal evidence organization and query-conditioned semantic alignment, respectively. Extensive experiments and analyses across multiple benchmarks demonstrate that HTEO improves temporal localization while providing an interpretable geometric organization of temporal evidence.

</details>

<a id="paper-83"></a>

### 83. [Beyond Gain: Measuring Skill Internalization for Agent Skill Retirement](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32322v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32322

**作者：** Yanze Wang、Pengfei Yao、Tianyi Sun、Hongda Chen、Yunyun Han、Jun Sun、Chuanrui Hu、Yafeng Deng

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32322v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32322v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文研究如何判定某模型已「内化」某技能、从而可在公共技能库中为其退役该技能；作者不沿用仅比较有无技能增益的规则，而是撤除技能后用由其合成的任务测模型的无辅助得分，并要求其他模型确实从该技能获益作为对照，另发布 1,251 条技能及四模型下的任务与分项评分。

**相关性简评：** 其「撤除某一外部组件＋用他人是否获益来排除无信息试探」的能力归因协议，可能可供核查 LVR 中 latent 状态或视觉旁路是否真被答案使用，并区分「模型本已会用而无增益」与「模型与组件都不提供该能力」两种零增益情形。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Beyond Gain: Measuring Skill Internalization for Agent Skill Retirement

**abstract/A1：** LLM agents retrieve skills from public hubs that hold up to about 100,000 entries and serve many models. As models improve, a hub must find the skills a given model has internalized, meaning it can do without them, and retire them for that model, yet internalization has not been measured with known reliability. Published rules that compare runs with and without a skill retire on the gain alone. The gain is near zero both for an internalized skill and when neither the model nor the skill delivers the capability, a state we call uncovered. We instead withhold the skill, score the model on a task synthesized from it, and trust a high unaided score only when another model gains from the skill, since nearly half of high scores come from tasks no model benefits from. Under four models, a published gain rule applied with one task per skill removes over half of the uncovered skills. On SkillsBench, 28 of its 31 retirements rest on probes no run passed in full, while retiring on the unaided score costs no detectable reward. Unaided verdicts reproduce across tasks more often than the gain rule's (Cohen's kappa 0.42 against 0.23) but differ across models, so a hub must read them per model. We release 1,251 skills with tasks and item scores under four models.

**comment/C1：** 12 pages. Code: https://github.com/EverMind-AI/SkillCorpus/tree/main/skillcorpus/lifecycle Data: https://huggingface.co/datasets/EverMind-AI/skillcorpus-lifecycle

</details>

<a id="paper-84"></a>

### 84. [When to Trust Memory: Retrieval-Guided Probabilistic Spatiotemporal Forecasting under Distribution Shift](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32329v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32329

**作者：** Haochen Lv、Jianhao Zhang、Zhichen Lei、Yang Jiang、Xiaowei Mao、Shengnan Guo、Youfang Lin、Huaiyu Wan

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32329v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32329v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 REMIND，一个面向分布偏移的时空概率预测框架：用时空检索构建记忆库，并以检索相似度与记忆离散度估计当前观测被历史模式支持的程度，据此在记忆校准分支与偏差感知分支间自适应门控，再用分层高斯混合刻画双分支的不确定性，辅以记忆扰动训练。

**相关性简评：** 用检索相似度判断「记忆是否可信」并据此切换两种预测策略，可能与 LVR 中何时依赖 latent/记忆先验、何时返回视觉证据的决策及不确定性校准形成可核查的借鉴线索，但属时间序列领域，迁移需自行判断。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** When to Trust Memory: Retrieval-Guided Probabilistic Spatiotemporal Forecasting under Distribution Shift

**abstract/A1：** Spatiotemporal forecasting plays an important role in real-world applications, where probabilistic approaches provide uncertainty estimates for decision-making. Real-world systems are inherently non-stationary, leading to distribution shifts between training and test data. However, existing probabilistic methods mainly focus on modeling data distributions under standard settings, while distribution shift methods often handle such shifts in a coarse manner, failing to characterize shift degree and adapt prediction strategy accordingly. As a result, they often produce miscalibrated and unreliable forecasts. To address this issue, we propose REMIND, a retrieval-guided probabilistic framework for spatiotemporal forecasting under distribution shift. REMIND leverages retrieval to estimate how well current observation are supported by historical patterns, enabling adaptive prediction under different shift regimes. It employs spatiotemporal retrieval to construct a memory bank and introduces a dual branch architecture, combining a memory-calibrated probabilistic branch for mild shifts with a deviation-aware probabilistic branch for severe shifts. A shift-adaptive gate, guided by retrieval similarity and memory dispersion, balances the two branches according to shift degree. We further design a hierarchical Gaussian mixture to capture heterogeneous uncertainty over the dual branch predictions, and introduce a memory perturbation training scheme to improve generalization. Experiments on real-world datasets with distribution shifts demonstrate that REMIND consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods in both forecasting accuracy and probabilistic prediction quality.

**comment/C1：** Accepted at NeurIPS 2026

</details>

<a id="paper-85"></a>

### 85. [TENET: Time-point Encoding Network for Multivariate Time Series Anomaly Detection](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32337v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32337

**作者：** Dongchan Cho、Juwon Hwang、Keumyeong Kang、Jiho Han、Namsoon Jung

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32337v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32337v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文将无监督多变量时间序列异常检测重构为「当前点系统状态」的潜空间兼容性估计，提出 TENET：用时间层检索窗口上下文、以当前点为中心的嵌入编码，并自适应混合上下文与当前表示，最后以固定的标准正态负对数似然在潜空间打分。

**相关性简评：** 其「围绕决策目标构造 latent 状态、在潜空间而非观测空间评估」的做法，可能为 LVR 中 latent 应编码哪些任务必要信息、以及答案是否真正使用 latent 内容提供可供核查的线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** TENET: Time-point Encoding Network for Multivariate Time Series Anomaly Detection

**abstract/A1：** In unsupervised multivariate time series anomaly detection (MTSAD), local variation can inflate reconstruction and forecasting errors, making observation-space discrepancy an unreliable indicator of abnormal system behavior. Since latent-space scoring can mitigate this limitation, we reframe one-class MTSAD as current-point system-state compatibility estimation and propose TENET, the Time-point Encoding Network for Multivariate Time Series Anomaly Detection. We define the current-point system state as a latent representation of the target time point, constructed from variable-level temporal context in the input window. TENET constructs this state through a temporal layer that retrieves window context relative to the current point, encodes time with current-point-centered embeddings, and adaptively mixes the retrieved context with the current representation. With this design, TENET treats the input window not as the object to reconstruct, forecast, summarize, or score, but as context for constructing a latent representation evaluated with a fixed standard-normal negative log-likelihood score. Across benchmark datasets, TENET achieves state-of-the-art performance. Additional experiments further show that TENET remains effective under alternative one-class objectives and outperforms substitutions based on generic sequence encoders, suggesting that its advantage comes from decision-aligned representation construction rather than from a specialized scoring rule. These results indicate that one-class MTSAD can be strong when the latent representation is designed around the current time point.

**comment/C1：** Accepted by NeurIPS 2026

</details>

<a id="paper-86"></a>

### 86. [Detection and Validation of Hidden Subgroup Disparities in Clinical AI](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32362v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32362

**作者：** Alceu Bissoto、Trung-Dung Hoang、Tim Flühmann、Nina Weng、Susu Sun、Christian F. Baumgartner、Lisa M. Koch

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32362v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32362v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该工作在 22 万余张胸片的心脏增大分类任务上评估「算法式子群发现」用于性能监测：从学习到的特征表示中聚类出语义连贯的患者子群，其准确率跨度为 0.61–0.90，差距大于基于元数据的分组分析，并初步用子群标注缓解已暴露的偏差。

**相关性简评：** 从内部特征表示中发现并验证隐藏子群的做法，可供核查 LVR 中 latent 状态聚类与失败归因、以及判断视觉细节与任务必要信息是否保留在表示中，但其本身不研究推理过程或 latent 生成。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Detection and Validation of Hidden Subgroup Disparities in Clinical AI

**abstract/A1：** Machine learning (ML) models used in clinical workflows often perform systematically differently across patient groups, raising concerns about equity and patient safety. Traditional subgroup analyses can expose such disparities only when rich metadata (e.g., patient demographics) is available and captures the sources of performance variation, conditions rarely met in practice. Algorithmic subgroup discovery methods offer a solution by identifying coherent groups from learned feature representations, but because true underlying stratifications are unknown, these methods are hard to evaluate. As a result, subgroup discovery has not been systematically applied or assessed for performance monitoring. Our main objective is to evaluate algorithmic subgroup discovery for performance monitoring, assessing the discovered subgroups' performance gaps and semantic coherence. Investigating cardiomegaly classification in a dataset of 223,228 chest X-rays, we showed that the discovered subgroups exhibited larger performance gaps (accuracies of 0.61-0.90) than traditional metadata-based analysis (0.80-0.94) and were frequently interpretable using patient, acquisition, and model metadata. As a proof of concept, we also showed that annotations from learned subgroups can be used to mitigate the biases they expose. We conclude that subgroup discovery is a practical and effective tool to enhance the performance validation and monitoring of ML systems in healthcare.

</details>

### 87. [EigenGradCAM with AugSmooth: Enhancing Explainability and Precision in Multi-Stage Alzheimer's Disease Detection](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32374v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32374

**作者：** Arsham Azam、Sagar Ashraf、Tayyaba Farhat、Rasikh Ali、Sheeraz Akram

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32374v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32374v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文面向四类阿尔茨海默病分期 MRI 分类，用基于特征向量分解的 Grad-CAM 配合梯度噪声平滑提升显著图稳定性，并以 WGAN-GP 生成样本缓解类别不平衡，报告 97.2% 测试准确率，热图集中于海马与内嗅皮层。

**相关性简评：** 对梯度加权卷积层做 PCA 分解并平滑显著权重，可能可供核查为定位模型内部视觉证据、评估通道/状态贡献的归因手段，但任务本身与 LVR 主线无直接联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** EigenGradCAM with AugSmooth: Enhancing Explainability and Precision in Multi-Stage Alzheimer's Disease Detection

**abstract/A1：** Alzheimer's disease (AD) is a progressive neurological disorder associated with memory loss, cognitive decline, and behavioral disturbances. Early and accurate diagnosis is essential, yet MRI-based deep learning models often lack interpretability, limiting their clinical use. This study presents an explainable deep learning approach for four-class AD staging using 2D axial MRI images. The method combines eigenvector-based Grad-CAM with gradient smoothing to improve saliency localization and heatmap stability. Principal component analysis is applied to gradient-weighted convolutional layers, while Gaussian noise is added to saliency weights before decomposition. To address class imbalance in the Moderate Dementia category, Wasserstein Generative Adversarial Network with Gradient Penalty (WGAN-GP) generates synthetic samples to balance the four-stage training distribution. Custom convolutional neural networks are trained on the Best Alzheimer MRI Dataset, achieving 97.2% test accuracy, 97.0% balanced accuracy, and a Matthews correlation coefficient of 0.944. The area under the ROC curve ranges from 0.96 to 0.99 across classes. Generated heatmaps highlight the hippocampus and entorhinal cortex, regions associated with Alzheimer's disease biomarkers. These findings suggest that the proposed approach combines strong classification performance with improved model interpretability, although further clinical validation is needed.

</details>

### 88. [Sam-Correction: Fully Adaptive Label Noise Reduction for Medical Image Segmentation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32377v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32377

**作者：** Takuro Shimaya、Masahiro Saiko

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、physics.med-ph

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32377v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32377v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文提出 SAM-Correction，在缺乏干净标注时借助 SAM 基础模型，并用一个同步训练的语料分割网络生成提示，以全自适应方式逐步纠正医学图像分割中的噪声 GT 掩码，从而训练出更稳健的分割模型。

**相关性简评：** 与主线无直接关系，但其「用基础模型的视觉证据迭代校正监督信号」的思路，可供核查 LVR 训练中视觉证据筛选与噪声监督如何影响内部表示学习；属纯分割任务应用，线索较弱。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Sam-Correction: Fully Adaptive Label Noise Reduction for Medical Image Segmentation

**abstract/A1：** The quality of the teacher label has a dramatic impact on the outcome of the machine learning process. Medical image segmentation often suffers from a noisy ground truth (GT) mask due to difficulties in consistent annotation. Although a recent study has proposed a promising method to curate the noisy GT mask, this requires additional well-curated GT masks that are not always accessible without the aid of multiple medical experts. As an alternative, we propose SAM-Correction, which utilizes a foundation model to correct the noisy GT mask in a fully adaptive manner instead of manually preparing well-curated masks. In our approach, we progressively mitigate label noise via the segment anything model (SAM), where prompts are given from another segmentation network that we train concurrently. Experiments with various types of label noise derived from actual human annotations demonstrate that SAM-C achieves precise noise reduction and high-performing segmentation models trained thereby. Our method paves the way for robust model training even when clean labels are entirely unavailable.

**comment/C1：** 5 pages, accepted to IEEE International Symposium on Biomedical Imaging (ISBI) 2024

</details>

### 89. [DealsFlow: An Evidence-Gated Agentic Harness for Venture Capital Deal Triage](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32380v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32380

**作者：** Ming Ze Tang、David Cutting、Boyd Carson、Vasiliki Carson

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32380v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32380v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文介绍 DealsFlow：一个部署在英国早期基金、人类在环的 LLM 投资初筛 harness，模型阅读演示文稿并引用证据逐项打分，代码再做聚合与封顶，并以 142 个已有结果的案例评估分数与投资表现的相关性。

**相关性简评：** 其「证据佐证上限」与「只降不升的怀疑者调用」构成一种保守的证据门控打分机制，可供核查是否可迁移为 LVR 中视觉证据筛选与置信校准的约束；但论文只处理金融尽调文本证据，不涉及内部状态或生成过程。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** DealsFlow: An Evidence-Gated Agentic Harness for Venture Capital Deal Triage

**abstract/A1：** Large language models (LLMs) can read startup pitch decks, but letting them emit investment scores directly invites score inflation, run-to-run variance and rationales that are hard to audit. We describe DealsFlow, a human-in-the-loop agentic harness deployed at a UK early-stage fund. The LLM reads the deck, cites evidence and rates each category; code combines the ratings, caps them and only ever lowers them. Three mechanisms implement this: a stage-aware corroboration ceiling (78, 70 or 60 out of 100) on factual categories without external evidence; a sceptic call that may lower but never raise a score; and a pre-fetch pattern that retrieves and indexes all evidence before a single generation pass. We evaluate on $N=243$ SEIS/EIS investments with recorded outcomes. Because recent investments have unresolved outcomes, we use the 142 that are at least three years old. There, the full pipeline's score has Spearman $ρ=0.45$ (95% CI 0.32 to 0.57) with the total-value multiple and AUC 0.71 for identifying write-offs, against $ρ=0.38$ and AUC 0.68 for an unconstrained LLM. The gain is small and comes mostly from the sceptic call; we did not test whether its lower-only rule matters. Investments that later exceeded $2\\times$ had 64% of factual categories corroborated, against 38% for write-offs (omnibus Kruskal-Wallis test over five outcome groups, $p=0.031$), and our retrieval was not date-restricted, so these figures may overstate screening skill. In a study of 20 inbound applications, analyst time per application fell from 44.7 to 18.9 minutes including verification and correction, and 84% of 240 cited claims were fully supported by their sources, but 70% of reports needed at least one correction.

**comment/C1：** 13 pages, 15 tables, 2 figures. Submitted to the AI4F 2026 workshop (LLMs and Generative AI for Finance) at ICAIF 2026

</details>

### 90. [UniWorld: A Unified World Model for Real-Time Visual World Simulation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32381v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32381

**作者：** Xin Zhang、Yabo Chen、Shuaiwei Wang、Zixuan Duan、Haibin Huang、Feng Xu、Xuelong Li

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32381v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32381v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** UniWorld 提出统一世界模型框架，将深度轨迹与参考外观耦合进同一视频生成模型：通过噪声对齐的重叠续接只提交新生成的 latent，并用滚动深度 latent 状态在采样窗口内固定几何条件，从而在有限活跃状态下保持 latent 历史的精确延续并达到实时吞吐。

**相关性简评：** 其「只提交新 latent + 窗口内固定条件 + 历史精确保留」的状态管理方式，可能为 LVR 中长程 latent 推理状态的增量更新、历史缓存保留以及深度/位置与注意力窗口结构对 latent 内容的影响提供可供核查的方法线索，但不直接研究视觉推理本身。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** UniWorld: A Unified World Model for Real-Time Visual World Simulation

**abstract/A1：** Continuous visual world simulation requires coupling evolving scene geometry with temporally persistent appearance. We present UniWorld, a unified framework that integrates depth trajectories and reference appearance within a shared video generation model. The central design maintains visual and geometric histories on a common timeline. Noise-aligned overlapping continuation places generated history at the prescribed sampling noise level and commits only newly generated latents. Rolling depth latent state preserves the corresponding geometric controls, incrementally reads newly available geometry, and keeps the conditioning fixed within each sampling window. Together, these mechanisms ensure exact preservation of latent history while bounding the active depth state by the window size. We evaluate continuous generation, paired history interventions, and long-horizon behavior across diverse scene categories. The same generator supports estimated, native, and on-demand simulator depth, while cached and incremental execution paths produce bitwise-identical outputs. Appearance changes and geometric control branches operate through the same interface without modifying committed history. The measured steady-state DiT execution path achieves real-time generation throughput, demonstrating the efficiency of UniWorld for continuous visual world simulation.

</details>

<a id="paper-91"></a>

### 91. [Interactive image diagnosis using test-time backpropagation from the auxiliary task](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32396v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32396

**作者：** Takuro Shimaya

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、physics.med-ph

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32396v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32396v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出多任务梯度辅助（MGA）方法，在测试时根据放射科医生对辅助任务预测的反馈进行反向传播，无需额外训练即可调整诊断建议，并在乳腺 X 线与 CT 数据上验证其准确率和鲁棒性。

**相关性简评：** 其测试时反向传播、以辅助任务反馈调节诊断输出的做法，可能为 LVR 中视觉证据筛选与推理期 latent 状态更新提供方法线索，但医学图像任务与 VLM 推理差异较大，梯度信号能否用于长程推理仍需核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Interactive image diagnosis using test-time backpropagation from the auxiliary task

**abstract/A1：** Multi-task deep learning has been developed to ensure explainability in computer-aided diagnosis (CAD) systems. These systems simultaneously predict interpretable characteristics of findings in auxiliary tasks to help radiologists assess the reliability of the diagnostic prediction. However, the CAD's opinion would not be referred to when the auxiliary task prediction deviates from the radiologist's expectation. To further leverage CAD's knowledge in the above situation, we proposed a multi-task gradient assist (MGA) method that brings interactivity to multi-task models without additional training. MGA allows radiologists to give feedback to the CAD and ask for additional diagnostic suggestions. Using two datasets of mammography and CT, we show that MGA successfully improves diagnostic accuracy upon feedback on various auxiliary tasks. Compared to an existing method using forward propagation only, MGA performs more robustly and can be combined to further improve accuracy. Our method paves a promising way for integrating artificial intelligence into image diagnosis.

**comment/C1：** 8 pages, accepted to SPIE Medical Imaging 2025

</details>

<a id="paper-92"></a>
