### 92. [LeafSkill: Efficient Leaf-Level Action Grounding for Recursive LLM Agents](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32412v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32412

**作者：** Shen Liu、Ze Zhou、Zhenguo Xu、Shaopu Wang、Yike Gao、Chunlei Wang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32412v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32412v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 LeafSkill：在模型参数外复用叶级动作接地经验，语义检索后绑定动作模板与当前可行动作，并由 1.7B 选择器决定采用或退回原 agent；在 Robotouille 上提升成功率并减少宿主模型 token 与调用。

**相关性简评：** 不涉及视觉或 latent 推理；其外部技能复用与「采用/延迟」选择器可能为 LVR 的推理状态缓存、计算复用及停止或委派决策提供可核查的类比线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** LeafSkill: Efficient Leaf-Level Action Grounding for Recursive LLM Agents

**abstract/A1：** Recursive large language model (LLM) agents solve long-horizon tasks through decomposition, execution, and environmental feedback. Yet after producing an atomic subtask, they often invoke the same large model again to translate a familiar instruction into an executable action. We observe that high-level planning is task-specific, whereas leaf-level action grounding recurs across tasks and can be checked against environment-admitted actions. We introduce LeafSkill, an external reuse layer that retrieves semantically related grounding experience, binds action templates to current objects and admissible actions, and uses a lightweight 1.7B selector to choose a bound action or defer the unchanged subtask to the original agent. Skills remain outside the model parameters, allowing a frozen selector to use post-training additions. Under lexical and syntactic paraphrases, exact matching retrieves none of the reference actions, while semantic retrieval preserves 96.0% and 97.0% candidate coverage; the frozen selector adopts 97.34% of unseen skill types. On an independent confirmation set of 200 Robotouille tasks, LeafSkill completes 137 tasks versus 121 for ReCAP (68.5% vs. 60.5%, exact McNemar p=.0259). On the 106 tasks completed by both, it reduces Qwen3.5-27B tokens by 14.42% and calls by 14.08%. A separate held-out repeat yields a consistent 9.0-point success gain and 18.98% token reduction. Reusable leaf grounding can thus improve task completion while reducing host-model inference without replacing high-level planning.

</details>

<a id="paper-93"></a>

### 93. [When Does Dynamics Randomization Test Context Inference?](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32414v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32414

**作者：** Oleg Smirnov、Sofiane Ennadir、John Pertoft、Bjartur Hjaltason、Sara Karimi

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32414v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32414v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文将随机动力学环境下 context encoder 能额外提供的一步预测信息形式化为 predictive headroom，并在固定与访问状态—动作分布下比较转移核，以区分查询覆盖变化与 context 权重变化；分析显示 headroom 在多种情形下偏低，而重置状态分量会使其升高。

**相关性简评：** 其「当前状态已包含多少证据、还缺多少」的量化框架可能可供核查迁移到 LVR 诊断：判断 latent 状态相对观测是否带来额外可用信息，并区分信息缺失与根本没被查询到，与贡献分配和证据累积问题相关。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** When Does Dynamics Randomization Test Context Inference?

**abstract/A1：** Environments with randomized dynamics are widely used to ask whether world models need explicit context inference. However, randomization can leave little predictive information for a context encoder to add, because distinct latent parameters may produce similar transitions at visited states, or those states may already reveal the active context. We formalize the remaining one-step information as predictive headroom and compare kernels under fixed and visited state--action distributions to distinguish changes in query coverage from changes in context weights. We analyze discrete and continuous environments and find low headroom both when kernels differ little and when visitation reduces an otherwise large discrepancy. At the same time, resetting state components increases headroom, showing that context evidence accumulates in state. We propose reporting this headroom alongside the randomized parameters, so that gains from explicit context inference can be interpreted relative to the information missing from the current state and action.

</details>

<a id="paper-94"></a>

### 94. [When to Stop Penalizing Actions? Martingale GRPO for VLA Reinforcement Learning](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32431v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32431

**作者：** Zongyao Zhao、Cheng-Jie Zhao、Ruihang Jiang、Bing Zheng、Zhaolin Wang、Yuanwei Liu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32431v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32431v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 MART-GRPO：用成功概率与策略得分鞅刻画失败反馈的理想截断点，并以 LoRA 微调的 VLM 在完整 rollout 视频中定位「最后可见且未被恢复的错误」，据此对该区间之后的梯度贡献做截断；在 RoboTwin 2.0 上以 OpenVLA-OFT 验证成功率提升。

**相关性简评：** 其用零条件期望的鞅停止条件决定「何时停止分配梯度」的思路，可能为 latent 推理轨迹中的停止决策与逐步贡献分配提供可核查的训练期工具，但研究对象是 VLA 动作序列，不直接涉及视觉 latent 状态生成。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** When to Stop Penalizing Actions? Martingale GRPO for VLA Reinforcement Learning

**abstract/A1：** A failed vision-language-action (VLA) rollout does not imply that every action contributed to failure. Yet in group-relative policy optimization (GRPO), a single trajectory-level advantage is assigned to all actions in a rollout. Potentially useful recovery attempts can thus be penalized alongside the errors they aim to correct. Ideal stopping conditions for failure feedback are characterized using success-probability and policy-score martingales. At these stops, the removed gradient contribution has zero conditional expectation given the retained information. Truncation therefore preserves the expectation of a reference GRPO group-gradient estimator and can reduce its covariance. One such stop occurs when the conditional probability of success is zero, motivating error and recovery cues for selecting candidate feedback boundaries. Martingale GRPO (MART-GRPO) is introduced with a vision-language model (VLM) for event-guided truncation. The VLM is fine-tuned on our annotated training set using low-rank adaptation (LoRA). The last visible error without observed recovery is localized by the frozen VLM in complete rollout videos. Failure feedback is retained through the selected error interval and removed afterward, while successful-rollout loss terms and original loss normalization are preserved. For retrospective boundaries, changes in expectation and variance are analyzed to assess departures from ideal stopping. Experiments with OpenVLA-OFT on RoboTwin 2.0 show that MART-GRPO improves success rates over full-trajectory GRPO across multiple tasks.

**comment/C1：** 31 pages, 9 figures

</details>

<a id="paper-95"></a>

### 95. [Learning Fine-Grained Visual Classification with Text as Privileged Information via Multimodal Distillation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32442v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32442

**作者：** Aishwarya Venkataramanan、Christian Ickler、Joachim Denzler

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32442v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32442v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 FG-LUPI：训练时用文本属性描述经门控 cross-attention 构建多模态教师，再在预测、patch 表示与注意力三个层面蒸馏到纯图像学生，在多个细粒度分类基准上同时提升准确率与置信度校准。

**相关性简评：** 其注意力与 patch 级蒸馏提示了把多模态监督下的视觉证据筛选结构压入单一模型内部状态的可能路径，可供核查 LVR 中视觉旁路与信息坍缩问题；但该文只做分类，不涉及推理期 latent 状态生成或动态计算，联系较弱。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Learning Fine-Grained Visual Classification with Text as Privileged Information via Multimodal Distillation

**abstract/A1：** Fine-grained visual classification (FGVC) requires distinguishing between highly similar categories by focusing on subtle, localized visual differences. Recent multimodal methods have demonstrated that textual attribute descriptions can provide valuable semantic supervision by explicitly describing discriminative object characteristics. However, these descriptions are typically unavailable at test time, limiting their direct use in deployment. We propose FG-LUPI, a Learning Using Privileged Information (LUPI)-based framework that leverages attribute descriptions during training. We train a multimodal teacher that integrates image patches and textual attributes via gated cross-attention, allowing semantic attributes to condition visual representations. We distill this knowledge into an image-only student using multi-level distillation at the prediction, patch, and attention levels, aligning global predictions, patch representations, and attention patterns with the teacher. This encourages the student to inherit attention structure learned from multimodal supervision while maintaining image-only inference. Experiments on multiple FGVC benchmarks show consistent improvements in classification accuracy over strong ViT-based baselines. Furthermore, evaluation using Expected Calibration Error (ECE) shows that FG-LUPI also produces better-calibrated predictions across all six benchmarks. These results suggest that textual attribute supervision can benefit not only fine-grained recognition performance but also the reliability of model confidence, while preserving the efficiency and practicality of image-only inference.

**comment/C1：** 16 pages

</details>

<a id="paper-96"></a>

### 96. [DualGSL: Prior-Guided Graph Structure Learning with Node-Adaptive Prediction Fusion](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32462v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32462

**作者：** Yuxia Sun、Yutszyuk Wong、Huihong Chen、Longhao Lv、Chang-Dong Wang、Li Zeng

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32462v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32462v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 DualGSL，用先验分支（特征图加虚拟类节点引入标签信息）与结构分支（学习到的亲和图）分别预测，并以基于投影表示、逐元素绝对差与预测置信度的节点自适应门控融合两路 logits，联合优化融合与分支分类损失。

**相关性简评：** 其按节点自适应门控、依据表示差异与置信度融合「先验路径」与「学习路径」的做法，可供核查是否能迁移到 LVR 中视觉旁路与 latent 推理路径的动态加权或按样本决定是否继续推理。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** DualGSL: Prior-Guided Graph Structure Learning with Node-Adaptive Prediction Fusion

**abstract/A1：** Graph structure learning (GSL) adapts propagation graphs to prediction tasks, but the relative predictive value of feature-derived priors and learned relations can vary across nodes. We propose DualGSL, a prior-guided framework for transductive node classification that learns node-specific weights for combining predictions from separate prior and task-adaptive propagation paths. The prior branch augments a feature graph with virtual class nodes to incorporate training-label information, while the structural branch combines learned affinity graphs with the feature-graph prior. The two branches share encoder parameters while preserving distinct representations and classification logits. A node-adaptive gate fuses the branch logits based on their projected representations, element-wise absolute difference, and prediction confidence. A joint objective integrates fused and branch-specific classification losses with prior-branch class-anchor alignment, coordinating graph construction, representation learning, and prediction fusion. Experiments against seven baselines on the AMG18 malware attribution dataset and the widely used 20News text classification and USPS handwritten-digit recognition benchmarks show that DualGSL achieves the highest cross-dataset average accuracy and Macro-F1 among the evaluated methods. Ablation studies on AMG18 further demonstrate the benefit of combining prior and learned propagation through logit fusion.

</details>

<a id="paper-97"></a>

### 97. [CauProphet: Teaching Agents to Develop Causal Cognition like Humans](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32477v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32477

**作者：** Kaiwen Wu、Chaochao Lu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32477v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32477v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 CauProphet 智能体：在长期「因果认知」知识库与当前证据之间构建结构化的 Causal Belief，用它做决策，并随新观测持续更新信念与长期因果知识。

**相关性简评：** 该方法用显式的中间信念状态和观测驱动的双层级更新来承载并累积知识，可能为 LVR 中推理状态的维护、视觉证据筛选与跨任务状态持久化提供可核查的借鉴线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** CauProphet: Teaching Agents to Develop Causal Cognition like Humans

**abstract/A1：** Large language model (LLM) agents increasingly support long-horizon tasks by leveraging external memory and past experience. However, existing agents mainly retrieve records or summaries, lacking the ability to form and refine causal understanding of the world. Inspired by human causal cognition, we introduce CauProphet, an agent that develops and updates causal knowledge within its Causal Cognition. For each task, CauProphet combines relevant knowledge in Causal Cognition with current evidence to construct a Causal Belief, a structured belief capturing causal factors and the effects of possible actions. It uses this belief for decision-making and continuously updates both the belief and Causal Cognition as new observations emerge, enabling causal knowledge that persists beyond individual experiences. Evaluations demonstrate that CauProphet progressively acquires causal knowledge and transfers it to future decision-making. In financial research, CauProphet leverages prior discoveries to improve downstream analyses and outperforms all baselines in mean rank correlation with next-day returns, achieving a 25.4% improvement over a baseline agent. It also improves prediction accuracy from 21.3% to 28.6% in dynamic real-world settings. Further experiments validate its ability to perform reliable causal analysis and action selection across scientific and everyday tasks, demonstrating the potential of causal experience learning for next-generation LLM agents.

**comment/C1：** 30 pages, including appendices

</details>

<a id="paper-98"></a>

### 98. [PathJev: Decision-Native Vision-Language Modelling for Histopathology](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32478v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32478

**作者：** Cristian Tommasino、Antonio M. Rinaldi

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32478v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32478v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文提出组织病理学 decision-native 模型 PathJev-P：把问题与运行时候选集写入输入，由小型决策头在一次前向中输出归一化概率，并与生成式基线、CONCH 等比较平衡准确率、校准及弃答表现。

**相关性简评：** 其候选集决策头与「none of these」弃答机制可能为 LVR 的停止决策和答案读出/校准提供核查线索，但论文未涉及 latent 推理状态、视觉证据获取或动态计算。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** PathJev: Decision-Native Vision-Language Modelling for Histopathology

**abstract/A1：** Vision-language models used for decisions in histopathology rarely return calibrated probabilities over the candidates a user cares about. Decision-native models write a question and a candidate set chosen at run time into the input and return a normalised distribution from a small decision head in one forward pass. We build PathJev-P, a decision-native model for histopathology: MedGemma-1.5-4B with LoRA adapters and a decision head, trained on 40,000 patch states from three public collections whose expert labels are turned into typed questions with organ-specific candidate sets and a "none of these" option. In a comparison over 3 seeds with an analysis plan fixed before testing, on test sets without documented overlap with the training data of the backbone, PathJev-P is more accurate than a generative model trained with the same backbone, data and selection rule, the zero-shot pathology model CONCH and a general medical decision model (balanced accuracy 0.541 against 0.492, 0.345 and 0.213); its advantage over the generative model comes from two organs absent from training. The calibration advantage depends on how the generative model's probabilities are read out. With the summed candidate log-likelihood used in previous work, PathJev-P has the lower calibration error (ECE 0.204 against 0.279); with a length-normalised read-out of the same generative model, examined post hoc, the two have similar mean calibration error (0.201) and the generative model is better calibrated on the unseen organs, where PathJev-P remains over-confident. A supervised linear probe on a pathology foundation model is better calibrated in every setting. In a qualitative whole-slide demonstration, PathJev-P labels unsupervised tissue clusters with abstention and separates carcinoma from other tissue while running the model on a fraction of the tiles.

**comment/C1：** 25 pages, 4 figures. Code: https://github.com/CosmoIknosLab/PathJev ; model and demo: https://huggingface.co/Cristiandieti/PathJev-P

</details>

<a id="paper-99"></a>

### 99. [When Agents Fail: Model or Harness--Who's to Blame?](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32483v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32483

**作者：** Shao Zhang、Hangfan Zhang、Yuanyuan Shi、Hao Li、Min-Le Su、Kangcong Li、Jingping Wang、Chunjiang Mu、Chen Zhang、Yang Chen、Bo Zhang、Shuyue Hu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.CL、cs.SE

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32483v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32483v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出以模型与执行框架交互为中心的五项指标（有效完成率 VCR、模型-框架匹配、跨模型匹配、交互残差、token 负载），在六个基准上评测四种通用 agent 框架与四种模型的组合表现。

**相关性简评：** 其将整体表现拆为组件效应与配对交互残差、并把失败尝试的成本计入 token 负载的做法，可能可供核查用于区分 LVR 中基础模型能力与推理脚手架（如 THINK/LOOK/STOP）各自的贡献及真实开销。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** When Agents Fail: Model or Harness--Who's to Blame?

**abstract/A1：** LLM agents combine models with execution harnesses, so agentic benchmark performance reflects their joint behavior. We argue that harnesses should therefore be evaluated through their interactions with underlying models. We develop a framework combining five complementary measures: (i) Valid Completion Rate (VCR), how reliably agents finish normally and deliver evaluable results, irrespective of task correctness; (ii) Model-Harness Fit, how well a harness supports task achievement through valid completion for a given model; (iii) Cross-Model Fit, a harness's overall suitability across evaluated models; (iv) Interaction Residuals, pairing-specific execution strengths and weaknesses hidden by component-level summaries; and (v) Token Load, the token demands of agent execution, including failed attempts. We evaluate four general-purpose harnesses (OpenCode, Codex CLI, Pi CLI, and DeepAgents) with four core models, plus two fixed native model-harness systems, on six benchmarks covering context management, tool use, long-context planning, multimodal interaction, and artifact handoff. Including native references and stress conditions, 111 evaluation settings yield 52,168 trajectories. Harness rankings vary across models and benchmarks; task performance and valid completion can favor different pairings, and similar task scores can accompany distinct execution profiles. OpenCode ranks first in cross-model performance, while DeepSeek V4 Flash Preview maintains consistently high Mean VCR across harnesses on the shared four-benchmark panel. These findings support evaluating model-harness interactions, training transferable harness-use capabilities in models, and harness designs adapted to the model and task.

**comment/C1：** Preprint under review

</details>

<a id="paper-100"></a>

### 100. [Efficient Zero-Shot Video Anomaly Detection via Signal-Aware Adaptive Fusion (SAAF)](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32486v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32486

**作者：** Khaled Waleed Dawoud、Muhammad Haris Khan

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI、cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32486v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32486v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 SAAF 框架，在 VLM 语义嵌入空间用 delta（帧间余弦距离）与 drift（偏离滚动时序上下文）双信号自适应采样，配合阈值门控级联架构——先做轻量帧级打分，仅在置信度需要更多上下文时才调用昂贵的视频级时序分析，实现免训练零样本视频异常检测并大幅降低成本。

**相关性简评：** 其「廉价打分先行、按置信度门控是否追加昂贵计算」的级联与早停设计，可作为核查 LVR 中 STOP 决策与动态计算预算分配的线索；但该文不涉及 latent 推理状态或视觉证据在内部表示中的保留，联系仍需较多核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Efficient Zero-Shot Video Anomaly Detection via Signal-Aware Adaptive Fusion (SAAF)

**abstract/A1：** Deploying Vision-Language Models for video anomaly detection confronts a fundamental constraint. Their semantic reasoning demands computational budgets incompatible with the scale of real-world video streams. We present Signal-Aware Adaptive Fusion (SAAF), a framework that achieves efficient zero-shot anomaly detection through three key innovations. First, we introduce a dual-signal adaptive sampler operating in semantic embedding space: a delta signal captures sudden events via frame-to-frame cosine distance, while a drift signal detects static and gradual anomalies through deviation from rolling temporal context. Their OR-gated combination with adaptive EMA-based thresholding provides comprehensive anomaly coverage without training data. Second, we propose a threshold-gated cascaded architecture where lightweight frame-level scoring precedes expensive video-level temporal analysis, invoking it only when confidence warrants additional context. Third, we design a signal-aware weighted fusion mechanism that dynamically adapts score combination based on which signal triggered frame selection. Across four benchmarks, SAAF delivers competitive accuracy at substantially lower cost. Among training-free zero-shot methods, SAAF sets a new state of the art on XD-Violence (92.07%), MSAD (86.70%), and ShanghaiTech (80.54%) AUC-ROC, with XD-Violence and MSAD also surpassing supervised baselines. SAAF sustains 44.95, 48.61, and 41.06 FPS on XD-Violence, UCF-Crime, and MSAD, a 2.43$\\times$, 2.63$\\times$, and 2.22$\\times$ throughput improvement over the prior best zero-shot method. Code: https://github.com/kdawoud91/SAAF

**comment/C1：** Accepted at WACV 2027

</details>

<a id="paper-101"></a>

### 101. [DRIVE: Fine-tuning via Data Contribution- and Diversity-aware Weighting with Prior Regularization](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32501v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32501

**作者：** Qing Liu、Xinrui Chen、Yi Du、Ou Wu、Weiyao Zhu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32501v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32501v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 DRIVE，一个单阶段微调中的逐样本权重分配框架：用可微的统一目标把 Shapley 可加性拆出的抗遗忘与适配两项贡献，与协方差多样性项、样本特征先验耦合，端到端梯度优化以平衡稳定性与可塑性，并给出理论保证与实验验证。

**相关性简评：** 其 Shapley 式贡献分解加多样性、先验正则的加权思路，可能为 latent 推理轨迹训练中的贡献分配与监督配比（如视觉证据样本与文本推理样本的权重）提供可核查线索，但原文并非多模态推理研究，迁移性需核验。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** DRIVE: Fine-tuning via Data Contribution- and Diversity-aware Weighting with Prior Regularization

**abstract/A1：** Single-stage fine-tuning is pervasive, yet catastrophic forgetting persists when the source pre-training data is inaccessible. Data weighting affords a natural lever to modulate sample influence. Yet existing weighting methods lack a principled mechanism to explicitly arbitrate knowledge retention against new-task adaptation, and provide no unified differentiable objective to accommodate auxiliary signals such as sample diversity and sample characteristics. This is because current schemes privilege one goal over the other, and no explicit gradient-compatible encoding exists for such signals. To address this, we propose DRIVE, a principled and efficient weight assignment framework. It derives per-sample weights via a unified differentiable objective where three coupled signals co-evolve: Shapley additivity splits contribution into anti-forgetting and adaptation components, which are reshaped by a covariance-based diversity term and stabilized by a sample-characteristic prior. Far from a mere combination, this yields an end-to-end gradient-based scheme balancing stability against plasticity. Theoretically, we prove that DRIVE enjoys superior stability-plasticity guarantees over existing weighting schemes and enhances parameter estimation robustness. Empirically, it attains superior overall performance, faster convergence, and stronger robustness. Code at https://github.com/luciabcd/DRIVE.

**comment/C1：** Published in NeurIPS 2026. 39 pages, including references and appendices

</details>

<a id="paper-102"></a>

### 102. [Fast and Useful: Adapting Distilled Generators for Visual Learning](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32512v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32512

**作者：** Patryk Bartkowiak、Dominik Michels、Sören Pirk、Wojciech Pałubicki

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32512v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32512v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文为蒸馏文生图模型拟合一个低秩更新，使其首步预测对齐到基座模型的中间状态，随后撤掉更新由原学生模型完成生成，从而在保持快速采样的同时产出能训练出更好分类器的图像。

**相关性简评：** 其「对齐学生早期状态到基座中间状态」的低秩轨迹修正与监督状态选择消融，可能为 LVR 中 latent 轨迹对齐、中间状态监督与贡献分配提供可核查的方法线索，但任务本身是图像生成数据，与 VLM 推理无直接关系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Fast and Useful: Adapting Distilled Generators for Visual Learning

**abstract/A1：** Distilled text-to-image models generate images quickly, but classifiers trained on those images can perform substantially worse than classifiers trained on images from their slower base models. We adapt released distilled generators to produce better training data while retaining fast generation. We fit a low-rank update to the student's first prediction so that it reaches an intermediate state produced by its unguided base, then disable the update and let the unchanged student finish generation. The base supplies training targets and is absent at inference. Guided targets and shorter jumps give classifiers lower accuracy in our ablations, showing why the state used for supervision matters. Across three generator families, the adapted images train better classifiers than images from the released students. With class-name templates at full ImageNet-1k scale, ResNet-50 top-1 rises from 8.4% to 34.2% for FLUX.2-klein and from 12.7% to 32.1% for SD-Turbo, using two and three denoiser evaluations per image, respectively. Fixed adapters also improve results on additional classes, fine-grained datasets, and other learner architectures and training recipes. Template-based adaptation remains useful against richer-prompt alternatives, while the benefit of combining adaptation with LLM generation prompts depends on the generator. Adapting a single generation step makes fast generators substantially more useful for training visual models.

</details>

<a id="paper-103"></a>

### 103. [Principled Nonlinear Concept Erasure and Steering](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32516v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32516

**作者：** Idan Simai、Shauli Ravfogel、Uri Shaham

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.CL

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32516v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32516v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出条件自编码器框架实现非线性概念擦除，证明完美重构加群体级独立可保证非线性 guardedness，并在多数据集与语言模型上取得更好的效用-擦除权衡，同时把表示 steering（概念转换）作为附带能力。

**相关性简评：** 其「只移除目标概念、保留其余信息」的重构加独立约束，可能可供核查 latent 状态中视觉细节是否保留、哪些信息仍可被解码；推理期概念转换也可类比 latent 状态干预，但需确认假设与多模态推理任务的可迁移性。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Principled Nonlinear Concept Erasure and Steering

**abstract/A1：** Learned representations often encode sensitive attributes, such as gender or race, that may be undesirable or unsafe to retain. Concept erasure aims to remove this information while preserving task-relevant content. Existing methods remain incomplete: linear methods provide guarantees only against linear probes, while nonlinear methods achieve stronger empirical erasure but lack formal guarantees and often sacrifice utility. In this work, we introduce a principled conditional autoencoder framework for nonlinear concept erasure. We follow the principle that erasure should remove only the sensitive attribute and nothing else, and we show that, under the stated assumptions, perfect reconstruction together with population-level independence is sufficient for perfect nonlinear guardedness, with preservation guarantees in the imperfect-reconstruction regime. Empirically, we attain a superior utility-erasure trade-off across multiple datasets, sensitive attributes, and language models, surpassing even the supervised variants of competing methods despite using no target-task labels, while being simpler to optimize and converging substantially faster. Beyond erasure, our framework yields concept conversion (representation steering) as a by-product: by changing only the target concept at inference time, it produces representations corresponding to different concept values - at no additional training cost - a capability no prior erasure method provides, and on which we likewise demonstrate strong empirical performance.

</details>

<a id="paper-104"></a>

### 104. [Shared Is Not Enough: Predictor Width and Factor Readability Under Masked Latent Prediction](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32562v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32562

**作者：** Nilotpal Kakati

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.CV、physics.data-an

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32562v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32562v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文构建因子已知的可控模拟器，用同样视图对训练小型编码器（I-JEPA、SimCLR、BYOL），再用探针测量两视图共享因子的可读性。结果显示 I-JEPA 采用 ViT-B 比例的半宽浅预测器时，因子可读性中位数约为 SimCLR 的 40%；将预测器加宽至编码器等宽后，在每个训练随机种子下都反超两种对比方法。

**相关性简评：** 预测器宽度改变哪些因子仍可读出，可能与 LVR 中预测器容量、latent 内容保留及信息坍缩的关系相关，可供核查；但该工作限于自监督视觉表征，未涉及推理或答案使用 latent。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Shared Is Not Enough: Predictor Width and Factor Readability Under Masked Latent Prediction

**abstract/A1：** Masked latent prediction methods such as I-JEPA aim to learn predictable features, but what determines which information they retain is less clear. We built a simulator in which every factor behind an image is known, so a probe can measure how readable each factor remains after training. We trained small encoders (0.7M parameters) with I-JEPA, SimCLR and BYOL on the same pairs of views and probed a factor that both views share. I-JEPA had a predictor with half the encoder's width and depth, the proportions it uses for ViT-B. Its median score was about 40% of SimCLR's. Widening the predictor to the encoder's width reversed this ordering at every training seed, against both other methods. We release the simulator and the trained models.

</details>

<a id="paper-105"></a>

### 105. [From Parameter Rebasin to Functional Transfer Across Heterogeneous Transformers](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32581v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32581

**作者：** Filippo Rinaldi、Aniello Panariello、Giacomo Salici、Simone Magistri、Angelo Porrello、Andrew D. Bagdanov、Simone Calderara

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32581v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32581v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 Ariadne，一种免训练方法：不搬运参数量纲不兼容的 task vector，而是用微调在匹配层块上引起的激活变化刻画任务行为，经局部 Procrustes 对齐后以闭式岭回归合成目标模型的原生更新，实现跨深度、宽度与语言模型的异构 Transformer 迁移。

**相关性简评：** 以激活空间的功能效应代替参数搬运、在匹配层块间对齐并闭式合成状态更新，这一线索可供核查是否能用于跨模型变体传递或对齐 LVR 的 latent 推理状态；但论文本身不涉及多模态推理，仅为方法借鉴。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** From Parameter Rebasin to Functional Transfer Across Heterogeneous Transformers

**abstract/A1：** Adapting pretrained Transformers to downstream tasks produces task-specific updates that are expensive to reproduce for every model variant. Reusing these updates across different depths and widths is challenging because their parameters have incompatible shapes and do not share a canonical coordinate system. We introduce Ariadne, a training-free method that transfers task-specific behavior across heterogeneous Transformers without resizing the source model or transporting its parameters. Rather than treating the source task vector, i.e. the difference in parameter space between the fine-tuned and the base models, as the object to move, Ariadne characterizes fine-tuning by the activation changes it induces at matched blocks. First, it aligns these effects to a target base through local Procrustes maps. Then, it directly synthesizes a native target update by closed-form ridge regression. Across Transformer expansion, reduction and language-model transfer, Ariadne improves target-model performance while substantially reducing the time and memory required to construct an update relative to previous cross-architecture rebasin approaches. These results show that local functional effects provide an efficient alternative to parameter transport.

</details>

<a id="paper-106"></a>

### 106. [Trajectory Matching: Discrete Flow Matching with Structured Actions](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32602v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32602

**作者：** Niklas Kemper、Marten Lienen、David Lüdke、Stephan Günnemann

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32602v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32602v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出「轨迹匹配」：将离散流匹配的条件从固定端点扩展为连接端点的完整动作序列，从而为用户定义的结构化动作空间（改变状态大小、多组件修改、依赖前置动作）给出可计算且免模拟的训练目标，并在树与分子图生成上验证其优于逐组件分解的 DFM 基线。

**相关性简评：** 其将「动作序列」作为条件与建模选择、并为依赖前置结构的动作提供免模拟训练目标的做法，可能为 latent 推理轨迹的构造、逐步监督与贡献分配提供可核查线索，但对象是离散图生成而非视觉推理。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Trajectory Matching: Discrete Flow Matching with Structured Actions

**abstract/A1：** The natural units of structured discrete generation are often not independent single-component edits but richer actions: attaching a fragment to a molecule, inserting a node together with an incident edge, or growing a hierarchy by adding a child. Yet most existing discrete flow matching methods (DFM) are restricted to independent component-wise edits over fixed-size states. This restriction stems from endpoint conditioning: endpoints fix the source and target during training but not the action sequence between them, making tractable conditional rates hard to define for complex actions that cannot be factorized component-wise. We propose trajectory matching, which conditions the flow on a full sequence of actions connecting the endpoints. This yields principled, simulation-free training targets for user-defined action spaces, including actions that change state size, modify multiple components, or depend on earlier actions. We prove that the resulting objective recovers the marginal flow and that trajectory matching strictly subsumes factorized DFM, while additionally capturing actions with prerequisite structure such as hierarchy growth or fragment-based molecule generation. Empirically, on tree and molecular graph generation, trajectory matching with domain-aligned actions matches or surpasses factorized DFM baselines (lifting MOSES validity from 92.8% to 99.2%) and transfers without retraining to substructure extension and linking. Trajectory matching enlarges the design space of discrete flow matching by turning the action vocabulary into a modeling choice. An interactive explanation of trajectory matching is available at https://niklaskemper.com/blog/trajectory-matching/.

</details>

<a id="paper-107"></a>

### 107. [LLaCO: Continuous Diffusion for Large Language Models with Competition-Aware Semantic Scheduling](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32604v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32604

**作者：** Zijian Jia、Peixuan Zhang、Genying Li、Di Wu、Yujie Fu、Si Li、Boxin Shi、Shuchen Weng

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32604v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32604v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文分析连续扩散文本生成中嵌入范数衰减造成的「伪超越」现象，提出竞争阈值理论刻画 token 身份的成对竞争，并用语义保持调度重新分配去噪轨迹时间，构建 8B 的 LLaCO 框架。

**相关性简评：** 其连续轨迹上的 token 身份衰减、方向性竞争与按对数秩衰减分配去噪时间，可供核查 diffusion 式 latent 推理状态更新、信息坍缩与计算预算分配问题，但工作限于纯文本生成，与视觉证据无直接联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** LLaCO: Continuous Diffusion for Large Language Models with Competition-Aware Semantic Scheduling

**abstract/A1：** Continuous diffusion models have emerged as a promising non-autoregressive paradigm for text generation over continuous embedding spaces. Along the continuous trajectory, the residual token identity directly shapes the denoising objective. However, the geometric trajectory misaligns with temporal progression, triggering severe temporal compression. In this paper, we show that embedding norm decay causes Euclidean distance to yield spurious overtaking, indicating that token competition must be evaluated directionally. In this space, a candidate overtakes the source token only when the effective noise magnitude surpasses their semantic separation. We formalize these pairwise dynamics as the Competition Threshold Theory (CTT). Crucially, high-dimensional overtaking requires extreme noise levels, causing standard trajectories to concentrate identity decay into a narrow window near the noisy endpoint. To resolve this, we propose Semantic Retention Scheduling (SRS) to align time allocation with the progression of identity decay, and demonstrate that existing token identity metrics are fundamentally statistical aggregations of these pairwise crossings. Building on these theoretical insights, we introduce LLaCO, a generative framework powered by competition-aware semantic scheduling. LLaCO adapts a pretrained backbone to continuous representations via an embedding directional adapter and timestep-conditioned modulation. To operate within the directional space, LLaCO employs a spherical noising process formulated as a geodesic interpolation toward a mask-biased endpoint, eliminating norm decay while preserving accurate token competition. Guided by SRS, LLaCO reallocates the denoising trajectory proportionally to the logarithmic rank decay. Instantiated as an 8B model, LLaCO outperforms previous continuous diffusion baselines and rivals discrete diffusion models of comparable scale.

</details>

<a id="paper-108"></a>

### 108. [Interpreting Supervised Pairwise Graph Representations: Local Evidence, Confounding, and Provable Post-hoc Correction](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32611v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32611

**作者：** Ilya Brekin、Ariela Moldavskaya、Elina Sidorova、Ural Valeev

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32611v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32611v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文在受控 Transformer 错误检测任务与公开 HCP Emotion 图数据上，检验监督式成对图表示的高阶拓扑是否真正携带信号，发现其相对模型置信度的优势在控制序列长度后消失、边幅度接近随机水平，并给出 Fisher 事后校正及交叉注意力失败回路分析。

**相关性简评：** 其「从 pooled hidden state 几乎完美还原序列长度、再据此消去表观增益」的混杂控制与事后校正思路，可能为核查 LVR 中 latent 表示的内容贡献与长度、位置等可解码冗余之间的贡献分配提供方法线索，需进一步确认能否迁移到多模态推理场景。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Interpreting Supervised Pairwise Graph Representations: Local Evidence, Confounding, and Provable Post-hoc Correction

**abstract/A1：** Supervised pairwise graph representations convert pairwise predictive relations into sample-specific graphs, but graph-level accuracy does not by itself show that higher-order topology carries the signal. In a controlled Transformer error-detection task, supervised pairwise graphs outperform attention-as-adjacency (ROC-AUC $0.839$ versus $0.559$; the comparable no-confidence pair is $0.687$ versus $0.559$), but their apparent advantage over model confidence disappears after controlling for sequence length, which is almost perfectly recoverable from the pooled hidden state ($R^2=0.9995$). On published HCP Emotion ensemble graphs, signed node aggregation already acts as an ensemble of supervised pairwise predictions. Edge magnitude alone is near chance; topology-aware augmentations change ROC-AUC by at most $0.004$, with only sign-separated degree showing small positive changes across all tested graph sizes, while a validation-tuned three-layer GCN provides no measurable improvement over the signed node-summary baseline. A Fisher post-hoc corrector satisfies its distribution-agnostic guarantees across the evaluated feature representations and remains stable under reduced labelled budgets; in a near-error-free fMRI calibration regime the bound correctly becomes statistically uninformative. A separate cross-attention analysis identifies a redundant reversal circuit and downstream EOS-timing and copy failures. Higher-order topology should therefore be credited only after local supervised evidence and recoverable nuisance variables have been excluded.

</details>

<a id="paper-109"></a>

### 109. [The Standard Deviation in VICReg Buys a Safety Margin](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32630v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32630

**作者：** Hawar Dizayee

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32630v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32630v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文在受控设置（ResNet-18、CIFAR-10/100）下实验检验 VICReg 中「标准差 hinge」与「方差 hinge」的防坍缩差异，扫描权重 μ 五个数量级；结果显示默认权重下两者线性探针精度相近，但标准差 hinge 的崩溃阈值低 35–130 倍，其梯度在方差变小时衰减更慢，相当于多出安全裕度。

**相关性简评：** 该文属自监督表示学习，不直接研究 VLM 推理；但其关于表示坍缩阈值与正则梯度衰减的分析，可供核查 LVR 中 latent 状态表示坍缩、正则强度选择与训练稳定性的借鉴线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** The Standard Deviation in VICReg Buys a Safety Margin

**abstract/A1：** VICReg prevents collapse with a hinge on the standard deviation of each embedding dimension, and its authors argue from the gradient that a hinge on the variance would fail. To our knowledge, this claim has not been tested experimentally. We test it in a controlled setting (ResNet-18, CIFAR-10 and CIFAR-100), changing only the hinge and sweeping its weight $μ$ over five orders of magnitude. At the default weight ($μ= 1$), the two hinges reach the same linear-probe accuracy on CIFAR-10 (about 89.5%). Both collapse below a threshold weight, but the standard-deviation hinge's threshold is 35-130$\\times$ lower. CIFAR-100 shows a consistent gap. In training, the variance hinge's gradient fades as the feature variance shrinks, while the standard-deviation hinge's does not until that variance is very small. The standard-deviation hinge therefore buys a safety margin: it keeps training at weights far below the point where the variance hinge collapses.

**comment/C1：** 11 pages. Code: https://github.com/hawardizayee/vicreg-hinge

</details>

<a id="paper-110"></a>

### 110. [EditJev: System One Routing for Image Editing Models](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32635v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32635

**作者：** Zhile Guan、Xu Jiang、Yule Duan、Gongbo Zhang、Shuyu Wang、Xiao Chen、Jian Zhang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32635v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32635v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 EditJev：在视觉语言骨干上加轻量 preference head，单次前向直接输出候选图像编辑模型的样本级偏好分数，并用离线成对偏好做排序训练，推理时结合速度分数平衡编辑质量与延迟。

**相关性简评：** 其「偏好头+成对排序」的轻量样本级打分方式可供核查是否能迁移为 LVR 中决定是否继续 LOOK／何时 STOP 或选择视觉证据源的决策头，但论文任务为编辑模型路由，与 latent 推理状态本身无直接联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** EditJev: System One Routing for Image Editing Models

**abstract/A1：** Instruction-based image editing models exhibit pronounced sample-specific performance variation: different models can produce substantially different results for the same source image and editing instruction, and the best-performing model often varies across requests. These models also differ substantially in inference latency, making fixed-model deployment inefficient. We therefore study image editing model routing, which dynamically selects an editor from a candidate pool given an input image and editing instruction. We introduce EditJev, a System One routing model that directly predicts sample-level preference scores over candidate image editing models in a single forward pass. A lightweight preference head on a vision-language backbone produces a fixed-dimensional score vector without autoregressive model-name generation or response parsing. To train EditJev, we construct pairwise preferences from offline candidate outputs and optimize a pairwise ranking objective to learn relative model suitability. At inference time, EditJev combines the predicted preference scores with model-level speed scores to balance editing quality and generation latency. Experiments on ImgEdit and GEdit-En show that EditJev improves aggregate quality over the strongest fixed editor, from 4.540 to 4.592 and from 8.194 to 8.255, respectively, while reducing estimated end-to-end latency by 60.8% and 31.7%. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of direct, structured model routing for efficient image editing.

**comment/C1：** 12 pages, 4 figures, 7 tables

</details>

<a id="paper-111"></a>

### 111. [DT-JEPA: Learning to Predict Future Representations for Decision-Making](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32657v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32657

**作者：** Changxu Ni、Yiyang Ni、Jun Li、Feiran You、Hongyang Du、Haitao Zhao、Shi Jin

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32657v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32657v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 DT-JEPA：以 JEPA 式潜空间未来表示预测替代 Decision Transformer 的回报条件，用概率预测器建模未来表示的条件分布与不确定性，先从无奖励离线轨迹预训练再在线微调，并给出互信息下界与有限样本性能界，在 D4RL 上验证。

**相关性简评：** 其「预测未来 latent 表示并显式建模其分布与不确定性」的思路，可能为 LVR 中推理状态的预测式更新与基于不确定性的继续/停止决策提供方法线索，但该工作处于离线 RL 场景、无视觉证据处理，迁移可行性尚待核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** DT-JEPA: Learning to Predict Future Representations for Decision-Making

**abstract/A1：** Decision Transformer (DT) has demonstrated strong performance in various offline reinforcement learning (RL) tasks by conditioning action generation on expected future returns and historical trajectories. However, treating expected future returns as the condition provides an incomplete description of the future, as trajectories with different state transitions and behaviors may share similar returns. Moreover, expected future returns inherently depend on task-specific reward functions, which makes policy learning reliant on reward-labeled datasets and restricts its scalability to tasks with similar but unseen rewards. Recently, Joint Embedding Predictive Architectures (JEPA) have emerged as a promising paradigm of self-supervised learning-based world models, which learn predictive representations of future dynamics in latent space rather than reconstructing future states in the original space. Inspired by JEPA, we propose DT-JEPA, an offline pretraining and online fine-tuning framework that learns general knowledge from reward-free offline trajectories and achieves excellent adaptation to unseen tasks. Instead of predicting a single deterministic future embedding, we design a novel probabilistic predictor to model the conditional distribution of future representations in the latent space, thereby capturing both predictive information and uncertainty about future dynamics. Theoretically, we show that 1) the JEPA objective seeks to maximize a variational lower bound on the mutual information between historical representations and future trajectories; and 2) predictive future-conditioned policy learning admits a finite-sample performance bound with an O(N-1/4) estimation term. Experiments on D4RL benchmarks demonstrate that DT-JEPA achieves efficient adaptation across varying offline RL tasks, outperforming traditional DT-based and other offline RL methods.

</details>

<a id="paper-112"></a>

### 112. [Do Language Models Trust Their Own Justifications? A Study on Functional Consistency](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32665v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32665

**作者：** Alisson Rosa Pereira、Bruno Iochins Grisci

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32665v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32665v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文提出自解释「功能自一致性」评估框架，通过隔离、移除或语义反转模型标注的关键 token，并用 sufficiency、comprehensiveness 与 counterfactuality 指标，检验多个 LLM 在评论分类中的解释是否与自身预测行为一致。

**相关性简评：** 其干预式忠实性指标可供核查 LVR 中筛选出的视觉证据或 latent 内容是否真正因果影响答案，但文本分类场景与视觉推理之间仍需迁移验证。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Do Language Models Trust Their Own Justifications? A Study on Functional Consistency

**abstract/A1：** Large Language Models (LLMs) have been widely adopted in text classification tasks, where they not only output class predictions but also generate explanations that highlight the tokens deemed most relevant for reaching the predicted label. Yet it remains unclear whether these highlighted elements are behaviorally consistent with the model's own predictions under targeted interventions, which we refer to as functional auto-consistency. While much of the literature evaluates the textual plausibility of such explanations, few studies assess their functional consistency with the model's actual behavior. In this work, we propose an experimental framework based on the principle of auto-consistency: if a model identifies certain tokens as decisive, then isolating, removing, or semantically inverting them should produce systematic and interpretable changes in its predictions. We operationalize this evaluation through sufficiency, comprehensiveness, and counterfactuality metrics, and conduct experiments on IMDB and Steam reviews across both closed-source (GPT-4o-mini) and open-source LLMs (Gemma3, Granite8B, DeepSeek). Results show that GPT-4o-mini follows the expected progression across all metrics, Gemma3 and Granite8B maintain coherence under sufficiency but lose consistency under more demanding interventions, while DeepSeek variants display structural deviations, either failing to preserve sufficiency or overreacting under comprehensiveness and counterfactuality. These findings show that explanation reliability varies across LLM families and scales, with smaller models displaying contradictions and larger ones exhibiting over-sensitivity. By combining sufficiency, comprehensiveness, and counterfactuality, our approach provides a systematic methodology for assessing the functional consistency of LLM self-explanations.

**comment/C1：** 6 pages, 2 figures, 1 table. Author accepted manuscript. Published in International Joint Conference on Neural Networks (IJCNN), Maastricht, Netherlands, 2026

</details>

<a id="paper-113"></a>

### 113. [Are Textual Residuals Truly Complementary? Learning Visual Complements under Semantic Superposition for Visible-Infrared Person Re-Identification](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32709v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32709

**作者：** Xiaoyang Li、Mingming Lu、Wei Zhou、Hao Li、Canhui Zhang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32709v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32709v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文重新检验「文本差即可视为视觉补充」这一假设，指出密集文本残差会把有用外观变化与冗余纠缠，进而提出稀疏残差学习与反事实校准，把文本差异转化为选择性、有视觉依据的补充信息，用于可见光-红外行人重识别。

**相关性简评：** 其「稀疏选择残差维度＋以视觉差异反事实校准，抑制冗余与干扰」的做法，可供核查 LVR 中如何筛选真正有视觉证据支撑的 latent 内容、避免表示坍缩或冗余旁路，但任务与推理机制相距较远，迁移性待判断。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Are Textual Residuals Truly Complementary? Learning Visual Complements under Semantic Superposition for Visible-Infrared Person Re-Identification

**abstract/A1：** Text-enhanced visible-infrared person re-identification (VI-ReID) exploits textual descriptions to compensate for appearance cues that are weak or unavailable under infrared imaging. Existing methods construct compensation from differences between modality-oriented textual representations, implicitly assuming that textual differences are visually complementary. We revisit this assumption through semantic superposition and show that dense textual residuals may entangle useful appearance variations with redundant, making direct residual fusion unreliable. We propose Superposition-Aware Residual Calibration, which transforms dense textual differences into selective and visually grounded complements. Low-Interference Sparse Residual Learning represents modality-oriented text embeddings as sparse activation strengths over a shared dictionary, selects positive strength differences, and suppresses both dictionary-level overlap and activation-weighted interference to reconstruct a compact, selectively controlled residual. Visual Complement and Counterfactual Calibration further calibrates the residual against identity-specific RGB-IR visual discrepancies, encouraging retained semantics to provide meaningful compensation while preserving the residual structure. Experiments on Tri-SYSU-MM01, Tri-RegDB, and Tri-LLCM show that SARC consistently improves the VI-ReID backbone and remains competitive with text-assisted methods. Mechanism analyses show that SARC produces more concentrated textual variations, reduces dictionary-level feature interference, and improves residual-visual-complement alignment for most samples. These results demonstrate that effective cross-modal compensation requires textual residuals to be selectively represented and explicitly calibrated, rather than directly treated as visual complements. Code and implementation details will be released upon publication.

**comment/C1：** 24 pages, 4 figures

</details>

<a id="paper-114"></a>

### 114. [ActionRAG: Evidence-Refining Graph Diffusion for Long-Term Memory in LLM Agents](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32752v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32752

**作者：** Navyansh Mahla、Sashank Vandrangi

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.CL

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32752v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32752v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 ActionRAG：面向 LLM agent 长期记忆的图记忆架构，索引端做类型标签校准与分层实体消解，查询端用双通道实体/三元组检索构造局部图，并以查询条件的接收端归一化扩散重估三元组分数（含种子保护、hub 惩罚、三元组实体化）。在 MemoryAgentBench 上平均 55.1，较最强检索基线高 5.4 点，并报告查询成本与延迟。

**相关性简评：** 其「不把初始相似度当最终分数、用局部图路径结构做查询时重估」的机制，可能可供核查如何用于 LVR 中视觉或跨模态证据的筛选与 latent 相似度的结构化重排；但全文为纯文本记忆检索，与视觉推理状态生成无直接联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** ActionRAG: Evidence-Refining Graph Diffusion for Long-Term Memory in LLM Agents

**abstract/A1：** LLM agents need long-term memory that can recover precise and mutually consistent evidence from growing interaction histories, particularly when answers depend on relations among facts rather than isolated semantic matches. Current memory pipelines remain vulnerable at two stages: indexing errors can fragment repeated entities or merge distinct ones, and online retrieval often treats initial similarity scores as final even when the surrounding graph provides evidence for revising them. We introduce ActionRAG, a graph memory architecture that couples cautious indexing with query-time graph retroevaluation. Its indexing pipeline reconciles type labels and applies tiered entity resolution. Given a query, dual-channel entity and triple retrieval forms an initial evidence set and induces a bounded local graph. Query-conditioned receiver-normalized diffusion refines triple scores using local path structure. Seed protection, hub-penalized flux, and triple materialization preserve direct evidence while recovering missed connections. We evaluate ActionRAG on MemoryAgentBench (MAB), which tests accurate retrieval, conflict resolution, long-range understanding, and test-time learning. Across its eight deterministic-scoring columns, ActionRAG achieves 55.1 average accuracy, improving over the strongest retrieval-based memory baseline by 5.4 points. Under MAB's query-time cost accounting, mean synthesis cost stays below \\$0.001 per query on the four reported cost columns, and the sum of separately measured retrieval-side p99 components is 440.7 ms on our consumer Apple Silicon setup.

</details>

<a id="paper-115"></a>

### 115. [Direction-Aware Token Credit Assignment with Reward-Model Gradients](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32772v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32772

**作者：** Yikuan Hu、Jifeng Zhu、Minle Su、Jiaqin Yue、Gujie Shao、Yanzhe Chen、Haoxuan Jia、Haoran Xu、Wangzhou Dai

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32772v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32772v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 DATC，用冻结奖励模型的梯度映射到策略 logit 空间，并按奖励方向与 GRPO 采样动作更新方向的对齐程度分配 token 级信用，同时保持响应级优势的符号与均值。

**相关性简评：** 其 token 级贡献分配与轨迹监督可启发 LVR 中隐式推理步骤的信用归因，但方法面向纯文本 GRPO，迁移到多模态推理状态仍需核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Direction-Aware Token Credit Assignment with Reward-Model Gradients

**abstract/A1：** GRPO shares a response-level advantage across tokens, leaving unresolved how strongly each token should contribute to learning. Updates induced by the shared advantage reinforce or suppress the sampled token at each position, but these updates do not always point toward increasing reward at the token level. This motivates a work-based view of credit: just as work depends on the component of force along a displacement, token credit should reflect the local reward change along the policy update direction. We introduce Direction-Aware Token Credit Assignment (DATC), which maps frozen reward-model gradients into policy logit space and allocates weights by evaluating the alignment between the reward direction and GRPO's signed sampled-action update direction, while preserving the sign and mean of the response-level advantage. DATC outperforms GRPO in all four settings across two model families.

**comment/C1：** 28 pages, 7 figures. Project page: https://datc-project.github.io/

</details>

<a id="paper-116"></a>

### 116. [Video-MemComp: Taylor-Guided Sensitivity-Aware Memory Management for Streaming Video Understanding](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32784v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32784

**作者：** Yonghui Niu、Yuhao Ji、Chang Nie、Jiao Zhang、Yilong Li、Junlan Feng、Ran He、Caifeng Shan、Chaoyou Fu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32784v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32784v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出免训练、敏感性感知的流式视频上下文管理框架 Video-MemComp，用一阶泰勒展开将压缩误差分解为局部近似误差与层间结构敏感性，在 pre-RoPE 安全区以注意力保持的 logit bias 做 token 聚合，并按离线梯度先验与在线上下文熵动态分配各层 KV cache 预算；在 MLVU、VideoMME、EgoSchema 上以约每层 6000 token 的缓存取得与全缓存或先进压缩方法相当或更优的精度。

**相关性简评：** 其「按层敏感性分配保留预算 + 保持注意力结构的聚合」为视觉证据在内部状态中的层间保留、以及缓存与注意力结构如何影响 latent 表示相似度提供了可供核查的方法线索，但论文本身面向视频长上下文效率，不涉及推理期 latent 状态生成或 THINK/LOOK/STOP 决策。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Video-MemComp: Taylor-Guided Sensitivity-Aware Memory Management for Streaming Video Understanding

**abstract/A1：** The rapid growth of video data has created an urgent need for efficient video understanding systems that can handle long and continuous visual inputs, posing significant challenges for Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) due to severe memory bottlenecks. Moreover, existing compression methods often overlook the explosive accumulation of approximation errors. To address this issue, we propose Video-MemComp, a training-free and sensitivity-aware context management framework for memory-constrained streaming video understanding. Our method theoretically decomposes compression error into orthogonal local approximation error and layer-wise structural sensitivity via a first-order Taylor expansion. To minimize local approximation error, we perform token aggregation in a pre-RoPE safe zone with an attention-conserving logit bias, mitigating geometric distortions caused by RoPE rotations. To manage layer-wise structural sensitivity, we dynamically allocate the KV cache budget based on offline gradient priors and online context entropy, prioritizing high-sensitivity layers. Video-MemComp outperforms or achieves performance comparable to full-cache and state-of-the-art compression methods on benchmarks including MLVU, VideoMME, and EgoSchema. Notably, it improves the overall accuracy of Qwen2.5-VL-3B from 62.3% to 64.1% while reducing the KV cache to an equivalent of 6,000 tokens per layer, saving over 90% of KV memory.

**comment/C1：** Accepted by ACM MM 2026. Project Page: https://hhnk666.github.io/Video-MemComp.github.io

</details>

<a id="paper-117"></a>

### 117. [Consommé: Conditioned Token Merging for Split Inference](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32794v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32794

**作者：** Man Ngo、Jan S. Rellermeyer

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32794v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32794v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 研究冻结 ViT 主干下 split inference 的 token 缩减：用信息瓶颈把 CLS 压缩为紧凑 latent 传输，并以其为条件学习 patch gate，通过软分配一步合并到任意 k 个 token。

**相关性简评：** 「压缩后的 latent 作为条件信号筛选视觉 token」可能与 LVR 的内部状态瓶颈、视觉证据筛选和计算预算控制相关，可供核查；但其目标主要是通信效率而非推理机制。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Consommé: Conditioned Token Merging for Split Inference

**abstract/A1：** Split inference partitions a Vision Transformer between a resource-constrained edge device and a server. However, transmitting intermediate tokens across the communication channel is costly. Deploying fine-tuned backbones to every endpoint becomes expensive as well. We study token reduction for split inference under a frozen backbone, where only a small adapter is shipped. We observe that the CLS token plays a dual role: it acts as the global summary the receiver needs for classification, and it serves as a signal on which patches to keep. We propose Consommé, a method utilizing both characteristics. There, an information bottleneck compresses the CLS token into a compact latent which is transmitted in place of the full CLS token and conditions a learned patch gate merging patches into exactly $k$ tokens via soft assignment. It reaches any budget in a single step, thus escaping the per-layer cap of bipartite matching (BPM). On DeiT-S/T, it leads all methods at aggressive token budgets and remains operable at shallow splits where BPM cannot operate, while matching fine-tuned methods at gentle budgets at a fraction of their deployment cost.

</details>

<a id="paper-118"></a>

### 118. [Multiplicative vs. Additive State Coupling in Learned Optimizer Schedules](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32801v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32801

**作者：** M. Harper Langston、Pierre-David Letourneau、Dalton Jones、Roland Memisevic、Peter Scott、Mingu Lee、Richard A. Lethin、Christopher Lott、Fatih Porikli、Harris Teague

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI、cs.CL、math.NA、math.OC

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32801v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32801v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文对 MAX-CUT 连续罚函数松弛展开经典求解器并反向传播，学习优化调度；比较状态无关调度、状态自适应双线性（乘性）调度，以及与双线性模型输入输出头相同的加性消融，考察其在更大实例上的分布外迁移，并配合 30 轮权重衰减扫描验证差异的稳健性。

**相关性简评：** 「求解器状态以乘性还是加性方式进入调度」的架构对照，可能为 LVR 中 latent 状态注入形式与规模/长度泛化之间的关系提供可核查线索，但属纯组合优化设定，迁移到视觉推理需进一步判断。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Multiplicative vs. Additive State Coupling in Learned Optimizer Schedules

**abstract/A1：** We learn schedules for a continuous penalty relaxation of MAX-CUT by backpropagating through an unrolled classical solver. A state-blind schedule generalizes to instances substantially larger than those used for training. A state-adaptive bilinear schedule also generalizes out of distribution, outperforming its untrained baseline across 15 seeds ($t=4.1$). By contrast, an additive ablation with identical inputs and output heads is statistically indistinguishable from the bilinear model in distribution but performs worse than its own baseline out of distribution ($t=-11.7$). This separation appears without regularization and persists across a $30$ round weight-decay sweep: the bilinear advantage remains significant at every tested value, whereas the additive model never recovers. The results in this setting isolate how solver state enters the schedule, rather than state adaptivity alone, as the architectural distinction associated with out-of-distribution transfer.

</details>

<a id="paper-119"></a>

### 119. [SPOOK: Sparse Pixel Optimization for Off-Manifold Knowledge in Vision Encoders](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32835v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32835

**作者：** Ankit Gupta、Akshat Pandya、Christoph Adami、Emily Dolson

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.CL、cs.HC

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32835v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32835v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 SPOOK：在空白画布上做稀疏像素级 hill-climbing 黑盒优化，检验这类视觉上无自然语义的输入能否在 OpenCLIP 与 DINOv3 中定向检索到指定类别或原型，并报告二者共享的 off-manifold 失效模式。

**相关性简评：** 其「黑盒定向优化＋语义盆地命中」探针可能可供核查 LVR 中 latent 状态是否真正承载目标视觉语义、以及离流形表示的可靠性，但与原论文推理任务的对应关系仍需人工确认。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** SPOOK: Sparse Pixel Optimization for Off-Manifold Knowledge in Vision Encoders

**abstract/A1：** Modern vision encoders are widely used as semantic representations, but the relationship between embedding similarity and human-visible semantics can become unclear far from the natural-image manifold. We probe this regime in a black-box setting by applying sparse pixel edits to blank canvases with a simple pixel-level hill-climbing procedure. Across ImageNet and Flickr30k, we evaluate whether these edited blank canvases can retrieve chosen targets in multimodal contrastive encoders and self-supervised visual encoders. On OpenCLIP, inputs optimized toward class prompts achieve strong top-1 and top-5 targeted retrieval performance; on DINOv3, inputs optimized toward class prototypes achieve high target alignment and near-top prototype ranks. These results show that sparse edits on otherwise blank, visually non-semantic inputs can enter target semantic basins in both language-aligned and self-supervised visual representation spaces. Our findings identify a shared off-manifold failure mode of modern vision encoders and suggest sparse blank-canvas optimization as a simple probe of representation geometry.

**comment/C1：** 12 pages, 3 figures, 3 tables, and 14 pages of Supplementary Material with 8 figures

</details>

<a id="paper-120"></a>

### 120. [Interpretable sparse fine-tuning with TopKLoRA enables faithful circuit discovery](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32866v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32866

**作者：** Marek Masiak、Andrzej Szablewski、Nicola Cancedda、Constantin Venhoff

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI、cs.CL

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32866v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32866v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 TopKLoRA：每个 token 只激活 k 个最大的 adapter latents，仅用下游损失训练，并在 gemma-2-2b 与 Qwen2.5 的 sleeper-agent 后门案例中定位、消融必要且充分的小回路，验证稀疏回路可精准移除恶意能力。

**相关性简评：** 逐 token 稀疏 latent 选择加因果消融式回路发现，可能为核查 latent 推理状态是否真正被答案使用、以及贡献分配与可解释瓶颈设计提供工具线索；但该工作限于纯文本微调，迁移到 VLM 与视觉旁路仍需核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Interpretable sparse fine-tuning with TopKLoRA enables faithful circuit discovery

**abstract/A1：** Fine-tuning on datasets of unknown origin can create a backdoor in the trained model when the data includes poisoned examples. Difficult to localise and remove, backdoors can persist through safety training. While parameter-efficient fine-tuning methods capture the full parameter changes introduced during training, they are not easily interpretable. We present the full method of TopKLoRA, extending the preliminary proposal of Masiak et al. (2025), an interpretable-by-design LoRA variant in which only the $k$ largest adapter latents are active at each token. Unlike SAEs, TopKLoRA is trained purely on downstream loss, where optimisation pressure incentivises learning functionally causal latents. We demonstrate this sparse LoRA adapter in a sleeper-agent backdoor case study where we create model organisms by fine-tuning gemma-2-2b, Qwen2.5-1.5B, and Qwen2.5-7B on a poisoned instruction-following dataset. For each organism, we identify and ablate a circuit that is both necessary and sufficient to implement the backdoor. Furthermore, our new fine-tuning method produces sparse circuits that are up to an order of magnitude smaller than a dense LoRA baseline, allowing surgical ablation of the malicious capability without sacrificing instruction-following performance. Finally, we propose a technique to construct model organisms with localised circuits using TopKLoRA latents and gradient-routing, which we use to validate the findings of our circuit search algorithm. We consider these findings as a step toward interpretable-by-design architectures that provide faithful units of analysis without compromising model performance. We share our code: https://github.com/interpretable-finetuning/TopKLoRA.

**comment/C1：** 29 pages, 10 figures

</details>

<a id="paper-121"></a>

### 121. [Do "True" and "False" Add Up to One? Complement Consistency in LLM-Agent Opinion Simulations](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32874v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32874

**作者：** Anouar Imel、Jin Qi

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.CL、cs.MA、cs.SI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32874v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32874v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文检验 LLM-agent 意见模拟中概率报告的「补集一致性」：把主张改写为补集并将 p 换成 1-p 后，两次报告本应相加为 1；作者在 Qwen3-4B、Llama-3.2-3B、Gemma-3-4B 上，用模拟中的 2,304 个 agent 状态、AVeriTeC 的 48 条真实主张和 24 个二结果受控世界测试，发现求和系统性偏离 1，且该偏差在重复采样与不同群体规模下持续存在，反馈到互补初始群体后两侧也不再镜像；用「答案与其配对答案补集的平均」可按构造恢复一致性并降低 Brier 损失，但末轮仅一个模型优于原始表述中较优者。

**相关性简评：** 其「同义改写/补集改写不变性」探测协议与补集平均聚合，可能可供核查 LVR 中的 latent 状态究竟编码任务语义内容还是表面措辞（如 latent 相似度对改写的敏感性）；但该工作纯文本、无视觉证据与推理轨迹，仅为间接方法线索，需核查其诊断设计能否迁移到多模态内部状态评估。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Do "True" and "False" Add Up to One? Complement Consistency in LLM-Agent Opinion Simulations

**abstract/A1：** In LLM-agent opinion simulations, each agent reports the probability that a claim is true after seeing its own previous probability and the probabilities reported by its neighbors, and that number becomes its state in the next round. If the number describes the agent's position rather than the sentence it was shown, then rewriting the claim as its complement and replacing every probability p with 1 - p should return the complementary answer, so the two reports should sum to one. We test this with three small open models: Qwen3-4B, Llama-3.2-3B and Gemma-3-4B. On 2,304 agent states taken from a running network simulation, Qwen's paired reports sum to 1.18 on average, and on 48 real fact-checking claims from AVeriTeC they again exceed one. A controlled benchmark of 24 worlds, each with exactly two possible outcomes, shows the same failure in all three models. The discrepancy is two to ten times larger than the change produced by a same-meaning paraphrase, and it persists under repeated sampling and across peer group sizes and report strengths. When the reports are fed back into two populations that start from complementary states, the populations do not remain mirror images: their divergence is positive after five rounds for every model. Averaging each answer with the complement of its paired answer restores consistency by construction and lowers Brier loss averaged over the two wordings, but at the final round it beats the better of the two original wordings for only one of the three models. A schedule-by-targeting experiment on the same model gives a small, stable interaction because such sweeps keep the claim wording fixed. Consistency under rewording therefore needs its own test before simulated probabilities are read as beliefs

</details>

<a id="paper-122"></a>

### 122. [Multimodal Generative AI Assists Comprehensive Echocardiographic Interpretation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32880v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32880

**作者：** Yi Qin、Marawan Elbatel、Shanshan Song、Taoran Huang、Jiarong Guo、Ningshu Li、Lehan Wang、Chenxu Zhao、Jiexin He、Machteld J. Boonstra、Stephan van der Zwaard、Marielle C. van de Veerdonk、Pere Lopez-Gutierrez、Lydia Dux-Santoy、Andrea Guala、Enrique Almar-Munoz、Sebastian Johannes Reinstadler、Radek Debiec、Yi-Hang Zhu、Huiyu Zhou、Adrian P Cheong、Yike Guo、Agnes Mayr、Karim Lekadir、Yong Jiang、Nilesh J. Samani、Dacheng Tao、Folkert W. Asselbergs、Hongwen Fei、Alex PW Lee、Xiaomeng Li

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32880v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32880v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 EchoChat，一个心脏超声多模态视觉语言模型，用 46,358 次检查的指令数据训练，并以 chain-of-thought 把诊断结论显式锚定到视觉证据上，报告在六个中心多队列上的诊断与量化表现，并做了阅片者研究。

**相关性简评：** 其「显式推理链＋视觉证据归因」的数据构造与训练方式，可供核查 latent 轨迹监督、视觉证据筛选以及显式推理与隐式状态对照的设计线索；但论文本身不研究内部状态或 latent 推理，实际是否依赖图像内容需读原文验证。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Multimodal Generative AI Assists Comprehensive Echocardiographic Interpretation

**abstract/A1：** Echocardiography is the cornerstone of cardiovascular diagnostics, yet its global utility is constrained by a critical shortage of experienced interpreters. Current artificial intelligence (AI) solutions remain limited, typically operating as opaque "black boxes" or focusing on narrow tasks that fail to capture the holistic, guideline-driven reasoning of a clinician. Here we introduce EchoChat, a generative multimodal vision-language model that performs comprehensive echocardiographic analysis to support clinician interpretation. By emulating the cognitive workflow of a cardiologist, EchoChat integrates vast medical knowledge with a chain-of-thought reasoning mechanism to explicitly ground its conclusions in visual evidence. Trained on echocardiography-instruction pairs from 46,358 examinations, our model establishes a new state-of-the-art across diverse cohorts from six international medical centers. It supports the broadest diagnostic scope of 33 cardiac conditions and findings, including 15 newly added conditions for which it achieves an average F1 of 73.7% across available external evaluations. It surpasses existing best-performing foundation models in cardiac condition recognition (improving average recall by 13.54% on shared tasks) and quantification (average R2 score increase of 0.275 on shared tasks). Furthermore, in a source-blinded echocardiographer reader study, echocardiography reports co-generated with EchoChat were rated higher in diagnostic accuracy and completeness by two senior echocardiographers, and improved diagnostic performance from 72.78% to 82.75% in terms of F1 score over routine-reader only reports. These results demonstrate that integrating clinical reasoning into multimodal AI can democratize specialist expertise and transform the standard of cardiovascular care.

</details>

<a id="paper-123"></a>

### 123. [LoCA: Enabling Multi-Modality in Geospatial Location Encoders](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32894v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32894

**作者：** Panagiotis Koromilas、Eleanna Vali、Diogenis Antonopoulos、Eleni Saka、Dimitris Christofis、Nikolaos Ioannis Bountos、Konstantin Klemmer、Ioannis Papoutsis、Yannis Panagakis、Mihalis A. Nicolaou

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32894v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32894v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文证明地理位置编码器受 Lipschitz 约束、内在维度不超过 2，会使对比损失严格高于渐近最优并使嵌入协方差低秩坍缩，据此提出 LoCA 两阶段流程：先联合训练 Sentinel-1/2 图像编码器获得共对齐高秩目标，再冻结图像端训练位置与表格元数据适配器。

**相关性简评：** 其「有效秩／协方差正则化」分析为诊断 latent 推理状态的信息坍缩与表示几何提供了可供核查的度量线索，但属地球观测表示学习，与 VLM 推理距离较远，需判断可否迁移。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** LoCA: Enabling Multi-Modality in Geospatial Location Encoders

**abstract/A1：** Location encoders map geographic coordinates to high-dimensional descriptors through contrastive alignment with paired Earth Observation imagery. We prove that this construction is structurally limited: any Lipschitz location encoder produces an output of intrinsic dimension at most 2, regardless of architecture or bandwidth, which forces the contrastive loss to strictly exceed its asymptotic optimum. Empirically, joint training also collapses the encoder's embedding covariance to low rank. Although dimension-limited, current location encoders sidestep this spectral collapse by training against a frozen image encoder, which we reinterpret as a covariance regularizer that supplies a high-rank target for the location encoder to fold onto. However, extending this approach to multimodal inputs is challenging, as location encoders should simultaneously map a single location to multiple, potentially incompatible, descriptors. We identify two requirements a regularizer must satisfy for location encoding in multimodal regimes: co-alignment across modalities, since adapters alone cannot align independently pretrained encoders; and high effective rank, since the location encoder inherits its target's spectrum. We propose LoCA (Location Contrastive Alignment), a two-stage pipeline that satisfies both. Stage 1 jointly trains Sentinel-1 and Sentinel-2 encoders with contrastive learning, producing a co-aligned, high-rank multimodal target. Stage 2 freezes the image encoders and trains a location and a tabular metadata encoder as projection adapters with multi-view contrastive learning. Against five state-of-the-art encoders on 17 tasks ranging from land cover and wildfire forecasting to geophysical, socio-economic, biodiversity and climate applications, LoCA achieves the most task wins, is the only model to outperform every other on a majority of tasks, and wins 68.8% of all per-task comparisons.

</details>

<a id="paper-124"></a>

### 124. [Recommender regret for improving regularised RL estimation with bandits and MCTS](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32910v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32910

**作者：** Danilo Vucetic、Alexandre Larouche、Mohammad Pedramfar、Audrey Durand、Gauthier Gidel

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32910v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32910v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文把正则化 RL 中由 bandit 采样或 MCTS 生成的策略、价值与 Q 值估计形式化为「推荐」过程，定义 regulariser-aware recommender regret 并分析其下界，进而提出统一 bandit 与搜索式目标改进的 recommender MCTS，在合成树、街机游戏和科学发现任务上验证其收敛更快、下游表现更好。

**相关性简评：** 其关注点不是视觉推理，而是搜索式估计本身的质量度量与目标改进，可能为 LVR 中候选 latent 状态或推理轨迹的筛选、验证与停止决策提供评估线索，需核查 regret 框架能否迁移到 latent 内容质量评价。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Recommender regret for improving regularised RL estimation with bandits and MCTS

**abstract/A1：** Regularised reinforcement learning (RL) algorithms rely on estimators, like Monte Carlo tree search or one-step look-ahead, to provide policy, value, or Q-value estimates in training and inference. However, existing bandit-based estimation frameworks evaluate sampling efficiency, not the quality of estimates. We address this by formalising estimate generation as a recommendation process, treating bandit-based sampling as an improvement operator. To evaluate the quality of a recommendation (estimate), we define regulariser-aware recommender regrets, analysing their lower bounds, and proving that simple algorithms like uniform sampling can achieve these bounds. Finally, we unify bandit- and search-based target improvement under recommender MCTS. Across synthetic trees, arcade games, and scientific discovery tasks, recommenders often yield faster target convergence and superior downstream performance. Our experiments characterise for the first time the potential of regularised MCTS in scientific applications.

**comment/C1：** 10 pages, plus appendices for proofs and additional information

</details>

<a id="paper-125"></a>

### 125. [SemantEq: Faithful Bidirectional Equivalence of Formal Statements](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32919v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32919

**作者：** Xinrui Li、Pawan Sasanka Ammanamanchi、Siddharth Bhat、Rianna Jamdar、Yongxi Lin、Stella Biderman

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32919v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32919v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文针对形式化语句的忠实等价判定，提出比逻辑等价更严格的 SemantEq 关系：要求两个语句只能通过受限的允许步骤链互相转化，并以 Lean 校验的证明项而非策略脚本作为判据，配套模型无关搜索 SemantEq-Eval 与证明审计 SemantEq-ProofAudit，在 ProofNetVerif 上评测。

**相关性简评：** 其「把等价证明的生成与接受分离」以及按允许步骤链约束推导的做法，可能为 latent 推理轨迹的逐步监督与验证提供借鉴，可供核查其思路能否迁移到推理链或状态转移的接受判据设计。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** SemantEq: Faithful Bidirectional Equivalence of Formal Statements

**abstract/A1：** Statement autoformalization translates mathematics from natural language into a proof assistant such as Lean 4, and models now do it at scale. As provers get stronger, the burden of correctness shifts from the proof to the statement, that is, to whether the formal statement faithfully translates its source.
