Given a trusted reference formalization, the natural test is logical equivalence, and it proves too much. $P \\leftrightarrow Q$ holds trivially for any two statements $P$ and $Q$ that the library proves, because a prover can prove each separately and pass through $P \\leftrightarrow \\mathrm{True} \\leftrightarrow Q$. The stronger the prover, the more unrelated pairs pass. Earlier methods restrict the prover and check that each proof names the other statement. We introduce SemantEq, a more principled relation that instead requires each statement to turn into the other only through a chain of allowed steps, such as logical rearrangement and rewriting with the library's own equations. Our programs judge a proof by the proof term Lean checks, not by its tactic script. SemantEq-Eval, a search without a model, reaches 100% precision and 65.6% recall on the ProofNetVerif test split under our adjudicated labels, against 95.8% and 61.8% for BEq+, and an 8.9-fold increase in its CPU time adds at most three pairs. A language model finds more proofs, but under two of four prompts it proves both directions of about two in five of the non-equivalent pairs. SemantEq-ProofAudit audits each model-written proof under a policy derived from the relation and accepts none of the 104 non-equivalent pairs, and it raises recall on 407 equivalent pairs from 65.4% to 81.1% under the best prompt. Separating the generation of equivalence proofs from their acceptance lets a stronger prover raise recall without changing what an acceptance means. We release code and data at: https://github.com/hlxr89/SemantEq

**comment/C1：** 37 pages, 4 figures, 6 tables. Code and data: https://github.com/hlxr89/SemantEq

</details>

<a id="paper-126"></a>

### 126. [Beyond Discrete Tokens: Preserving Numerical Continuity in Medical Vision-Language Models](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32948v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32948

**作者：** Dianye Huang、Jiamei Guo、Yue Zhou、Mingde Li、Yi Xiong、Wei Wang、Nassir Navab、Zhongliang Jiang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32948v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32948v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文提出 HAD-VLM，在共享骨干内把自回归文本生成与扩散式连续数值预测结合，用扩散过程输出包含卡尺端点坐标与长度的五分量表示，并配以坐标-长度一致性后处理与集成预测，用于超声等医学图像的定量测量。

**相关性简评：** 以扩散生成连续向量替代离散数值 token 的做法，可能为 LVR 中连续 latent 状态的生成与解码提供线索，但其目标是测量输出而非推理期状态，视觉证据的使用方式尚需核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Beyond Discrete Tokens: Preserving Numerical Continuity in Medical Vision-Language Models

**abstract/A1：** Objective: To develop a unified vision-language model for prompt-conditioned quantitative ultrasound measurement that addresses the mismatch between continuous measurement targets and discrete token-based numerical prediction. Methods: This article presents HAD-VLM, a hybrid autoregressive-diffusion framework that combines autoregressive text generation with continuous measurement prediction within a shared backbone. Visual prompt tuning adapts the vision encoder to ultrasound images. A diffusion process predicts a five-component representation comprising caliper endpoint coordinates and measurement length. A post-processing strategy combining the proposed coordinate-length consistency mechanism with ensemble prediction is introduced to refine measurement estimates. We evaluate four echocardiographic measurement tasks on EchoNet-LVH, with additional assessments of medical question answering and X-ray vertebral measurement. Results: HAD-VLM achieves mean absolute errors of 1.36, 2.77, 3.05, and 1.16 mm for IVSd, LVIDd, LVIDs, and LVPWd, respectively, reducing errors by 21.4-37.6\\% relative to the Vicuna-based autoregressive baseline. It also outperforms fine-tuned Qwen3-VL and direct MLP regression across all four tasks. Medical question answering performance is preserved on the evaluated dataset. For X-ray vertebral measurement, HAD-VLM reduces mean absolute error from 11.4 to 6.7 pixels. Conclusion: Integrating autoregressive language generation with diffusion-based continuous prediction improves quantitative medical image measurement while retaining prompt-conditioned task flexibility. Significance: HAD-VLM provides a unified approach to combining language interaction with anatomically grounded numerical prediction for quantitative medical image analysis.

</details>

<a id="paper-127"></a>

### 127. [Where Rounding Happens Matters: Rounding-Location Alignment Stabilizes LLM Reinforcement Learning](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32951v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32951

**作者：** Yijing Ke、Tianle Cai、Ruosong Wang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32951v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32951v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文研究 BF16 强化学习训练中推理与训练系统舍入位置不一致导致 on-policy 更新失配的问题，通过在 RoPE、SwiGLU 与残差 RMSNorm 三处保留 FP32 中间值以对齐训练侧舍入位置，并在四个 Qwen 模型上验证其可避免训练崩溃。

**相关性简评：** 方法线索是「只对齐部分执行栈的舍入位置」这一局部精度对齐思路，可供核查 LVR 或 VLM 推理训练的 RL 流程中训练—推理数值错配是否扭曲轨迹监督与贡献分配；但该文不涉及视觉状态或 latent 生成，迁移需自行验证。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Where Rounding Happens Matters: Rounding-Location Alignment Stabilizes LLM Reinforcement Learning

**abstract/A1：** Reinforcement learning for large language models often uses separate systems for inference and training. Even with identical weights and BF16 tensors, implementation differences can make an intended on-policy update off-policy and destabilize training. Existing remedies align the entire inference and training stacks, change global precision, or modify the learning algorithm. We ask whether aligning selected parts of the execution stack can stabilize BF16 RL while other implementation differences remain. We align training-side rounding locations with inference in three regions---RoPE, SwiGLU, and residual RMSNorm---by retaining selected intermediates in FP32 until their BF16 outputs. Across four Qwen-family models, aligning all three regions prevents the collapse observed under the default BF16 baseline; RoPE-path alignment alone also suffices in the studied runs, despite remaining numerical discrepancies. The interventions keep the inference engine, matrix-multiplication and attention kernels, and learning objective unchanged.

</details>

<a id="paper-128"></a>

### 128. [Flownomaly: Single-Step Conditional-Mean Recovery for Few-Shot Anomaly Detection](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32964v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32964

**作者：** Bernardo Perrone Ribeiro、Erkut Akdag、Egor Bondarev

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32964v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32964v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文把少样本工业异常检测改写为「正常对应物恢复」：在冻结的 DINOv2 patch 特征上用 conditional flow matching 学速度场，沿直线路径单步取后验均值替代轨迹积分，并以仅用正常支持样本的 Jacobian 增益-噪声准则选择该步时间，用残差做定位与图像级检测。

**相关性简评：** 单步条件流匹配直接产出后验均值状态、以及无需异常样本即决定「更新多强」的准则，可能为 LVR 中视觉 token 状态的更新幅度与停止/计算分配提供可核查线索，但任务差距较大，迁移价值需阅读原文判断。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Flownomaly: Single-Step Conditional-Mean Recovery for Few-Shot Anomaly Detection

**abstract/A1：** Few-shot industrial anomaly detection requires modeling category-specific normality from only a handful of defect-free images. Common approaches tie inference to stored exemplars, impose rigid linear structure, or risk reproducing anomalous content. Flownomaly instead casts the problem as normal-counterpart recovery in frozen DINOv2 feature space. Given normal supports, a velocity field is learned through conditional flow matching over frozen patch tokens to transport Gaussian noise toward normal features. Along straight paths, a single recovery step using one evaluation of the optimal field yields the posterior mean of the clean normal token without trajectory integration. The operating time governs how strongly recovery modifies a query token. With no anomalous validation samples available for tuning, a normal-only Jacobian gain-to-noise criterion selects this time from the supports alone. Squared residuals between query tokens and their recovered counterparts yield anomaly signals for both dense localization and image-level detection. Across 1-, 2-, and 4-shot benchmarks on MVTec-AD and VisA, together with sample-shot evaluations on Real-IAD, Flownomaly achieves state-of-the-art performance against recent category-specific methods. In the 1-shot setting, image- and pixel-level AUROC reach 97.8% and 98.0% on MVTec-AD and 95.3% and 98.7% on VisA, with a 2.1-point image-level AUROC improvement over the strongest baseline on VisA. Code is available at https://github.com/b-rbmp/Flownomaly.

**comment/C1：** Accepted to WACV 2027

</details>

<a id="paper-129"></a>

### 129. [Physics-Aware Self-Supervised Pretraining on Multi-Domain Raman Spectra](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23955v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.23955

**作者：** Charalampos Davalas、Georgios Levantinos、Anastasios Dimitriou、Nikolaos Papanikolaou、Georgios Th. Papadopoulos、Dimitrios Michail

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.CV、eess.IV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23955v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.23955v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文提出面向多领域拉曼光谱的 MAE 自监督预训练框架，在掩码强度重建之外加入可微 Voigt 峰参数稀疏解码器与去噪目标，并在物质分类、成分识别、浓度估计和去噪任务上比较线性探针、注意力探针、端到端微调与从头训练。

**相关性简评：** 其「注意力探针比线性探针读出更多冻结表示信息、线性评估可能低估表示质量」可供核查 LVR 中 latent 内容是否被答案使用的评价设计；用可微物理参数解码强制潜在状态保留结构化峰信息，也可能与视觉细节保留和瓶颈设计类比。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Physics-Aware Self-Supervised Pretraining on Multi-Domain Raman Spectra

**abstract/A1：** Raman spectroscopy generates large volumes of unlabeled data that are difficult to exploit with conventional supervised learning. This work introduces a multi-domain self-supervised learning framework based on a masked autoencoder (MAE) for learning transferable Raman spectral representations from heterogeneous biological, chemical, forensic, and materials datasets. The proposed pretraining strategy incorporates physics-aware objectives by combining masked intensity reconstruction with a sparse Voigt-profile decoder that models spectral peak structure through differentiable peak centers, widths, and amplitudes. A denoising objective is further included to improve robustness to spectral corruption. The learned encoder is evaluated on substance classification, component identification, concentration estimation, and spectral denoising under linear probing, attention-based probing, end-to-end fine-tuning, and training from scratch. Experimental results show that self-supervised pretraining is most beneficial for challenging tasks with limited labeled data. Attention-based probing also reveals substantially more useful information in the frozen representation than linear probing, indicating that conventional linear evaluation may underestimate representation quality. In addition, the benefits of pretraining become more pronounced with increasing encoder capacity. Overall, the proposed framework demonstrates that physics-aware, multi-domain self-supervised pretraining can improve label efficiency, robustness, and transferability across diverse Raman spectroscopy tasks.

</details>

<a id="paper-130"></a>

### 130. [Residual Calibration via Local Feature-Space Refinement](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30652v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.30652

**作者：** Xihao Wang、Chengyao Yu、Ruixing Ming

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.LG、stat.ML

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30652v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.30652v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出后处理校准框架 LAPE：以全局校准器输出为稳定锚点，用邻域校准样本的非参数局部证据对单条预测做残差精修，并扩展到多视图输入（CFAF）。

**相关性简评：** 其「特征空间局部区域存在空间变化的误校准」及局部证据修正式置信度估计，可能与 LVR 中基于置信度的停止决策、答案筛选和 latent 局部几何分析有关，可供核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Residual Calibration via Local Feature-Space Refinement

**abstract/A1：** Deep neural networks often struggle to provide reliable confidence estimates, making post-hoc calibration essential for reliable decision-making. Existing post-hoc methods largely rely on score-based transformations, which improve marginal calibration but often fail to capture spatially varying miscalibration in local regions of the learned feature space. To address this gap, we propose Local Adaptive Probability Estimation (LAPE), a post-hoc residual calibration framework. Specifically, LAPE treats the global calibrator output as a stable anchor and refines the individual prediction by leveraging non-parametric local evidence from neighboring calibration samples. As a plug-and-play refinement module, LAPE effectively utilizes the information about feature-space geometry and can reuse the calibration data that are used to construct the global calibrator. Furthermore, we extend LAPE to the setting with multi-view inputs through Cascade Feature Augmentation Fusion (CFAF). Extensive experiments demonstrate that the proposed methods can improve the calibration quality of various existing baselines and remain efficient and robust across different settings.

**comment/C1：** Accepted at NeurIPS 2026

</details>

<a id="paper-131"></a>

### 131. [Collaborative In-Context Learning for Indoor Radio Map Estimation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31702v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.31702

**作者：** Yuanzhe Peng、Jie Xu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31702v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.31702v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文将室内无线电地图估计表述为协作式 in-context learning 问题，提出 Poly-Ripple：多个客户端各用私有示例提示大型自回归视觉模型、不更新参数地预测同一场景，并采用「波纹序」自回归建模与重要性感知聚合。

**相关性简评：** 其按传播因果结构安排自回归生成顺序、以及在上下文长度限制下加权聚合异质上下文，可能为 LVR 中视觉证据的生成次序与多假设融合提供类比线索，但领域相隔较远，需核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Collaborative In-Context Learning for Indoor Radio Map Estimation

**abstract/A1：** Indoor radio map estimation (RME) is essential for wireless applications such as coverage planning, localization, and network deployment. However, most existing methods follow a supervised learning paradigm that requires costly labeled data and task-specific model training. Moreover, indoor environments involve privacy-sensitive information, such as building layouts and structures, that often cannot be centralized. To address these challenges, we make the first attempt to formulate indoor RME as a collaborative in-context learning (ICL) problem. We propose Poly-Ripple, a framework in which multiple clients perform ICL using large autoregressive vision models (LAVMs) without model updates. Each client is prompted by its private demonstrations and predicts the radio map for the same scene query. To capture the causal structure of wireless propagation outward from the transmitter (Tx), we adopt ripple-ordered autoregressive modeling. We further introduce importance-aware aggregation to exploit heterogeneous contextual knowledge distributed across clients under the context length constraint. We provide theoretical analysis and conduct extensive experiments on a public indoor RME benchmark. The experimental results show that Poly-Ripple consistently outperforms representative ICL baselines, while aligning with our theoretical analysis.

**comment/C1：** Accepted as a regular paper at the 2026 IEEE International Conference on Big Data (IEEE BigData 2026)

</details>

<a id="paper-132"></a>

### 132. [Does LeWorldModel Preserve Local Action Geometry?](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31716v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.31716

**作者：** Vasil Vasilev、Siddharth Nayak

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.RO

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31716v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.31716v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文检验预训练 LeWorldModel 是否保留局部动作几何：在 Reacher 与 OGBench Cube 上，用三种扰动尺度比较模拟器与模型的 Gram 矩阵、动作敏感方向及嵌入响应误差。

**相关性简评：** 其「多尺度扰动＋表示几何」诊断可能为核查 LVR/VLM 内部状态是否保留任务必要的局部结构提供思路，但研究对象是世界模型的动作敏感度，与推理期 latent 生成和使用的联系较间接。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Does LeWorldModel Preserve Local Action Geometry?

**abstract/A1：** We examine how closely pretrained LeWorldModel (LeWM) reproduces the effects of small action changes. Using matched simulator and model responses, we measure action sensitivity with Gram matrices at three perturbation scales. Across nearly a thousand states from Reacher and OGBench Cube, the most sensitive action directions differ by median angles of about $36^\\circ$ and $42^\\circ$, respectively, at the smallest scale. Increasing the perturbation tenfold reduces median normalized Gram discrepancy by roughly 70\\% on Reacher and 47\\% on Cube. Geometry measured in pixels and embeddings varies more across scales than the chosen physical-state geometry. On held-out directions, environment Grams achieve median embedding response errors of 17\\% and 28\\%, compared with 26\\% and 38\\% for an isotropic reference with the same trace. LeWM's errors are higher at this scale. These findings support scale-specific evaluation of action sensitivity; its effect on control remains an open question.

**comment/C1：** 13 pages, 3 figures. Accepted as a poster at the NeurIPS 2026 Workshop on Symmetry and Geometry in Neural Representations (NeurReps), Extended Abstract Track

</details>

<a id="paper-133"></a>

### 133. [Cognitively Constrained Partners for Zero-Shot Coordination in Real-Time Partitioned Control](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31726v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.31726

**作者：** Ash Simmons、George De Ath、Richard M. Everson

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.HC

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31726v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.31726v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出用源自生理文献的程序化约束（反应延迟、感知限制）生成「认知受限伙伴」，在改造版 Lunar Lander 中训练协作智能体，并初步检验这些伙伴是否更像真人、以及所训智能体在 cross-play 中是否不依赖伙伴生成方式，另设计人类被试研究对照评估排名。

**相关性简评：** 与 LVR/VLM 推理主线无直接交集，但其以感知与反应约束而非奖励塑形诱导行为多样性、并将 cross-play 排名与真人判断对照的思路，可能可供核查受限推理者的建模与 latent 代理评价指标的有效性。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Cognitively Constrained Partners for Zero-Shot Coordination in Real-Time Partitioned Control

**abstract/A1：** Effective human-machine teaming requires agents that coordinate with unpredictable human partners. Reinforcement learning from live human feedback or large behavioural datasets is expensive, while purely simulated training overfits to artificial conventions and fails at Zero-Shot Coordination with real people. We propose a framework for training teaming agents against Cognitively Constrained Partners: simulated partners whose diversity emerges from procedural constraints grounded in human physiological literature, specifically reaction delays and perceptual limitations, rather than from reward shaping or historical training checkpoints. In a modified Lunar Lander environment, we report preliminary evidence that these partners play more like real people than unconstrained agents do, and that agents trained against them match the strongest cross-play baselines while being the only ones whose performance does not depend on how their training partners were generated. We also describe a human-participant study designed so that its rankings can be compared directly against cross-play, testing how well that standard evaluation predicts real human-agent teaming.

**comment/C1：** 30 pages, 6 figures, 5 tables. Accepted at the NeurIPS 2026 Workshop on Human-AI Coevolution: Measuring Human-Agent Teams in the Agentic Era

</details>

<a id="paper-134"></a>

### 134. [PnP\*: A certifiable solver for Perspective-n-Point](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31797v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.31797

**作者：** Bang-Shien Chen、Caitrin E. Eaton、David M. Rosen

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.RO、math.OC

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31797v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.31797v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 PnP\*，首个可扩展到实际规模且返回可认证全局最优解的 PnP 求解器：证明其凸松弛在中等噪声下精确，并借助弦稀疏结构与商环约简使松弛可解，实测能以 10 Hz 以上给出可证明正确的相机位姿。

**相关性简评：** 其「可认证最优性 + 噪声下稳定性保证」可能为检验 VLM 几何推理的 latent 状态是否与视觉证据自洽提供评价思路，结构化稀疏约简也可供核查状态表示的压缩方式。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** PnP\*: A certifiable solver for Perspective-n-Point

**abstract/A1：** Estimating a camera pose from 3D-2D correspondences - Perspective-n-Point - is one of the most fundamental problems in computer vision. Existing methods either solve a minimal problem or replace the objective with an easier one. Surprisingly, no scalable certifiable solver for the actual Maximum Likelihood Estimation (MLE) exists. We propose PnP\*, the first practical solver that guarantees efficient computation and returns a certifiable globally optimal solution. We prove that the underlying convex relaxation is exact under moderate noise, which to our knowledge is the first stability guarantee for any relaxation for the MLE of PnP. Furthermore, we reduce the relaxation by exploiting chordal sparsity pattern and quotient ring reduction such that the relaxation is tractable and scalable. The stability and scalability results are verified numerically, as PnP\* returns a provably correct camera pose at over 10 Hz.

**comment/C1：** 28 pages, 3 figures

</details>

<a id="paper-135"></a>

### 135. [StackGen3D: Stable 3D Block Stack Generation from Multiview Silhouettes](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31803v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.31803

**作者：** Xiaopeng Zhang、Luzhe Sun、Yiqing Xu、Matthew R. Walter

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.RO

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31803v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.31803v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 StackGen3D，在给定积木清单与三张正交剪影的条件下生成物理稳定的积木结构：先用物理启发的 GNN 作为快速稳定性预测器筛选数据集，再训练条件 flow matching 学习多峰分布，推理时用轻量几何选择与精修减少穿模。

**相关性简评：** 其用学习到的快速稳定性预测器做数据筛选与 Best-of-N 候选选择（稳定性 45.3%→72.4%），可能可供核查 LVR 中 latent 假设的评分、验证与停止／筛选机制，但任务是 3D 装配生成，与 VLM 推理无直接关联，联系较弱。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** StackGen3D: Stable 3D Block Stack Generation from Multiview Silhouettes

**abstract/A1：** Generating physically stable 3D structures from visual specifications requires resolving geometric ambiguity while accounting for physical interactions among objects. We present StackGen3D, a framework for generating stable block assemblies conditioned on a given block inventory and three orthographic silhouettes. Our approach consists of two stages. First, we develop a physics-informed graph neural network (PI-GNN) that models support relations between blocks and serves as a fast stability predictor for curating a diverse dataset of stable structures. Second, we train a conditional flow-matching model on this dataset to capture the multimodal distribution of block configurations consistent with the target silhouettes. At inference time, lightweight geometric selection and refinement reduce inter-block penetration without physics simulation. Preliminary experiments show that the PI-GNN generalizes from simple stacks to complex structures substantially better than a parameter-matched multilayer perceptron. For structure generation, Best-of-10 selection improves physical stability from 45.3% to 72.4% while achieving a silhouette IoU of 0.970. These results highlight the potential of combining relational physical reasoning, scalable data curation, and conditional generative modeling for creative 3D assembly. Project website: \[https://ripl.github.io/StackGen3D-web/\](https://ripl.github.io/StackGen3D-web/)

**comment/C1：** A poster of this work is publicly available on the website of the Creativity in Assembly Workshop at IROS 2026 (non-archival)

</details>

<a id="paper-136"></a>

### 136. [Speculative Diffusion is a Tale of Two Tradeoffs](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31819v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.31819

**作者：** Aaron Zorel、Guy Van den Broeck、Aditya Grover、Daniel Israel

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31819v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.31819v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文研究 speculative diffusion 中「扩散草稿模型的去噪投入」与「投机解码接受宽松度」两个参数如何共同影响速度-质量权衡，通过联合调节二者刻画可达到的 Pareto 前沿，并与单步扩散草稿对比。

**相关性简评：** 其联合扫描「计算投入—验收宽松度」并绘制速度-质量前沿的做法，可能为 LVR 中 THINK/LOOK 额外计算与停止准则的成本-收益评估提供可核查的分析视角，但该文属纯文本生成加速，不涉及视觉证据或内部推理状态。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Speculative Diffusion is a Tale of Two Tradeoffs

**abstract/A1：** Speculative decoding is a technique that accelerates the inference speed of autoregressive large language models (LLMs) by sampling from a faster, lower quality draft model. Traditionally, a fundamental characteristic of speculative decoding is that it is lossless with respect to the verifier distribution, guaranteeing speed without sacrificing quality. This differs from recently developed diffusion LLMs (dLLMs), which are notably able to achieve substantial speedups by degrading the quality of their outputs with less diffusion effort. Because diffusion models are able to sample faster than autoregressive models by generating tokens in parallel, they are considered strong candidates for draft models in speculative diffusion. While it is widely understood that diffusion models can sacrifice quality for speed, speculative decoding itself can also be generalized to generate lossy samples faster using more lenient acceptance criteria with respect to the verifier distribution. Presented with the two parameters of diffusion effort and speculative decoding leniency, this paper explores the full achievable Pareto frontier via joint variations of these parameters. Our experiments show that jointly varying diffusion effort and acceptance leniency can extend the speed-quality Pareto frontier over single step diffusion drafting.

</details>

<a id="paper-137"></a>

### 137. [Rewriting Scaling Laws for Synthetic Data with Compute and Data Constraints](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31867v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.31867

**作者：** Nicholas Roberts、Ahmed Ahmed、Suhas Kotha、Percy Liang、Yu Wang、Alex Renda、Nitya Mani

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.CL

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31867v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.31867v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文提出合成改写数据的 scaling law，用模型规模、训练 token 数、改写 token 占比和自然数据 epoch 数预测 loss，并将生成改写的算力计入成本；在 compute-limited 与 data-limited 两种设定下拟合，发现最优改写比例随算力预算上升，并外推验证大模型预测。

**相关性简评：** 其把「生成数据的计算开销」显式纳入 scaling law、并据此分配生成与训练预算的思路，可能可供核查 LVR 中轨迹/改写式推理数据的生成成本与收益权衡；但论文纯属文本预训练数据，不涉及视觉状态或 latent 推理，联系较远。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Rewriting Scaling Laws for Synthetic Data with Compute and Data Constraints

**abstract/A1：** Synthetic rewrites, where a language model rewrites a natural document e.g. into a target style, are now a standard ingredient of pretraining corpora. However, critical aspects of their use remain poorly understood, such as how much compute should be spent rewriting training data and whether a model trained on rewrites can outperform the rewriter. We study these two questions by proposing a rewriting scaling law that predicts loss from model size, training tokens, the fraction of training tokens that are rewrites, and the number of epochs over the natural data, charging the compute spent generating rewrites. We fit this scaling law in two settings. In the compute-limited setting, natural data is plentiful and rewrites come from a third-party rewriter trained with more compute than our largest run. We find that the estimated optimal rewrite fraction increases with compute, from under $10\\%$ at $3\\times10^{18}$ FLOPs to $32\\%$ at $10^{22}$ FLOPs. Our scaling forecast extrapolates $33\\times$ in FLOPs to train a 22B-parameter model, whose final loss is within $1.1\\%$ of our prediction. This model achieves a better BPB than its rewriter on several web domains at one-tenth the rewriter's training compute. In the data-limited setting, we assume we only have a fixed seed set of natural data and that our rewriter is a model of the same size as the target, pretrained on that seed set. Again, we find the optimal rewrite fraction increases with the compute budget. We also demonstrate that a model trained on rewrites of its own pretraining data, generated by a rewriter of the same size, beats the best run on natural data alone at every model size up to 3B. Our results show that synthetic rewriting can be studied through the scaling law lens: that optimal methodological choices are predictable and scaling does not necessarily break down beyond the rewriter's capabilities.

</details>

<a id="paper-138"></a>

### 138. [FSVL: Taxonomic Fast-Slow Vision-to-Language Collaborative Framework for Interpretable Confusable Fish Species Identification and Knowledge Generation in the Middle Yangtze River](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31880v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.31880

**作者：** Meng Liang、Fucheng Wang、Lang Zhao、Jun Chen、Ziying Li、Yuxiang Zhao、Haozhuang Chi、Wenkai Fang、Qiong Zhou、Junlin Yang、Jinming Wu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31880v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.31880v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 FSVL 快慢双通道框架：用轻量 ResNet50 对长江中游 51 种鱼类做初步分类，将易混物种组路由到由 YOLOv8n 检测头部、背鳍、尾部等形态区域的慢通道精判，并结合检索增强的 Qwen3-14B 与 LoRA 生成物种知识。

**相关性简评：** 其按易混程度决定是否触发慢通道与关键区域检测的做法，或可启发 LVR 中「何时额外 LOOK、投入多少视觉证据计算」的路由与停止设计，但无 latent 状态与内部表示分析，借鉴性需核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** FSVL: Taxonomic Fast-Slow Vision-to-Language Collaborative Framework for Interpretable Confusable Fish Species Identification and Knowledge Generation in the Middle Yangtze River

**abstract/A1：** The Yangtze River Basin is a critical region for freshwater biodiversity conservation. The implementation of the Yangtze River Ten-Year Fishing Ban has increased the importance of effective aquatic biodiversity monitoring and species-level identification. However, fine-grained identification of closely related freshwater fish species remains challenging due to subtle morphological differences. Existing deep learning methods often struggle to balance classification accuracy and inference efficiency, while LLMs may generate unsupported information when producing specialized biological knowledge. To address these challenges, this study proposes FSVL, a fast-slow dual-pathway framework integrating visual recognition with LLM-based Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG). A lightweight ResNet50 fast path performs preliminary classification across 51 fish species in the Middle Yangtze River Basin and routes predefined groups of easily confused species to a slow path for refined identification. Guided by taxonomic criteria, the slow path uses YOLOv8n to detect key morphological regions, including the head, dorsal fin, and tail, leveraging morphometric and outline traits to improve discrimination among closely related species. The framework further integrates a retrieval-enhanced Qwen3-14B backend, using soft tokens and Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) for parameter-efficient domain knowledge generation. On the evaluated test set of 607 images, the fast classifier achieved 99.67% accuracy, whereas the cascade corrected the two errors, yielding 100.00% overall accuracy; further validation on independent specimens and external conditions remains needed. Across 255 knowledge generation samples, scientific name accuracy rises from 91.37% to 100.00%, genus accuracy from 90.20% to 98.82%, and ROUGE-L from 0.335 to 0.655, supporting intelligent biodiversity monitoring and fisheries management.

</details>

<a id="paper-139"></a>

### 139. [Permission to Retry: A Study of Authorization and Recovery in AI Agent Workflows](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31916v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.31916

**作者：** Deekshith Marla、Kushagra Bhatnagar

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.SE

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31916v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.31916v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文研究工具型 AI agent 的执行期可靠性，把检查点状态、派发许可、外部效果证据与对账许可拆为不同义务，并在 LangGraph 与 Temporal 上以 336 条历史做有限一致性检验，比较多种重试策略的通过情况。

**相关性简评：** 其「状态留存—效果证据—是否重试/对账」的义务划分，可能为 LVR 中重新观察视觉证据、停止与重试决策提供概念框架，可供核查；但论文本身不涉及视觉推理或 latent 状态。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Permission to Retry: A Study of Authorization and Recovery in AI Agent Workflows

**abstract/A1：** A durable agent can resume a consequential action while its authority changes and the action's external outcome remains uncertain. We study execution-time reliability of tool-using AI agents, isolating recovery decisions from language-model behavior. Checkpoint state, permission to dispatch, evidence of an external effect and permission to reconcile are distinct obligations. We present a finite conformance study that checks these obligations through histories scored against a provider effect ledger hidden from the recovering application. A prospectively frozen campaign executes 336 histories on native LangGraph and Temporal hosts, crossing 14 schedules, three provider contracts and four matched application policies. All 168 paired host cells have identical checked outcomes. Per host, retained-ledger reconciliation passes all 42 histories: 21 checked completions, 15 denials and six unknown outcomes with reservations retained. Cached retry, revalidation before retry and stable-key retry pass 30, 36 and 31 histories respectively. A separate 16-case component study of InfinityOS, an enterprise AI platform developed by Arya.ai, exercises exported authority, worker, recovery and settlement code over repository fakes. The results isolate the roles of current authority, provider contracts and retained observations in recovery, with safety, completion and unresolved obligations reported separately for the declared application profile.

**comment/C1：** 7 pages, 2 figures, 3 tables

</details>

<a id="paper-140"></a>

### 140. [Beyond Local Accuracy: Benchmarking the Rollout Reliability of PDE Foundation Models](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31935v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.31935

**作者：** Jaewan Park、Seid Koric、Giri Krishnan、Sudhakar Pamidighantam、Surresh Marru

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31935v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.31935v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出误差放大比 G（全时程误差与短时程误差之比），在七个二维时变 PDE 数据集上评测七种 PDE 基础模型（含从头训练与微调、冻结 in-context 模型），并用 VRMSE、相对 L2、相对 MAE 检验短时程优势能否延续到长时程自回归 rollout。

**相关性简评：** 「自身预测反复作为输入导致误差放大」的度量与分时程报告方式，可能类比 LVR 中 latent 状态多步更新/循环推理的长度泛化与可靠性评价，可供核查；但该文属科学计算 PDE 领域，与视觉多模态推理无直接联系，迁移价值需较多判断。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Beyond Local Accuracy: Benchmarking the Rollout Reliability of PDE Foundation Models

**abstract/A1：** PDE foundation models enable data-efficient adaptation but are deployed autoregressively, so short-horizon accuracy need not imply reliable simulation. We ask whether the short-horizon advantage of a foundation-model recipe persists when its predictions are repeatedly fed back as inputs for a long horizon. We define error amplification as G=E(H\_f)/E(H\_s), which measures full-horizon error relative to short-horizon error. We evaluate seven models on seven two-dimensional time-dependent PDE datasets: scratch-trained FNO and DeepONet; fine-tuned DPOT, GPhyT, Poseidon, and Walrus; and frozen in-context VICON. We report VRMSE, relative L\_2, and relative MAE. Six datasets provide paired short- and full-horizon scores, while CE-RM contributes short-rollout sweeps only. At least one fine-tuned foundation model outperforms both scratch baselines in 26 of 30 short-rollout dataset--budget cells under every metric. Nevertheless, full-horizon VRMSE exceeds short-horizon VRMSE in 140 of 144 paired trained configurations, with a median error amplification of $2.04$. The count below VRMSE one falls from 104 to 53, and 12 full rollouts exceed ten although none does at the short horizon. Walrus achieves the lowest full-horizon VRMSE in 16 of 24 cells, yet its median error amplification is $2.70$. Relative L\_2 and relative MAE show the same qualitative pattern. These results show that favorable short-horizon rankings can coexist with substantial error amplification. PDE foundation-model benchmarks should therefore report horizon-resolved absolute errors, amplification statistics, and failure incidence rather than a single local forecast score.

</details>

<a id="paper-141"></a>

### 141. [$M^3$: A Multi-LLM System for Multi-Turn Jailbreaking as a Markov Decision Process](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31937v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.31937

**作者：** Yuqing Huang、Jack Ji、Jithin Pradeep、Sagarika Suresh、Jekaterina Novikova

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31937v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.31937v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 将多轮越狱形式化为有限时域 MDP，由状态 LLM 把交互历史压缩成紧凑内部状态，攻击 LLM 据此与目标模型交互，并在两个基准、六个目标模型上以 ASR 等指标评测。

**相关性简评：** 其「显式紧凑状态 + 有限时域决策」可能与 LVR 中推理状态的压缩维护、轮次/长度扩展与停止决策建模有概念联系，可供核查；但工作本身不涉及视觉或多模态推理。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** $M^3$: A Multi-LLM System for Multi-Turn Jailbreaking as a Markov Decision Process

**abstract/A1：** Modern Large Language Models (LLMs) remain vulnerable to carefully orchestrated multi-turn interactions that progressively bypass safety mechanisms. However, many existing red-teaming approaches rely on heuristics or impose strong assumptions that limit their applicability in fully black box settings. We address this gap by introducing $M^3$, an automated red-teaming framework that formalizes multi-turn jailbreaking as a finite-horizon Markov Decision Process (MDP). In $M^3$, an attacker LLM interacts with a target LLM while operating over a compact internal state produced by a state LLM. Across two benchmarks and six target models, $M^3$ consistently outperforms state-of-the-art multi-turn red-teaming methods in Attack Success Rates (ASR), achieving improvements of up to 53% in ASR@5. Even with lightweight attacker, $M^3$ attains gains up to 26% in ASR@1 and 34% in ASR@5, compared to baselines that use higher capacity attackers. Beyond improved effectiveness, $M^3$ exhibits steeper risk escalation over conversational turns and achieves more consistent performance across risk categories (up to 4.82 improvement in Gini index) without relying on predefined risk taxonomy, providing a scalable and principled framework for multi-turn red-teaming and safety evaluation.

</details>

<a id="paper-142"></a>

### 142. [Sharp Bounds on the Winner's Bias under Independent and Nonnegatively Correlated Noise](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31948v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.31948

**作者：** Ivan Viakhirev、Kirill Borodin、Grach Mkrtchian

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、math.ST

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31948v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.31948v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文确定「胜者偏差」（M 个含噪无偏分数取最大导致的高估）在独立非同分布与非负相关噪声两类情形下的尖锐常数，用 Lean 4 形式化验证，并在 448 个语言模型与分类器选择流程上测量实际偏差。

**相关性简评：** 与 best-of-N、验证器筛选候选等推理侧评价相关：从多条候选轨迹或答案中取最优会系统性高估收益，且噪声相关结构（共享题项 vs 各自题项）可能影响候选评分设计与增益报告，可供核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Sharp Bounds on the Winner's Bias under Independent and Nonnegatively Correlated Noise

**abstract/A1：** Reporting the best of $M$ noisy, unbiased scores overstates the winner's value by up to $c\\sqrt{V}$, where $V$ is the total noise variance and the sharp constant $c$ depends on how the noise is dependent. It was known only at the two extremes, arbitrary dependence ($\\sqrt{(M-1)/M}$) and i.i.d. noise. We determine it for two classes in between: for independent, not identically distributed noise it is $\\sqrt{(M-1)/(2M)}$, and for pairwise nonnegatively correlated noise it is $(M-1)/M$; for uncorrelated noise this answers the maximum version of an open question about the expected range. Explicit finite constructions attain both. As a corollary, the bias of the cross-validation estimator is bounded sharply within each class. All theorems are formally verified in Lean 4. Across 448 language-model and classifier selection pipelines, designs with per-candidate items realize a median 0.75 of the independent-class ceiling. At a fixed item budget, scoring all candidates on shared items is markedly less optimistic than giving each candidate its own items.

</details>

<a id="paper-143"></a>

### 143. [Knowing Whom to Trust is not Enough: The Trust--Agreement Gap in Repeated Multi-Party LLM Negotiation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31971v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.31971

**作者：** Amit Sarker、Moumita Choudhury、Saaduddin Mahmud、Shlomo Zilberstein

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.CL、cs.MA

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31971v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.31971v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文构建重复、多方、多议题的 LLM 谈判框架，让 agent 每局后评估对手并维护可跨局携带的两两信任估计，比较信任感知与不感知 agent，发现即使能在数局内识别出暗中的破坏者，准确的信任估计也未必带来更好协议，除非显式引导其使用。

**相关性简评：** 信任估计可视为跨轮持续更新的内部信念状态，其「估得准却不被采纳」的 trust–agreement gap 可能与 latent 状态存在却未被生成答案使用（信息坍缩、贡献分配）的问题同构，可供核查；但无视觉输入与 latent 生成机制，联系仅为概念层面。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Knowing Whom to Trust is not Enough: The Trust--Agreement Gap in Repeated Multi-Party LLM Negotiation

**abstract/A1：** Large language model (LLM) agents increasingly participate in negotiations on behalf of individuals and organizations, yet they remain vulnerable to manipulation, particularly in repeated multi-party interactions. Classical multi-agent systems address this challenge through explicit trust and reputation models, whereas existing work on LLM negotiation largely treats trust as an emergent phenomenon. We investigate whether LLM agents can form useful trust estimates from prior interactions and leverage them to improve future negotiation outcomes. We introduce a repeated, multi-party, multi-issue negotiation framework in which recurring agents participate in a sequence of negotiation games while one participant covertly attempts to prevent agreement. After each game, agents assess their counterparts and maintain pairwise trust estimates that carry over to subsequent negotiations. We compare trust-aware and trust-agnostic agents and analyze how explicit trust influences negotiation outcomes. Our results reveal a \\emph{trust--agreement gap}: trust-aware agents can identify the adversary within a few games, but accurate trust estimates do not consistently translate into better agreements. Trust increases agreement rates by up to 23 percentage points, but only when agents are explicitly guided to use it in their decisions. Explicit trust can therefore improve repeated LLM negotiations, but its value depends critically on how trust is assessed and incorporated into agents' reasoning.

</details>

<a id="paper-144"></a>

### 144. [Reproducible Evaluation as the Substrate for Safe Prompt Compression in Production Agents](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31972v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.31972

**作者：** Prabal Vashisht、Zheng Hu、Ying Yang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31972v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.31972v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出面向生产对话代理的离线评测装置：在后端数据持续变动时保持可复现，并可在基准失效后按需重建；再以此作为 GEPA prompt 优化的适应度函数，配合正确性门控抑制 reward hacking，使 prompt 体积压缩 3.5–7.6% 且不损留出集正确性。

**相关性简评：** 其「正确性门控下的压缩式优化」与可重建评测思路，可能为 latent 推理训练中避免奖励劫持和信息坍缩提供评测与监督线索，但属纯文本生产代理场景，迁移性需核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Reproducible Evaluation as the Substrate for Safe Prompt Compression in Production Agents

**abstract/A1：** Evaluating conversational agents that act on live, mutable enterprise data is hard: replayed sessions drift as backend state changes, and a benchmark built today can silently go stale as the data it describes moves on. We present an offline evaluation harness for a production conversational agent that stays reproducible despite the live data it operates on changing, and that can be regenerated on demand once it goes stale. We then use this harness as the fitness function for GEPA-based prompt optimization under a correctness gate that guards against reward hacking. Applied to a production merchant-facing agent, correctness-gated compression shrinks the agent's full prompt surface by 3.5-7.6% without regressing held-out task correctness, consistently across three independent subject-model families, and for one, correctness measurably improves.

**comment/C1：** 8 pages (4 pages main text plus references and appendices), 1 table. Accepted at the NeurIPS 2026 Workshop on Agents in the Wild

</details>

<a id="paper-145"></a>

### 145. [RSI-Opt: Recursive Self-Improvement Optimizer Agents For Model Training](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32026v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32026

**作者：** Binghang Lu、Yige Sun、Guang Lin

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32026v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32026v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** RSI-Opt 让元智能体依据以往训练运行的证据改写优化器智能体的提示、流程手册、代码库与决策节奏，并通过策略归档实现递归自我改进，在多个训练任务上验证了性能提升与跨模型迁移。

**相关性简评：** 其「决策节奏」与基于历史运行证据的策略演化、归档机制，可能为 LVR 中何时继续思考或停止（STOP）、以及跨轨迹的经验积累与贡献分配提供可供核查的线索，但领域距离较远。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** RSI-Opt: Recursive Self-Improvement Optimizer Agents For Model Training

**abstract/A1：** Conventional neural-network training typically follows a predefined optimization recipe, motivating a LLM agent that monitors training dynamics and adjusts optimizer settings as training progresses, but a fixed agent policy does not accumulate experience across runs. We introduce RSI-Opt, a plug-and-play Recursive Self-Improvement Optimizer Agents which evolves the knowledge that guides this process: a prompt, a playbook of procedures, a library of tested optimizer code, and a decision cadence. A meta-agent rewrites this policy using evidence from earlier runs, and an archive retains evaluated policies for further evolution. Across 5 generations on three training tasks, the meta-agents develop reusable procedures and code and conduct their own CPU proxy experiments. RSI-Opt substantially improves optimizer-agent performance, with representative raw-space summary $z$-scores increasing from $+10.48$ to $+14.33$ on the evolution suite with Opus 5.5 and from $-0.36$ to $+3.11$ on held-out tasks with GPT-5.5 through cross-model policy transfer. On the Burgers PDE, it reduces mean relative $L\_2$ error by approximately 2.7-fold with Opus 5.5 and 8.8-fold with GPT-6.1 over the fixed-prompt agent baseline, although PDE transfer varies across LLMs and tasks. Controlled comparisons show that the evolved policy combines reusable optimization knowledge with adaptation to each task: initial decisions capture most observed gains on seen tasks, while continued adaptation improves outcomes on unseen tasks. These findings demonstrate how procedures and executable tools accumulated across training runs can improve optimizer-agent performance and support transfer to scientific machine learning.

</details>

<a id="paper-146"></a>

### 146. [Rethinking Representation Geometry in Supervised Contrastive Learning under Long-Tailed Data](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32039v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32039

**作者：** Joonhyeok Jeong、Jy-yong Sohn、Kibok Lee

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI、cs.CV、stat.ML

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32039v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32039v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出理论框架，分析长尾监督对比学习目标在最优点能否形成正则单纯形几何，指出常用目标因依赖每类样本数而无法形成该结构，并给出可证明形成正则单纯形的全平衡对比目标，在长尾识别与迁移任务上验证表示空间更均衡。

**相关性简评：** 「正则单纯形」几何判据与结构平衡构造，可能为判断 latent 推理状态是否塌缩、过度集中或类间不均衡提供可核查的度量视角，但该文属图像分类表示学习，与视觉推理无直接关联，迁移价值需自行判断。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Rethinking Representation Geometry in Supervised Contrastive Learning under Long-Tailed Data

**abstract/A1：** Supervised contrastive learning and its balanced variants have demonstrated their effectiveness for representation learning under long-tailed distributions. However, although prior analyses suggest that their optimal representations may exhibit a regular simplex geometry, whether such a geometry is indeed exhibited remains insufficiently understood. In this work, we present a theoretical framework for these objectives that characterizes whether a regular simplex is formed at the optimum. By applying this to representative objectives, we show that these objectives fail to form a regular simplex at the optimum because they remain dependent on the number of samples per class. Motivated by this analysis, we introduce structural balancing that can be applied to contrastive objectives, and propose a fully balanced contrastive objective that provably forms a regular simplex geometry at its optimum. Both theoretical and empirical results demonstrate that the proposed method yields a more balanced representation space and improves performance on long-tailed recognition and transfer learning tasks.

</details>

<a id="paper-147"></a>

### 147. [Understanding Communication Failures in Multi-Agent LLM Systems](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32060v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32060

**作者：** Priya Pitre、Jun Hou、Yi Fang、Naren Ramakrishnan、Xuan Wang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32060v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32060v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文基于医疗、代码、金融、多跳等六类任务共 3211 条失败的多轮轨迹，构建多智能体 LLM 通信失败分类法，并分析轮级错误动态、恢复难度、多数转移与少数意见抑制等现象，据此提出改进建议。

**相关性简评：** 「过早收敛」「少数信号被压制」「首轮之后才出现的恢复困难」等失败维度，可能可供核查 LVR 多轮或多假设推理中视觉证据被丢弃、状态过早坍缩与停止决策的评价视角。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Understanding Communication Failures in Multi-Agent LLM Systems

**abstract/A1：** Multi-agent LLM systems aim to improve reasoning through debate, evidence sharing, and mutual correction, but interaction can also introduce failures that final accuracy alone cannot explain. Agents may converge too early, ignore dissent, suppress correct minority answers, or preserve a wrong framing across rounds. We present a corpus-grounded taxonomy of communication failures, built from 3,211 failed multi-round trajectories across medical QA, coding, finance, multi-hop reasoning, ethics, and embodied text tasks. Using this taxonomy, we analyze failure distributions across datasets and system designs, round-level error dynamics, recovery hardness, majority transitions, minority-signal suppression, and self-reported confidence. We find that failures are dominated by recovery/correction and consensus/decision problems rather than isolated first-round mistakes, with many emerging after agents first observe peer responses. Based on these findings, we recommend stronger disagreement handling, evidence verification, minority-signal preservation, and recovery checks before finalization.

</details>

<a id="paper-148"></a>

### 148. [Behaviour analysis for large language model-driven agents](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32087v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32087

**作者：** Tianqiang Yan、Lizhen Qu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.CL

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32087v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32087v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出一个通用框架，把数据驱动的行为发现、轨迹内依赖关系的可解释假设、统计评估与干预实验（含 prompt steering）串联起来，分析 LLM agent 轨迹中行为如何组织并与上下文和结果相关，并以迭代自我反思为案例演示。

**相关性简评：** 其轨迹级行为标注、统计检验与 prompt 干预流程可能可供核查能否迁移到 LVR 推理轨迹的测量与评估，例如刻画 THINK/LOOK/STOP 模式组织与停止决策的因果作用；但材料未涉及视觉或多模态 latent 状态，联系较间接。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Behaviour analysis for large language model-driven agents

**abstract/A1：** Understanding language-agent behaviour requires learning how actions are organised across a trajectory and how this organisation relates to context and outcomes. We propose a general framework that connects data-driven behavioural discovery with analysis and experimental investigation. Categories developed from recorded activity define what is measured. Analyses preserve the behaviours' positions in the process and express their relationships as interpretable hypotheses. Statistical assessment and tests in held-out contexts examine the strength and scope of the evidence. Experiments, including prompt steering, then test how behaviour and outcomes change under deliberately varied conditions. A study of iterative self-reflection illustrates how discovery, dependency learning and prompt comparisons can inform one another. We develop this approach for trajectories of varying length, event order and agent roles, and identify advances in coding, analysis and experimentation needed to support its wider use.

**comment/C1：** Under review by Nature Machine Intelligence

</details>

### 149. [Learning Nonlinear Waveform Fusion Across Scales for Integrated Sensing and Communication](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32099v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32099

**作者：** Jiayu Liu、Kai Wan、Shuo Shao、Giuseppe Caire

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32099v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32099v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文研究通感一体化中的非线性波形融合，用可学习的时间特征加权与频谱更新把图像特征与确定性 LFM 参考合并进共享复数状态，实现图像传输与多目标距离—角度定位。

**相关性简评：** 其以学习式非线性融合替代固定叠加的「共享状态」思路，以及带学习式局部修正与残差更新的序贯定位，或可为 LVR 中视觉证据与推理状态的合并方式及迭代精化、停止判断提供类比线索，需核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Learning Nonlinear Waveform Fusion Across Scales for Integrated Sensing and Communication

**abstract/A1：** Fixed source superposition and frequency partitioning constrain how communication content interacts with a probing reference in integrated sensing and communication (ISAC). We learn nonlinear waveform fusion across scales for image transmission and localization of multiple targets in range and angle. Reception uses the information available at each end. Adaptive temporal feature weighting and spectral updates combine image features and a deterministic linearly frequency-modulated reference in a shared complex state, generating a waveform with fixed total energy over the array frame. At the user equipment (UE), the reference and channel state information condition a neural demixer for image reconstruction. At the base station, the realized transmit pulse defines a coherent dictionary for sequential localization with learned local correction, physical refinement, and residual updates. We compare the proposed waveforms with superposition in the time domain and learned spectral linear fusion on 512 independent test images, using a shared fixed UE receiver and a baseline sensing receiver using coherent peak search. Averaged across eleven task weights, the proposed waveforms reduce normalized localization error by 29.0\\% and 27.0\\%, respectively, while increasing mean peak signal-to-noise ratio by 1.40 and 0.82~dB. With the proposed waveforms fixed, learned local correction reduces mean normalized localization error by 12.2\\% relative to the same sequential receiver without learned correction.

</details>

Mo、Peiwen Li、Defu Cao、Xu Hu、Haixin Wang、Taoran Li、Haoming Li、Zheyuan Deng、Jingwen Zhang、Pengsong Zhang、Haichao Zhang、Guangze Luo、Yi Daniel Lu、Zihuai He、Chenxi Qiu、Dianzhuo Wang、Zhejian Peng、Shushu Liang、Wenyi Lu、Yifan Wang、Jintao Huang、Yijun Wang、Xiaomin Li、Yuexing Hao

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32106v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32106v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 构建 PersonaScienceBench，用 200 个跨四领域任务和带不同画像的模拟研究者，考察科学智能体在仅通过对话获知合作者偏好的情况下完成任务、恢复事实与调整工作流的表现。

**相关性简评：** 隐式画像与「定向澄清、流程修订」的交互轨迹，可能为 LVR 中隐藏假设的主动取证与何时停止追问提供评价类比，可供核查；但该工作本身不涉及视觉状态或 latent 推理。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** PersonaScienceBench: Evaluating Scientific Agents with Diverse Simulated Researchers

**abstract/A1：** Scientific agents collaborate with researchers whose expertise and preferences differ, but benchmarks usually hold the collaborator fixed. We introduce PersonaScienceBench: 200 tasks from five benchmarks across four scientific domains. Simulated researchers provide context and feedback according to persona profiles, while task objectives and grading remain fixed. Agents encounter researcher preferences only through conversation. Across eighteen modelconfigurations, task performance, fact recovery, and self-repetition reveal different strengths. Detailed GPT 6 Astra analyses show distinct agent responses across researcher profiles; interaction traces illustrate targeted clar ification and workflow revision. These behaviors can differ even when final scores match. PersonaScienceBench therefore evaluates how agents collaborate with diverse researchers alongside whether they complete the task.

</details>

### 151. [ImageRSI: Verification-Driven Recursive Self-Improvement for Agentic Image Generation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32120v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32120

**作者：** Shuo Han、Zhilin Wang、Peng Ling、Wenjia Gong、Yang Liu、Guanjun Jiang、Yukun Cao、Xike Xie

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32120v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32120v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 ImageRSI：冻结图像生成器，结合五维验证—修复、跨任务经验库与离线更新的评判器，经验须经配对 A/B 验证才入库，并发布含 961 条难例的 ImageReq-Bench。

**相关性简评：** 其经验入库前的配对 A/B 验证与评判器复用前评估，可能为 LVR 中 latent 轨迹的筛选、验证及训练贡献分配提供类比线索，但任务与内部状态机制相距较远，需核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** ImageRSI: Verification-Driven Recursive Self-Improvement for Agentic Image Generation

**abstract/A1：** Complex text-to-image requests remain difficult to satisfy reliably. Verifying and repairing an image can fix an individual output, but a successful repair does not necessarily carry over to other requests. We present ImageRSI, a framework that keeps the image generator frozen and combines three components: five-dimensional verification and repair within each task, an experience library shared across tasks, and offline updating of the judge. Candidate experiences enter the library only after paired A/B validation, and the judge is updated with supervised fine-tuning and GRPO on accumulated image-judgment records and evaluated before reuse. We also introduce ImageReq-Bench, a benchmark of 961 requests built around hard cases in real-world image-generation requests, each paired with an evaluation checklist. ImageRSI achieves a Checklist Strict Accuracy (CSA) of 52.6% on MindBench (with Nano Banana Pro as the generator) and 65.66% on ImageReq-Bench, and we compare system outcomes across judge-training configurations.

</details>

### 152. [Data Quality in RVL-CDIP: A Systematic Audit](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32126v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32126

**作者：** Stefan Larson、Kevin Leach

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.CL

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32126v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32126v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文对文档分类基准 RVL-CDIP 做系统性数据审计，发现约 10.5% 标签错误、35.2% 测试集存在重复，页面上的识别码单独即可达到 69% 准确率，并重新基准了 25 个模型、发布修正语料与分布外数据。

**相关性简评：** 其揭示的识别码捷径、去除此特征后掉点及跨来源掉 16–28 分，可供核查 VLM 推理是否真正使用视觉证据而非文本或版面旁路，并为视觉证据筛选与 OOD 评价设计提供参照。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Data Quality in RVL-CDIP: A Systematic Audit

**abstract/A1：** RVL-CDIP is the de facto standard benchmark for document classification, and reported accuracy on it is now 98.8%. In this paper, we audit RVL-CDIP for data quality issues and find that approximately 10.5% of its 400,000 documents have erroneous labels and that 35.2% of the test set has a duplicate or near-duplicate in the training set. Identification codes on the pages are sufficient to classify at 69% accuracy, and a state-of-the-art model loses 11 points without them. Image-only models trained on RVL-CDIP lose 16 to 28 points on documents from other sources, and the choice of OCR engine changes accuracy by 4.1 to 8.3 points. We find that nearly 16,000 RVL-CDIP documents contain sensitive personal data, and produce de-identified versions of these documents. We re-benchmark 25 models, and with all defects remediated, the 13 that we retrain are 0.9 to 2.9 points more accurate than in the original condition; the most accurate of them, LayoutLMv3, is 96.5% accurate. We release the corrected corpus, the annotations and the out-of-distribution data.

</details>

### 153. [CFBrainAug: Modularity-Induced Counterfactual Brain Network Augmentation for Interpretable fMRI-based Autism Diagnosis](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32184v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32184

**作者：** Yifan Qiao、Sheng Li

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32184v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32184v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文提出 CFBrainAug，通过检索并模块数约束精修「标签相反但拓扑相似」的脑网络，把反事实样本与真实样本联合训练，并用二者的连接差异给出内在解释，用于小样本 fMRI 自闭症诊断。

**相关性简评：** 其「标签翻转的反事实样本＋差异即解释」思路可能为 LVR 中的反事实监督、证据筛选与贡献分配提供概念类比，但属脑网络分类任务，能否迁移到多模态 latent 推理仍需核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** CFBrainAug: Modularity-Induced Counterfactual Brain Network Augmentation for Interpretable fMRI-based Autism Diagnosis

**abstract/A1：** Machine learning on resting-state functional magnetic resonance imaging (rs-fMRI) brain networks is promising for diagnosing Autism Spectrum Disorder (ASD), but existing methods are prone to overfitting on limited training data, and their post-hoc statistical attribution offers limited clinical interpretability. We propose CFBrainAug, a counterfactual brain network learning framework that unifies data augmentation and counterfactual explanation by learning label-altered brain networks. Unlike conventional label-preserving augmentation, CFBrainAug first retrieves a label-opposite yet topologically similar candidate and then refines it under a module-number constraint. Jointly training on factual and counterfactual networks enriches the training distribution around the decision boundary, enabling a lightweight feature-selection-based SVM to perform competitively with recent graph deep learning methods. Meanwhile, the counterfactuals provide intrinsic explanations by identifying the connectivity differences between each subject and its counterfactual. Experiments on the ABIDE I dataset show that CFBrainAug reaches an accuracy of 74.1% on the NYU site, suggesting that counterfactual augmentation can complement more complex models in small-sample brain network analysis.

</details>

<a id="paper-154"></a>

### 154. [Principia: Test-Time Hypotheses to Principle Construction for Scientific Discovery](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32195v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32195

**作者：** Dongki Kim、Sangwoo Park、Jinheon Baek、Soyeong Jeong、Sung Ju Hwang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32195v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32195v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 Principia：一个 LLM 框架，在 test time 从相关输入与观测结果中形成对比性因果假设，再将跨假设的重复关系归纳为原理，并依据新观测修订假设、重建原理，在分子优化、蛋白质工程等基准上验证。

**相关性简评：** 其「假设—原理」在 test time 反复修订、以新观测驱动状态更新的结构，可能与 LVR 的循环推理状态更新与证据筛选问题形成概念类比，可供核查；但工作面向科学发现与纯文本 LLM，缺少视觉表示与 latent 生成线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Principia: Test-Time Hypotheses to Principle Construction for Scientific Discovery

**abstract/A1：** Scientific discovery requires identifying the scientific principles underlying observed phenomena to guide further investigation. As these empirical principles are often unknown in complex discovery processes, they are constructed via a hypotheses-to-principle pipeline to obtain recurring causalities from progressively accumulated observations during test-time discovery. However, constructing this knowledge requires substantial domain expertise, time, and effort, making this translation stage a bottleneck for scientific discovery. To address this limitation, we propose Principia, an LLM-based framework that constructs and continually refines scientific principles at test time. Drawing on how human researchers develop principles, Principia forms comparative causal hypotheses from related inputs and their observed outcomes, then synthesizes recurring relationships across these hypotheses into inductive principles. By applying the constructed hypotheses and inductive principles to the task, it obtains new observations, revises the hypotheses used based on these new observations, and reconstructs principles from the updated hypotheses, allowing scientific knowledge to continuously evolve throughout test-time discovery. Across benchmarks spanning molecular optimization, protein engineering, gene perturbation, and molecular property prediction, Principia outperforms baselines relying on internal knowledge, external sources, or experience summaries, while generating principles that are plausible and consistent with the scientific literature, demonstrating its effectiveness in constructing and refining task-specific hypotheses and principles at test time for scientific discovery. Our code is available at https://github.com/DongkiKim95/Principia.

</details>

<a id="paper-155"></a>

### 155. [SWE-Shift: Evaluating Coding Agents in Evolving Workspaces](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32196v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32196

**作者：** Victor Shea-Jay Huang、Rongqing Li、Zhangyue Yin、Kelong Mao、Yaqi Zhao、Xinyuan Wang、Yao Yin、Lirui Luo、Renrui Zhang、Hongsheng Li

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.CL、cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32196v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32196v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 SWE-Shift 基准：在编码 agent 执行过程中注入与任务相关的环境变更，覆盖 120 个场景、12 类变更与 9 个干预位置，比较 Clean 与 Shifted 续跑，发现 17 个系统在变更后成功率普遍下降，并暴露出工作保留、状态恢复与陈旧证据刷新上的失败。

**相关性简评：** 其「陈旧证据」检查与执行中状态更新设定，可能为 LVR 多轮推理中视觉证据何时刷新、哪些既有中间结论仍可保留，以及是否重启或继续的判断提供评价思路，可供核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** SWE-Shift: Evaluating Coding Agents in Evolving Workspaces

**abstract/A1：** Repository-level coding benchmarks largely assume that tasks and environments remain unchanged throughout execution. In software development, code, dependencies, runtime state, and requirements can change while an agent is still working. We introduce SWE-Shift, a benchmark for evaluating coding agents under such mid-execution changes. From the same saved history and workspace, a Clean continuation proceeds without intervention, while a matched Shifted continuation receives a task-relevant update. Success requires completing the current task, preserving still-valid work, and satisfying the applicable state and evidence checks. The benchmark contains 120 scenarios across 12 change families, each evaluated at nine scheduled intervention positions. We evaluate 17 coding-agent systems, all of which have lower Shifted than Clean success. GPT-6 Astra drops from 67.35% to 24.79%, with 20.34% paired success. Tool/session changes and stale evidence yield zero paired success across all systems. Late-position paired success is lower for some systems and higher for others. These results show that success on the unchanged task can coexist with failures to preserve work, recover state, or refresh evidence after an update.

**comment/C1：** 47 pages. Code: https://github.com/JeixHuang/SWE-Shift

</details>

<a id="paper-156"></a>

### 156. [ACWM-RM: A Reference-Free Reward Model from Complementary Mismatch Preferences](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32222v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32222

**作者：** Shibo Hong、Siyu Ren、FengJiao Chen、Zhongyuan Peng、Wei Shi、Yixin Cao

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32222v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32222v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文针对动作条件世界模型提出无参考奖励模型 ACWM-RM，用「动作错配」与「视频错配」两类互补偏好对分别监督动作跟随性与视觉质量，并构建 AVM-Eval 诊断 IDM 在生成视频上的可靠性。

**相关性简评：** 其用互补错配偏好把「是否忠实于条件」与「质量是否可信」拆成两个可分别监督的维度，可能为 LVR 中区分「答案是否真正使用 latent 视觉内容」与「表面合理性」的奖励/验证设计提供可核查线索，但领域较远，需自行迁移判断。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** ACWM-RM: A Reference-Free Reward Model from Complementary Mismatch Preferences

**abstract/A1：** Reinforcement learning has gained attention for improving action-conditioned world models (ACWMs), as it enables reward-driven optimization of visual quality (VQ) and action-following fidelity (AF) beyond flow-matching optimization. Realizing this potential requires reward signals that reliably rank videos by both VQ and AF. However, existing rewards capture only one dimension: metric-based rewards assess VQ but require ground-truth videos and do not directly assess AF, while inverse dynamics model (IDM) rewards assess AF without references but do not guarantee VQ. Moreover, IDMs, typically trained on real videos, may provide unreliable rewards for generated videos containing visual artifacts and physical inconsistencies. In this work, we introduce the Action and Video Mismatch Benchmark (AVM-Eval), comprising 67.2k pairs that evaluate how reliably IDMs assess AF on real and generated videos. We then propose ACWM-RM, a reward model that jointly assesses VQ and AF without ground-truth videos during reward computation. To train it, we first construct the Action and Video Mismatch Preference Dataset (AVM-Pref) with 506.3k pairs providing complementary supervision: action-mismatch pairs supervise AF, while video-mismatch pairs supervise VQ and expose the model to generated videos. We then pretrain an action-conditioned predictor on real videos to learn forward dynamics before jointly training it with a patch-weighted score head on AVM-Pref to transfer these dynamics to AF assessment on generated videos. Evaluation on AVM-Eval reveals the limited reliability of IDMs on generated videos, while downstream RL experiments show that ACWM-RM outperforms reward baselines in both criteria.

</details>

<a id="paper-157"></a>

### 157. [Correct Answers, Closed Gates: SolveBind for GUI Agents at the Verification Barrier](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32223v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32223

**作者：** Xinhao Song、Haoxuan Peng、Su Su、Hongliang Wu、Sirui Song、Gongshen Liu、Linfeng Zhang、Dongrui Liu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32223v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32223v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 SolveBind，一个面向受保护 GUI 环境的 agent 框架：用 Perceive、Ground、Solve、Act、Verify 构成的层次求解树分解验证任务，并由运行时监督者依据工具证据修正动作承诺、在显式恢复预算下执行，在九类 CAPTCHA 与 AndroidWorld 上评测。

**相关性简评：** 「正确但被拒」的验证屏障及证据到达后修正承诺、恢复预算带来的成功-成本权衡，可能与 LVR 中的证据获取、停止/继续决策和动态计算分配有概念联系，但论文不涉及内部表示或 latent 状态，仅供核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Correct Answers, Closed Gates: SolveBind for GUI Agents at the Verification Barrier

**abstract/A1：** Websites and applications deploy CAPTCHAs and anti-automation mechanisms that use behavioral signals such as interaction trajectories and timing, not outcomes alone, to distinguish humans from automated agents. We call these protected GUI environments, where correctly solving a verification task is insufficient for end-to-end task completion. Agents must turn solver outputs into accepted interactions and adapt execution to runtime feedback under time constraints. We introduce SolveBind, a GUI-agent framework combining hierarchical solving with runtime supervision. A hierarchical solving tree decomposes verification into interdependent Perceive, Ground, Solve, Act, and Verify capabilities. A runtime supervisor grounds action commitments in tool-derived evidence, revises them as evidence arrives, and mediates execution under explicit recovery budgets. Across nine CAPTCHA task families and nine backbone models, SolveBind achieves the highest success rate on eight backbones, outperforming the strongest baseline by 13.3-34.5 percentage points. It achieves the highest success rate on all six AndroidWorld backbones. In a paired GPT-5.5 analysis, MobileAgent produces correct answers but fails dynamic verification on 13 of 90 tasks; SolveBind succeeds on seven of these tasks. With automatic submission, removing binding reduces success from 82.22% to 71.56% and increases latency and token usage. Recovery caps reduce execution cost with a small observed success trade-off: removing them raises success from 82.22% to 83.56%, while increasing latency and token usage to 3.7x and 2.4x their full-supervision values, respectively. These results support evidence-grounded execution control while identifying a success-cost trade-off in recovery and failure boundaries of heuristic submission rules.

**comment/C1：** 41 pages, 16 figures

</details>
