### 158. [Towards Effective and Transferable Physical Camouflage against Multi-View BEV-based 3D Perception in Autonomous Driving](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32230v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32230

**作者：** Linye Lyu、Jiawei Zhou、Daojing He、Yu Li

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32230v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32230v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 BEVCA，面向自动驾驶多视角 BEV 三维感知模型生成对抗性物理伪装，将基于 BEV 特征的对抗损失与多视角神经渲染结合，以提升跨模型、跨任务的可迁移攻击效果。

**相关性简评：** 与 VLM 推理不直接相关，但其在特征层面注入可迁移扰动并结合渲染生成物理载体的做法，可能作为核查 latent 状态是否真正承载任务必需视觉证据的扰动探针。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Towards Effective and Transferable Physical Camouflage against Multi-View BEV-based 3D Perception in Autonomous Driving

**abstract/A1：** Modern autonomous driving systems widely adopt multi-view Bird's-Eye-View (BEV) based 3D perception models due to their superior performance. Despite their success, the robustness of these models against adversarial camouflage attacks remains largely unexplored. Existing camouflage attacks primarily target single-view 2D detectors, limiting their effectiveness in multi-view 3D perception settings. To address this gap, we propose BEVCA, a novel adversarial camouflage generation framework tailored for multi-view BEV-based 3D perception. Our framework integrates a BEV-feature-based adversarial loss with a multi-view neural rendering module, enabling effective and transferable camouflage attacks across different models and tasks. Extensive experiments in both digital and physical settings demonstrate that BEVCA outperforms state-of-the-art baselines and exhibits strong robustness under real-world conditions. Our code is available at https://github.com/WAVE-AT-IDEA/BEVCA.

</details>

<a id="paper-159"></a>

### 159. [TileShare: Training-Free Acceleration of Overlapping Tiled ViT Decoding](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32233v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32233

**作者：** Xiangzhong Ye、Zhongshen Li、Shen You、Ziyang Meng、Liang Xu、Junkai Ji、Qiuzhen Lin、Xiangtao Li、Ka-Chun Wong

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32233v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32233v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 针对重叠分块 ViT 解码器中重复计算问题，提出免训练方案：保留各分块注意力，其余算子按 token 只算一次，并将重叠 token 的逐块结果按原始权重融合为共享状态，浅层因注意力读取共享状态而保留逐块计算。

**相关性简评：** 其「逐块结果融合为共享状态、按层深度决定共享是否损伤质量」的现象，可能为并行 latent 假设的合并方式与深度敏感性提供可供核查的线索，但属视频解码加速，不涉及推理状态生成与停止决策。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** TileShare: Training-Free Acceleration of Overlapping Tiled ViT Decoding

**abstract/A1：** Fixed-window Vision Transformer (ViT) decoders run at deployment resolution as overlapping tiles: every token in an overlap band is processed by several full decoder instances. In the 2.4B-parameter MiniMax-H3 video decoder, about half of all per-token computation is duplicated. Removing the tiles fails because attention, global tokens and coordinates are bound to them. TileShare keeps each released tile's attention and computes every other operation once per token. The released computation changes at one kind of step only: an overlap token's per-tile results are blended into one shared state with the released weights. Interventions show that this blend costs quality where attention reads the shared states, mainly in the first few layers, which TileShare therefore keeps per tile. Without training, keeping six layers per tile decodes 1.38-1.51x faster than batched and compiled tiling on two GPU types (up to 1.96x with FP8) and is equivalent to the released decoder in PSNR, LPIPS and DISTS on DAVIS videos. On unseen UVG videos with the setting frozen beforehand, it matches PSNR and DISTS and improves LPIPS. TileShare carries over to two further decoders and is again confirmed on unseen data. It is equivalent in PSNR and DISTS on MAGI-1. On the ViTok-v2 image autoencoder at 2K-4K, it matches overlapping tiling in PSNR, improves LPIPS and DISTS and decodes 1.5-1.8x faster. How many layers stay per tile is measured once per model (for decoders, without labels); we report it for 14 decoders and encoders. Optionally, two GPU-hours of self-distillation let MiniMax-H3 share every layer without a loss in PSNR, and a coordinate field trained while the network stays frozen brings fully shared CLIP-based open-vocabulary segmentation to parity with sliding windows on seven benchmarks.

**comment/C1：** 8 pages main paper + supplementary material

</details>

<a id="paper-160"></a>

### 160. [Minimum Description Length Unsupervisedly Separates Law-Grade from Statistical-Grade Knowledge in a Self-Improving Science Agent - and Reveals 86% of Its Own Discoveries Are Statistical](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32245v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32245

**作者：** Wenbin Mao、Pei Zou

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32245v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32245v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文考察一个自改进科学发现 agent 的 252 条候选关系账本，发现其用于模型选择的 Minimum Description Length 压缩比可作为无监督的「知识分级器」：压缩比最高的条目恰好对应经典定律与跨测量恒等式，而 86% 的条目压缩比约等于 1，实为统计规律；作者据此提出预测价值与压缩价值的双轴记账，并给出可falsify的前瞻预测。

**相关性简评：** 其用内部压缩度量区分「真正结构」与「什么也没压缩」的统计规律，可供核查是否能迁移为判断 latent 推理状态是否承载任务必要信息、以及为推理贡献分配提供无监督评估准则。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Minimum Description Length Unsupervisedly Separates Law-Grade from Statistical-Grade Knowledge in a Self-Improving Science Agent - and Reveals 86% of Its Own Discoveries Are Statistical

**abstract/A1：** AI systems that discover scientific relations now generate candidate "laws" at scale, but the bottleneck has shifted from generation to vetting: which candidates deserve belief? We show that a deployed self-improving science agent - one that has admitted 252 relations through held-out prediction courts - already computes the quantity that answers this question: the Minimum Description Length compression ratio used for model selection doubles, when read under discrete accounting, as an unsupervised epistemic grader. The compression-ranked top band of the agent's ledger recovers exactly the canonical laws among its discoveries (vis-viva across seven planets, Balmer/Rydberg) plus cross-measurement identities - enrichment $p=3.6\\times10^{-12}$ under a hypergeometric test - with zero counterexamples and physically coherent band structure. The same lens retroactively reveals that 86% of the ledger compresses nothing ($\\mathrm{cr}\\approx 1$): the agent had been recording statistical regularities, including a learned predictor with held-out $R^2=0.541$ admitted through the prediction gate, under a narrative of compression as the unified value of knowledge - a narrative that was never true for them. We formalize the resulting dual-axis bookkeeping - prediction value ($R^2$) and compression value (cr) as nearly decoupled axes of knowledge - demonstrate its robustness to quantization, and register forward falsifiable predictions. The episode is, to our knowledge, the first measured instance of a discovery agent auditing its own knowledge ledger by an internal measure and finding itself over-claiming.

**comment/C1：** 6 pages, 2 figures. Companion paper: arXiv:2610.14843

</details>

<a id="paper-161"></a>

### 161. [MedCaseAgent: From Multimodal Clinical Evidence to Rare Disease Case Reports](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32258v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32258

**作者：** Hongkang Chu、Yifan Shen、Tianjiao Yu、Zhuoqing Zhong、Jianguo Cao、Meihuan Huang、Xu Cao

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32258v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32258v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 MedCaseAgent 端到端 agent 框架，分层挖掘患者级临床记录并结合迭代式工具调用推理、动态检索文献与知识库，从非结构化记录生成罕见病病例报告，同时发布 MedCase-150K 语料、MedCase-Bench 评测集与经后训练的专用模型。

**相关性简评：** 其按需取证、评估病例新颖性的多轮检索推理循环，可能与 LVR 中 LOOK/STOP 的证据获取、验证与停止决策问题有概念联系，但论文面向临床报告生成、未涉及 latent 状态或视觉表示分析，借鉴价值需核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** MedCaseAgent: From Multimodal Clinical Evidence to Rare Disease Case Reports

**abstract/A1：** Although Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated potential in clinical documentation, generating the rigorous, evidence-based reasoning required for unprecedented, atypical, and rare disease case reports remains a significant challenge. We present MedCaseAgent, an end-to-end agentic reasoning paradigm that bridges this gap by synthesizing publication-quality reports from unstructured clinical notes. MedCaseAgent leverages a hierarchical structure, integrating patient-level data mining with iterative, tool-integrated reasoning. By dynamically querying medical literature and knowledge bases, the agent maintains high clinical fidelity and assesses case novelty. The framework is inherently modular, supporting various backbone LLMs in a plug-and-play fashion. To facilitate this research, we release MedCase-150K, a large-scale multimodal clinical case-report corpus, and MedCase-Bench, a robust temporally held-out evaluation suite. We also introduce an open-source model post-trained from Qwen3.5-9B and optimized specifically for this task. Under both LLM-judged and rule-based evaluation, MedCaseAgent outperforms existing general-purpose and specialized medical LLMs and agents. Blinded expert reviews and ablations further support the system's clinical relevance and evidence grounding.

</details>

<a id="paper-162"></a>

### 162. [TGR-EvoDAG: A DAG-based Multi-Agent Code Evolution Framework for Generative Recommendation on Tencent](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32326v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32326

**作者：** Junlin He、Junzhang Zhu、Yudong Li、Yihong Tang、Xuefeng Jiang、Wei Ma、Chengxiang Zhuo、Zang Li

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32326v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32326v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 TGR-EvoDAG，将生成式推荐的代码演化建模为 DAG，用多智能体维护多条演化分支并合并互补创新，配合阶段感知的 GPU 成本规划与下游协同适配，在 Amazon 数据与腾讯线上环境中验证。

**相关性简评：** 其「阶段非对称成本规划」与「为暂时指标回退的上游创新保留分支」的思路，可能与 LVR 多阶段推理的计算预算分配、延迟剪枝与停止决策、贡献分配问题形成类比，可供核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** TGR-EvoDAG: A DAG-based Multi-Agent Code Evolution Framework for Generative Recommendation on Tencent

**abstract/A1：** Large Language Model (LLM) agents have enabled automated code evolution for algorithm discovery. However, current recursive self-improvement (RSI) research for sequential recommendation faces a dilemma: general-purpose frameworks lack domain-specific feedback, while recommender-specific frameworks rely on linear evolution. Crucially, naively integrating them fails when applied to Generative Recommendation (GR). Unlike end-to-end models, GR relies on a module-cascaded pipeline (tokenization, pre-training, and alignment). In this paradigm, existing evolution methods suffer from: i) cost blindness, squandering budgets by ignoring stage-asymmetric retraining overheads; and ii) short-sighted evaluation, discarding promising upstream innovations that temporarily regress metrics before downstream adaptation. To bridge these gaps, we propose TGR-EvoDAG, a multi-agent code evolution framework tailored for GR. TGR-EvoDAG models code evolution as a Directed Acyclic Graph (DAG) to maintain multiple evolution branches and merge complementary innovations, unifying advanced search with rich GR diagnostic feedback. We further introduce stage-aware cost planning to optimize GPU-hour budgets, and active downstream co-adaptation to navigate temporary regressions and unlock cross-stage gains. On public Amazon datasets, TGR-EvoDAG enables the first autonomous evolution of a complete GR pipeline, improving offline metrics by up to 37.4% and Agent4Rec simulated user satisfaction by 19.7% over the strongest baseline. Furthermore, in industrial deployment on Tencent's large-scale commercial media platform, the evolved GR achieves a 12.16% relative improvement in offline Hit@1 and a statistically significant 0.40% increase in online click-through rate (CTR).

</details>

<a id="paper-163"></a>

### 163. [Optimal Multiclass Online Regret with Arbitrary Label Spaces](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32339v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32339

**作者：** Sajad Ashkezari、Shai Ben-David

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、stat.ML

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32339v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32339v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文在任意（可无限）标签空间的 agnostic 多分类在线学习中，证明以 Littlestone 维数 d 为参数的紧遗憾界 O(min{T,√(dT)})，方法是把单边专家覆盖强化为分数覆盖，再归约到有限专家博弈。

**相关性简评：** 属纯在线学习理论，与视觉推理无直接关联；但其逐轮遗憾与专家博弈视角可能为 LVR 中动态计算分配、停止决策的在线评估提供概念工具，可供核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Optimal Multiclass Online Regret with Arbitrary Label Spaces

**abstract/A1：** We determine the optimal regret in agnostic multiclass online learning with arbitrary, possibly infinite, label spaces. For every hypothesis class of multiclass Littlestone dimension $d&lt;\\infty$, we prove a regret upper bound of $O(\\min\\{T,\\sqrt{dT}\\})$ over $T$ rounds, matching the known lower bound. This resolves a question of Hanneke et al. (2023) and extends the optimal binary regret bound of Alon et al. (2021). Our proof strengthens the one-sided expert cover of Hanneke et al. (2023) to a fractional cover. We combine this cover with a minimax reduction to a finite expert game, whose best-response rewards provide the disagreement bounds needed for chaining.

</details>

<a id="paper-164"></a>

### 164. [FOCUS: A Lightweight Mamba-2 Model for EEG-Based Cognitive Workload Classification](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32361v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32361

**作者：** Ioannis Kyprakis、Stefanos Gkikas、Chariklia Chatzaki、Zongyi Jiang、Randy Gomez、Raul Fernandez Rojas

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32361v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32361v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 FOCUS：用固定 FFT 五频带分解编码多通道 EEG，经深度可分离卷积与权重共享的 encoder–bottleneck Mamba-2 对组成 0.04M 参数模型，做二分类认知负荷识别；11 人 LOSO 试点达 74.75% 准确率，并消融权重共享（+4.55 点但不显著）。

**相关性简评：** 与 LVR 主线无直接联系；但其共享参数的 SSM 对偶结构与固定频带预分解，可能为循环式 latent 状态更新和任务必要信息的预筛提供参照，可供核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** FOCUS: A Lightweight Mamba-2 Model for EEG-Based Cognitive Workload Classification

**abstract/A1：** Cognitive workload estimation from physiological signals is increasingly relevant in high-risk environments, where continuous and unobtrusive monitoring can inform adaptive systems and safety interventions. This paper presents FOCUS (Frequency-band Optimized Cognitive-load Ultralight SSM), a compact Mamba-2-based model for EEG-based binary workload classification under constrained data conditions. Rather than learning frequency sensitivity from data, FOCUS encodes preprocessed multi-channel EEG using a fixed FFT decomposition that covers the five canonical EEG bands. The proposed model combines depthwise-separable convolution and a weight-shared encoder-bottleneck Mamba-2 pair, with 0.04 million parameters and 0.11 GFLOPs. Evaluated in a pilot study with 11 participants under leave-one-subject-out cross-validation across three cognitively distinct tasks, FOCUS achieves 74.75% accuracy on the full dataset at 0.08 ms GPU model-forward latency. In the weight-sharing ablation, the shared configuration achieves 4.55 percentage points higher accuracy on the full dataset than the non-shared variant while reducing the SSM parameter budget; however, this difference does not reach statistical significance under a two-sided Wilcoxon signed-rank test ($p=0.083$). Overall, the selected FOCUS configuration provides a favorable trade-off between classification performance and computational cost within this controlled pilot setting.

</details>

<a id="paper-165"></a>

### 165. [Past as State, Present as Attention: Persistent-State Blockwise Flow Matching for Long-Horizon Co-Speech Motion Generation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32373v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32373

**作者：** Yi Liu、Xiangyue Zhang、Jianfang Li、Jia Ma、Yisheng He、Jianqiang Ren

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.GR、cs.MM

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32373v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32373v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 PASPA：用块间持久状态紧凑总结历史动作、块内双向自注意力建模当前片段，在 blockwise flow matching 框架下生成长时程语音驱动动作，并配以多块监督、prefill-decode 状态缓存和混合 classifier-free guidance。

**相关性简评：** 其「过去压缩为持久状态、当前保留细粒度注意力」的划分与状态缓存更新机制，可能与 LVR 中长程 latent 工作区的状态维护、长度泛化和缓存成本问题存在概念联系，可供核查；但属动作生成领域，迁移有效性需验证。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Past as State, Present as Attention: Persistent-State Blockwise Flow Matching for Long-Horizon Co-Speech Motion Generation

**abstract/A1：** Co-speech motion generation remains challenging over long horizons due to the need for temporal coherence and consistent style. Existing methods typically generate long motions segment by segment, either conditioning on a short seed of recent frames, which gradually drifts, or retaining ever-longer explicit histories, which are costly to maintain and hard to learn from limited data. We trace these limitations to a key asymmetry between past and present: past motion carries slowly varying style that should be compactly summarized, whereas the current segment exhibits fast-changing local dynamics that require fine-grained modeling. This asymmetry motivates a simple Past-As-State, Present-as-Attention principle for long-horizon co-speech motion generation. Following this principle, we propose PASPA, which summarizes past history with an inter-block persistent state and models the current segment with intra-block bidirectional self-attention. We instantiate PASPA within a blockwise flow matching framework, with multi-block supervision for effective training over variable histories, prefill-decode state caching for efficient rollout, and hybrid classifier-free guidance for enhanced history and speech conditioning. Extensive public-benchmark experiments show that PASPA achieves state-of-the-art FGD performance and preserves motion style across long-sequence generation.

**comment/C1：** Accepted at NeurIPS 2026. 38 pages, 10 figures, 17 tables

</details>

<a id="paper-166"></a>

### 166. [An Error Budget for Shuffled Multimodal Windows in the 3rd WEAR Challenge: Stacking, Count Constraints and Seam Matching](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32398v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32398

**作者：** Soheil Kooklan

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32398v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32398v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该技术报告分析一个第三届 WEAR 挑战赛的赛后系统：以堆叠的窗口分类器、参与者图上的视频近邻特征平均、带软计数约束的平均场式离线解码器，以及肢体接缝距离排序来为乱序窗口打标签，并给出分项误差预算与自举区间。

**相关性简评：** 其「误差预算」式分解（如 oracle 按真实片段平均后仍余 0.973、多数剩余错误集中于 null 类）与带全局计数约束的离线解码，可能为 LVR 中证据聚合、贡献分配与误差归因的评价表述提供参照，但任务领域相距较远，需核查可迁移性。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** An Error Budget for Shuffled Multimodal Windows in the 3rd WEAR Challenge: Stacking, Count Constraints and Seam Matching

**abstract/A1：** The 3rd WEAR Dataset Challenge asks for an activity label for every one-second window of four unseen participants. Each window has 15 VideoMAEv2 frame embeddings, the accelerometer signal of one random limb, and the windows are shuffled. This report is an empirical analysis of one system built after the challenge deadline. Window classifiers are stacked with features averaged over per-participant graphs of video neighbours and scored successor candidates, and a mean-field style decoder with a soft count constraint per activity labels all windows of a participant together. The setting is offline and transductive, and the count constraint relies on each activity being performed once. In a participant-wise simulation with participant-level bootstrap intervals, stacking added 0.051 macro F1 over stage-1 fusion and the count constraint 0.011 (95% interval 0.006 to 0.017). A quadratic seam distance ranks the true same-limb successor first among about 720 candidates in 83.9% of cases, but adding it to the link model changed macro F1 by -0.0001 (-0.0006 to 0.0005). With the decoder settings and the mixing weight chosen inside the folds and the mislabelled spans left out of training, the system scores 0.936 in simulation (0.940 for participants who performed each activity once); the submitted configuration scores 0.938 (0.943). Per-participant scores range from 0.80 to 0.97. These results come from a non-nested stacking protocol that uses unlabelled features transductively, so they are not a fully inductive estimate. An oracle that averages the final unary scores over the true segments reaches 0.973, and 79% of the remaining errors involve the null class. On the private leaderboard, which no choice used, the selected Kaggle submission scored 0.9231 (public 0.9254, with bounds partly tuned there), and a later one with bounds chosen in simulation scored 0.9247 (public 0.9253).

**comment/C1：** 12 pages, 10 tables, 1 figure. Technical report on a post-deadline system for the 3rd WEAR Dataset Challenge at HASCA 2026. Code: https://github.com/soheilkooklan/wear-seam-decoder

</details>

<a id="paper-167"></a>

### 167. [TIE: Training-free CLIP Embedding Guidance for Compositional Text-to-Image Alignment](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32407v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32407

**作者：** Sujung Hong、Tae Eun Choi、Youngjun Jun、Chanyong Yoon、Seong Jae Hwang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32407v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32407v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文分析 CLIP 文本嵌入层面的组合性失败，发现靠后概念嵌入熵更高、首个 CLIP 注意力层反映属性绑定强度，并提出训练无关的单次嵌入更新（熵感知奇异值放大与插值外推绑定）以减少概念丢失与混淆。

**相关性简评：** 其用嵌入熵与注意力绑定强度刻画「概念丢失/混淆」的做法，可能可供核查 LVR 内部状态中信息保留与实体关系是否坍缩；但对象仅为文本嵌入与图像生成对齐，与推理期状态生成无直接关联。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** TIE: Training-free CLIP Embedding Guidance for Compositional Text-to-Image Alignment

**abstract/A1：** Text-to-image (T2I) diffusion models have advanced notably, but still fail to satisfy prompt conditions, resulting in attribute mismatches and missing objects. These errors normally fall into two categories: concept loss, when an object or attribute (i.e., concept) goes missing, and concept confusion, when an attribute is assigned to the wrong object or multiple objects blend together. Recent studies suggest that these challenges are caused by the limited capacity of CLIP text encoder to capture fine-grained semantic details. Although several methods have been proposed, concept loss and concept confusion persist in recent T2I models when handling multi-object, multi-attribute prompts. In this paper, we conduct an embedding-level analysis to develop a training-free, inference-time solution for concept loss and concept confusion across CLIP-based T2I models. We find that (1) concepts mentioned later exhibit higher embedding entropy, reflecting less concentrated representations that are more often associated with concept loss, and (2) the first CLIP attention layer captures the strength of binding between each object and its attribute. Guided by our findings, we introduce TIE, a method that improves semantic alignment through a single text-embedding update. TIE addresses concept loss via entropy-aware singular value amplification and resolves concept confusion through interpolation-extrapolation binding based on CLIP attention scores, all in a training-free manner. Extensive experiments demonstrate that TIE enhances semantic fidelity in multi-concept scenarios with minimal sampling overhead. The project page is available at https://hong-sujung.github.io/TIE/.

</details>

<a id="paper-168"></a>

### 168. [Async-RBench: Benchmarking Asynchronous Task Execution in LLM Agents](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32429v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32429

**作者：** Detao Zeng、Guilin Ou、Nuo Chen、Yunhao Feng、Hongliang Wang、Qinli Yang、Junming Shao、Zhong Zhang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32429v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32429v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 Async-RBench：包含 200 个可执行任务与八类场景，由场景控制器按预设任务状态条件投递子代理结果，并用 Event Response Contract 规定每次响应必须改变、保留、回避与重新核验的内容；另设 Dynamic Replanning Score 将过程与结果等权计分，与任务成功率分开报告，用于评测 LLM 主代理在异步结果到达时的处理能力。

**相关性简评：** 论文关注「新证据异步到达后，哪些状态该更新或保留、哪些检查要重做」，这一证据纳入与状态修订问题可能为 LVR 中的视觉证据筛选、latent 推理状态更新及复核/停止决策提供评价设计线索，可供核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Async-RBench: Benchmarking Asynchronous Task Execution in LLM Agents

**abstract/A1：** Large language model (LLM) agents increasingly execute tasks asynchronously: a main agent continues working while its subagents run, and the task state may change before their results arrive. The main agent must decide which work to update or preserve and which checks to repeat, while avoiding prohibited changes. Final task success alone does not reveal whether these results were handled correctly, and natural asynchronous arrivals are hard to reproduce. We introduce Async-RBench, a benchmark for asynchronous task execution in LLM agents, with 200 executable tasks covering eight scenarios. A scenario controller regulates result delivery using predefined task-state conditions, while an Event Response Contract (ERC) specifies what each response must change, preserve, avoid, and re-verify. The Dynamic Replanning Score (DRS) weights the response process and outcome equally and is reported separately from task success. We evaluate nine agents and find DRS scores of 19.2 to 45.1, with the lowest scores on duplicate reports and subagent failures. Under our execution protocol, all nine agents obtain lower task success with asynchronous than with batched delivery (26.0% vs. 29.7% on average). For three agents, prompting the main agent to rebuild its plan after every result improves DRS by only 2.6 to 3.5 points. Together, these findings highlight the challenges of reliable asynchronous task execution and the need to evaluate how agents handle incoming results alongside end-to-end task success.

**comment/C1：** 25 pages, 16 figures. Code is available at https://github.com/pope8zdt/Async-RBench

</details>

<a id="paper-169"></a>

### 169. [DIGOM: Disentangled Interaction Graphs for Outcome prediction in Multimodal healthcare](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32451v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32451

**作者：** Hakima Laribi、Olivier Lefebvre、Dan Poenaru、Martin Vallières

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32451v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32451v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 DIGOM，一个图框架，同时建模患者内跨模态、跨患者同模态与跨患者跨模态三类交互；通过可微采样得到患者自适应的稀疏邻接矩阵，并用正则项控制稀疏度，再把关系上下文经消息传递融入表示以做临床结局预测。

**相关性简评：** 其「分离式交互类型 + 可微自适应稀疏邻接」可能与 LVR 中关系/证据的结构化筛选与内部状态的关系建模相通，但属医疗多模态预测场景，迁移价值需核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** DIGOM: Disentangled Interaction Graphs for Outcome prediction in Multimodal healthcare

**abstract/A1：** Multimodal healthcare data contain relational information that extends beyond individual patient observations. Effective clinical outcome prediction therefore requires not only integrating information from multiple modalities, but also modeling the relationships between them. Standard fusion approaches do not explicitly capture these interactions, while existing graph-based approaches typically model only a subset of them. We propose DIGOM (Disentangled Interaction Graphs for Outcome prediction in Multimodal healthcare), a graph-based framework that jointly models three complementary interaction types: Intra-patient cross-modal, Cross-patient same-modal, and Cross-patient cross-modal interactions. DIGOM learns a sparse multiplex graph with disentangled views, each encoding interactions between pairs of modalities across patients. Discrete adjacency matrices with patient-adaptive connectivity are sampled via a differentiable mechanism, enabling end-to-end learning and allowing the model to select informative relationships without imposing a fixed number of neighbors. A regularization objective maintains overall graph sparsity, while message passing integrates this relational context into patient representations for outcome prediction. We evaluate DIGOM on two multimodal healthcare datasets, MIMIC-IV and NIH-TB, across five clinical prediction tasks. DIGOM achieves the highest AUROC on all five tasks while using fewer connections than existing multi-interaction approaches. Ablation studies reveal that each interaction type's contribution varies across tasks, highlighting the need to adapt relational structures per task. DIGOM also demonstrates greater robustness to missing modalities, suggesting learned relationships provide complementary information when modalities are unavailable. Post-hoc analyses further reveal task- and outcome-specific connectivity patterns in the learned graphs.

</details>

<a id="paper-170"></a>

### 170. [MDMR-Bench: A Multi-Dimensional Benchmark for Multi-Reference Image Generation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32459v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32459

**作者：** Bowen Zheng、Jintao Lin、Changheng Yi、Haoran Yang、Binxiao Huang、Chenyang Lei、Rui Liu、Xihui Liu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32459v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32459v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 MDMR-Bench，一个面向多参考图像生成的多维度基准，按实体融合、属性迁移、单图多元素抽取、信息图操控和视觉推理密集生成等设置划分子集，并采用含全局与原子级查询的清单式评估协议做细粒度诊断。

**相关性简评：** 该基准的清单式细粒度评估与「视觉推理密集生成」子集，可能为 LVR 中视觉细节保留和证据使用提供可核查的场景与评价思路；但其主体仍是图像生成评测，与 latent 推理状态机制联系较远。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** MDMR-Bench: A Multi-Dimensional Benchmark for Multi-Reference Image Generation

**abstract/A1：** Image generation models have rapidly advanced, with many models now supporting generation conditioned on multiple reference images and textual instructions. Such models enable a wide range of applications, including entity fusion and attribute transfer across images. However, existing benchmarks for multi-reference image generation rely on limited evaluation dimensions and fail to capture the diverse challenges of the task. To address this limitation, we propose MDMR-Bench, a Multi-Dimensional benchmark for Multi-Reference image generation. MDMR-Bench is organized around representative capability settings and consists of multiple evaluation subsets spanning diverse dimensions of multi-reference image generation, including (1) common entity fusion and attribute transfer, (2) multi-element extraction from a single image, (3) infographic manipulation, and (4) visual reasoning-intensive generation. This design enables systematic analysis of model capabilities and failure modes across different challenge dimensions. Furthermore, to achieve more reliable and interpretable evaluation, we introduce a checklist-based evaluation protocol that decomposes generation quality into fine-grained evaluation aspects. Instead of relying solely on holistic scoring, our framework uses structured checklists composed of both global-level and atomic-level queries, enabling more diagnostic assessment of model performance. By benchmarking both open-source and proprietary models, we demonstrate that MDMR-Bench provides fine-grained insights into model performance across different dimensions. The dataset and code are publicly available.

**comment/C1：** Bowen Zheng and Jintao Lin contributed equally to this work. Accepted at NeurIPS 2026, Evaluations &amp; Datasets Track

</details>

<a id="paper-171"></a>

### 171. [ALLMTS: Automated LLM-based Medical Triage System](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32469v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32469

**作者：** Michał Remigiusz Janiszewski、Artur Stopa、Łukasz Lenkiewicz、Wojciech Achtelik、Bartosz Gonczarek

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32469v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32469v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 ALLMTS：由 LLM 理解电话分诊记录并选择、执行可审计的临床分诊指南，按首个肯定回答确定处置，在合成场景上明显优于直接让 LLM 分诊，并大幅降低过度升级。

**相关性简评：** 其将推理外化为指南遍历与「首个肯定回答即停止」的确定性规则，可能为 LVR 的 STOP 决策、证据筛选与升级／降级评价提供对照线索；但属纯文本医疗应用，无 latent 状态或视觉推理，需较多核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** ALLMTS: Automated LLM-based Medical Triage System

**abstract/A1：** Telephone triage is a safety critical task: from a brief intake note, a nurse must decide whether a caller needs an ambulance, an emergency department, an appointment, or self care at home. Queried directly, large language models (LLMs) are unreliable: they over-escalate routine cases and cannot justify their recommendations against an auditable clinical standard. We present the Automated LLM-based Medical Triage System (ALLMTS), which grounds every disposition in the same commercially maintained telehealth guidelines nurses use. Rather than asking an LLM to triage, ALLMTS makes it execute the guideline: it selects the appropriate guideline and evaluates its triage assessment questions. The disposition follows deterministically from the first positive answer, using the same traversal rule a nurse follows. We evaluate ALLMTS on 566 synthetic, expert-validated scenarios spanning 14 dispositions and designed to test specific guideline questions. ALLMTS achieves 0.95 accuracy, compared with 0.47 for a raw-LLM baseline. On a curated 50-scenario subset released as an open benchmark, ALLMTS reaches 0.98 accuracy, on par with the 0.944 correct disposition rate that a five nurse expert panel recorded on those same scenarios. Critically, on that subset the baselines have over-triage rates of up to 0.50 (0.44 on the full dataset), whereas ALLMTS reduces the over-triage rate to at most 0.02 while maintaining low under-triage. These results support coupling LLM language understanding with executable clinical guidelines for triage from intake notes.

**comment/C1：** Accepted at the GenAI4Health Workshop at NeurIPS 2026

</details>

<a id="paper-172"></a>

### 172. [Online Continual Learning in Edge-Cloud Applications](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32472v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32472

**作者：** Charalampos Davalas、Dimitrios Michail、Christos Diou、Iraklis Varlamis、Konstantinos Tserpes

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32472v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32472v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出一种边缘-云协同方法，将持续学习与 OOD 检测、概念漂移检测结合，用 ODIN 与 ECDD 识别值得标注的样本并触发基于回放的模型更新，在汽车、医疗与农业数据上验证其可行性。

**相关性简评：** 其「检测到分布偏移或漂移即触发更新」的机制，可能为 LVR 中「何时重新获取证据、何时更新或停止推理状态」的触发式决策提供类比线索，但领域与任务差异较大，需较多核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Online Continual Learning in Edge-Cloud Applications

**abstract/A1：** Deployed machine learning models in real-world IoT and edge/cloud systems face a critical reliability bottleneck: the requirement for online adaptation to non-stationary data streams while operating without access to the full original training dataset. Performance is severely degraded when models encounter Out-of-Distribution (OOD) inputs or suffer from catastrophic forgetting. Addressing these challenges requires adaptation mechanisms capable of learning from evolving data streams while preserving previously acquired knowledge. This paper proposes an edge-cloud methodology for integrating Continual Learning (CL) with Out-of-Distribution (OOD) Detection and Concept Drift Detection in IoT applications. The methodology incorporates ODIN and ECDD as alternative mechanisms for identifying informative samples for annotation and triggering rehearsal-based model updates. Experimental results across automotive, healthcare, and agricultural datasets demonstrate the applicability of the proposed methodology and its potential to support model adaptation while mitigating catastrophic forgetting.

</details>

<a id="paper-173"></a>

### 173. [CASE: Confusion-Aware Skill Evolution for Frozen Multimodal Emotion Models](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32481v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32481

**作者：** Siyu Wu、Zeyu Liang、Linghao Wang、Qiyun Zheng、Yi Yao、Ling Lo、Hongxia Xie

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32481v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32481v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 CASE：针对语义相近、多模态线索重叠的易混淆情绪，维护一个稀疏、自演化的成对 EmotionSkill 技能库，在冻结 MLLM 主干的前提下于推理时检索对应技能以强化边界推理，并在 CA-MER、DFEW、EMER 上验证效果与跨主干迁移性。

**相关性简评：** 该文把易混淆情形的知识外化为可检索、可演化的技能而非改动参数，可能为 LVR 中按混淆/不确定度触发的外部证据检索与技能路由提供类比；但它不涉及内部 latent 状态、视觉表示或推理轨迹，与主线的实际联系较远，可供核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** CASE: Confusion-Aware Skill Evolution for Frozen Multimodal Emotion Models

**abstract/A1：** Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have substantially advanced Multimodal Emotion Recognition (MER) by enabling fine-grained emotion understanding through reasoning over multimodal evidence. However, they still exhibit systematic confusion between semantically adjacent emotions with highly overlapping multimodal cues. Existing approaches typically address these errors through global parameter adaptation, making confusion correction costly and inflexible. We instead observe that emotion confusion is highly localized and therefore better addressed through reusable pair-specific expertise. Building on this insight, we propose Confusion-Aware Skill Evolution (CASE), the first agent-skill framework for MER. CASE maintains a sparse, self-evolving library of pair-specific EmotionSkills that externalize affective expertise from model parameters, enabling the learned expertise to be continuously refined and readily transferred across diverse frozen MLLM backbones. During inference, CASE dynamically retrieves the appropriate EmotionSkill to reinforce boundary-aware reasoning while keeping the MLLM backbone frozen. Extensive experiments on CA-MER, DFEW, and EMER demonstrate the effectiveness and transferability of CASE across both general-purpose and emotion-specialized MLLMs. Notably, CASE improves the average accuracy of Qwen3-VL-8B on the CA-MER benchmark from 46.80% to 59.13%, while achieving consistent gains across MLLMs.

**comment/C1：** 9 pages, 6 figures, 4 tables

</details>

### 174. [K-CLUE: A Segmentation-to-Clustering Framework for Ki67 Cell Typing](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32493v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32493

**作者：** Ana Beatriz Vieira、Sara P. Oliveira、Diana Montezuma、João Fraga、Jaime S. Cardoso、Arlindo L. Oliveira

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32493v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32493v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 K-CLUE：分割细胞核后用冻结预训练编码器表示、PCA 降维并以 k-means 区分 Ki67 阳性与阴性细胞，无需任务训练和标注；并发现跨编码器差异来自 DAB 相关变化是否占据主方差方向，用白化将其消除。

**相关性简评：** 与 LVR/VLM 推理无直接联系，但其「线性可解码却不等于可无监督分离」「方差结构决定表征可用性」的诊断视角，可能为判断 latent 状态中任务相关视觉信息是否真正可被利用、是否发生信息坍缩提供评价类比，具体对应关系需较多核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** K-CLUE: A Segmentation-to-Clustering Framework for Ki67 Cell Typing

**abstract/A1：** Ki67 is a key marker of tumor proliferation in breast cancer, but its quantification from immunohistochemistry images normally depends on supervised classifiers trained per dataset. We examine the role of frozen pretrained visual representations in annotation-free Ki67 cell typing. To this end, we use K-CLUE, a framework that segments individual nuclei, represents each nucleus with a pretrained encoder, reduces the resulting embeddings with principal component analysis, and separates Ki67-positive from Ki67-negative nuclei using k-means clustering, without task-specific training or manual annotations. Clustering the raw components is highly sensitive to the choice of encoder: across seven encoders, the Ki67-positive F1-score on matched MIST nuclei varies by 44 points, although Ki67 status remains linearly decodable from every representation, with supervised probe AUCs above 0.98. Analysis of the embedding spaces shows that clustering succeeds when DAB-related variation occupies the leading directions of variance. Whitening the retained principal components removes this dependence, reducing the spread across encoders to 1.00 F1-score point. Handcrafted color representations are also competitive, confirming that Ki67 status is strongly reflected in chromatic information, but they require an explicit choice of color space. In end-to-end evaluation, K-CLUE reaches F1-scores of 85.00% and 81.91% for Ki67-positive and Ki67-negative nuclei on MIST, and 79.59% and 81.16% on BCData, outperforming DeepLIIF and IHCScoreGAN on both datasets. These results show that pretrained representations can support annotation-free Ki67 typing when their variance structure is accounted for.

**comment/C1：** Accepted at the BD-MedVis Workshop, BMVC 2026

</details>

### 175. [BAARC: Uncertainty-Driven Adaptive Partial Spectral Measurement to Full Reconstruction for Material Classification](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32505v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32505

**作者：** Adithya Ashok Chalain Valapil、Aishwarya Venkataramanan、Joachim Denzler

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32505v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32505v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 BAARC 框架，用 masked 训练学习 Raman 光谱特征，在联合训练的重建与分类两级结构中逐步采集小波数片段，由重建不确定性选择下一测量位置、并与分类不确定性共同判定终止，从而以少量通道完成材料识别。

**相关性简评：** 用不确定性闭环驱动「下一步看哪里」和「何时停止」，可能与 LVR 中 LOOK/STOP 的证据获取与停止决策问题相类比；其信息价值估计与终止准则是否可迁移，需核查方法细节。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** BAARC: Uncertainty-Driven Adaptive Partial Spectral Measurement to Full Reconstruction for Material Classification

**abstract/A1：** Spectroscopic instruments with limited wavenumber coverage require accurate scan positioning or multiple measurements to complete the material analysis. This often requires prerequisite knowledge about the material under diagnosis to measure the spectral regions with distinguishing peaks. In our study, we simulate a Bayesian Adaptive Acquisition with Reconstruction-driven Classification (BAARC) framework for Raman spectral measurements, using masked training to learn spectral features that dynamically drive laser channel selection until the material is identified. We present a jointly trained two-level framework that takes progressive partial measurements of small wavenumber patches across the complete spectral range and, at each step, first attempts to reconstruct the full spectra until the model uncertainty is low. Secondly, material classification is performed at each reconstruction step until stoppage. Uncertainty quantification during reconstruction steps helps determine the measurement position of our next patch, while combining it with classification uncertainty determines measurement termination. By closing the loop between hardware acquisition and deep probabilistic modeling, the proposed system minimizes physical measurement steps while maintaining both spectral fidelity and diagnostic accuracy. Experimental evaluations demonstrate that our framework achieves high-accuracy sample identification using only a fraction of full-spectrum channels, offering a scalable solution for real-time, high-throughput spectroscopic instruments.

</details>

<a id="paper-176"></a>

### 176. [Amortized Thompson Sampling and Efficient Information-Directed Sampling for Simulator-Based Design](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32514v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32514

**作者：** Michal Kobiela、Michael U. Gutmann

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32514v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32514v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出在收集任何数据之前就预先摊销 Thompson sampling，使观测到达后能立即得到完整采样分布的近似；并借此高效估计关于最优动作的期望信息增益，支撑多轮序列设计与 information-directed sampling，在似然不可解的两个合成生物学模拟器设计问题上验证。

**相关性简评：** 其「信念状态→摊销采样→信息增益→采集决策」的链条可能为 LVR 中的视觉证据筛选与 LOOK/STOP 的价值估计提供概念线索，但属较远领域，迁移性有待核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Amortized Thompson Sampling and Efficient Information-Directed Sampling for Simulator-Based Design

**abstract/A1：** Thompson sampling is a widely used strategy for sequential decision-making under uncertainty, offering a principled way to balance exploration and exploitation. This strategy involves maximizing a target objective under a single sample drawn from the current belief over unknown quantities. Conventionally, this optimization is performed once the belief has been inferred from data, and it becomes computationally burdensome when many Thompson samples are required, especially in continuous action spaces. In this work, we introduce an approach that amortizes this process ahead of time, before any data is collected, so that an approximation of the full Thompson sampling distribution is available immediately once observations arrive. We employ this approach to solve the multi-round setting, involving sequential data acquisition and Thompson-sample recommendation across rounds. Beyond accelerating Thompson sampling, our approach enables efficient estimation of expected information gain about the optimal action. This can be used to select more informative Thompson samples or to obtain maximally informative designs with optimization. Combining this estimate of expected information gain with immediate regret supports information-directed sampling (IDS), which can outperform Thompson sampling by explicitly balancing these two quantities. We demonstrate our approach on two simulator-based design problems in synthetic biology, where the simulators have intractable likelihoods.

</details>

<a id="paper-177"></a>

### 177. [Contextual Decision Making: Predictive or Generative](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32584v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32584

**作者：** L. Jeff Hong、Weihuan Huang、Xinyao Li

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、math.OC、stat.ML

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32584v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32584v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文比较上下文决策中的两条路线：「先预测再优化」（学习条件期望目标）与「先生成再优化」（学习条件不确定性并采样场景后优化），用 regret 界和优化可行性分析二者在光滑性、维度负担与凸性保持上的取舍，并以合成实验与真实案例说明其随数据量和问题结构变化。

**相关性简评：** 其「预测期望目标 vs 生成条件场景」的统计—优化权衡，或可类比 LVR 中直接预测答案的 latent 与建模分布/多样假设的 latent 之间的选择，光滑性与维度代价的分析可供核查是否会迁移到推理状态设计。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Contextual Decision Making: Predictive or Generative

**abstract/A1：** Contextual decision making uses observed covariates to guide decisions under uncertainty. Two natural approaches are predict-then-optimize (PtO), which learns the conditional expected objective, and generate-then-optimize (GtO), which learns conditional uncertainty and optimizes using generated scenarios. We study when each approach is preferable by examining how the choice of learning target affects statistical accuracy and optimization tractability. Although PtO learns a narrower target, our regret bounds show how differences in smoothness can offset GtO's additional dimensional burden. Exploiting smoothness also raises an optimization tradeoff: within the class of nonnegative-weight PtO methods studied, a lower bound limits the gains from additional smoothness, while more flexible methods may introduce signed weights and lose the guarantee of preserving convexity. GtO preserves scenario-wise convexity, but requires learning a richer object and generating scenarios for optimization. Synthetic experiments and a real case study illustrate how these tradeoffs vary with data availability and problem structure. Together with the scope for reusing a model across tasks, our findings provide practical guidance for choosing between the approaches without identifying a universal winner.

**comment/C1：** 63 pages

</details>

### 178. [Earth embeddings are decomposable records of the land surface](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32617v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32617

**作者：** Robin Young、Srinivasan Keshav

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.CV、q-bio.PE

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32617v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32617v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文探究「地球嵌入」（把一年卫星观测压缩为每个十米像素的向量）的实际含义，通过分解两个广泛使用的嵌入产品，并用六大洲的独立事件参考数据验证其包含火灾、森林砍伐、作物类型等可分离的生态特征。

**相关性简评：** 把冻结嵌入拆解为可命名的概念并以外部参考数据核验，这一思路可能对考察 LVR 内部状态是否保留任务必要视觉细节、以及如何评价 latent 内容提供借鉴；但研究对象是遥感表征而非多模态推理，联系较远。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Earth embeddings are decomposable records of the land surface

**abstract/A1：** Ecological monitoring, carbon accounting and biodiversity assessment increasingly rely on annual Earth embeddings--learned vectors that compress a year of satellite observation for every ten-meter pixel on Earth, consumed in place of the imagery itself. What these vectors mean has not been established. Here we decompose two widely used embedding products and show that they contain individual ecological features, including fire, forest clearing and crop type, validated against independent reference data on events across six continents. The recovered concepts date abrupt canopy loss to the first post-disturbance growing season and resolve severity well enough to separate prescribed fire from wildfire. Small fires that recover quickly are detectable in one product but not the other, which instead separates crops more strongly.

</details>

### 179. [What Transfers Across Protein Pockets? Experience Reuse in Molecular Optimization for Agentic Structure-Based Drug Design](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32620v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32620

**作者：** Zaifei Yang、Weiyu Chen、Yaqing Wang、James Kwok

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、q-bio.BM

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32620v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32620v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文分析分子优化智能体在蛋白口袋中逐步编辑配体时，被丢弃的探测经验能否存入经验库，并以「键」（接触位点、动作、轮次）检索迁移到未见口袋，检验这些键能否区分使结果变好或变差的编辑。

**相关性简评：** 其「付出额外探针成本—丢弃—按键跨实例复用」的结构与 LVR 中视觉证据探查的复用、相似度键及长度/实例泛化问题存在概念对应；负结果指出迁移信号主要描述编辑本身而非目标，可供核查 LVR 中类似键或相似度是否同样捕获捷径特征而非内容。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** What Transfers Across Protein Pockets? Experience Reuse in Molecular Optimization for Agentic Structure-Based Drug Design

**abstract/A1：** LLM agents optimize a ligand in a protein pocket by editing it step by step and docking each edit. Recent agents first spend extra dockings probing the complex with controlled edits; guidance induced from these probes is reported to help more than the chemical knowledge the model already carries. The probes are then discarded with the instance that produced them, so the obvious next step is to keep them in an experience base, letting a new pocket inherit what earlier ones already paid for. Such a base is read through a \\emph{key}: the record properties under which past edits are pooled and a new one is looked up. Using the full log of one such run over the CrossDocked2020 test-set pockets, we ask whether its keys recur on a pocket the base has never seen and still separate improving from worsening edits. \\crchange{Using} the contacts at the site being edited, the action, and the round as \\crchange{a} key, the base separates them sharply. Within our analysis, this separation is largely explained by how much the edit changed the molecule's size, the action, and the round, none of which describes the target. That is not because the target is irrelevant: naming it outright separates them several times more sharply. For a pocket absent from the base, transfer must rely on a description rather than its identity. Among the exact-match descriptions we evaluate, the more specific ones rarely recur, whereas the coarser ones provide little separation; the signals that do transfer primarily describe the edit and the molecule it produced rather than the target. Under the representations and lookup scheme studied here, an experience base may be more useful for prioritizing an agent's own proposals for docking than for directly prescribing what to build.

</details>

<a id="paper-180"></a>

### 180. [LGMT: A Tool for Testing the Logical Reasoning Reliability of LLMs](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32627v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32627

**作者：** Zenghui Zhou、Xiaoke Fang、Zheng Zheng

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32627v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32627v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** LGMT 是一个开源工具，利用基于一阶逻辑的蜕变关系，把前提-结论形式的问题改写成逻辑等价的新用例，在无需真值标签的情况下比较模型的蕴含判断是否一致，并提供内置与可经 Z3 校验的自定义关系；在六个 LLM 共 3,091 个蜕变组上测得不一致率约 20.84%–29.44%。

**相关性简评：** 「等价改写下的推理一致性」这一无需标注的检验框架，可能可供核查 LVR 中 latent 状态是否保留任务必要的逻辑信息；但该工作纯文本、不涉及视觉状态或生成机制，联系较远，需较多核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** LGMT: A Tool for Testing the Logical Reasoning Reliability of LLMs

**abstract/A1：** Large Language Models (LLMs) increasingly serve as reasoning components in modern software systems, yet a model that answers a logical query correctly can contradict itself when the same problem is restated in an equivalent form. Static benchmarks evaluate queries in isolation and therefore fail to expose such inconsistencies. To address this limitation, we present LGMT, an open-source tool that detects reasoning inconsistencies through metamorphic relations grounded in first-order logic. Given a source test case in premise-conclusion form, LGMT generates logically equivalent follow-up cases and checks whether the model's entailment predictions agree across the pair, without ground-truth labels. The tool provides 20 built-in metamorphic relations, supports custom ones verified by the Z3 theorem prover, and runs from a CLI or a web studio, including an offline mock mode requiring no API key. Across six widely used LLMs on a bundled benchmark of 3,091 metamorphic groups, LGMT reveals inconsistency rates ranging from 20.84% to 29.44%. LGMT is publicly available on GitHub at https://github.com/MaxwelsDonc/lgmt-tool, with a demonstration video at https://youtu.be/gou4opptu1U.

</details>

<a id="paper-181"></a>

### 181. [WL-SeqVAE: Sequence-Based Graph Generation Using Variational Autoencoders and Weisfeiler-Leman Colors](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32650v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32650

**作者：** Iakovos Evdaimon、Giannis Nikolentzos、Achilleas Tsimichodimos、Christos Xypolopoulos、Michalis Vazirgiannis

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32650v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32650v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** WL-SeqVAE 将图映射为 Weisfeiler-Leman 颜色序列，用变分自编码器把该序列编码为高斯潜分布，再由自回归解码器重建图，并以 WL 颜色导出的兼容性约束指导节点与边的预测，从而把图生成转为序列生成。

**相关性简评：** 其「离散结构摘要 token + 全局潜变量 + 约束式自回归解码」的组合，可能为 LVR 中把视觉实体关系压缩为离散结构状态、并在解码时保持结构一致性提供概念线索，具体可迁移性有待核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** WL-SeqVAE: Sequence-Based Graph Generation Using Variational Autoencoders and Weisfeiler-Leman Colors

**abstract/A1：** In this paper, we introduce WL-SeqVAE, a sequence-based variational autoencoder for generating graphs. The key observation behind WL-SeqVAE is that node colors induced by the Weisfeiler-Leman (WL) algorithm provide compact discrete summaries of local graph structure and can therefore serve as structural tokens for generation. Rather than learning a distribution over adjacency matrices directly, our method maps each graph to a sequence of WL colors and employs a variational autoencoder that encodes this sequence into a Gaussian latent distribution, from which an autoregressive decoder reconstructs the graph. During generation, compatibility constraints derived from the WL colors guide node and edge predictions, combining global information from the latent representation with local structural consistency. This formulation turns graph generation into a sequence generation problem and avoids directly parameterizing a quadratic adjacency matrix. We evaluate WL-SeqVAE on molecular and general graph generation tasks. Across the evaluated datasets, WL-SeqVAE is competitive with established baselines on several local structural and molecular quality metrics. Code is available at https://github.com/iakovosevdaimon/WL-SeqVAE

</details>

<a id="paper-182"></a>

### 182. [WiMoT: Replay-Free Continual Wi-Fi Sensing via Moment Transport](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32660v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32660

**作者：** Chunchun Yang、Yang Cao、Zhitang Chen

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32660v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32660v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 WiMoT，在无回放的持续 Wi-Fi 感知中仅保存各历史域的特征均值与协方差，并用仿射映射将旧统计随之迁移，以闭式损失保持旧类可分。

**相关性简评：** 其「冻结特征基上的仿射变化 + 统计量迁移」可能为 LVR 中表示漂移下的 latent 统计、缓存与跨阶段状态复用提供可核查线索，但模态与任务距主线较远。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** WiMoT: Replay-Free Continual Wi-Fi Sensing via Moment Transport

**abstract/A1：** Continual Wi-Fi sensing must balance plasticity, learning new rooms or users, against stability, keeping earlier ones, yet nodes often cannot retain channel state information (CSI) for privacy and storage reasons. We propose WiMoT (Wi-Fi Moment Transport), which stores the per-class feature mean and covariance of every earlier domain instead of samples. When the representation changes, stored statistics become stale. WiMoT restricts the change to an affine map on a frozen feature basis and applies the same map to the statistics, so they stay valid without old samples. Supervised contrastive learning learns the new domain, a closed-form loss on the stored statistics keeps earlier classes separable, and a Gaussian discriminant classifier, refitted from the transported statistics with equal weight per domain, predicts without domain identity. On 3DO, WiMANS, and Widar3.0, WiMoT's average final accuracies are 94.02%, 89.61%, and 95.16% with at most 0.58 percentage points (pp) of forgetting. On 3DO and Widar3.0, it also has the highest balance of new- and earlier-domain accuracy and is 3.77 and 13.11 pp ahead of the strongest of 21 baselines, which uses replay, staying ahead across domain orders and label budgets. On WiMANS, it matches the strongest replay baseline in accuracy (+0.41 pp) while reducing forgetting from 2.78 to 0.00 pp, and has the highest six-order average accuracy. Because full covariances dominate the memory, storing them as rank-32 factors shrinks the moment memory to 6.4%, losing at most 0.49 pp of accuracy, within edge-node storage budgets.

**comment/C1：** 13 pages, 6 figures, 9 tables. Submitted to IEEE Internet of Things Journal. Code: https://github.com/Newsteinwell/WiMoT

</details>

<a id="paper-183"></a>

### 183. [SemGeo-Gen: Unsupervised Generation of Approximate Cross-Instance Semantic-Geometric Correspondences](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32663v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32663

**作者：** Roy Amoyal、Shira Ifergane、Oren Freifeld

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32663v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32663v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** SemGeo-Gen 提出一种无监督方法，从仅做粗类别级旋转对齐的 3D 物体集合中生成近似跨实例语义-几何对应：把多视角 DINOv2 特征提升到 3D，用连续分段仿射配准对齐实例，再以语义一致性剪枝候选匹配，从而无需人工标注地产出 3D-3D、3D-2D、2D-2D 监督，并用其预训练提升真实图像对应模型的性能。

**相关性简评：** 该文不涉及 VLM 推理或 latent 状态生成；但其「特征提升到 3D 后用语义一致性剪枝筛选候选匹配」的思路，或可作为视觉证据筛选与几何一致表示的核查线索，与 LVR 主线的联系较远。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** SemGeo-Gen: Unsupervised Generation of Approximate Cross-Instance Semantic-Geometric Correspondences

**abstract/A1：** We introduce SemGeo-Gen, an unsupervised method for generating approximate cross-instance semantic-geometric supervision from weakly aligned 3D object collections. Our goal is not to recover exact point-level correspondences, which are often ambiguous across different instances and would require expensive, impractical manual supervision at scale. Instead, we automatically produce approximate correspondences that are semantically meaningful, geometrically consistent, and diverse enough to train modern correspondence models. Given only coarse category-level rotation alignment, SemGeo-Gen lifts multi-view DINOv2 features to 3D, aligns object instances using continuous piecewise-affine registration, and prunes candidate matches using semantic consistency. The resulting 3D correspondences can be projected into rendered views, yielding scalable 3D-3D, 3D-2D, and 2D-2D supervision without manual annotation. We validate the generated correspondences against sparse human annotations from KeypointNet, obtaining 93% PCK@0.10 and 97% PCK@0.15. More importantly, we show that using the generated correspondences for synthetic pretraining improves recent state-of-the-art semantic correspondence models on SPair-71k after fine-tuning on real data, with gains of up to 5 PCK@0.10 points. Together, these results indicate that approximate semantic-geometric supervision generated from 3D assets can improve real-image correspondence learning and serve as a scalable alternative to costly manual annotation. Project page: https://bgu-cs-vil.github.io/SemGeo-Gen/.

**comment/C1：** Accepted to NeurIPS 2026

</details>

### 184. [SP-Guide: Enhancing Aerial Vision-Language Navigation with Spatial Priors](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32697v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32697

**作者：** Haoxiang Tang、Dong Zhou、Shen Tan、Minxuan Jiang、Xitong Li

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32697v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32697v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 SP-Guide，一个基于 transformer 的空中视觉语言导航目标预测框架，用空间先验跨模态编码器、指令增强解码器和目标收敛过滤器在 CityNav 上提升成功率，且未见环境增益最大。

**相关性简评：** 论文把物理空间先验作为跨模态几何对齐的归纳偏置，可能与「视觉几何与任务必要信息是否保留在内部状态」这一问题相关，末端目标收敛过滤也可供核查其对停止/终止决策的启示；但整体是导航任务框架，不含显式 latent 推理轨迹，迁移价值需进一步核实。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** SP-Guide: Enhancing Aerial Vision-Language Navigation with Spatial Priors

**abstract/A1：** Aerial vision-language navigation (AVLN) requires a UAV to reach a language-described target from partial egocentric observations while reasoning over large outdoor scenes. Existing approaches typically fuse view-centered RGB observations and globally anchored history maps as generic visual embeddings, leaving cross-modal spatial alignment to be learned from sparse navigation supervision. We present SP-Guide, a transformer- based goal-prediction framework that addresses this weakness of alignment through a Spatial Prior Cross-Modal Encoder, an Instruction Enhanced Decoder, and a Goal Convergence Filter for terminal target refinement. Experiments implemented on the CityNav benchmark show that SP-Guide improves success rate over the representative baselines, with the largest gains on unseen environments. These results indicate that physically grounded spatial priors provide an effective inductive bias for robust AVLN.

</details>

<a id="paper-185"></a>

### 185. [Internal Discrepancy for Contrastive Learning: Generalization Bounds and Downstream Behaviors](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32729v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32729

**作者：** Jialiang Wang、Qing Ma、Xiong Zhou、Gangfeng Hu、Haoliang Li、Xianming Liu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32729v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32729v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出「internal discrepancy」来度量正样本平均表示与潜在簇中心之间的差异，据此在无额外假设下证明对比学习后下游监督任务的泛化界，并用 relaxed k-means 损失近似降低该差异，还分析了下游监督损失的行为及类别不平衡下的作用。

**相关性简评：** 其潜在簇中心与表示几何的泛化界分析，可能为刻画 LVR 中 latent 推理状态的结构化程度与下游收益关系提供度量视角，但迁移到视觉推理状态是否成立仍需核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Internal Discrepancy for Contrastive Learning: Generalization Bounds and Downstream Behaviors

**abstract/A1：** Contrastive learning has emerged as a popular paradigm for training representation models. While prior studies pursue more practical and less restrictive assumptions to enhance theoretical understanding of contrastive learning, they essentially describe the interconnections among representations within the data or induced by the function class. To encapsulate these assumptions, we propose the measure of internal discrepancy that quantifies the difference between the mean representation of positive samples and latent cluster centers. We then prove an internal discrepancy-aware generalization bound for downstream supervised tasks following contrastive learning, without requiring additional assumptions. This generalization bound not only serves as a unifying framework connecting various assumptions but also motivates us to further reduce the internal discrepancy, thereby introducing the relaxed k-means loss as an approximation. Moreover, we investigate the downstream behaviors of contrastive learning from the perspective of contrastive learning-incurred supervised losses. In the presence of class imbalance, we shed light on why contrastive learning can facilitate imbalanced learning. Finally, we investigate the optimality of these incurred supervised losses, proving several intriguing properties.

</details>

<a id="paper-186"></a>

### 186. [Strongly Adaptive Online Learning with Time-Varying Movement Costs](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32740v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32740

**作者：** Andrew Jacobsen、Emmanuel Esposito、Hao Qiu、Mengxiao Zhang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、stat.ML

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32740v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32740v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 研究带时变移动代价的在线凸优化中的强自适应遗憾，通过归约到带移动代价的 sleeping experts 给出依赖路径长度与区间梯度的界，并扩展到延迟反馈与时变记忆。

**相关性简评：** 其「切换/更新需付费」与任意子区间自适应遗憾的形式化，可能为 LVR 中思考步数、状态更新与 THINK/LOOK/STOP 切换的代价权衡提供可核查的分析视角，但无视觉或 latent 状态内容，联系较远。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Strongly Adaptive Online Learning with Time-Varying Movement Costs

**abstract/A1：** We study strongly adaptive regret for Online Convex Optimization (OCO) with time-varying movement costs, a setting that evaluates a learner's performance over arbitrary sub-intervals. This framework captures complex sequential decision-making problems where both the environment and the cost of switching decisions fluctuate over time. We propose a novel algorithm which, for any interval $\[a,b\] \\subseteq \[T\]$, achieves a data-dependent regret bound of $ \\widetilde{\\mathcal{O}}\\left( \\sqrt{(D^2+DP\_{\[a,b\]})\\sum\_{t \\in \[a,b\]}(\\\|g\_t\\\|^2+λ\_t\\\|g\_t\\\|)} \\right) $, where $D$ is the diameter of the decision domain, $P\_{\[a,b\]}$ is the path length of the comparator sequence over $\[a,b\]$, $g\_t$ is the gradient of the loss, and $λ\_t$ is the movement-cost coefficient at round $t$. Key to our approach is a novel reduction to sleeping experts with movement costs, a previously unexplored variant that is of independent interest. As a result of this development, we further establish the first strongly-adaptive guarantees under delayed feedback. Our approach also naturally extends to OCO with time-varying memory, where we obtain strongly-adaptive guarantees which improve the best-known bounds.

</details>

<a id="paper-187"></a>

### 187. [On the Identifiability of Hybrid Deep Generative Models: Meta-Learning as a Solution](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32748v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32748

**作者：** Yubo Ye、Maryam Toloubidokhti、Sumeet Vadhavkar、Xiajun Jiang、Huafeng Liu、Linwei Wang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32748v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32748v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文首次从理论上考察将物理表达式与神经生成器结合的 hybrid 深度生成模型的可辨识性，并提出以 meta-learning 构造可辨识 hybrid-DGM 的方案，在合成与真实数据基准上给出经验证据。

**相关性简评：** 其关于 latent 参数可辨识性及「条件化元学习」使潜在量可唯一确定的论证，可能为诊断 LVR 内部状态是否稳定携带任务必要信息、以及缓解视觉信息坍缩提供概念与评价视角，但原文不涉及视觉或多模态推理，需核查可迁移性。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** On the Identifiability of Hybrid Deep Generative Models: Meta-Learning as a Solution

**abstract/A1：** The interest in leveraging physics-based inductive bias in deep learning has resulted in recent development of hybrid deep generative models (hybrid-DGMs) that integrate known physics-based mathematical expressions in neural generative models. To identify these hybrid-DGMs requires inferring parameters of the physics-based component along with their neural component. The identifiability of these hybrid-DGMs, however, has not yet been theoretically probed or established. How does the existing theory of the un-identifiability of general DGMs apply to hybrid-DGMs? What may be an effective approach to construct a hybrid-DGM with theoretically-proven identifiability? This paper provides the first theoretical probe into the identifiability of hybrid-DGMs, and presents meta-learning as a novel solution to construct identifiable hybrid-DGMs. On synthetic and real-data benchmarks, we provide strong empirical evidence for the un-identifiability of existing hybrid-DGMs using unconditional priors, and strong identifiability results of the presented meta-formulations of hybrid-DGMs. Code is available at https://github.com/GoForIt-YYB/Meta-Hybrid-VAE.

**comment/C1：** NeurIPS 2024. 16 pages, 5 figures

</details>

### 188. [CARES: Coverage-Aware Replay Exemplar Selection for Continual Learning](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32799v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32799

**作者：** Arundhati S. Shanbhag、Brian B. Moser、Federico Raue、Tobias C. Nauen、Andreas Dengel

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32799v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32799v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 CARES，一种免去持续模型优化的回放缓冲区构建方法：利用冻结预训练特征空间的类感知、密度加权覆盖度挑选样本，并在平衡、不平衡与标签噪声的在线增量流上评测速度与精度。

**相关性简评：** 与 LVR 主线无直接关联，但「固定预算下用冻结特征几何做覆盖度驱动的样本筛选」这一训练无关准则，可能为视觉证据筛选与缓存组成提供可借鉴线索，值得核查其迁移可行性。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** CARES: Coverage-Aware Replay Exemplar Selection for Continual Learning

**abstract/A1：** Replay-based Continual Learning mitigates catastrophic forgetting by keeping a buffer of past samples, making buffer composition critical under a fixed memory budget. Recent coreset-based replay methods improve buffer quality using model-derived gradients, losses, or auxiliary optimization, but incur substantial computational overhead as memory is repeatedly updated. We propose CARES, a continual model-optimization-free approach for buffer construction that instead exploits the geometry of a frozen pretrained feature space using class-aware, density-weighted coverage. We evaluate CARES across balanced, imbalanced, and label-noisy streams under online class- and task-incremental settings. On Tiny-ImageNet, CARES is 2.3 to 8.8 times faster than existing coreset-based selectors. Although CARES achieves this speedup using no training signal from the continual learner, it ranks among the state of the art across balanced, imbalanced, and label-noisy streams under online class- and task-incremental settings, outperforming the strongest baseline by up to 7.58 percentage points under class imbalance while also attaining higher average accuracy on Tiny-ImageNet. Code will be released upon publication

**comment/C1：** 17 pages, 2 figures. Includes supplementary material

</details>

### 189. [CompMVR: Compression-Aware Multi-View Restoration Using Diffusion Models for Geometrically Consistent 3D Reconstruction](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32800v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32800

**作者：** Dong-hwi Kim、Chaewon Moon、Hojun Song、Dongbeom Kim、Junyeong Jang、Aro Kim、Gahyeon Kim、Heejung Choi、Jehee Kim、Gianella Cravioto、Sohyun Lee、Gyeongjin Choi、EunHye Jeong、Soo Ye Kim、Jaehyup Lee、Sang-hyo Park

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32800v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32800v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文指出有损压缩会造成跨视图几何不一致这一失败模式，提出压缩感知的多视角扩散修复框架 CompMVR，在恢复视觉细节的同时借助跨视图对应关系保持一致性，并构建压缩数据集评估修复质量与相机位姿估计、新视角合成等下游 3D 任务。

**相关性简评：** 与 LVR 推理主线无直接关系，但其多视图一致性约束下的扩散修复可能为多路视觉证据进入 latent 状态时如何保持几何与实体一致性提供线索，可供核查其一致性机制是否可迁移。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** CompMVR: Compression-Aware Multi-View Restoration Using Diffusion Models for Geometrically Consistent 3D Reconstruction

**abstract/A1：** Real-world 3D reconstruction pipelines often rely on multi-view images degraded by lossy compression. While compression artifacts have been extensively studied in single-image or video restoration, their impact on multi-view geometry remains largely overlooked. In this work, we identify compression-induced cross-view geometric inconsistency as a critical failure mode, where view-dependent artifacts disrupt feature matching, dense correspondence, camera parameter estimation, and downstream 3D reconstruction tasks such as novel view synthesis. To address this problem, we propose CompMVR, a compression-aware multi-view diffusion restoration framework that jointly refines compressed views while preserving cross-view consistency, and construct compressed datasets for systematic evaluation. Unlike conventional restoration methods that optimize each image independently for 2D perceptual quality, CompMVR leverages learned compression priors and cross-view correspondence to recover visual details while improving geometric reliability across views. Experiments across diverse datasets, codecs, and compression levels demonstrate gains in restoration quality and downstream 3D tasks, including novel-view synthesis, camera pose estimation, and view matching. These results establish compressed multi-view restoration as a distinct problem and highlight its importance for 3D reconstruction under lossy compression. Code and data for this paper are available at https://mchaewon.github.io/CompMVR-project-page/.

**comment/C1：** Accepted to SIGGRAPH Asia 2026 (Technical paper)

</details>
