### 190. [On the Limits of Sparse Linear Model with Shuffled Differential Privacy](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32822v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32822

**作者：** Yan Zhang、Jiong Tuo、Meng Ding、Yuheng Ma、Di Wang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32822v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32822v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 研究 shuffled differential privacy 下稀疏线性回归的可达误差极限：给出非交互协议下界与匹配算法，并提出两轮交互算法（先用 heavy hitter 恢复支撑集、再估计参数），证明其在用户级 DP 下近最优。

**相关性简评：** 「先筛出稀疏支撑、再在有限信息预算下估计」的两阶段交互结构与误差下界分析，可能为 LVR 中视觉证据筛选及计算/信息预算分配提供类比，但属远领域线索，需核查其可迁移性。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** On the Limits of Sparse Linear Model with Shuffled Differential Privacy

**abstract/A1：** Sparse linear regression is a fundamental problem in high-dimensional statistics, and its private counterparts leave a large gap between the central and the local model of differential privacy. Shuffled differential privacy (SDP) sits between the two, offering accuracy closer to the central model without the trust requirement of a central curator. This paper studies sparse linear regression under both non-interactive and interactive SDP protocols. In the non-interactive setting, any non-interactive $(ε,δ)$-SDP protocol is shown to incur an $\\ell\_2$-norm estimation error of $Ω( \\frac{\\sqrt{dk}}{nε} )$ with dimension $d$, sparsity $k$, and $n$ users, revealing an inherent dimensionality barrier. In addition, a non-interactive algorithm is proposed that achieves an $\\ell\_2$-norm upper bound of $\\tilde{\\mathcal{O}}( \\sqrt{\\frac{k}{n}} + \\frac{d\\sqrt{k}}{nε})$. To overcome this barrier, we turn to the interactive user-level setting, where each user holds $m$ samples. A two-round interactive algorithm is developed that first recovers the support through a heavy hitter step and then estimates the parameter, achieving an error of $\\tilde{\\mathcal{O}}( \\sqrt{\\frac{k}{nm}} + \\frac{k}{n\\sqrt{m}ε} )$ with no polynomial dependence on $d$. Furthermore, by extending the score attack framework to user-level data, a new lower bound $Ω( \\sqrt{\\frac{k\\log(d/k)}{nm}} + \\frac{k\\log(d/k)}{n\\sqrt{m}ε} )$ is established for any user-level $(ε,δ)$-DP, showing that the proposed algorithm is near-optimal with only a gap of the factor $\\log(d/k)$.

**comment/C1：** 39 pages, 1 figure. Under review

</details>

<a id="paper-191"></a>

### 191. [Neural LoFi with Backward Coupling: A Spectral Theory of Cross-Layer Feature Learning](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32849v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32849

**作者：** Yatin Dandi、Matteo Vilucchio、Hugo Tabanelli、Florent Krzakala

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cond-mat.dis-nn、stat.ML

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32849v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32849v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文将层次式谱代理 Neural LoFi 扩展到联合训练，从精确反向传播梯度导出神经元入边权重与上一层列的局部耦合，说明小初始化下动态会放大跨层相关算子的奇异模式，并给出三层网络的自洽连续时间动力学、特征恢复分离结论及 CIFAR-10 二分类实验。

**相关性简评：** 其「跨层耦合」谱动力学与样本相关有效标签的刻画，可能为分析循环/迭代式 latent 推理中后层反馈如何重塑前层视觉特征、以及跨层贡献分配提供理论视角，但该文属纯监督特征学习理论，迁移可行性需核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Neural LoFi with Backward Coupling: A Spectral Theory of Cross-Layer Feature Learning

**abstract/A1：** During joint training, backpropagation allows later layers to reshape earlier representations, a feedback mechanism absent from purely layer-wise learning. We extend Neural Low-Degree Filtering (Neural LoFi), a recently proposed layer-wise spectral surrogate for feature learning, to capture this interaction. Starting from the exact backpropagation gradients, we derive a local coupling between a neuron's incoming weights and its corresponding next-layer column. In a controlled small-initialization regime with smooth activations and fixed surroundings, these dynamics amplify singular modes of a cross-layer correlation operator. Each growing mode pairs an earlier feature with an upper-layer response through shared neuron coefficients. This motivates a backward-coupling algorithm in which upper-layer sensitivities generate sample-dependent effective labels for earlier spectral steps, while corrected features refine upward transmission. A function-space variational principle characterizes the selected feature-response pairs by maximizing the label correlation of their product under kernel-norm constraints. For three-layer networks, we derive self-consistent continuous-time dynamics for the filtered hidden-layer features, the activation kernels, and the network output under the proposed backward coupling. In a hierarchical multi-index model, we establish a feature-recovery separation: backward coupling recovers lower-layer directions invisible to the original forward label-weighted statistic. The linear specialization recovers residual-dependent singular-mode dynamics, while paired modes provide a dynamical mechanism for linked low-rank covariance components consistent with the rainbow-network picture. Experiments with fully connected and convolutional networks on binary CIFAR-10 show improved accuracy over forward Neural LoFi at matched widths.

</details>

<a id="paper-192"></a>

### 192. [Reimagining Enterprise RAG for Multi-Agent Systems: A Knowledge Layer That Organizes Before It Retrieves](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32851v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32851

**作者：** Garima Agrawal、Prasun Das、Akshay Sarvade、Priyanka L、Minisha N、Sravani Joshna、Hemath Manivannan、Lavanya Korada、Sambhrit Pattanayak、Srashti Gupta、Siddu R、Prasad Kalyanasundaram

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32851v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32851v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文面向企业软件开发生命周期的多智能体系统，提出一个「先组织再检索」的知识层：用自描述目录、稀疏元数据注册表和基于 artifact（而非抽取实体）的导航超图，把查询先解析为 artifact 标识，再用其约束向量检索。

**相关性简评：** 其「先用来源/服务等元数据把候选范围结构化收窄、再让相似度排序」的做法，可能可供核查 LVR 中视觉证据筛选的问题，即如何避免高相似但任务无关的证据被统计排序选中；但本文为纯文本企业 RAG，与多模态推理状态无直接关系，迁移性需较多核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Reimagining Enterprise RAG for Multi-Agent Systems: A Knowledge Layer That Organizes Before It Retrieves

**abstract/A1：** Enterprises are deploying agentic AI across the software development lifecycle, and they need the behavioral logic of the systems they act on, not stakeholder documentation. A seven-agent reverse-engineering pipeline recovered this logic, processing 241 repositories across 18 services into thousands of artifacts describing scope, journeys, rules and acceptance criteria. Recovery does not confer usability. Entities like offer, eligibility, and activation recur across services under a shared vocabulary, related in name but operationally distinct, so one end-to-end journey implicates over a thousand near-identical rules that must not be conflated. Flat vector retrieval fails, returning an identical rule from the wrong service at high similarity with no signal of error. Content-level ontology, the traditional remedy, did not exist, and relating every occurrence of recurring entities would have cost more than the engagement and not survived a release cycle. We place the ontology at the artifact level. In this machine-generated corpus, each artifact retains its producer, component, service, domain and type, provenance that disambiguates before content is read. We present a knowledge layer that organizes before retrieving: a self-describing catalog, a sparse metadata registry, and a navigation hypergraph defined over artifacts rather than extracted entities. Queries resolve to artifact identifiers that constrain vector search, so the competing rule from another service is absent rather than outranked, making disambiguation structural, not statistical. Built in under a week and admitting new services by convention, the layer serves SDLC agents, developers, quality engineers and analysts. It supported root-cause analysis and resolution of 100+ production defects with developer verification and attributed 35 release regression failures to specific rules and services.

</details>

<a id="paper-193"></a>

### 193. [Regularised RL improves sim-to-real transfer](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32855v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32855

**作者：** Danilo Vucetic、Mohamad H. Danesh、Florence Clerc、Hsiu-Chin Lin、Jonathan Tremblay

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.RO

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32855v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32855v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文考察常用强化学习算法在 sim-to-real 迁移中的表现，并提出基于 soft mellowmax 值函数的正则化算法 SMM-AC，在 MuJoCo 与真机上比较回报及对观测、环境与动力学扰动的鲁棒性。

**相关性简评：** 其「正则化带来对观测扰动与分布偏移的鲁棒性保证」这一思路，可能可供核查 LVR 中视觉证据受扰时 latent 状态稳定性与训练约束的问题，但与原论文主线相距较远、联系需人工判断。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Regularised RL improves sim-to-real transfer

**abstract/A1：** Regularised reinforcement learning (RL) has provable robustness guarantees that should ease the transfer of policies from simulation to robots, known as sim-to-real. Yet, weakly regularised algorithms like Proximal Policy Optimisation (PPO) remain the de facto choice in sim-to-real pipelines, while the practical benefits of regularisation for transfer remain poorly characterised. In this work, we study the sim-to-real performance of commonly used RL algorithms, with the aim of characterising the effects of regularisation on robotic performance. To that end, we derive Soft MellowMax Actor-Critic (SMM-AC), a novel regularised RL algorithm based on the soft mellowmax value function, which carries strong robustness guarantees. On MuJoCo continuous control benchmarks, SMM-AC is competitive with baselines like Soft Actor-Critic (SAC). On real robots, SMM-AC achieves higher return, while exhibiting greater robustness to perturbations in observations, the environment, and dynamics than baselines. Together, these results demonstrate that regularised RL improves real-world policy deployment through better robustness to the sim-to-real gap, and changing real-world conditions.

**comment/C1：** 9 pages of main-body content, with supporting details in the appendices. Under submission

</details>

### 194. [A Foundation Model for Policy In-Context Longitudinal Causal Effect Estimation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32863v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32863

**作者：** Wenxin Chen、Kyra Gan、Mingyu Lu、Zilin Jing、Fei Wang、Hoifung Poon、Tristan Naumann、Peniel Argaw

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32863v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32863v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 将 prior-data fitted networks 用于纵向因果效应估计，用 in-context 的历史—决策示范表示动态治疗策略，在合成纵向结构因果模型上预训练，新数据集上单次前向即预测条件平均潜在结果。

**相关性简评：** 与视觉无关，但其「用示范把策略编码进上下文、一次前向完成摊销推断并给出校准不确定性」可能为 LVR 中任务/证据条件下的潜在状态表示与停止决策提供概念线索，需核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** A Foundation Model for Policy In-Context Longitudinal Causal Effect Estimation

**abstract/A1：** Longitudinal causal effect estimation is complicated by time-varying confounding and treatment-confounder feedback loops. Existing methods generally require data-specific nuisance-model fitting, and many are tailored to prespecified treatment sequences or prediction horizons, leading to time-consuming computation at deployment. We introduce \*Policy In-Context Longitudinal causal Effect estimation with Prior-data Fitted Networks\* (\*\*PICLE-PFN\*\*), the first foundation model for longitudinal causal effect estimation that supports \*dynamic\* treatment policies. PICLE-PFN represents target policies through in-context history-decision demonstrations, providing a unified interface for a broad class of treatment policies. Pretrained over synthetic data sampled from longitudinal structural causal models, PICLE-PFN amortizes causal effect estimation across datasets, policies, and horizons. Given a new observational dataset and target policy, it directly predicts conditional average potential outcomes (CAPOs) in one forward pass without task-specific training or hyperparameter tuning. Across synthetic and semi-synthetic longitudinal benchmarks, PICLE-PFN achieves competitive RMSE and substantially improved uncertainty calibration with a fraction of deployment runtime, compared to state-of-the-art conventional estimators trained and tuned separately for each dataset.

</details>

### 195. [From Deliberately Constructed Systemic Representations of the World of Activity to Dialogue: A Five-Level Communication Architecture for Strong Language-Based ASI](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32869v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32869

**作者：** Maxim Osovsky

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32869v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32869v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出面向强语言 ASI 的五层沟通架构，区分活动世界的系统表征、从中切出的情境、情境的叙述描述、讯息的完整语义与文本，并主张语言模型上下文本身无法呈现话语建构的基础。

**相关性简评：** 其「情境图式模型不等同于关于它的故事」以及由缩写文本重建完整语义的论述，可能为 LVR 中隐状态与显式推理文本的分工、内部表征是否承载证据基础提供概念线索，但属纯理论框架，可操作化程度待核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** From Deliberately Constructed Systemic Representations of the World of Activity to Dialogue: A Five-Level Communication Architecture for Strong Language-Based ASI

**abstract/A1：** This paper proposes a five-level organization of message formation and understanding within thinking and activity in which ASI participates. Its central thesis is that a language model's context is fundamentally incapable of presenting the foundations of utterance formation for examination and verification. These foundations are established in advance by acquired schemas, systemic representations of the world of activity, and connections accepted as true within them. Neither context nor trained LLM weights, in themselves, attest to the presence of deliberately constructed systemic representations. The five levels distinguish systemic representations of the world of activity, a particular situation cut out from those representations, its narrative description, the full sense of a message, and text. A situation's schema-model is not identical to a story about it. A recipient reconstructs the full sense from an abbreviated text through jointly constructed and agreed-upon representations and joint activity. Reverse work proceeds from the full sense through an extended description and the situation's schema-model to the reconstruction of systemic foundations. The theoretical foundations draw on the systems methodology of G. P. Shchedrovitskiy, the structural differential of A. Korzybski, and the Meaning-Text theory of I. A. Mel'chuk. The contribution is a specific systemic-activity organization of content for examining and adjusting message foundations and controlling the consequences of their transformation. Further research concerns large graphical models of activity on reconstructible systemic foundations, distinct from text generation from context.

**comment/C1：** 36 pages, 1 figure

</details>

### 196. [NgramRL: Accelerating RL Post-Training for Structured Generation via Rollout-Aware N-gram Speculative Decoding](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32881v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32881

**作者：** Nilesh Prasad Pandey、Jangseon Park、Jason Kong、Onat Gungor、Tajana Rosing

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32881v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32881v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 NgramRL：用同一 prompt 近期 rollout 历史构建 n-gram 草稿，配合自适应 fan-out 预取与因果预算分配，加速 RL 后训练中的 rollout 生成，并以目标模型验证保持分布。

**相关性简评：** 与视觉推理无直接关系，但其对多个候选续写的验证结果预测与预取预算分配，可能为 LVR 中并行假设生成与验证的成本收益取舍提供可供核查的线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** NgramRL: Accelerating RL Post-Training for Structured Generation via Rollout-Aware N-gram Speculative Decoding

**abstract/A1：** Reinforcement learning (RL) post-training improves the reasoning capabilities of large language models, but its cost is dominated by autoregressive rollout generation, which can exceed 70% of training time. Existing speculative-decoding methods are poorly suited to this setting: model-based drafters consume scarce GPU memory and may become misaligned as the policy evolves, while existing model-free methods do not explicitly exploit repetition across training epochs. We observe that recent rollouts of the same prompt provide a high-quality, self-updating draft source. Based on this insight, we propose NgramRL, a per-prompt n-gram drafter constructed from bounded recent rollout history. NgramRL requires no auxiliary model, drafter training, or persistent GPU-resident drafter state, while target-model verification preserves the rollout distribution. To hide CPU cache lookup and scheduling, we further introduce an adaptive fan-out prefetcher that predicts likely verification outcomes (j, b), consisting of the accepted draft length j and target-emitted continuation token b. A causal budget allocator concentrates prefetches on high-probability outcomes, while uncovered outcomes fall back to sequential lookup without changing committed tokens. Across two model families and three reasoning and structured-generation workloads, NgramRL commits 3.60-7.77 tokens per target-model forward in the warm-cache regime, up to 1.99x more than SuffixDecoding and 7.0x more than prompt-lookup decoding. On Spider, the prefetcher covers 96.6% of Qwen3-8B outcomes at a candidate budget of 16 and improves measured rollout throughput by 1.38x over synchronous NgramRL, approaching its 1.40x overlap ceiling.

</details>

<a id="paper-197"></a>

### 197. [Reinforcement Learning with Multi-Step Transition Predictions: A Bellman Theory](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32887v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32887

**作者：** Chenbei Lu、Zaiwei Chen、Tongxin Li、Chenye Wu、Adam Wierman

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32887v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32887v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文在折扣有限 MDP 的块反馈设定下研究 K 步转移预测的价值：证明最优值满足原状态空间上的贝叶斯 Bellman 方程，用 Bellman–Jensen 分析量化预测时域、预测误差、动作覆盖与块内反馈的影响，并提出 BF–BOLA 算法及匹配的样本复杂度上下界。

**相关性简评：** 其「预测信息通过改变 Bellman 展开中取最大与期望的局部顺序而产生价值」及对时域、误差、覆盖度的量化，可能为 LVR 中多步 latent 预测与获取证据的频率（THINK/LOOK 间隔）的收益–成本分析提供概念线索，但结论建立在 tabular MDP 上，可供核查能否迁移到 VLM 推理。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Reinforcement Learning with Multi-Step Transition Predictions: A Bellman Theory

**abstract/A1：** Multi-step predictions of future transition realizations can improve reinforcement learning, but their value remains difficult to quantify. Directly including these predictions in the state can lead to exponential state-space growth, making direct dynamic programming computationally expensive. We study discounted finite MDPs under a block-feedback protocol: at the start of each K-period block, the decision maker observes $K$-step probabilistic transition predictions for a subset of actions and adapts decisions to observed states within the block. We show that the optimal value satisfies a Bayesian Bellman equation on the original state space. This representation reveals that predictive information creates value by recursively changing the local order of maximization and expectation in the Bellman expansion. We formalize this effect through a Bellman--Jensen analysis and quantify the effects of prediction horizon, error, action coverage, and within-block feedback. We propose block-feedback Bayesian offline learning with online adaptation (BF--BOLA), a model-based algorithm for learning near-optimal policies, and establish upper and lower sample-complexity bounds that match up to logarithmic factors in the stated regimes. The results show that predictions can reduce transition learning to uncovered rows and that learning a near-optimal policy can require fewer prediction samples than estimating the Bayesian value function.

</details>

<a id="paper-198"></a>

### 198. [Closing the Modality Gap via Simplex-Constrained Representations](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32890v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32890

**作者：** Shubham Gupta、Siva Reddy、Perouz Taslakian、Valentina Zantedeschi、Cem Subakan

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32890v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32890v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文将多模态对比模型（CLIP、CLAP）的嵌入域从单位球改为概率单纯形，附加轻量 softmax adapter，并用总变差相似度替代余弦，在五个检索基准上将质心 ℓ2 模态间隔缩小 97–99%，检索性能持平或更优。

**相关性简评：** 该文不涉及 latent 推理或证据使用，但把单纯形的非负、单位质量约束当作跨模态校准先验，并以 TV 相似度替代余弦，可供核查 LVR 中 latent 相似度度量与视觉-语言状态对齐的几何选择。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Closing the Modality Gap via Simplex-Constrained Representations

**abstract/A1：** While multimodal contrastive models like CLIP and CLAP successfully learn shared representations, their embeddings typically exhibit a persistent \\emph{modality gap}. We propose addressing this separation through a purely geometric approach: changing the embedding domain from the unit sphere, $S^{d-1}$, to the probability simplex, $Δ^{d-1}$. Although both manifolds share the same intrinsic dimension, the simplex acts as a structural prior; its nonnegative, unit-mass constraints serve as a cross-modal calibration that forces both modalities to ground themselves in the same coordinate system. By attaching lightweight softmax adapters and using Total Variation similarity, we reduce the centroid $\\ell\_2$ gap by 97--99\\% across five multimodal retrieval benchmarks. This confirms that simplicial geometry induces genuine semantic alignment, matching or exceeding the retrieval performance of standard unconstrained models.

</details>

### 199. [A Tutorial on Physics Constrained Machine Learning for Hybrid Modeling, Optimization, and Control](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32914v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32914

**作者：** Rahul Golder、Bimol Nath Roy、MM Faruque Hasan

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.ET

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32914v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32914v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该教程系统介绍模块化物理约束机器学习框架 KKT-HardNet：用微分投影层在保证等式与不等式约束满足的前提下获得神经网络快速推理，并借助隐函数定理实现端到端训练，同时给出工程基准问题。

**相关性简评：** 其可微投影层与隐函数定理反传可能为在 latent 推理状态的更新中硬性施加几何或实体关系约束、并低成本训练此类约束层提供线索，是否适用于 VLM 内部状态尚需核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** A Tutorial on Physics Constrained Machine Learning for Hybrid Modeling, Optimization, and Control

**abstract/A1：** Physics-constrained machine learning (PCML) integrates domain knowledge, first-principles, and physical constraints into AI/ML models. In this work, we present a step-by-step tutorial of a modular PCML framework, KKT-HardNet, and illustrate how it could be used for hybrid modeling, process simulation, optimization and model predictive control in a single platform. KKT-HardNet combines fast inference capabilities of neural networks with guaranteed satisfaction of algebraic constraints through differential projection. It can handle heterogeneous data and enforce both linear and nonlinear equality and inequality constraints. The modular design supports custom neural-network backbones and user-defined projection mechanisms for PCML. To enable efficient end-to-end training, KKT-HardNet exploits implicit function theorem to backpropagate through the projection layer. This avoids computationally expensive automatic differentiation. This work also introduces an exact reduced-order Fischer-Burmeister reformulation and a primal-dual interior-point method as an alternative projection solve strategy. We also provide a diverse set of benchmarking problems for PCML in engineering applications. KKT-HardNet achieves orders of magnitude better performance in terms of both constraint satisfaction and inference time. The KKT-HardNet code and the benchmark datasets are available at https://github.com/souls-tamu/kkt-hardnet .

</details>

<a id="paper-200"></a>

### 200. [ZENO: Nonlinear filtering with Zakai Equation Neural Operators](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32926v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32926

**作者：** Aaditya Prasad、Steven W. Flavell、Brian DePasquale

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、q-bio.NC

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32926v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32926v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 ZENO：以物理信息损失训练神经网络算子拟合 Zakai 方程，逼近潜随机动力系统的未归一化滤波分布，并结合 EM 做参数学习与状态推断，在三个模拟系统和线虫全脑成像数据上验证。

**相关性简评：** 其滤波后验的连续时间信念状态演化与观测似然更新，可能为 LVR 中 latent 状态的逐步更新与证据整合提供概念参照，但无视觉语言推理实验，需核查可迁移性。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** ZENO: Nonlinear filtering with Zakai Equation Neural Operators

**abstract/A1：** State inference and parameter learning for nonlinear filters is a core computation for scientific machine learning with varied applications in engineering and neuroscience. The unnormalized filtering distribution is governed by the Zakai equation, a stochastic partial differential equation (SPDE) that combines the forward Kolmogorov operator (known as the Fokker-Planck equation) with observed likelihood updates. Although solving these high-dimensional SPDEs is often impossible analytically and intractable numerically, advances in operator learning and physics-informed machine learning have made it feasible. Harnessing these advances, we introduce the Zakai Equation Neural Operator (ZENO), a model that optimizes a neural operator against a Zakai Equation based physics-informed loss to approximate the exact unnormalized filtering distribution for latent stochastic dynamical systems. We use this ZENO-inferred posterior distribution in an expectation-maximization (EM) framework to perform parameter learning and state inference. We demonstrate ZENO's ability to accurately infer the posterior distribution and learn its corresponding SDE parameters in three simulated stochastic dynamical systems (Double-Well bistable system, Van Der Pol (VDP) oscillator, and Lorenz system) and compare its performance against established methods for latent SDE inference and learning. We show that ZENO recovers previously observed limit cycles representing forward and backward locomotion from a whole-brain imaging dataset of immobilized \\textit{C. elegans} nematodes. ZENO is a novel approach to the problem of nonlinear filtering and dynamical inference through the lens of partial differential equations and operator theory.

</details>

<a id="paper-201"></a>

### 201. [STROKE: Simple Sparse Structural Occlusions for Black-Box Adversarial Attacks](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32932v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32932

**作者：** Ankit Gupta、Akshat Pandya、Paul Lin、Christoph Adami、Emily Dolson

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.CR、cs.HC

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32932v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32932v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 STROKE：一种基于分数查询的黑盒对抗攻击，用少量有向矩形遮挡拼成扰动，并以轻量贪心「添加—精修」流程在低查询数、1–2% 面积预算下优化攻击效果。

**相关性简评：** 其稀疏结构化遮挡与查询预算下的贪心搜索，或可作为探测 VLM 对特定视觉区域依赖与筛选最小充分证据的核查手段，与 LVR 的视觉旁路、证据使用诊断可能存在联系，但需人工核查是否可迁移。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** STROKE: Simple Sparse Structural Occlusions for Black-Box Adversarial Attacks

**abstract/A1：** We introduce STROKE, a query-efficient score-based black-box adversarial attack that constructs perturbations from simple oriented rectangular occlusions. STROKE optimizes these perturbations through a lightweight greedy add-and-refine procedure. Across diverse image classifiers, STROKE achieves high attack success with low query counts at nominal 1--2\\% area settings, showing favorable performance relative to existing patch-based attacks in several configurations. Matched comparisons further demonstrate substantial reductions in wall-clock attack time, while ablation studies examine the key design choices and effectiveness across structural primitives. These findings suggest that simple structural occlusions offer an effective and computationally efficient search space for black-box adversarial attacks.

**comment/C1：** 13 pages, 4 figures, 4 tables, and 16 pages of Supplementary Information with 3 figures and 5 tables

</details>

### 202. [UniAction: Unified Action Conditioning for World Models](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32977v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32977

**作者：** Bowen Xue、Zizhang Li、Hong-Xing Yu、Jiajun Wu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32977v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32977v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 UniAction，将异质动作表示为带离散语义身份与连续时空坐标的稀疏动作元素集，映射到预训练视频模型的原生位置空间以统一条件化，并用 cardinality-calibrated attention 缓解动作粒度差异带来的偏置，在力、相机控制、手部姿态和机器人姿态上验证。

**相关性简评：** 该文的「稀疏元素集＋共享时空坐标」统一条件表示与粒度校准注意力，可能为 LVR 中异质视觉证据如何写入同一 latent 状态、以及证据粒度不均衡引起的偏置提供可供核查的线索，但本身不涉及推理或 latent reasoning。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** UniAction: Unified Action Conditioning for World Models

**abstract/A1：** Actions that condition world models are highly heterogeneous, and thus existing action-conditioned world models are typically built for individual action types, fragmenting already limited action-annotated data across separate models. We introduce UniAction, a unified action-conditioning framework that represents any action as a sparse set of action elements, each with a discrete semantic identity and a continuous spatiotemporal grounding coordinate. By mapping these coordinates directly into the pretrained video model's native positional space, UniAction allows heterogeneous actions to condition a shared world model within the same spatiotemporal structure. We further introduce cardinality-calibrated attention to address the bias from differing action representation granularities. We evaluate UniAction on four representative action forms: force, camera control, hand pose, and robot pose. UniAction outperforms conditioning alternatives in action following, and a single jointly trained model surpasses the action-specific experts, showing that heterogeneous action data benefit from unified model and joint training.

**comment/C1：** Project page: https://uniaction.github.io/

</details>

### 203. [Tradeoff-Aware Territory Design: A Multi-Objective Decision System for Enterprise Customer-to-Seller Allocation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31701v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.31701

**作者：** Nan Wang、Mitchell Turiel

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31701v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.31701v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文提出面向企业 GTM 的客户—销售分配决策系统，整合账户价值打分、基于合成控制式反事实的关系连续性估值与多目标优化，用于权衡增长、均衡、容量与可行性。

**排除原因：** 属业务资源分配与运营优化应用，不涉及视觉或多模态内部状态、推理轨迹生成、证据获取或停止决策，其中“continuity valuation”也与 latent 贡献分配无关，故无实质借鉴线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Tradeoff-Aware Territory Design: A Multi-Objective Decision System for Enterprise Customer-to-Seller Allocation

**abstract/A1：** Enterprise customer journeys are shaped not only by product experiences and marketing, but also by recurring resource-allocation decisions. In B2B go-to-market (GTM) organizations, territory carving determines which accounts receive seller coverage, which seller-account relationships are preserved, and how limited field capacity is distributed. Yet the process often remains manual, making it difficult to compare opportunities, value continuity, and evaluate tradeoffs among growth, balance, capacity, and feasibility. We present a production decision system for tradeoff-aware customer-to-seller allocation in enterprise GTM planning. It integrates three components: a multidimensional account-value layer that combines predictive signals to score opportunity; an observational continuity-valuation layer that estimates the cost of breaking seller-account relationships using a synthetic-control-style counterfactual framework; and a multi-objective optimization layer that allocates coverage to high-value accounts while considering continuity, opportunity balance, and operational constraints. Deployed as a scenario-based decision-support system, the framework enables planners to inspect and compare allocation tradeoffs. Historical backtesting shows that optimizer-generated scenarios substantially improve assignment compliance while maintaining comparable seller-level opportunity distribution. In production planning, adopted scenarios improved both assignment compliance and opportunity balance relative to the previous baseline. Directing scarce seller capacity toward higher-value accounts also offers incremental revenue potential at enterprise scale. The key contribution is an integrated, inspectable framework that turns territory carving into a governable decision process.

</details>

<a id="paper-204"></a>

### 204. [The Path to NDE 4.0: A Review of Algorithms, Techniques, and Strategies for Autonomous Inspection](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31704v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.31704

**作者：** Ashiqur Rahmana、Venkata Devesh Reddy Seethi、Zachary Kral、Austin Yunker、Rajkumar Kettimuthu、Hamed Alhoori

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.ET

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31704v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.31704v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文综述无损检测（NDI/NDE 4.0）领域，从算法、物理技术、工业应用与研究目标四个角度梳理从人工判读到深度学习与生成模型自动缺陷识别的演进，并指出数据集稀缺与模型黑箱等瓶颈。

**排除原因：** 属工业缺陷检测的领域综述，讨论重点是缺陷识别精度、可解释性与数字孪生协议，未涉及内部推理状态、视觉证据筛选、动态计算或停止决策等 LVR 问题，也无可具体借鉴的状态更新或监督线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** The Path to NDE 4.0: A Review of Algorithms, Techniques, and Strategies for Autonomous Inspection

**abstract/A1：** Non-Destructive Inspection (NDI) is a cornerstone of modern quality assurance, critical for ensuring the safety and reliability of infrastructure and manufactured components without causing damage. This paper presents a comprehensive review of the current NDI landscape, analyzing the field through four distinct lenses, namely, computational algorithms, physical techniques, industrial applications, and research objectives. We synthesize a broad range of academic literature to illustrate the rapid evolution from manual, analog interpretation to a digitized, data-centric paradigm known as NDE 4.0. Our analysis highlights the dominance of ultrasound and visual inspection as primary physical techniques, while revealing a significant shift toward deep learning and generative models for automated defect recognition. Despite these technological strides, the industry faces critical bottlenecks, notably the scarcity of standardized open-access datasets and the "black box" nature of AI models, which hinders widespread adoption. By charting these developments and identifying key research gaps, such as the need for explainable AI (XAI) and standardized digital twin protocols, this review offers a strategic roadmap for future research. It concludes that the future of NDI research should focus on the successful integration of intelligent, autonomous fusion of inspection data with advanced sensor technologies into asset lifecycle management.

</details>

### 205. [Towards Adversarially Robust Multigraph Neural Networks for Financial Crime Analysis](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31718v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.31718

**作者：** Dennis Heijmans、H. Çağrı Bilgi、Rui Wang、Kubilay Atasu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31718v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.31718v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文研究金融交易多重图神经网络的对抗鲁棒性，提出基于节点-边关联矩阵的可扩展结构攻击框架，用投影梯度与随机块坐标下降学习结构扰动，并加入有向 2/3 环计数正则项衡量攻击合理性，同时验证对抗训练可提升鲁棒性。

**排除原因：** 研究对象是金融犯罪检测中的多重图结构攻击与防御，不涉及视觉或多模态推理中的 latent 状态、证据筛选、计算分配或停止决策，也未提供可迁移至 LVR 的具体方法线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Towards Adversarially Robust Multigraph Neural Networks for Financial Crime Analysis

**abstract/A1：** Financial transaction networks are naturally represented as multigraphs, where repeated transactions between the same pair of accounts are modeled as distinct edges. Recently proposed multigraph neural network architectures leverage this structure explicitly to achieve a strong performance on anti-money laundering (AML) tasks. However, their robustness to adversaries that manipulate transaction structures to evade detection remains largely unexplored. We present the first scalable framework for structural attacks and adversarial training of multigraph neural networks using node-edge incidence matrices, adapting projected gradient descent and projected randomized block coordinate descent methods to optimize structural perturbations over such matrices. We further introduce a cycle-based regularizer that penalizes changes in the global counts of directed 2- and 3-cycles, providing a structural measure of attack plausibility. Experiments on synthetic pattern-detection and realistic AML datasets show that our learned attacks substantially outperform non-learnable perturbation baselines in reducing detection performance (PR-AUC), while adversarial training consistently improves robustness against these attacks. These results demonstrate that multigraph neural networks are vulnerable to learned structural attacks and that adversarial training provides an effective defense. Our code is available at https://github.com/denheijmans/robust\_multignn.

**comment/C1：** 20 pages, 10 figures, 3 tables. Accepted at the Fifth Learning on Graphs Conference (LoG 2026). Code: https://github.com/denheijmans/robust\_multignn

</details>

**作者：** Zhuoyuan Jiang、Weijie Lyu、Xiaoyuan Wang、Tiancheng Shen、Ming-Hsuan Yang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31747v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.31747v1>)

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** AudioLift: Audio-Driven Single Image to Full-Head Gaussian Avatar via Multi-view Video Generation

**abstract/A1：** Existing 3D talking avatar methods mainly rely on parametric face models including 3DMM or FLAME, whose compact motion representations limit their ability to synthesize extreme expressions and to model accessories such as headwear. We introduce AudioLift, a two-stage framework that uses generated multi-view videos as the intermediate representation for audio-driven avatar generation without parametric face models or per-instance optimization. Our framework combines a synchronized multi-view video generation stage with an inconsistency-robust Gaussian reconstruction stage to recover coherent full-head geometry from a single portrait image. Extensive experiments demonstrate that AudioLift produces 3D talking avatars capable of capturing more extreme facial expressions than existing methods, with improved geometric completeness and strong multi-view consistency across the full $360^\\circ$ viewing range.

**comment/C1：** Project page: https://zhuoyuanjiang.github.io/audiolift-page/

</details>

### 207. [PaperScorer: How Predictable Is Paper Acceptance?](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31791v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.31791

**作者：** Sachin Konan、Jonathan Liu、Zhuang Liu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31791v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.31791v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 PaperScorer，仅凭论文文本或页面截图预测同行评审的录用决策，并比较视觉模型、文本模型与前沿推理模型的可预测性。

**排除原因：** 研究目标是预测论文录用结果与评审噪声，与 LVR 的内部状态生成、视觉证据筛选和推理期决策无实质联系，页面截图仅为输入模态而非推理状态。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** PaperScorer: How Predictable Is Paper Acceptance?

**abstract/A1：** Automated research is largely evaluated by benchmark performance, but the value of research rarely reduces to a single score. Humans judge a work's holistic contribution through peer review, which is hard to model because it is noisy and depends on many factors. However, what if, given only the paper, we could predict its peer-review decision directly? We introduce PaperScorer, a family of models that predict a paper's accept or reject decision from its extracted text or page screenshots, training only on the decision after removing author identity and formatting shortcuts. Vision models are the most predictive (68% accuracy on ICLR papers and 77% on ML papers from arXiv), beating text-only and frontier reasoning models. Unlike plain text, page images retain figures, layout, and presentation. This signal is robust, surviving smaller models and even compressing the whole paper into a single image. But accurate predictions are not evenly spread. PaperScorer is strongest on the lowest and highest rated papers, while papers rated in the middle stay unpredictable for our models and frontier systems alike. Additionally, its confidence is calibrated, so its most confident predictions are also its most accurate. Overall, PaperScorer shows how much of the decision a paper carries on its own, marking a first step toward AI-driven research evaluation.

**comment/C1：** Accepted to NeurIPS 2026

</details>

**title/T1：** Patronizing and Condescending Language Detection in English and Mandarin

**abstract/A1：** Patronizing and condescending language (PCL) is a form of harmful language that belittles and assumes pity for vulnerable groups, reinforcing negative societal stereotypes. However, unlike overt hate speech, it is often difficult to detect, even for human judges. This paper proposes a system for the automated detection of PCL. Our first approach uses static word embeddings and a convolutional neural network for classification, while our second approach uses pre-trained BERT embeddings fine-tuned for the PCL task. Our second approach was considerably more successful, and achieved an F1 score of 0.528 over the development set.

</details>

### 209. [Nested Sparsity for Scalable Pretraining and Continual Learning](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31818v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.31818

**作者：** Tianzhe Chu、Druv Pai、Yi Ma

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31818v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.31818v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**排除原因：** 该工作提出与 MoE 稀疏性层级嵌套的注意力条件计算机制，用于语言模型预训练与持续预训练的计算效率，属纯文本架构与训练效率研究；其稀疏性是静态结构而非推理期状态生成、视觉证据筛选或停止决策，与 LVR 主线缺少具体联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Nested Sparsity for Scalable Pretraining and Continual Learning

**abstract/A1：** Mixture-of-experts (MoE) transformers, the standard backbone for modern large-scale deep learning, employ conditional computation: only a sparse subset of model weights is used in each forward pass. In MoE transformers, this sparsity arises in the MoE operator, which generalizes the multi-layer perceptron in the classical transformer. In this work, we explore an alternative mechanism for conditional computation that analogously generalizes the attention operator. The sparsity induced by this mechanism naturally nests (i.e., hierarchically composes) with the sparsity induced by the MoE operator. We show that a transformer employing this nested sparsity mechanism, which we call Nestor, performs better than a conventional MoE transformer at equal compute and equal sparsity in language model pretraining. We also show that Nestor is well-suited for continual pretraining: it can be adapted to longer contexts more effectively than conventional MoEs, as well as adapt more efficiently to new domains while forgetting less on old domains. Furthermore, we demonstrate that these benefits persist as model size, dataset size, and context length grow. Our results demonstrate the potential of alternative conditional computation mechanisms for deep neural network architectures. Code and checkpoints are available on GitHub at https://github.com/Ma-Lab-Berkeley/Nestor .

**comment/C1：** 10 pages main body

</details>

**排除原因：** 研究目标是细胞形态与免疫突触分类的标注高效预训练，未涉及视觉推理中的内部状态、证据获取或停止决策，也无可用于 latent 表示筛选的具体线索，故排除。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** IFC-SSL-Bench: Benchmarking Self-Supervised Learning for Annotation-Efficient Multispectral Imaging Flow Cytometry

**abstract/A1：** Imaging Flow Cytometry (IFC) generates large multispectral datasets, yet the need for expert annotations bottlenecks the development of supervised deep learning models. While Self-Supervised Learning (SSL) offers a solution, its application to IFC remains fragmented and lacks systematic benchmarking. To address this, we present IFC-SSL-Bench, a modular framework for evaluating SSL in IFC. We benchmark six SSL approaches across four backbones on two heterogeneous datasets. By assessing downstream classification performance across annotation regimes of the training sets (1%-100%), we quantify annotation efficiency and identify optimal configurations for high-dimensional biological imaging. SSL matches the performance of fully supervised baselines trained on 100% of annotated data while using as little as 5% of annotations for morphological classification, with advantages in low-data regimes below 5%. Across both datasets, the contrastive approach MoCo-v2 paired with ConvNeXt backbones emerged as the top-performing configuration, with ConvNeXtV1-T best for immunological synapse classification and ConvNeXtV2-T for morphological classification. We identify a clear pattern in transfer dynamics: ImageNet initialization benefits morphological classification, consistent with its visual similarity to natural images, whereas random initialization better captures the more domain-specific immunological synapse classification. SSL elevates class-specific F1-scores by over 35 percentage points for structurally complex classes at 1% of annotated data. We synthesize these insights into a unified set of guidelines for deploying self-supervised workflows in high-throughput IFC, and release the benchmark as a resource for future work.

**comment/C1：** 35 pages, six main figures, seven supplementary figures, five tables and four supplemntary tables

</details>

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Aligning Large Language Models with Human Feedback: Mathematical Foundations and Algorithm Design

**abstract/A1：** This article provides an introduction to the mathematical foundations and algorithmic frameworks used to align Large Language Models (LLMs) with human intentions, preferences, and values. We discuss standard alignment techniques, such as fine-tuning (SFT), reinforcement learning with human feedback (RLHF), and direct preference optimization (DPO). We also explore the theoretical underpinnings of learning from human preferences, drawing connections to inverse reinforcement learning (IRL) and discrete choice models. We present state-of-the-art algorithms in a tutorial style, discuss their advantages and limitations, and offer insights into practical implementation. Our exposition is intended to serve as a comprehensive resource for researchers and practitioners, providing both a foundational understanding of alignment methodologies and a framework for developing more robust and scalable alignment techniques.

</details>

**内容简述：** 该论文提出 TACS-RF 框架，用 LLM 编码智能体直接调用 Cadence Virtuoso/Spectre、Calibre、EMX、HFSS 等 EDA 工具，半自主完成 90 nm SiGe 工艺下 240–251 GHz 亚太赫兹发射机各子模块及级间匹配网络的设计与验证。

**排除原因：** 属于射频集成电路的智能体自动化设计流程，不涉及视觉或多模态信息表示、latent 推理状态生成与使用，其工具调用循环也只是一般性工程流程，未提供可供核查的 LVR 线索，故排除。

<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** TACS-RF: Tool-driven Agentic Circuit Synthesis of RF and mmWave Modules

**abstract/A1：** We present the framework of TACS-RF - a Tool-driven Agentic Circuit Synthesis of RF and mmWave modules. In this framework, a large-language-model (LLM) coding agent (Claude Opus 4.8) directly drives industry-standard electronic-design-automation (EDA) tools such as Cadence Virtuoso/Spectre, Calibre, Cadence EMX, and Ansys HFSS to design RF and mmWave modules. We validate the framework through the agentic design of a 240-251 GHz sub-THz transmitter in a 90 nm SiGe BiCMOS process. The agent semi-autonomously designed the sub-blocks including a 60 GHz push-push Colpitts VCO with second-harmonic extraction, multistage differential cascode power drivers, a push-push frequency doubler, a differential bowtie antenna, and all the interstage matching networks. The transmitter chain with parasitic-extracted devices and EM-verified passives delivers +5.1 to +6.3 dBm across an 11 GHz varactor-only tuning range, phase noise of -80 to -88 dBc/Hz at 1 MHz offset while consuming a DC power of 361 mW. We describe the agent-driven design flow and the resulting division of labor between human and agent, taking a step toward a "text-to-ASIC" future for designing RF/mmWave hardware.

**comment/C1：** Accepted for presentation at the 2027 IEEE Radio and Wireless Week (RWW), Silicon Monolithic Integrated Circuits in RF Systems (SiRF)

</details>

<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** StateBridge: Phase- and Track-Grounded Reasoning for Synthetic-to-Real Traffic Video Captioning and VQA

**abstract/A1：** Traffic safety videos provide rich evidence for understanding interactions between pedestrians and vehicles, but synthetic-to-real evaluation makes this setting challenging because models must learn from simulated scenes and generalize to real-world traffic. In this work, we present StateBridge, a traffic video-language framework designed to operate under this synthetic-only training protocol. Our approach uses phase-aware clip construction, pedestrian and vehicle track summaries, and structured prompts to ground vision-language models in stable traffic evidence rather than appearance alone. We fine-tune public vision-language models on synthetic captioning and visual question answering examples, then combine their outputs through fact-locked caption fusion and evidence-diverse VQA consensus to improve consistency across actor roles, behavioral phases, and question types. StateBridge achieves an official S2 score of 55.4679 on AI City Challenge 2026 Track 2 using only synthetic training data. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of structured evidence transfer for traffic safety captioning and visual question answering.

**comment/C1：** 25 pages, 5 figures, ECCV conference

</details>

**title/T1：** Automated Nucleus Segmentation in Histology Images: An Attention U-Net Approach with Comprehensive Preprocessing and Inference Pipelines

**abstract/A1：** Objectives: In the area of digital pathology, nucleus segmentation is an essential step for quantitative analysis; however, high nuclear density and staining heterogeneity in histology images pose a great challenge. Methods: This study implements a lightweight Attention U-Net model on the MoNuSeg 2018 dataset to overcome these challenges. The proposed pipeline integrates comprehensive image and mask preprocessing, including stain deconvolution and multi-pass mask smoothing, with a high-fidelity overlapping patch-stitching strategy during inference on high-resolution images. Results: The model achieved an Intersection over Union (IoU) score of 0.8745 on full-resolution, unseen test images. This result demonstrates the effectiveness of attention mechanisms in enhancing feature localization and validates the stitching methodology for generating seamless and accurate segmentation maps. Conclusion: Attention U-Net is an effective and competitive method for automated, scalable nucleus delineation in challenging clinical contexts.

<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Topological Equivalence of Spectral and Density Cluster Hierarchies

**abstract/A1：** We compare two canonical ways to recover hierarchical clustering from an underlying density $f$: the \\emph{density cluster tree} given by connected components of superlevel sets $\\{f\\geλ\\}$, and a \\emph{spectral hierarchy} obtained by applying single linkage to diffusion distances induced by a Laplacian operator. We give conditions under which, at an appropriate diffusion time, the diffusion hierarchy is \\emph{topologically equivalent} to the density cluster tree up to a monotone rescaling of heights. It further shows that a $k$-NN graph implementation inherits this equivalence from uniform approximation of diffusion distances, yielding a finite-sample reduction from spectral graph constructions to density-level-set topology.

</details>

### 216. [Decode Late, Multiply Once: Rethinking the Dot Product for Quantized Weights](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31913v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.31913

**作者：** Jonathan Tapson

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AR

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31913v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.31913v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**排除原因：** 该文面向量化权重的低比特点积硬件实现（地址累加、延迟解码、SA-PE 与 RTL 面积/能耗分析），既无视觉或多模态推理状态，也不涉及 latent 表示、证据使用或停止决策，与 LVR 主线缺少可核查的实质联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Decode Late, Multiply Once: Rethinking the Dot Product for Quantized Weights

**abstract/A1：** Large neural networks are increasingly deployed with 1-4 bit weights and 16- or 32-bit activations, yet their dot products still use one multiplier per element. We show that a length-n dot product with k-bit weight codes can be computed with n+2^k additions and at most 2^k decodes and 2^k multiplications. Each weight code is used directly as an address offset that selects one of 2^k accumulators; each activation is added into the selected accumulator; and the accumulated sums are decoded and multiplied only once, at the end, in a fused decode-and-multiply step. The decoded weight vector is never formed. The refactoring is exact for integer arithmetic, works with integer or floating-point operands and with arbitrary, including nonlinear, decoders, and extends to group-wise scales and to matrix and tensor products. The multiplier count falls by n/2^k, while total arithmetic energy improves by a smaller, bounded factor that we quantify with a cost model. We describe a scatter-accumulate processing element (SA-PE) that replaces the MAC unit in a weight-stationary systolic array, with a load/stream/reduce/drain schedule in which a small, time-shareable multiplier runs only 2^k times per tile. Because decoded weights are not stored, local weight memory shrinks by up to w\_d/k for w\_d-bit decoded weights. An RTL implementation synthesized for FPGA and compared with a matched MAC array shows that an unshared SA-PE is larger than a MAC PE, and that the saving appears when the reduction multiplier is shared across PEs. Software models confirm exact integer equivalence and floating-point error no worse than the conventional pipeline. We do not claim the underlying identity is new, and we discuss related work on weight sharing and lookup-based kernels.

</details>

<a id="paper-217"></a>

### 217. [Every Weight Is a Path of Shared Discrete Decisions](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31931v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.31931

**作者：** Ammar Ali、Stamatis Lefkimmiatis

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31931v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.31931v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 TWIG 表示，把网络权重写成共享离散决策（符号、2 的幂位移、零门、共享表索引）构成的树路径，从而统一量化、剪枝、低秩与权重共享，并可映射为固定权重电路、用退火类求解器选择决策；在 LLM 权重矩阵上比较加法开销。

**排除原因：** 该文属于权重格式与压缩/硬件映射工作，虽涉及树状共享离散决策，但不涉及 VLM 推理的 latent 状态生成、视觉证据使用或停止决策，未发现可供核查的迁移线索，故排除。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Every Weight Is a Path of Shared Discrete Decisions

**abstract/A1：** We introduce TWIG (Tree-structured Weights with Inherited Gates), a representation in which every weight of a neural network is the end of a path of small discrete decisions: a sign, a shift by a power of two, a zero gate, an index into a shared table. Weights that belong together share the upper part of their paths, and each decision is inherited by every weight below it, so it is stored once and applied once. This simple change has three consequences. Any trained network can be written as a TWIG, and when the decisions are powers of two its weight layers become circuits made almost entirely of wires and adders, suited to chips whose weights are fixed at fabrication. Pruning, quantization, low-rank factorization, codebooks and weight sharing become edits of one object, so a single optimizer can choose among all of them for a whole network. And choosing the decisions is the ground-state problem of a higher-order Boltzmann machine whose interactions follow the paths of the tree, the problem class that annealers and quantum optimizers are built to solve. Today these questions are handled apart: each compression method brings its own format and solver, a circuit with fixed weights needs a dedicated constant multiplier for every weight, and discrete solvers reach only narrow subproblems such as rounding. Weight formats have long been moving toward shared decisions, through group scales, shared exponents, microexponents and sparse weights; TWIG takes this trend to its end, and every common format is a shallow TWIG at no extra cost. On five weight matrices of a large language model, a flat tree whose signed power-of-two terms are chosen per weight for circuit cost, with no group scales, needs 9--15\\% fewer additions than GPTQ at equal layer error at 3- and 4-bit accuracy on typical matrices, and 20--55\\% fewer on matrices with strong outliers.

</details>

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31968v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.31968v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文用瑞典 Skåne 地区救护车历史行程数据，分析行程时间随小时、星期和任务优先级的变化，并构建结合 SHAP 与置换重要性解释的机器学习救护车行程时间估计系统。

**排除原因：** 主题为急救调度中的行程时间预测与可解释性，不涉及视觉或多模态推理状态，也未提供可迁移至 LVR 的状态表示、证据筛选或训练监督线索，故排除。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Beyond Heuristics and Commercial Navigation: An AI-Augmented Decision-Support for Ambulance Travel Time Estimation

**abstract/A1：** Accurate ambulance travel time estimation is critical for emergency medical dispatch, yet existing decision-support practices often rely on static lookup tables or simplified assumptions regarding how ambulance travel time differs from ordinary passenger-car travel. For instance, they assume ambulances simply travel a fixed percentage (e.g., 5%) faster than ordinary passenger cars. However, such assumptions are rarely validated using real ambulance operational data and may fail to capture the effects of trip priority, temporal context, and origin-destination. We address these limitations using historical ambulance trips from the Skåne region in Sweden. Our analysis reveals that ambulance travel times vary substantially by hour, day, and trip priority, and that commercial navigation systems fail to capture emergency-specific dynamics. We contribute a machine learning system to more accurately estimate ambulance travel time. To make the system interpretable, we explored SHapley Additive exPlanations and permutation importance techniques to ensure that our system captures operationally meaningful patterns.

**comment/C1：** 8 pages, 4 figures, 2 tables, accepted at CoopIS 2026

</details>

### 219. [The Helpful Agent Problem: When Emergent Cooperation Among LLM Agents Bypasses Intended Workflows](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31985v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.31985

**作者：** Moumita Choudhury、Amit Sarker、Shlomo Zilberstein

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.CL、cs.MA

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31985v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.31985v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出「helpful agent problem」：在共享资源、需要审查者中介的多智能体长期环境中，善意智能体在审查缺失时会自发互相代办越界操作，并构建 FavorBench 基准与 CooperBench 适配来测量此类行为及其外部危害。

**排除原因：** 研究焦点是多智能体协作绕过审查流程的安全问题，不涉及视觉细节表示、latent 状态生成或推理证据使用，也无可迁移的状态更新、监督或停止决策方法线索，与 LVR 主线缺少实质联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** The Helpful Agent Problem: When Emergent Cooperation Among LLM Agents Bypasses Intended Workflows

**abstract/A1：** LLM agents are increasingly deployed in collaborative settings where they pursue separate tasks while sharing resources and operating under review or oversight processes. Such settings create safety risks not only when agents are malicious, but also when they are simply trying to help. Prior work has largely studied adversarial or explicitly collusive behavior; we show that benign agents pursuing separate objectives can spontaneously cooperate in ways that bypass intended workflows. We call this the \\emph{helpful agent problem}. We study long-horizon shared-resource environments in which agents sometimes depend on resources owned by their peers and cross-owner changes are normally mediated by a reviewer. When review becomes unavailable, agents begin making changes on one another's behalf despite having no shared reward or explicit incentive to cooperate. Crucially, these actions remain within each agent's local permissions while circumventing the system's intended oversight structure. We introduce \\textsc{FavorBench}, a controlled benchmark with measurable downstream side effects, and adapt \\textsc{CooperBench} to test the phenomenon in realistic repositories. Across two benchmarks and four models, we find that out-of-workflow favors emerge frequently. Further, in \\textsc{FavorBench}, favors from at least three models cause significant harm to parties outside the interaction. These results reveal a distinct multi-agent safety challenge: cooperation itself can create pathways around system-level safeguards.

</details>

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** OpenHome: From Perception to Insight in Smart-Home Camera Videos

**abstract/A1：** Vision language models (VLMs) have been increasingly adopted in consumer smart-home camera systems to interpret recorded videos and provide insights. However, video clips captured from smart-home cameras are different from curated web videos dominating existing VLM benchmarks. Smart-home video clips often do not contain subtitles or narration and are captured from different viewing angles, under various lighting conditions, and in diverse scenarios. However, the capabilities of the model in this domain, under sparse visual signals and limited context, are not fully assessed by existing VLM benchmarks. To close this gap, we introduce OpenHome, a visual-only, four-option multiple-choice benchmark for evaluating smart-home video understanding. It contains 2,254 questions on 1,118 real-world video clips and organizes 10 tasks into three capability levels. Perception evaluates visible content, Reasoning evaluates causal, spatial, and temporal relationships, and Insight evaluates subject intent, supporting evidence and appropriate homeowner responses. Based on the dataset, we describe the task design, data pipeline and evaluation protocol, and report results for 32 closed-source and open-weight VLMs. The best model, Gemini 4 Argon, reaches 81.2% overall accuracy. Frontier closed-source models outperform open-weight models on every level. The best closed-source model leads the best open-weight model by 16.3% on Perception, 22.4% on Reasoning and 37.4% on Insight.

</details>

### 221. [BARC-Bench: Benign Asks, Risky Consequences](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32024v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32024

**作者：** Kangkang Li、Kelvin Luu、Lily Ma、Tu Trinh、Ying Liu、Mohamed Elfeki

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32024v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32024v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 BARC-Bench，包含九个模拟工作区环境中的 110 个任务，用户请求本身无害但存在越界操作空间，用以评估智能体是否能遵守记录、环境隐含语境与未明示社会规范所界定的安全边界。

**排除原因：** 该工作关注文本化工作流智能体的指令欠定与安全边界，不涉及视觉或多模态推理中的内部状态、证据筛选或停止决策，未发现可迁移至 LVR 的具体线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** BARC-Bench: Benign Asks, Risky Consequences

**abstract/A1：** Task instructions from users for agents are inherently underspecified, especially regarding safety boundaries--creating behavioral risks. To study these risks, we introduce BARC-Bench, a collection of 110 tasks across nine simulated workspace environments where benign requests leave openings for unsafe actions. In this benchmark, we evaluate agent behavior against safety boundaries established by workspace records, implicit environmental context, and unstated social norms. For example, an agent asked to schedule off-boarding discussions must arrange the meeting without prematurely revealing an employee's resignation through a shared calendar invitation. Evaluating four frontier model configurations across five attempts per task reveals that models are not consistently safe: the share of tasks completed safely on all five attempts ranges from 9.1% to 72.7%, with even the strongest configuration producing a safety violation on 27.3% of tasks. These results highlight an asymmetric operational risk: while models frequently complete complex workflows, their inability to consistently respect unstated boundaries presents a substantial barrier to safe autonomous delegation.

</details>

### 222. [PPO vs. SAC: A Comparative Study for Cooperative Motion Control of Dual-Arm Space Robots](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32034v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32034

**作者：** Nasim Fallahiarezoodar、Zheng H. Zhu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32034v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32034v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文面向双臂空间机器人协同运动规划，在固定基座仿真中构建集中式状态表示与多目标奖励，比较 PPO 与 SAC 在避碰、对准和泛化上的表现。

**排除原因：** 属于机器人运动控制的算法对比与任务应用，不涉及内部推理状态、视觉证据使用或 latent 生成；奖励塑形导致策略停在「徘徊」局部最优的观察虽属训练现象，但与 LVR 的监督设计无具体可核查线索，故排除。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** PPO vs. SAC: A Comparative Study for Cooperative Motion Control of Dual-Arm Space Robots

**abstract/A1：** The growing population of orbital debris has intensified interest in active debris removal and on-orbit servicing, for which dual-arm space manipulators offer improved grasping stability and load distribution relative to single-arm systems. However, coordinating two manipulators under inter-arm collision avoidance, orientation alignment, and sensing uncertainty remains challenging. This paper develops a coordinated reinforcement learning framework for cooperative motion planning of a dual-arm space manipulator in a target-reaching task with inter-arm collision-avoidance constraints. A centralized state representation combines joint-space, end-effector, and target-relative information, while a multi-objective reward function promotes target-reaching accuracy, orientation alignment, motion smoothness, synchronized task completion, and collision avoidance. Within this framework, Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO) and Soft Actor-Critic (SAC) are compared as representative on-policy and off-policy algorithms, and the learned policies are further evaluated for generalization to previously unseen target configurations. Across five random seeds, SAC substantially outperforms PPO within the considered fixed-base simulation scenarios: it reaches an 80% success threshold in every seed (median 1.6M environment steps) whereas PPO does not reach it within a 4M-step budget, and it attains a higher mean success rate (78.6% versus 7.4%) with lower position and orientation errors and tighter bimanual synchronization. Both algorithms degrade on unseen boundary targets, but SAC retains most of its performance while PPO's collapses. The results also expose a failure mode of PPO: under the two-phase shaped reward, it tends to converge to a reward-loitering local optimum rather than committing to the precision task. This behavior highlights the sensitivity of on-policy learning to reward shaping.

**comment/C1：** Presented at the 2026 AAS/AIAA Astrodynamics Specialist Conference, Whistler, British Columbia, Canada, July 26-30, 2026. Paper AAS 26-740

</details>

### 223. [NUViT: Vision Transformers for Fourier Space Data](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32041v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32041

**作者：** Jeffrey Gu、Robert C. Heeter、Ellen D. Zhong

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32041v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32041v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**排除原因：** 该文为傅里叶域数据重新设计 ViT 的 tokenization、数据表示与位置编码，属视觉骨干架构改造，未涉及 VLM 推理状态生成、视觉证据使用或停止决策，也与 latent 表示内容无具体联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** NUViT: Vision Transformers for Fourier Space Data

**abstract/A1：** Vision Transformers (ViTs) have demonstrated strong performance in many computer vision tasks but do not naturally extend beyond the natural image domain. One important example is Fourier-space data, which is used to represent a rich and diverse set of modalities, including medical imaging and microscopy. Standard ViT tokenization partitions an image into square patches on a Cartesian grid, which poorly fit the frequency and phase structure of Fourier-space data. We introduce Non-Uniform ViT, a simple, general-purpose vision transformer that redesigns image tokenization, data representation, and positional encoding to respect this structure. Experiments on Fourier-domain image classification, 2D crystal lattice identification, MRI annotation, and cryo-EM protein heterogeneity analysis show that NUViT consistently captures the structure of Fourier data better than standard architectures.

</details>

### 224. [ACVA-Bench: Beyond Prompt Following in Abstract Concept Visualization Ability for Text-to-Image Models](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32058v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32058

**作者：** Mengyan Wang、Jiyuan An、Yining Liu、YaoWen Zhang、Liner Yang ang Erhong Yang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32058v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32058v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文提出 ACVA-Bench，一个基于心理语言学规范、类别平衡的基准，用于评估文生图模型在低干预条件下将「忠诚」等抽象概念自主转化为准确、多样且可区分视觉表达的能力，并配套提出受概念语义约束的细粒度视觉-语义问答式评价方法。

**排除原因：** 研究对象是文生图模型的抽象概念可视化质量，不涉及 VLM 推理期 latent 状态、视觉证据获取或停止决策；其语义问答分解仅用于评判生成图像的输出质量，与内部表示保留哪些视觉细节缺乏具体可核查的联系，故排除。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** ACVA-Bench: Beyond Prompt Following in Abstract Concept Visualization Ability for Text-to-Image Models

**abstract/A1：** Text-to-image (T2I) models have achieved remarkable progress in image quality and instruction following, prompting growing research interest in their capacity for deep understanding that goes beyond literal prompt matching. However, without an explicit visual scheme provided, it remains unclear and has not been systematically evaluated whether T2I models can autonomously translate abstract concepts lacking a fixed visual referent, such as "loyalty," into visual representations that are accurate, diverse, and distinguishable from closely related concepts. We define this capability as Abstract Concept Visualization Ability (ACVA) and introduce ACVA-Bench, a category-balanced benchmark grounded in psycholinguistic norms of abstract concepts, which examines models' ACVA along three dimensions semantic sufficiency, representational diversity, and conceptual distinctiveness under a low-intervention setting that withholds concrete objects, scenes, or visual metaphors. Since abstract concepts lack a unique reference image, conventional evaluation metrics that rely on reference-image matching are difficult to apply. To address this, we propose a fine-grained visual-semantic evaluation method constrained by concept semantics, which decomposes concept semantics into independently verifiable visual-semantic questions to assess the semantic representation quality of generated images, with its validity further confirmed by human evaluation. Benchmarking results on mainstream T2I models show that these models rely on a small set of prototypical visual patterns, leaving room for improvement in representational diversity and conceptual distinctiveness for abstract concepts, thereby providing an interpretable and reproducible evaluation foundation for future research.

</details>

### 225. [DBCM: Dual-Branch Calibration Module for Test-Time Adaptation in Time Series Forecasting](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32071v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32071

**作者：** Jaebyeong Jeon、Kunwoo Kang、Sang Yup Lee、Kibok Lee

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、stat.ML

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32071v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32071v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 针对时间序列预测的测试时分布漂移，提出双分支校准模块：共享适配器加低秩通道适配器，并辅以通道混合与门控调制，在冻结预测器外围以在线观测量更新。

**排除原因：** 属时间序列预测的测试时残差校准，不涉及视觉或多模态推理状态、证据获取与停止决策，其共享/低秩参数化与 LVR 的表示或训练监督无具体可迁移线索，故排除。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** DBCM: Dual-Branch Calibration Module for Test-Time Adaptation in Time Series Forecasting

**abstract/A1：** Non-stationarity induces test-time distribution shifts in time series forecasting (TSF), resulting in forecast residuals between predictions and the corresponding ground truth. Recent works on test-time adaptation for TSF address this by introducing lightweight calibration modules around frozen forecasters, updated using observations that become incrementally available at test time. Existing approaches typically employ channel-wise adapters, implicitly assuming that forecast residuals are inherently channel-specific. However, we observe that forecast residuals often exhibit substantial shared structure across channels, suggesting that fully independent channel-wise parameterization may be redundant. Motivated by this observation, we propose the Dual-Branch Calibration Module (DBCM), which shares adaptation capacity across channels through a shared adapter while retaining limited channel-specific flexibility through a low-rank channel-wise adapter, further enhanced by channel mixing and nonlinear gated modulation. Extensive experiments show that DBCM consistently outperforms prior methods across diverse datasets, horizons, and backbones.

</details>

### 226. [Forging Emotions: a deep learning experiment on emotions and art](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32163v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32163

**作者：** Amalia Foka

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.CY、cs.HC

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32163v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32163v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**排除原因：** 该文讨论用 GAN 生成可传达情绪的视觉艺术作品，并批评情绪图像数据集与 GAN 组合结构，属情感计算与计算艺术，未涉及视觉推理中的状态表示、证据使用或停止决策，缺乏可核查的借鉴线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Forging Emotions: a deep learning experiment on emotions and art

**abstract/A1：** Affective computing is an interdisciplinary field that studies computational methods that relate to or influence emotion. These methods have been applied to interactive media artworks, but they have focused on affect detection rather than affect generation. For affect generation, computationally creative methods need to be explored that have recently been driven by the use of Generative Adversarial Networks (GANs), a deep learning method. The experiment presented in this paper, Forging Emotions, explores the use of visual emotion datasets and the working processes of GANs for visual affect generation, that is, for generating images that can convey or trigger specified emotions. This experiment concludes that the methodology used so far by computer science researchers to build image datasets for describing high-level concepts such as emotions is insufficient and proposes utilizing emotional networks of associations according to psychology research. Forging Emotions also concludes that to generate affect visually, merely corresponding to basic psychology findings, such as bright or dark colours, does not seem adequate. Therefore, research efforts should aim to understand the structure of trained GANs and compositional GANs in order to produce genuinely novel compositions that can convey or trigger emotions through the subject matter of generated images.

**comment/C1：** 10 pages. Published in Artnodes no. 31 (2023), Universitat Oberta de Catalunya, under CC BY 4.0. Extended version of a paper presented at ISEA 2022, Barcelona

</details>

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32174

**作者：** Vineet Prasad、Shiron Melvin、Sheikh Azid、Hai Wang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32174v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32174v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文提出一个用于商业转盘式挤奶厅的 RGB-D 感知前端，在单次前向中定位目标奶牛、十个骨性关键点及四个背部解剖区域，并评估其精度、实时性与跨场地迁移。

**排除原因：** 属农业机器人中的关键点与区域分割应用，虽涉及多模态融合与密集监督，但无 latent 推理状态、证据获取或停止决策层面的具体线索，与 LVR 主线缺少实质联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Real-Time Localisation of Dorsal Anatomical Regions on Dairy Cows in Commercial Rotary Parlours from RGB-D

**abstract/A1：** Automated monitoring of dairy cows increasingly measures one part of the body, such as the rump for temperature or the back for posture, yet the region itself is usually placed by hand. Existing systems either score the cow as a whole or detect sparse keypoints, often in controlled walkways, and neither yields the named region masks that such measurements need on a crowded commercial rotary platform. We present a perception front end that finds the target cow, ten bony landmarks and four named dorsal regions (withers, back, rump and tail-head) in a single forward pass over a whole RGB-D frame from a commercial rotary parlour. Two encoders, one for colour and one for depth, are fused late and share one decoder with three heads. On an anatomical region dataset that we built on public rotary-parlour RGB-D images (538 annotated frames, cow-disjoint test set), the network reaches PCK@10 0.905 for landmarks and region IoU 0.70 to 0.91, and runs end to end in 26.4 ms per frame on a T4 GPU, against an 11 fps camera. Dense region supervision is what makes the landmarks accurate: removing the region head costs 0.15 PCK@10. Agreement between the two heads flags unreliable frames without labels. On our own rig at the largest dairy farm in Western Australia, the model transfers with ten labelled frames (PCK@10 0.84), and its regions are carried through depth into a radiometric thermal camera, giving one repeatable temperature per region and cow.

**comment/C1：** 9 pages, 7 figures, 3 tables. Submitted to ACRA 2026

</details>

**title/T1：** Searching with Sparse Histories: Cross-Task Architecture Search from Incomplete Search Logs and Zero-Cost Proxies

**abstract/A1：** Neural architecture search (NAS) is expensive, but cross-task transfer can reduce costs by reusing knowledge from previous tasks. However, existing methods struggle with sparse search histories and task-specific transfer failures. We propose a method, a risk-robust strategy that combines sparse source logs with zero-cost proxies through champion-based initialization and joint pairwise-ranker pretraining. On TransNAS-Bench-101, the proposal mitigates severe worst-case failures, reducing worst-10% ranks from 290 to 43 for Micro autoencoding and from 465 to 45 for Macro room layout. Across 13 cell-space targets, it outperforms a proxy-only ranker on 10 targets in both mean and worst-case performance, while maintaining competitive average performance.

</details>
