### 229. [Benchmarking Data-Free Continual Model Merging for Whole-Slide Image Survival Analysis](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32207v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32207

**作者：** Vu Minh Tran、Doanh C. Bui、Maï K. Nguyen、Khang Nguyen

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32207v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32207v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该工作为全切片图像生存分析建立无数据持续模型合并的端到端基准，在四个 TCGA 队列、三种任务顺序下比较六种合并策略，并用 C-index、遗忘与后向迁移等指标评估。

**排除原因：** 研究聚焦医学影像生存预测的权重空间持续合并与删失感知评价，未涉及视觉 latent 状态、证据筛选或推理期计算分配，缺乏可核查的具体借鉴线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Benchmarking Data-Free Continual Model Merging for Whole-Slide Image Survival Analysis

**abstract/A1：** Continual survival analysis on whole-slide images (WSIs) requires models to integrate knowledge from sequentially arriving cancer cohorts while preserving previously learned information. Model merging offers a data-free alternative by combining independently fine-tuned task experts without retaining previous training data. However, the relative behavior of different merging strategies for WSI-based survival prediction remains insufficiently understood. In this work, we establish an end-to-end benchmark for data-free continual model merging, covering task-specific fine-tuning, sequential merging, and inference under both TASK-IL and CLASS-IL settings. We systematically evaluate six representative merging strategies across four TCGA cancer cohorts and three task orders using C-index, IPCW C-index, Forgetting, and Backward Transfer under a controlled protocol. Results show that no merging strategy consistently dominates across all evaluation criteria, while censoring-aware evaluation can substantially alter the relative ranking of the compared methods. This benchmark provides a unified foundation for developing and evaluating more effective continual model merging methods for WSI-based survival analysis.

</details>

### 230. [Training an MoE LLM in MXFP4: How We Closed the Gap to BF16](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32229v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32229

**作者：** Han Wang、Zhitao Wang、Natalia Frumkin、Xuanwu Yin、Alireza Khodamoradi、Dong Li、Emad Barsoum

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32229v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32229v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**排除原因：** 论文研究 MoE LLM 的 MXFP4 低精度训练配方（分块量化、Hadamard 变换、随机舍入、权重去振荡），目标是缩小与 BF16 的收敛差距，与视觉或多模态推理状态、证据使用和停止决策均无实质联系，也无可借用的 latent 推理方法线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Training an MoE LLM in MXFP4: How We Closed the Gap to BF16

**abstract/A1：** Training large language models (LLMs) in sub-8-bit arithmetic promises substantial gains in memory bandwidth and compute throughput, yet FP4 formats introduce quantization error severe enough to destabilize optimization if left unaddressed. We present ALTO, an open-source training recipe for GPT-OSS-20B, a 20-billion-parameter mixture-of-experts (MoE) model, on the MLPerf Small MoE Training benchmark under the MXFP4 microscaling format. The recipe combines established techniques from Abecassis, Felix et al. \[arXiv:2509.25149\] (hybrid 1D/2D block quantization, randomized Hadamard transforms, and stochastic rounding) with a simplified weight de-oscillation scheme adapted from TetraJet-v2 \[arXiv:2510.27527\]. On C4 validation at global batch size 16, MXFP4 + RHT + SR + de-oscillation reaches a validation loss of 3.3350 at 16,128 steps, only 0.007 above the BF16 baseline (3.3283), closing roughly 50% of the gap left by MXFP4 without de-oscillation (3.3418). Measured by steps to reach the MLPerf quality target (validation loss 3.34), de-oscillation cuts the FP4 convergence overhead relative to BF16 from +20.0% to +5.0%. We also report negative end-to-end results from differential gradient estimation, outlier clipping, and macro-block scaling, despite each showing operator-level SNR gains. All experiments use simulated MXFP4 kernels on AMD MI300 hardware and wall-clock speed has not been optimized.

</details>

### 231. [Financial Context-Rich Strategy for Actor-Critic based Agents: A Deep Reinforcement Learning Approach for Stock Trading](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32237v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32237

**作者：** Hrishikesh Singh、Aarti Sharma、Millie Pant

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、q-fin.CP、q-fin.PM

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32237v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32237v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文研究在股票交易中把情感分析深度融入 actor-critic 类 DRL 智能体的策略设计，并比较仅策略优化与结合 Q-learning 的智能体在收益与风险上的表现。

**排除原因：** 属于金融交易决策应用，不涉及视觉或多模态推理的内部状态、证据筛选与停止决策，缺少可迁移到 LVR 的具体方法线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Financial Context-Rich Strategy for Actor-Critic based Agents: A Deep Reinforcement Learning Approach for Stock Trading

**abstract/A1：** In modern financial markets, more than 3/4th of the large volume trade is executed by algorithmic trading based on advanced trading strategies to attain higher precision and speed than traditional human traders. The conventional strategies focused on price-based indicators are inadequate in capturing the market dynamism arising from real-world events such as social sentiments, political instability, or sudden economic shifts. With the rise of Deep Reinforcement Learning (DRL) and data availability, there is a requirement for more efficient algorithms that can incorporate these external factors in trading strategies. DRL has shown potential in simulating real-world scenarios and in simplifying complex decision-making since DRL agents can automate the trading process i.e. executing trades in a manner comparable to human traders. They have shown reliable performance since they can update their policies based on risk and quantitative factors. In this paper, we propose a DRL approach based on partial and deep-infusion of sentimental analysis. It features distinct policy designs of actor-critic-based agents: only policy optimization (A2C &amp; PPO) and policy optimization along with Q-learning principles (DDPG, TD3 &amp; SAC). Our findings reveal, that the agents based on policy optimization combined with Q-learning, significantly and consistently demonstrate improved performance upon sentiment deep-infusion in profit maximization as well as risk-averse trading.

</details>

### 232. [BiLoCo: Binary Low-Rank Corrections for Post-Training Quantization](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32243v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32243

**作者：** David Jin、Beshr IslamBouli、Tarushii Goel、Han Guo、Yoon Kim

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32243v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32243v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 BiLoCo，用二值符号向量存储低秩校正因子以补偿 FP4 后训练量化的精度损失，并在 B300 上实现与 FP4 GEMM 并行执行的融合 kernel。

**排除原因：** 论文针对权重 FP4 量化误差的数值校正与 kernel 加速，不涉及视觉或多模态推理状态、证据使用或停止决策，未提供可迁移到 LVR 的具体方法线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** BiLoCo: Binary Low-Rank Corrections for Post-Training Quantization

**abstract/A1：** Post-training quantization (PTQ) to FP4 has emerged as a key technique for reducing large language model inference cost, especially on Blackwell GPUs, which offer native FP4 tensor core support. Direct PTQ to FP4, however, still leaves a noticeable accuracy gap. Recent work on SVDQuant mitigates the errors introduced through FP4 quantization for diffusion models by adding high-precision low-rank corrections obtained via SVD. However, this high-precision low-rank correction requires many bits per rank, and it runs on the same tensor cores as the main FP4 GEMM. We introduce BiLoCo, a low-rank correction method whose left and right factors are stored as binary ${\\{\\pm 1\\}}$ sign vectors rather than as BF16 numbers. The signed format uses $16{\\times}$ less storage per rank-one component, and since the correction now consists of additions/subtractions, this can be run on CUDA cores concurrently with the FP4 tensor-core GEMM. On 4B--72B LLMs, BiLoCo matches or improves memory-matched SVDQuant for PTQ. We implement BiLoCo on B300 as a fused single kernel, where the FP4 GEMM runs on tensor cores, the bit-packed correction on the CUDA cores of the same launch, and the two are combined in the GEMM epilogue. With batch size $1$, the fused kernel is at most $11\\%$ slower than the FP4-only GEMM for every rank up to $512$. At rank $128$, it is at most $20\\%$ slower up to batch size $8$.

</details>

<a id="paper-233"></a>

### 233. [Easy-3Dfy: Object-Level Geometric Composition for Single-Image 3D Scene Construction](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32250v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32250

**作者：** Ziang Zhang、Wenyou Yi、Zehan Wang、Jiachen Xu、Xintong Han、Chunchao Guo、Zhou Zhao

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32250v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32250v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 Easy-3Dfy，将单图 3D 场景构建解耦为物体网格生成与场景布局估计，并用基于 RPF 的对齐器在物体局部网格与部分场景点图之间建立几何对应，从而组合出场景。

**排除原因：** 属于纯 3D 重建与生成流水线，围绕几何对齐和网格质量，未涉及 VLM 内部推理状态、证据筛选或停止决策，与 LVR 主线缺乏具体可核查的联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Easy-3Dfy: Object-Level Geometric Composition for Single-Image 3D Scene Construction

**abstract/A1：** Single-image 3D scene construction is a fundamental problem in 3D vision. Recent methods such as SAM-3Dfy enable end-to-end reconstruction of 3D assets and their scene layouts, but still struggle with fine-grained scene geometry and asset quality. Meanwhile, specialized 3D object generators provide detailed object geometry, while 3D foundation models offer strong and generalizable spatial understanding. We propose Easy-3Dfy, a compositional workflow that decouples object generation from scene layout estimation, allowing specialized models to fully exploit their respective strengths. An RPF-based aligner then bridges these capabilities by establishing geometric correspondence between object-local meshes and partial scene pointmaps, recovering object layout for scene composition. Without requiring complete scene-level supervision, the proposed workflow consistently outperforms SAM-3Dfy across multiple geometric metrics on Scene-Maker and 3D-FUTURE200. Our results demonstrate that explicit geometric composition can effectively combine high-quality object generation with strong spatial understanding, while enabling the workflow to benefit independently from advances in both 3D object generation and foundation models.

</details>

### 234. [RSIHub: A Composable Framework for Agent-Evolution Methods](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32253v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32253

**作者：** Zimu Wang、Xiaobo Wang、Siyu Ye、Jiaqi Li、Silin Chen、Yuling Shi、Ruobing Wang、Jingjing Zhang、Chaofan Wang、Kai Wu、Mengnan Qi、Kai Cai、Xiaodong Gu、Zilong Zheng

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32253v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32253v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 RSIHub，一个开源框架，将 agent 进化方法拆解为可组合的 operators 与 recipes，并提供执行、评估、恢复与谱系管理，用于在统一基础设施上构建和比较 AHE、GEPA 等方法。

**排除原因：** 工作停留在 agent 进化策略与实验基础设施层面，未涉及视觉或多模态推理中的内部状态、证据筛选、latent 生成与停止决策，缺乏可核查的具体联系，故排除。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** RSIHub: A Composable Framework for Agent-Evolution Methods

**abstract/A1：** Agent-evolution methods use execution feedback to improve prompts, skills, tools, and orchestration, but their implementations often rebuild the same experimental machinery. We present RSIHub, an open-source framework for building, comparing, and extending these methods on shared infrastructure. Recipes compose reusable operators and declare mutable resources; RSIHub supplies execution, evaluation, recovery, and lineage. Researchers can focus on evolution policy while evaluation stays outside the mutable surface and results remain tied to candidates and evaluation conditions. We instantiate AHE, Hyperagents, A-Evolve, and GEPA without modifying the framework core, keeping method-specific logic in recipes and operators. We evaluate them with MiniSWE and Codex on Terminal Bench 2 and $τ^3$ Banking under fixed model and evaluator configurations. The largest observed overall pass@1 gain is 13.5 percentage points, while method rankings vary across settings and optimization gains do not always transfer to held-out tasks. RSIHub provides a common protocol for comparing evolution policies, inspectable candidate histories, and reusable components for developing new methods.

**comment/C1：** NeurIPS 2026 Meta-Agents Workshop

</details>

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32263v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32263v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 LapaFM，一个基于两万余分钟腹腔镜手术视频、采用多阶段持续学习训练的手术基础模型，支持去烟、手术阶段识别、器械与息肉分割等多项任务。

**排除原因：** 属医疗影像多任务基础模型与持续学习工程，重点在跨任务泛化与灾难性遗忘，未涉及内部推理状态生成、视觉证据筛选或停止决策等议题，缺乏可核查的 LVR 借鉴线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** LapaFM: A Laparoscopic Foundation Model via Multi-Stage Continual Learning

**abstract/A1：** Artificial intelligence-based analysis of intraoperative laparoscopic videos has the potential to improve surgical decision-making and precision. However, effective surgical assistance requires models that can generalize across diverse intraoperative tasks. Here we present LapaFM, a foundation model for laparoscopic surgery trained on more than 20,000 minutes of surgical videos using a multi-stage continual learning strategy that mitigates catastrophic forgetting and inter-task interference. LapaFM learns multimodal visual representations and supports multiple tasks, including smoke removal, surgical phase recognition, instrument and polyp segmentation, and lesion identification. The model was evaluated on 13 retrospective and two prospective cohorts collected from different imaging devices. LapaFM consistently outperformed task-specific models and existing foundation models across datasets, and also generalized to gastrointestinal endoscopy tasks, including polyp detection and classification. Notably, it recovered over 90% of smoke-obscured frames and achieved clinician-level or superior performance in lymph node metastasis prediction and prospective polyp classification.

</details>

### 236. [Hyperparameter-Free Boundary-Interior t-vMF Dice Loss for Medical Image Segmentation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32266v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32266

**作者：** Hinako Mitsuoka、Kazuhiro Hotta

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32266v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32266v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出一种免调参的边界/内部区域自适应 t-vMF Dice 损失，按上一轮各区域预测与目标的平均余弦相似度确定形状参数，用于医学图像分割。

**排除原因：** 属于分割损失函数与区域加权设计，未涉及 VLM 推理状态、视觉证据使用或推理决策，与 LVR 主线缺少可核查的具体联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Hyperparameter-Free Boundary-Interior t-vMF Dice Loss for Medical Image Segmentation

**abstract/A1：** Class imbalance and errors near anatomical boundaries remain challenges in medical image segmentation, motivating loss functions that account for these difficulties. Adaptive t-vMF Dice Loss adjusts the similarity function during training but requires hyperparameter tuning. We propose Self-Tuned Boundary-Aware t-vMF Dice Loss (STB-tvMF Dice), which adapts the function separately for boundary and interior regions without tuning shape-adaptation parameters. Each region's shape parameter of t-vMF function is determined from its mean prediction-target cosine similarity in the preceding epoch and remains fixed throughout the current epoch. This rule maximizes the slope of the similarity function at the observed mean similarity, providing a principled basis for adaptation without validation-based parameter selection. Experiments on three datasets and architectures demonstrated its effectiveness. On Synapse with U-Net, STB-tvMF Dice achieved a mean DSC of 70.75%, compared with 63.03% for Adaptive t-vMF Dice Loss. Our code will be available soon.

</details>

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32275

**作者：** Gerardus Croonen、Andreas Trondl、Julia Simon、Daniel Steininger

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32275v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32275v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文面向收获后甜菜质量检测，提出端到端图像检测流程，构建部分遮挡甜菜的基准，利用 SAM 3 自动生成标注框并与人工标注对比，同时在边缘设备上评测推理精度、时延与功耗。

**排除原因：** 属于农业场景下的目标检测与边缘部署基准，不涉及视觉推理状态、证据获取或停止决策，自动标注仅是数据生产效率问题，缺少可供 LVR 借鉴的具体线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** RootDown: Deploying and Benchmarking Models for Beet-Root Detection on Edge Devices

**abstract/A1：** Quality inspection of sugar beets after harvest and storage is critical to preserve sugar content and ensure optimal processing efficiency. The transfer of harvested beets to field clamps or trucks provides an opportunity to visually inspect individual beets automatically on loader conveyors. However, this is a challenging task as the large number of beets causes partial overlap and occlusion, complicating both annotation and detection. In this work, we propose an end-to-end image-based pipeline for detecting partially occluded sugar beets in loading scenarios as a first step toward automated quality inspection. We introduce a dedicated benchmark and present an automatic annotation strategy that leverages the SAM 3 foundation model to generate bounding boxes. We compare models trained on manually annotated and automatically generated labels. Additionally, we investigate deployment considerations by benchmarking multiple inference configurations, including execution on an edge device, under realistic operational constraints regarding precision, inference time and power consumption. Our results show that, despite substantial occlusion and inherent annotation ambiguity, foundation-model-based labels can substitute for a substantial share of manual annotation, trading labeling effort for data volume at a modest accuracy cost. Furthermore, we demonstrate that a Raspberry Pi 5 equipped with a Hailo accelerator enables high-performance, low-power inference, making it a practical solution for mobile outdoor deployment.

**comment/C1：** Accepted at MLCSA Workshop at Asian Conference on Computer Vision (ACCV 2026). Benchmark, code and models available at https://github.com/semanticsugarbeets/rootdown

</details>

### 238. [Polyp-VSSD: A Pre-trained Transformer with Local-Global Joint VSSD Decoding for Polyp Segmentation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32282v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32282

**作者：** Xiaobo Zhu、Shiwei Wu、Yu An、Yuechen Tao、He Sun、Song Zhang、Zhenyu Liu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32282v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32282v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文提出 Polyp-VSSD，在预训练 Transformer 骨干上构建局部-全局联合解码框架，用于内窥镜下的息肉分割，并在多个公开数据集上报告 Dice 与 IoU 提升。

**排除原因：** 这是纯医学图像分割的精度提升工作，只涉及单次前向的特征解码，没有 latent 推理状态、证据选取、停止决策或 VLM 多模态推理相关内容，与研究主线缺少实质联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Polyp-VSSD: A Pre-trained Transformer with Local-Global Joint VSSD Decoding for Polyp Segmentation

**abstract/A1：** Early detection of colorectal polyps is essential for preventing colorectal cancer, making accurate polyp segmentation a key component in computer-aided diagnosis systems. However, existing endoscopic segmentation methods often exhibit limited representational capacity and insufficient integration of global and local contextual information. To address these limitations, we propose Polyp-VSSD, a novel local-global joint decoding framework built upon a pre-trained Transformer backbone for high-precision polyp segmentation. Polyp-VSSD leverages the strong feature expressiveness of large-scale visual pre-training and unifies global semantic modeling with local fine-grained reconstruction to enhance segmentation fidelity. Extensive experiments on multiple public endoscopic datasets demonstrate that our approach significantly outperforms state-of-the-art methods in both Dice and IoU. On the EndoScene dataset, Polyp-VSSD achieves mIoU and mDice scores of 0.903 and 0.946, respectively, highlighting its robustness and strong generalization capability. This work presents an effective and scalable solution for intelligent endoscopic image analysis.

</details>

### 239. [ReLEAP: Upcycling Vision Datasets through Cross-Task Unification and Semi-Supervised Annotation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32289v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32289

**作者：** Julia Simon、Daniel Steininger、Andreas Trondl

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32289v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32289v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 ReLEAP，一个用于统一异构图像数据集并跨任务复用标注的 Python 框架，并在精准农业场景整合 15 个数据集，评估半监督等方式自动生成高层级标注的效果。

**排除原因：** 属于数据集整理与标注复用工程，不涉及视觉推理的内部状态、证据筛选、动态计算或 latent 生成，与 LVR 主线缺少可核查的具体联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** ReLEAP: Upcycling Vision Datasets through Cross-Task Unification and Semi-Supervised Annotation

**abstract/A1：** Developing robust vision models requires large amounts of annotated data, especially for tasks involving fine-grained class distinctions under variable environmental conditions. Even in domains with numerous existing datasets, differences in annotation formats, label definitions and targeted learning tasks hinder data reuse and integration. To address this challenge, we introduce ReLEAP, a scalable Python framework for unifying heterogeneous image datasets through data-source abstraction, systematic reuse of annotations across learning tasks, and mapping of labels to a shared hierarchical taxonomy. We demonstrate its applicability in precision agriculture by integrating fifteen diverse datasets covering a broad range of plant species and annotation types. Beyond unification, ReLEAP enables systematic recycling of existing data and provides a robust foundation for learning-based annotation upcycling. Leveraging this foundation, we conduct extensive cross-dataset and fine-tuning experiments to investigate how domain-specific models trained on unified data and foundation models can be used to automatically generate higher-level annotations under unsupervised, weakly supervised and semi-supervised settings, and how dataset characteristics influence their effectiveness. Our results show that unified data access and annotation upcycling substantially expand the pool of reusable training data and annotations while improving data transparency, enabling more sustainable and resource-efficient use of existing datasets. We release the framework and trained models for academic use to support accessible and collaborative research, particularly in data-scarce and heterogeneous domains.

**comment/C1：** Accepted at Asian Conference on Computer Vision (ACCV) 2026. Code and models available at https://github.com/releap-vision/releap

</details>

OlmoEarth v1.3 is designed to condense multimodal, multitemporal remote sensing data into a single digestible 64 or 128-dimensional vector. OlmoEarth v1.3 produces state-of-the-art embeddings. In addition, OlmoEarth v1.3 is a strong encoder backbone -- competitive with OlmoEarth v1 and v1.2 -- while requiring 18% and 53% of the GPU hours to train, respectively. We use OlmoEarth v1.3 to compute global, annualized 10m embeddings, which we release under the OlmoEarth artifact license.

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文提出 DeformStruct，用于点监督时序动作定位：从单点标注出发挖掘结构锚点，经形变感知匹配对齐潜在动作部件，并建模部件间相对时序关系以形成结构表示。

**排除原因：** 研究目标是视频时序动作定位，既无视觉语言模型的推理状态生成，也无视觉证据筛选与决策停止机制，其中的「结构部件」是视频时序片段匹配而非推理 latent，与 LVR 主线缺少可核查的具体方法或概念线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** DeformStruct: Deformable Structure Modeling for Point-Supervised Temporal Action Localization

**abstract/A1：** Point-supervised Temporal Action Localization (PTAL) aims to localize action instances using only a single annotated timestamp for each instance. Such sparse supervision offers limited guidance for modeling the structural organization of an action instance. Existing PTAL methods primarily infer action regions from classification responses, leaving the structural organization of multiple action-related parts within each instance insufficiently modeled. To address this problem, we propose DeformStruct, an instance-level deformable structure modeling framework for PTAL. DeformStruct progressively constructs the structure of each action instance through three stages. First, Structural Anchor Mining discovers multiple anchors from annotated points and action-responsive regions, providing structural nodes grounded in the current instance. Second, Deformable Part Alignment associates latent structural parts with these anchors through deformation-aware matching, allowing their temporal supports to adapt to instance-dependent variations. Third, Relational Structural Organization explicitly models the relative temporal relations among the aligned parts, forming a relational representation that characterizes how different action-related parts are organized together. Extensive experiments on THUMOS14, ActivityNet-v1.3, GTEA, and BEOID demonstrate consistent improvements in point-supervised temporal localization and validate the effectiveness of deformable structural modeling.

</details>

<a id="paper-242"></a>

### 242. [A Regularity Condition Under Which a CPAB Transformation is a Diffeomorphism](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32369v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32369

**作者：** Amit Aflalo、Oren Freifeld

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32369v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32369v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文指出现有 CPAB 变换仅保证为双 Lipschitz 同胚而非微分同胚，并给出保证其成为微分同胚的充分正则性条件及在 1D、2D 上的可实现性。

**排除原因：** 这是关于分段仿射速度场积分与微分同胚性质的纯数学注记，不涉及 latent 推理状态、视觉证据使用或 THINK/LOOK/STOP 等决策过程，也未提供可迁移到 VLM 推理的表示或训练线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** A Regularity Condition Under Which a CPAB Transformation is a Diffeomorphism

**abstract/A1：** Continuous Piecewise-Affine Based (CPAB) transformations, introduced by Freifeld et al. (2015, 2017), are flows obtained by integrating continuous piecewise-affine (CPA) velocity fields. These expressive transformations have proven useful in applications including nonlinear temporal warping and trainable activation functions in 1D, as well as nonrigid spatial deformations in 2D and 3D. While computational costs were not an issue in 1D, they originally limited the practical expressiveness of CPAB transformations in higher dimensions, especially in 3D. Our recent work (Aflalo et al., 2026) removed this bottleneck, enabling efficient optimization with much finer tessellations and substantially greater expressiveness. There is, however, a mathematical subtlety that was overlooked in the original CPAB papers (Freifeld et al., 2015, 2017). CPAB transformations are always bi-Lipschitz homeomorphisms, implying that both the transformations and their inverses are differentiable almost everywhere. They are not, however, necessarily diffeomorphisms. This note remedies the omission by identifying sufficient regularity conditions under which a CPAB transformation is guaranteed to be a diffeomorphism. We explain how differentiability can fail and how the proposed conditions prevent this failure. In 1D and 2D, we show that these conditions can be easily enforced and, for commonly used tessellations, hold with probability one under standard sampling assumptions. We expect analogous behavior in 3D.

</details>

<a id="paper-243"></a>

### 243. [PASTA: Prompt-Aware Annealed Diffusion Sampling](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32390v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32390

**作者：** Ren Ajiki、Ryugo Morita、Stanislav Frolov、Brian Bernhard Moser、Ko Watanabe、Riku Takahashi、Andreas Dengel、Jinjia Zhou

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI、cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32390v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32390v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出免训练的扩散采样框架 PASTA，通过在采样中用掩码将完整提示与上下文提示的语义引导融合于共享 latent 轨迹，并按退火调度抑制早期语义泄漏，以严格保留版式指定区域。

**排除原因：** 属于图像生成中的版式保留采样控制，其 latent 仅指扩散去噪状态，与视觉推理中的 latent 表示、证据筛选或停止决策无实质联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** PASTA: Prompt-Aware Annealed Diffusion Sampling

**abstract/A1：** While Diffusion models produce high-quality images, real-world visual design requires intentionally preserving clean regions for headlines, logos, or UI elements. Existing layout-aware text-to-image methods struggle to strictly preserve reserved regions, often introducing unwanted content that complicates downstream editing. Existing approaches either manipulate attention to displace content or suppress conditioning via noise-level interventions. However, these methods sacrifice visual coherence, producing flat regions or noticeable style inconsistencies between reserved and non-reserved areas. To address this limitation, we propose a Prompt-Aware Shared-Latent Training-Free Annealed Diffusion Sampling (PASTA), a training-free framework that enforces reserved-region constraints by selectively applying semantic guidance to object-allowed regions during sampling. PASTA operates on a shared latent trajectory, where full-prompt and context-prompt guidance are fused through mask-guided sampling. An annealed schedule suppresses semantic leakage during early denoising stages while gradually restoring visual consistency in later stages. Extensive experiments demonstrate that PASTA improves reserved-region fidelity while preserving overall image realism and perceptual consistency.

**comment/C1：** 15 pages, 4 figures, 2 tables. Accepted to ICONIP2026

</details>

### 244. [AlphaAnything: Extending Pretrained RGB Generators to RGBA](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32413v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32413

**作者：** Qijie Wang、Zhixin Xu、Zhe Li、Haowei Zhu、Honghao Qiao、Bo Peng、Dawei Li、Bin Wang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32413v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32413v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文提出 AlphaAnything，通过在原 latent 空间中插入 LoRA 与冻结 RGB VAE 解码器上的 alpha 分支，将预训练 RGB 生成器扩展为 RGBA 生成，并设计 EA Loss 强化透明度监督。

**排除原因：** 属于图像生成与透明度表示扩张，无推理状态、视觉证据使用或停止决策等与 LVR 相关的问题，虽涉及共享 latent，但线索过于宽泛，缺乏可核查的具体联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** AlphaAnything: Extending Pretrained RGB Generators to RGBA

**abstract/A1：** Existing RGBA generation methods commonly learn a transparency-specific representation through reconstruction, introducing an additional training stage and potentially altering the RGB decoding function inherited from pretrained models. We present AlphaAnything, a lightweight framework that extends pretrained RGB generators to RGBA directly in their original latent space, without RGBA reconstruction pretraining. AlphaAnything inserts LoRA modules into the generative transformer and attaches an alpha branch to the frozen RGB VAE decoder. Unidirectional RGB-to-alpha feature sharing allows opacity supervision to shape the generative latent while guaranteeing that, for any fixed latent, RGB decoding remains identical to that of the pretrained decoder. We further introduce Endpoint-Aware Alpha Loss (EA Loss), which derives opacity supervision from the complementary foreground--background weights used in compositing. It supports exact opacity endpoints and continuous partial transparency while emphasizing localized alpha errors. Experiments on text-to-RGBA and image-to-RGBA tasks across multiple pretrained RGB backbones demonstrate competitive generation quality, and controlled ablations validate the shared-latent training design and EA Loss.

</details>

### 245. [Data Quality Audit and Bivariate Temperature-Humidity Analysis of a Long-Term LoRaWAN Network: Anomaly Detection, PCA, Clustering and Machine Learning](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32438v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32438

**作者：** Mamadou Sow

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.DC、physics.ao-ph、stat.ML

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32438v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32438v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**排除原因：** 该文对 LoRaWAN 环境传感器数据做覆盖率审计、异常检测、PCA、聚类与温湿度回归，属通用 IoT 数据质量分析，未涉及视觉/多模态推理、latent 状态或证据使用，也无可迁移到 LVR 的具体方法线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Data Quality Audit and Bivariate Temperature-Humidity Analysis of a Long-Term LoRaWAN Network: Anomaly Detection, PCA, Clustering and Machine Learning

**abstract/A1：** Long-term environmental IoT/LPWAN deployments often aggregate heterogeneous data streams from diverse devices without systematic auditing of each variable's actual coverage prior to analysis-an increased risk when multiple sensor types coexist within the same network. This work proposes a C++17 processing pipeline applied to the Tour Perret LoRaWAN dataset (421 937 frames, June 2021--June 2023, 5 devices). A systematic coverage audit integrated into the extraction phase reveals that CO2 is completely missing (0% coverage) and pressure is reported only by an isolated base module (Wyres, 4.24% of the corpus), invalidating the initial hypothesis of a joint four-variable analysis. The analysis is refocused on the Temperature--Humidity pair, jointly available across 205{,}005 frames (95.76%) from 4 Elsys EMS sensors. The pipeline sequentially performs anomaly detection (modified Z-score, Isolation Forest), bivariate Principal Component Analysis, clustering (K-means, DBSCAN), and a multilayer perceptron predicting humidity from temperature and time, whose residuals serve as a detector for slow drift. On real-world data: Temperature--Humidity correlation r=-0.662, two-component PCA (PC1 =83.1% of variance), Isolation Forest detects 2.12% multivariate anomalies while univariate Z-score detects none, calibrated DBSCAN isolates 0.17% noise across 13 clusters, and the MLP achieves $R^2=0.672$ (RMSE =10.54%) in cross-validation. This work demonstrates that per-variable coverage auditing prior to modeling is a necessary methodological step for heterogeneous multi-sensor IoT datasets.

</details>

### 246. [POSEIDON: Scaling Training Environments for Scientific Agents from Code Repositories](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32448v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32448

**作者：** Qifan Zhang、Yuhan Li、Nuo Chen、Bowen Liu、Xiao Hu、Miao Peng、Chenyi Zi、Lixin Liu、Lydell Huang、Jing Tang、Jia Li

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32448v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32448v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 Poseidon 自动化流水线，从科学代码仓库大规模抽取可执行、可验证的智能体训练环境，并构建包含约 9.8 万个环境的 Pearl 集合用于训练科学发现智能体。

**排除原因：** 该工作聚焦代码型科学智能体的环境生成与训练，不涉及视觉或多模态内部表示、证据获取或停止决策；其四类任务分解与失败候选递归修复属通用数据合成流程，未提供与 LVR 状态生成或监督分配的具体线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** POSEIDON: Scaling Training Environments for Scientific Agents from Code Repositories

**abstract/A1：** Training environments remain a major bottleneck for agents in data-driven scientific discovery. Existing collections of executable and verifiable training environments are small, and their construction remains difficult to scale. To address this limitation, we introduce Poseidon, an automated pipeline for generating diverse, verifiable training environments from scientific code repositories at scale. From the same repository budget, Poseidon extracts nearly 10 times as many training environments as a prior approach by decomposing scientific workflows into four complementary task types: Reproduction, Subflow, Completion, and Correction, where each type targets a distinct capability for scientific research. A recursive improvement loop further refines failed candidates and recovers otherwise discarded environments. Using Poseidon, we construct Pearl, a collection of 97,659 executable and verifiable environments from 7,110 public repositories spanning 10 scientific disciplines. To the best of our knowledge, Pearl is the largest and most broadly multidisciplinary collection of scientific-agent training environments to date. Qwen3.5 models (9B, 27B, and 35B-A3B) trained on Pearl achieve consistent gains across six challenging scientific-agent benchmarks. Overall, the scale and diversity of Pearl offer opportunities for training scientific discovery agents.

</details>

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32452v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32452v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 AutoMIL，一个面向全切片病理图像分类的开源自动化多示例学习流水线框架，自动完成预处理、训练与评估，并在多个数据集上给出基准结果。

**排除原因：** 属医学影像任务应用与 AutoML 工程化，未涉及视觉推理中的内部状态、证据筛选或停止决策等机制，也未提供可核查的 latent 表示线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** AutoMIL: Automated Multiple Instance Learning for Whole-Slide Image Classification

**abstract/A1：** Developing multiple instance learning pipelines for the classification of whole-slide images involves many interdependent design decisions and expertise in both pathology and machine learning. We present AutoMIL, an open-source framework that applies principles from the field of automated machine learning to tackle this task by automating integral pipeline steps, such as preprocessing, training and evaluation. In a benchmark evaluation with default settings, AutoMIL reached ROC-AUC scores of 0.94 on UBC-OCEAN, 0.99 on PCam and 0.93-0.96 on a TCGA-NSCLC subset, comparable to specialised pipelines in related work. Ensembling mainly improved performance on imbalanced data, showing that an automated pipeline must adapt to dataset characteristics. AutoMIL thus provides an accessible baseline for whole-slide image classification without manual pipeline configuration.

</details>

### 248. [Sharpness-aware Orthogonalized Momentum Algorithms](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32465v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32465

**作者：** Geetika、Shivani Singh Chauhan、Gauranshi Gupta、Anant Jain、Kaushik Kalyanaraman、Bapi Chatterjee

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、math.OC

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32465v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32465v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文提出 SOMA，将一种计算更省、理论上有依据的扰动方案（利用过时的纯随机梯度噪声）与 Muon 优化器结合，用于减轻后训练量化带来的质量下降和微调中的灾难性遗忘。

**排除原因：** 这是面向 LLM 训练的优化算法与泛化/鲁棒性研究，未涉及视觉推理状态、证据获取、停止决策或 latent 表示，仅“微调稳定性”与轨迹训练有极弱关联，缺少可核查的具体借鉴线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Sharpness-aware Orthogonalized Momentum Algorithms

**abstract/A1：** Adversarial sharpness penalty per iteration of a base optimizer, known as sharpness-aware minimization (SAM), is an accepted technique to improve generalization in deep learning. Recently, Muon has emerged as a dominant optimization algorithm for training large language models (LLMs). It achieves lower sample complexity for heavy-tailed gradient updates - a phenomenon common in LLM training. However, sharpness penalties with Muon updates are yet largely unexplored in the literature. Moreover, the behavior of SAM under heavy-tailed noise is unknown both theoretically and empirically. A potential reason for this gap is the additional cost of extra gradient computation per iteration for penalty perturbations in SAM, which becomes prohibitive for LLMs. This paper introduces Sharpness-aware Orthogonalized Momentum Algorithm (SOMA) to combine a computationally efficient and theoretically grounded perturbation scheme with the Muon algorithm. The key idea is to use stale (purely) stochastic gradient noise for adversarial perturbations. Theoretically, we show that SOMA's sample complexity is invariant to the perturbation direction. We provide explorations and insights into SOMA's robustness to heavy-tailed gradient noise. We extensively explore SOMA's efficacy in (a) mitigating quality degradation due to post-training quantization, and (b) catastrophic forgetting during fine-tuning of LLMs.

</details>

### 249. [A curated multi-endpoint drug-protein bioactivity dataset with construct-aware protein targets](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32490v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32490

**作者：** Lina Abou-Abbas、Nour Mhanna、Hasan Bazzi、Sleiman El Hajj、Abdulkader Helwan、Rabah Omrani、Khadidja Henni

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** A curated multi-endpoint drug-protein bioactivity dataset with construct-aware protein targets

**abstract/A1：** Public drug-protein bioactivity resources commonly identify targets by UniProt accession, although one accession may represent distinct experimental sequences. We present Curate-DTI, a construct-aware dataset derived from the 1 July 2026 BindingDB export, with IC50, Ki, Kd and EC50 processed independently. The release contains 2,919,717 measurements, 1,291,872 standardized ligands, 9,668 sequence-defined protein entities and 2,275,683 endpoint-specific interactions spanning 8,336 reviewed UniProt accessions. Protein entities combine the accession with the reported experimental sequence, preserving constructs that accession-only processing would merge. Across the four endpoints, 4,204 endpoint-specific ligand-accession groups showed different classification labels across sequence-defined protein entities. Exact and inequality-qualified observations provide relation-aware classification evidence, whereas regression targets use exact values only. Connected Parquet tables preserve individual measurements, standardized structures, protein sequences, aggregated interactions and available assay and source provenance. The resource supports endpoint-specific classification and regression while allowing users to select molecular representations, protein representations and evaluation splits. Preprocessing code, a column dictionary, exclusion reports and validation summaries accompany the release.

</details>

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、math.OC

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32491v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32491v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文面向非配对脑部 CT→MR 图像合成，用平滑有界函数近似 L0 范数替代 CycleGAN 的 L1 循环一致性惩罚，以提升对小残差的敏感度并抑制大残差，在 9 张测试图像上报告 MSE、PSNR、SSIM 的改善。

**排除原因：** 属于跨模态图像翻译的损失函数改进，不涉及 latent 推理状态、证据筛选或停止决策，从摘要看缺少与 LVR 研究主线的可迁移联系，故排除。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** L0-Norm-Based CycleGAN for Unpaired Brain CT-to-MR Synthesis

**abstract/A1：** Computed tomography (CT) and magnetic resonance (MR) imaging provide complementary information for the brain, but acquiring both modalities is not always possible. The cycle-consistent adversarial network (CycleGAN) enables unpaired CT-to-MR synthesis, but because the standard L1-norm cycle?consistency penalty grows linearly with the residual magnitude, the network tends to focus on large residuals. This paper introduces an L0-norm-based cycle?consistency term, in which the L0-norm is approximated by a smooth and bounded function. This approximation not only increases sensitivity to small residuals but also effectively suppresses the influence of large residuals. The proposed model and an otherwise identical L1-norm baseline were trained on 102 images and evaluated on 9 test images. The proposed model achieves a lower mean squared error (0.02746 versus 0.03464, a 20.7% reduction), a higher mean peak signal-to-noise ratio (15.80 versus 14.77 dB), and a higher mean structural similarity index (0.5689 versus 0.5407). It also has a lower maximum MSE and higher minimum PSNR and SSIM than the baseline. These results indicate that modifying the cycle-consistency penalty can improve reconstruction accuracy on this dataset.

**comment/C1：** 13 pages, 2 figures

</details>

### 251. [Distortion-Aware Spatiotemporal Learning for Real-Time Wide-Angle Video Rectification](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32500v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32500

**作者：** Lang Nie、Yihan Yang、Wenbo Nie、Hongguang Zhu、Yang Wei、Chunyu Lin、Bin Xiao

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32500v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32500v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**排除原因：** 该文面向广角视频的几何畸变校正，属于图像/视频复原应用，虽含几何拟合与时序建模，但不涉及推理期内部状态、证据使用或 VLM 推理，缺少可核查的联系线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Distortion-Aware Spatiotemporal Learning for Real-Time Wide-Angle Video Rectification

**abstract/A1：** While wide-angle lenses provide an expanded field of view (FoV), they exhibit severe nonlinear distortion near image boundaries, unnaturally stretching people in these regions. Existing methods mostly focus on facial regions, require high computational costs, and lack temporal modeling, resulting in incomplete full-body rectification, impractical real-time deployment, and noticeable inter-frame shakes. To tackle these issues, we propose \\textbf{FastVC}, an efficient full-body video rectification framework driven by distortion-aware spatiotemporal learning. Grounded in lens physics, FastVC introduces a distortion-aware thin-plate spline (DA-TPS) module that nonlinearly remaps control points into a peripheral-dense layout, enabling more accurate geometric fitting without additional computational cost. We further develop a dual-level spatiotemporal learning scheme for causal and stable video rectification. At the model level, neighborhood spatiotemporal attention (NST) aggregates local context and selectively fuses historical features with low overhead. At the optimization level, sparse spatiotemporal supervision applies spatial alignment only to anchor frames, decoupling frame-wise accuracy from temporal stability. Besides, we contribute the first benchmark dataset for wide-angle full-body video correction. Extensive experiments show that FastVC achieves state-of-the-art rectification fidelity and visual stability at 57.0 FPS.

**comment/C1：** 9 pages, 7 figures

</details>

### 252. [Augmentation Strategy for DINO in Glaucoma Classification](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32511v1>)

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 分析 DINO 的默认自监督增强策略在视网膜眼底图像上产生不可靠表示，并依据“裁剪视图冗余最小、保留临床信号”的原则重新设计增强，在冻结表示上训练线性分类器，于七个青光眼基准数据集上评估。

**排除原因：** 属于医学图像自监督预训练与分类任务，不涉及 latent 推理状态生成、视觉证据筛选或停止决策，也未处理多模态推理，缺乏具体可借鉴线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Augmentation Strategy for DINO in Glaucoma Classification

**abstract/A1：** Self-supervised learning (SSL) offers a promising approach to medical image representation learning, yet augmentation strategies of SSL frameworks validated on natural images do not reliably transfer to specialized medical imaging domains. We analyze DINO, a knowledge-distillation based SSL framework, and empirically demonstrate that its default augmentation strategy produces unreliable representations for retinal fundus images, degrading downstream glaucoma classification performance. Guided by the principle that effective cropped views should share minimal redundant information while retaining clinically relevant signal, we redesign DINO's augmentation strategy. Our approach biases the learned representations toward diagnostically critical features rather than low-level image statistics. We evaluate our method for glaucoma classification by training a linear classifier on the frozen representations, achieving strong generalization across seven benchmark datasets. URL for code and weights: https://github.com/ashish-256/task\_dino

**comment/C1：** 11 pages, 3 figures. Accepted at the 17th International Workshop on Machine Learning in Medical Imaging (MLMI 2026), held in conjunction with MICCAI 2026

</details>

We compare the proposed embeddings with raw channel tokens and multiple baseline models, under three different data regimes, for multiple downstream tasks --- Line-of-Sight/Non-Line-of-Sight (LoS/NLoS) classification, timing advance (TA) estimation, and beam selection. Evaluation is done in both in-distribution and out-of-distribution configuration settings. Experimental outcomes show that the proposed embeddings consistently outperform and/or are competitive to the baselines, across experiments in both seen and unseen configuration scenarios.

### 254. [HEALPix-Based Implicit Neural Compression of Omnidirectional Images](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32561v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32561

**作者：** Yulong Cheng、Youneng Bao、Junfeng Zhou、Mu Li、Jie Wen

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32561v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32561v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文提出基于多尺度 HEALPix 球面表示的过拟合隐式神经编解码器，并设计球面跨尺度条件熵模型与多环球面卷积，以降低全向图像压缩码率并保持实时解码。

**排除原因：** 研究目标是球面图像压缩的码率与解码复杂度，未涉及视觉推理中的 latent 状态生成、证据使用、停止决策或监督分配，也无具体可核查的迁移线索，故排除。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** HEALPix-Based Implicit Neural Compression of Omnidirectional Images

**abstract/A1：** Real-time decoding of omnidirectional images is essential for immersive virtual reality (VR) experiences. Compared with conventional transform-based learned image compression (LIC), overfitted codecs offer lower decoding complexity by representing each image with a lightweight implicit neural representation (INR). However, existing approaches typically operate on the equirectangular projection (ERP), which introduces nonuniform spherical sampling and spatial distortion, leading to redundant sampling near the poles and latitude-dependent receptive fields. To address these limitations, we propose a spherical overfitted codec based on a multi-scale Hierarchical Equal Area isoLatitude Pixelization (HEALPix) representation, which enables equal-area sampling and efficient hierarchical modeling of spherical signals. Building on this representation, we develop a Spherical Inter-Scale Conditional entropy model that exploits spatial dependencies and cross-scale correlations among HEALPix latents to improve coding efficiency. We further introduce a Multi-Ring Spherical Convolution that aggregates neighboring pixels over a nearly uniform spherical receptive field across latitudes. Experiments on a public omnidirectional image dataset demonstrate BD-rate reductions of 16.3%, 26.5%, and 19.5% over VVC in terms of V-PSNR, V-MS-SSIM, and V-LPIPS, respectively. Our method also achieves a 13.1% BD-rate reduction over ERP-based Cool-Chic 5.0 in terms of V-PSNR, while providing 2.6-3.6x faster decoding than the evaluated learned autoencoder codecs.

**comment/C1：** \[N\] pages, 10 figures, 4 tables. Submitted to IEEE Transactions on Image Processing

</details>

### 255. [Reuse, Don't Retrain: Efficient, Modality-Versatile Geospatial Foundation Models through Feature Distillation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32566v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32566

**作者：** Mario Hamon、Pierre Adorni、Minh-Tan Pham

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32566v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32566v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该工作研究地理空间基础模型的高效复用：通过 patch 级特征蒸馏把冻结的 CROMA、TerraMind 教师压缩到更小的 ViT，并提出 TEDI，用独立 patch embedding 将无配对的多模态输入接入共享主干，在 PANGAEA 基准上评估精度与算力权衡。

**排除原因：** 属于遥感模型压缩与多模态教师合并的工程工作，不涉及推理期 latent 状态、视觉证据筛选或停止决策，蒸馏仅用于骨干压缩而非推理轨迹监督，与 LVR 主线缺少可核查的具体联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Reuse, Don't Retrain: Efficient, Modality-Versatile Geospatial Foundation Models through Feature Distillation

**abstract/A1：** Geospatial foundation models (GeoFMs) are costly to pretrain, yet model families often repeat this process across backbone scales and sensing modalities. We investigate feature distillation as a more efficient way to reuse existing representations. First, we distill frozen CROMA and TerraMind teachers into smaller Vision Transformers through patch-level feature matching. Across nine PANGAEA tasks, distilled TerraMind Tiny, Small, and Base outperform their scale-matched released counterparts by 2.76, 3.02, and 1.87 mean IoU points, respectively; each also matches or exceeds the next-larger released model. Distilled CROMA-Base gains 2.05 points. TerraMind-Base required only 43 A6000 GPU-hours, approximately 0.5\\% of the reported cost of independent pretraining. Second, we introduce TEDI, an unpaired multi-expert approach combining TerraMind for multispectral imagery and DINOv3 for high-resolution RGB. Separate patch embeddings route unpaired inputs into a shared Transformer trunk, removing the need for co-registered data. TEDI-Large achieves the highest single-model mean in our PANGAEA-9 comparison (62.51 mIoU), outperforming both teachers overall while retaining their modality-specific strengths. TEDI-Base reaches 61.75 mIoU with 72\\% fewer parameters. These results show that distillation improves the GeoFM accuracy-compute frontier while consolidating complementary experts into a single versatile backbone.

</details>

### 256. [Standing on Open Shoulders: Regional Fine-Tuning of Open-Weight Camera-Trap Models for UK Wildlife with Modest Data and Compute](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32573v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32573

**作者：** Paul Fergus

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32573v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32573v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文在单 GPU 与有限数据条件下微调开放权重相机陷阱物种分类器 DeepFaune，构建面向英国物种的区域模型，并在 MammalWeb 人工核验图像上与 DeepFaune、SpeciesNet 等基线比较图像级准确率。

**排除原因：** 工作属于区域物种分类的监督微调与基准评测，未涉及推理期 latent 状态生成、视觉证据筛选、动态计算或停止决策，也未提出可供 LVR 借鉴的状态或训练机制。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Standing on Open Shoulders: Regional Fine-Tuning of Open-Weight Camera-Trap Models for UK Wildlife with Modest Data and Compute

**abstract/A1：** Camera-trap species classification is dominated by large models from well-resourced organisations, which often lack the species-level distinctions regional conservation needs, such as native red versus invasive grey squirrels in the UK. We ask whether open weights let a small team with a modest dataset and a single GPU take a real part in AI development. We fine-tuned the open DeepFaune classifier on 28 UK classes, paired it with the unmodified MegaDetector v6, and compared the resulting DeepFaune-UK v1.2 with DeepFaune v1.5.0 and SpeciesNet on 20,212 hand-verified MammalWeb citizen-science images, after removing sequences sharing any of 1,039 images with our training archive. DeepFaune-UK achieved a strict image-level accuracy of 88.8%, including empty frames, against 56.5% and 59.0% for the baselines, but at DeepFaune's coarser label resolution DeepFaune v1.5.0 was more accurate, though not significantly under contributor resampling (91.4% vs. 88.8%). As DeepFaune v1.5.0 uses a newer DINOv3-based classifier, we applied the identical recipe, without new data, to that classifier. The resulting DeepFaune-UK v1.3 reached 90.6% strict accuracy, narrowed the like-for-like gap to 0.8 points and reduced roe deer misclassified as fallow deer by 69%. On 1,850 images from the 26 contributors with no overlapping images, the three DeepFaune-based models did not differ significantly (90.5%, 90.6%, 89.9% like-for-like). Fine-tuning on 10% of the training crops (about 150 per class) gave out-of-distribution accuracy similar to using all of them, and a DINOv2 run on this subset took about 22 minutes with under 4 GB of GPU memory. Open weights thus make regional model development feasible for small teams, but the largest like-for-like gain came from a newer base model trained by others, so this democratisation depends on large actors continuing to release open weights, under licences that may change.

**comment/C1：** 39 pages, 9 Figures, and 8 Tables

</details>

### 257. [Hair Segmentation and Skin Characterization on Macroscopic Dermatological Images for Laser Treatment Parameterization](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32609v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32609

**作者：** Alex Navarro Navarro、Marc Arriaga Hidalgo、Carlos Moreno Rubio、Maxime Talarmain、Noelia Vaquero Gallardo

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32609v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32609v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文构建了一个面向宏观皮肤镜图像的毛发分割与皮肤特征提取流水线，结合定制 YOLOv9 实例分割与经典图像处理，并通过分光光度计颜色校准估计光型、毛发密度、颜色和粗细等参数，用于激光治疗参数化。

**排除原因：** 属于特定临床影像的工程分割与参数测量应用，既不涉及 VLM 内部状态或 latent 推理，也未提供关于证据筛选、状态更新或停止决策的可借鉴线索，故排除。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Hair Segmentation and Skin Characterization on Macroscopic Dermatological Images for Laser Treatment Parameterization

**abstract/A1：** Current light-based devices, such as laser hair removal, intense pulsed light (IPL), vascular laser therapy, and dermatologic phototherapy, are widely used in clinical dermatology but rely heavily on subjective manual parameterization or uncalibrated point-based colorimeters. To overcome these limitations, this work presents a unified system for hair segmentation and skin characterization in macroscopic dermatological images. Unlike most existing literature that relies on dermoscopic datasets captured under highly controlled conditions, our approach is specifically designed for macroscopic images that replicate real clinical laser treatments. The proposed pipeline integrates a custom-trained variant of the YOLOv9 deep learning architecture for instance segmentation and classical image processing within a single workflow to extract objective skin and hair parameters such as phototype, hair density, color, and thickness. A color calibration stage using spectrophotometric measurements ensures accurate feature estimation regardless of illumination conditions. Experimental results show competitive segmentation metrics (mAP@50 $\\approx$ 0.80) on a custom dataset featuring short, shaved hairs, typical of pre-treatment scenarios. These findings demonstrate the feasibility of the proposed pipeline in providing the objective, foundational skin and hair features required for future automated laser parameterization, paving the way for its extension to broader light-based medical therapies.

**comment/C1：** Published open access in Journal of Imaging Informatics in Medicine (2026)

</details>

### 258. [DriveOSS: Dataset and Benchmark for 3D Anomaly Segmentation and Open-set Semantic Segmentation in Autonomous Driving](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32612v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32612

**作者：** Zhiran Yan、Savankumar Bhanderi、Sourabh Lolge、Rui Song、Girdharbhai Paneliya、Shiva Agrawal、Gordon Elger

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32612v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32612v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文构建 DriveOSS——面向自动驾驶的 3D 异常分割与开放集语义分割真实多模态数据集与基准，含 12,661 帧环视相机、128 线 LiDAR 与 GPS/IMU 数据，并划分 ID/OOD 场景以评测现有 3D 感知系统。

**排除原因：** 这是面向自动驾驶 3D 感知的开放集分割数据集与基准，未涉及多模态模型内部推理状态、证据获取或停止决策，与 LVR 主线缺少可核查的具体联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** DriveOSS: Dataset and Benchmark for 3D Anomaly Segmentation and Open-set Semantic Segmentation in Autonomous Driving

**abstract/A1：** Recent advances in 3D semantic segmentation have significantly improved scene understanding in autonomous driving. However, existing methods are typically developed under a closed-set assumption and evaluated under standard driving conditions, where all categories are known in advance. In real-world driving, autonomous vehicles inevitably encounter previously unseen objects, making closed-set perception insufficient for safe deployment. To address this challenge, we introduce DriveOSS, which, to the best of our knowledge, is the first real-world multimodal dataset and benchmark specifically designed for 3D anomaly segmentation and open-set semantic segmentation. It contains 12,661 synchronized and annotated frames captured using six surround-view RGB cameras, a 128-beam LiDAR, and a GPS/IMU unit. The dataset comprises 52 real-world driving scenes collected under clear, nighttime, cloudy, rainy, and snowy conditions and is organized into ID (in-distribution) and OOD (out-of-distribution) subsets. The ID scenes contain only known classes and are used for training, whereas the OOD scenes additionally contain unknown objects annotated as the outlier class and are used for validation and testing. Extensive experiments show that unseen objects remain a major challenge for current 3D perception systems. The dataset, benchmark protocols, and code are available at https://github.com/chrisyan/DriveOSS.

**comment/C1：** Accepted to WACV 2027

</details>

### 259. [Retiming Robot Demonstrations with a Learned Speed Prior](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32676v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32676

**作者：** Seungyong Moon、Deokjae Lee、Hansang Lee、Munkhtselmeg Battsengel、Youngin Kim、Seonghyeon Yun、Sungjin Jeon、SongEun Kim、Jeongmin Park、Hyun Oh Song

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.RO

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32676v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32676v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**排除原因：** 该文学习示教数据的执行速度先验并据此对机器人演示重定时，属于机器人动作节奏归一化与实时控制层，未涉及 VLM 内部状态、视觉证据使用或推理期决策，难以形成具体借鉴线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Retiming Robot Demonstrations with a Learned Speed Prior

**abstract/A1：** The pace of a robot demonstration is often determined by the teleoperator rather than by the task itself. As a result, the same motion can appear at widely different speeds across demonstrations, and policies trained on such data may inherit operator-specific timing along with task behavior. We propose to learn a speed prior directly from large-scale demonstration data. Given a task instruction, a motion path and the images along it, it predicts a distribution over execution speed along the path, from which a chosen quantile defines a consistent clock for retiming each demonstration. Existing methods derive the acceleration signal from the very data they accelerate, so each new task costs another fit, annotation, or pair of predefined rates. A single trained speed prior instead covers every task in a corpus. We fit a speed prior on both robot teleoperation data and handheld demonstrations in which a human arm, rather than a robot arm, moves the gripper, and use it to retime robot demonstrations to the handheld pace before fine-tuning a vision-language-action policy. For execution, we pair the policy with a real-time optimal-control layer that tracks predicted action chunks under robot constraints using the predicted pace. On 120 RoboLab tasks, policies fine-tuned on our learned clocks succeed more often and move with up to half the jerk compared with policies fine-tuned on the original demonstration timing and uniformly accelerated to the same speed. On a real robot, our policy stacks cups in 3.50s, 1.72$\\times$ faster than the default policy's 6.02s while succeeding as often, in 29 of 30 trials. Videos and further material are available at https://mllab.snu.ac.kr/projects/learned\_speed\_prior.

</details>

### 260. [PlayerEmbed: Learning Behavioral Fingerprint Representations from Raw Input Sequences](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32727v1>)

**作者：** Tobias Schneider、Lorenz Sparrenberg、Priya Tomar、Shahzeb Qamar、Tobias Deußer、Rafet Sifa

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32727v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32727v1>)

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文提出 PlayerEmbed，用 CNN-LSTM 混合编码器和深度度量学习，从原始游戏玩家输入序列中学习紧凑、可区分的玩家行为嵌入，并评估对已见与未见玩家的识别与泛化能力。

**排除原因：** 研究目标是游戏玩家行为指纹与身份识别，缺少视觉或多模态推理、latent 推理状态生成与使用等实质联系；度量学习式序列嵌入虽可用于判别，但未提供与 LVR 表示、证据筛选或停止决策相关的具体借鉴线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** PlayerEmbed: Learning Behavioral Fingerprint Representations from Raw Input Sequences

**abstract/A1：** Raw player input sequences in video games contain fine-grained behavior, but are high-dimensional and noisy, which makes large-scale analytics challenging. To address this, we introduce PlayerEmbed, a framework that learns compact and discriminative player embeddings using deep metric learning. Our approach uses a hybrid CNN-LSTM encoder trained on raw input sequences from 1,000 players. We evaluate the model on held-out sequences from these players and on sequences from 1,000 players entirely unseen during training to assess generalization to unseen identities. For player identification, a nearest-centroid classifier built from reference embeddings achieves strong performance, reaching balanced accuracies of 0.950 for players seen during training and 0.879 for players unseen during training. Systematically increasing the number of training players substantially improves generalization to unseen players, indicating a transition from simple fingerprinting to learning transferable behavioral patterns.

**comment/C1：** Accepted at IEEE DSAA 2026

</details>

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** CityLift: Compositional 3D Urban Generation from a Single Image

**abstract/A1：** Generating editable 3D urban scenes from a single image requires detailed building assets with a coherent spatial arrangement. Occlusion and perspective ambiguity make it difficult to infer complete building geometry together with consistent relative scales and positions. We present CityLift, a framework that generates individual textured building meshes from an urban image by coupling scene-level spatial modeling with building-level synthesis. CityLift first learns building shape and placement jointly through coupled scene and instance streams, producing coarse sparse structures in a common coordinate system. We then introduce generative spatial inheritance, which maps each building's coarse occupancy and scene-conditioned 3D feature field into a local volume to guide a flow model that generates finer structure. The same coordinate mapping places the generated meshes back into the scene, preserving their spatial relationship to the scaffold. To address the scarcity of urban data pairing scene images with individual 3D buildings, we construct CityLift-5K, comprising 4,997 synthetic scenes with instance masks, building meshes, and placement annotations. Experiments on CityLift-5K and scene blocks from UrbanScene3D and InstanceBuilding demonstrate improved building geometry and scene composition. The project page is available at https://ranhaogong.github.io/CityLift/.

</details>

<a id="paper-262"></a>

### 262. [Cross-Architectural Feature Fusion for Coronary Artery Stenosis Detection in X-ray Angiography](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32730v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32730

**作者：** Haobo Zhu、Mojtaba Lashgari、Jason Chai、Giovanni Luigi De Maria、Vicente Grau、Abhirup Banerjee

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32730v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32730v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文面向 X 射线冠脉造影的狭窄检测，将 YOLOv11-L 检测器与在 ARCADE 上预训练的 UNet++ 通过通道注意力门控融合，并结合测试时自适应与增强缓解跨域退化。

**排除原因：** 属于医学影像检测的工程性特征融合与域适应工作，通道注意力门控仅为通用多尺度融合，不涉及推理期状态生成、证据筛选或决策机制，缺少可供 LVR 核查的具体线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Cross-Architectural Feature Fusion for Coronary Artery Stenosis Detection in X-ray Angiography

**abstract/A1：** Coronary artery disease remains a leading cause of mortality, with stenosis detection from invasive X-ray Coronary Angiography (XCA) critical for diagnosis. Manual interpretation is time-consuming, subjective, and variable, especially under low contrast and vessel overlap. Existing deep learning methods often rely solely on detection or segmentation, limiting performance.We propose a hybrid framework, Fusion-YOLOv11-L, combining a YOLOv11-L detector with a pretrained UNet++ via a channel attention gating module. This enables fusion of semantic and vessel-aware structural features, improving localisation of stenotic regions. The UNet++ is pretrained on ARCADE vessel segmentation dataset to provide anatomically informed representations. Experiments on the relabeled ARCADE (ARCADE\*) dataset show improved performance over YOLOv11-L, achieving AP@50 of 0.585 and F1-score of 0.596. Cross-domain evaluation on a dataset from the Oxford Acute Myocardial Infarction (OxAMI) Study reveals performance degradation due to domain shift, mitigated using test-time adaptation and augmentation. Test-time augmentation improves AP@50 from 0.434 to 0.480, indicating better generalisability. These results show that cross-architecture feature fusion and lightweight adaptation improve robustness and generalisability of automated stenosis detection in XCA.

**comment/C1：** 12 pages, 3 figures

</details>

### 263. [Agentic Hazard Mapper: Emulating Expert Map-Making with AI Agents for Risk Communication](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32738v1>)

<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Agentic Hazard Mapper: Emulating Expert Map-Making with AI Agents for Risk Communication

**abstract/A1：** Hazard maps are essential tools for public risk communication, preparedness, and evacuation planning, yet effective cartographic design is not always ensured due to limited access to cartographic expertise across local governments and communities. We present Agentic Hazard Mapper, a multi-agent system that automates hazard-map production by emulating professional cartographic workflows and iterative design trade-offs while adapting design decisions to different data, locations, and hazard contexts. We conducted a formative study with map experts to inform the system design, followed by a user study and interviews with map experts and hazard practitioners. The generated maps improved participants' spatial understanding and evacuation-planning performance, reduced perceived workload and task completion time, and were generally preferred over corresponding municipal maps. We further discuss human oversight, adaptation across users and communication contexts, and opportunities to extend agentic support across broader stages of hazard-map production and risk communication.

### 264. [SCDM-HTD: Spectral-Wise Conditional Diffusion Model for Hyperspectral Target Detection](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32768v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32768

**作者：** Dunbin Shen、Wenfeng Kong、Jianjun Liu、Xiaorui Ma、Hongyu Wang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32768v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32768v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文面向遥感高光谱目标检测，提出频谱维条件扩散模型，将检测图生成视为任务，用像素光谱作条件、以轻量 MLP 去噪网络和确定性反向链加速采样，并组合约束能量最小化损失与 BCE 损失。

**排除原因：** 工作属于高光谱像素谱级的检测与生成式建模，不涉及 VLM 推理状态、视觉证据筛选或 latent 推理轨迹，也未提供可核查的表示或决策层面借鉴线索，故排除。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** SCDM-HTD: Spectral-Wise Conditional Diffusion Model for Hyperspectral Target Detection

**abstract/A1：** Hyperspectral target detection (HTD) aims to locate and recognize objects at a fine-grained scale, playing a pivotal role in remote sensing. Despite significant progress, current HTD methods primarily focus on feature comparison while neglecting distribution learning for background-target separation, which can limit detection robustness in practical applications. This paper addresses this issue by introducing a spectral-wise conditional diffusion model that reformulates HTD as a task of generating a detection map. Specifically, we treat background and target confidence scores as training samples and use the corresponding pixel spectra as guiding conditions to capture the distributional discrepancy between background and target through diffusion and denoising. To enhance practical applicability, we propose a lightweight conditional denoising network based on multilayer perceptron to map noisy confidence scores to their ideal counterparts and adopt a deterministic reverse non-Markov chain to skip sampling steps, thereby accelerating the generation process. Furthermore, we introduce a composite loss to improve background-target separation. This loss integrates constrained energy minimization loss to enhance target saliency and suppress the background by maximizing the target response while minimizing average output energy, alongside binary cross-entropy loss to classify background and target using pixel-wise labels. Experimental results on four benchmark datasets demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed method, with improvements in both quantitative metrics and qualitative visual effects.

</details>

### 265. [Deep Learning-Based Prediction of HER2 Status from Whole-Brain MRI in Breast Cancer Brain Metastases](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32774v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32774

**作者：** Sara Early、Chris Kang、Vibujithan Vigneshwaran、Kimberly Amador、Roger Y. Tsang、Nils D. Forkert

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32774v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32774v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该工作利用公开 BCBM-Radiogenomics 数据集，比较影像组学模型与全卷积网络（单独 T1w 及附带专家肿瘤掩膜作为额外通道）从全脑 MRI 预测乳腺癌脑转移患者 HER2 状态的判别能力。

**排除原因：** 属于医学影像生物标志物分类任务，既无 latent 推理状态生成，也不涉及视觉证据获取、停止决策或 VLM 训练监督，与 LVR 主线缺少可核查的具体联系，故排除。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Deep Learning-Based Prediction of HER2 Status from Whole-Brain MRI in Breast Cancer Brain Metastases

**abstract/A1：** Purpose: Human epidermal growth factor receptor 2 (HER2) status is a key biomarker relevant for prognostication and treatment decision making in metastatic breast cancer. Thus, the aim of this work was to explore whether HER2 status can be predicted directly from whole-brain post-contrast T1-weighted (T1w) MRI scans in breast cancer brain metastases (BCBM) using deep learning, and whether providing explicit tumour segmentation information improves prediction. Methods: Using the publicly available BCBM-Radiogenomics dataset, we analysed 135 patients, including 70 HER2+ and 65 HER2- patients. Three approaches were implemented and evaluated: a conventional radiomics model, a simple fully convolutional network (SFCN) using T1w MRI scans alone, and an SFCN using T1w MRI scans with an expert-derived tumour mask as an additional input channel. Results: The radiomics model achieved an area under the receiver operating characteristic curve (ROC-AUC) of 0.602 (95% confidence interval \[CI\]: 0.528--0.677) while the SFCN achieved a ROC-AUC of 0.667 (95% CI, 0.63--0.71) using T1w MRI scans alone, which increased to 0.692 (95% CI, 0.58--0.81) when an explicit tumour mask was additionally provided. Conclusion: However, the modest performance across all approaches demonstrates the difficulty of predicting HER2 status from structural MRI scans alone. The difference between the segmentation-free and mask-guided SFCN models suggests that much of the predictive signal captured by the deep-learning model may be learned directly from whole-brain T1w MRI without requiring explicit tumour delineation. These findings support further investigation of segmentation-free deep-learning approaches as a potential non-invasive strategy to support HER2 status determination in BCBM.

</details>

### 266. [SREdit: Scene Response Guided Appearance Editing for Projection Mapping](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32792v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32792

**作者：** Yuqiao Xia、Yuchen Deng、Ziheng Chang、Bingyao Huang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.MM

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32792v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32792v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文提出 SREdit，用从重建场景估计的 scene response map 引导预训练图像编辑器的采样与编辑强度，使投影映射结果在投影范围内保持几何对齐与着色一致。

**排除原因：** 研究目标是投影外观编辑与投影补偿的显示质量，不涉及 VLM 推理状态、证据获取或停止决策；response map 只是显示约束，与 latent 推理中的表示或状态更新缺少具体联系，故不入选。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** SREdit: Scene Response Guided Appearance Editing for Projection Mapping

**abstract/A1：** Projection-based appearance editing changes selected visual attributes of a physical object through projection mapping (PM). To display a desired appearance, PM typically uses projector compensation, which computes a projector input that counteracts the geometric and photometric distortions of the scene. Compensation can only approximate this appearance within the available projection range, and it cannot revise the appearance itself. Pretrained LLM-based image editors provide a direct way to produce the desired appearance from a text prompt or reference image; projector compensation can then attempt to reproduce the edited result through projection. However, these editors do not have an accurate measure of the scene geometry or scene response. Therefore, their outputs may contain geometric misalignment, inconsistent shading, or changes outside the available projection range, which downstream projector compensation cannot correct. To address this problem, we introduce SREdit, an efficient response-guided framework for projection-based appearance editing. SREdit estimates a scene response map from the reconstructed scene that reflects how strongly projected illumination can affect different surface regions. SREdit uses the source appearance to constrain the early sampling process and preserve registration with the fixed physical object, while the scene response map adapts the amount of editing across surface regions. Experiments across two pretrained image editors show that SREdit improves projected appearance quality and reproduction fidelity. It adds less than one second per image with negligible VRAM overhead. Participants also consistently preferred the results produced with SREdit in our user study.

**comment/C1：** 28 pages, 65 figures

</details>

### 267. [U-NeXt: General-purpose event detection for neonatal EEG](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32811v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32811

**作者：** Robert Hogan、Sean R. Mathieson、Aurel Luca、Soraia Ventura、Sean Griffin、Geraldine B. Boylan、John M. O'Toole

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、eess.SP、physics.med-ph

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32811v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32811v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 U-NeXt，用卷积编解码器对新生儿 EEG 逐通道做语义分割以检测低电压区间、伪迹与癫痫事件，并与 ConvNeXt 分类器比较检测性能与推理速度。

**排除原因：** 该工作是单模态生理时序信号的医学事件分割与临床监测，未涉及 VLM 推理、latent 视觉状态或证据筛选，也无可核查的跨域借鉴线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** U-NeXt: General-purpose event detection for neonatal EEG

**abstract/A1：** We developed U-NeXt, a general framework for neonatal electroencephalography (EEG) event detection based on semantic segmentation. Its convolutional encoder-decoder directly segments individual channels using long input segments to preserve temporal context. A shared architecture and training framework were developed using low-voltage interval, artefact, and seizure datasets containing 51, 98, and 278 neonates, respectively, comprising over 11,000 hours of EEG and 82,000 annotated events. Performance was compared with task-specific ConvNeXt classifiers. For seizure detection, we also evaluated U-NeXt+, trained on an expanded dataset. Both U-NeXt variants and the ConvNeXt baseline were evaluated on two independent held-out seizure datasets, Helsinki and Cork, comprising 130 neonates, with recordings annotated by three experts. U-NeXt improved detection across all three event types, with particular gains in sensitivity for short events. U-NeXt+ achieved Matthews correlation coefficients of 0.862 and 0.891 on Helsinki and Cork, respectively, and improved hourly seizure burden correlations by 30-40% relative to ConvNeXt. U-NeXt+ met the criterion for expert-level performance on both datasets (p &lt; 0.001). In separate computational benchmarks, U-NeXt reduced batch inference time relative to ConvNeXt by 254-fold on a central processing unit and 200-fold on a graphics processing unit. A common segmentation framework improves detection across diverse neonatal EEG event types while substantially reducing inference time. Accurate seizure burden estimates and performance comparable to expert reviewers support its potential use in continuous monitoring and clinical decision support.

</details>
