**作者：** Yichun Peng、Yansong Li、Jin Hou、Qi Song、Xi Guo、Ning Zhang、Degang Wei、Huang Yang、Yu Guan、Jianping Ge、Lei Bao、Tianming Wang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32848v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32848v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文提出 OpenStripe，一个用于野生东北虎红外相机图像的多智能体框架，重建虎躯干二维规范图案并编码四类可解释条纹局部结构，实现开放集个体识别，并分析条纹相似度与地理距离的关系。

**排除原因：** 属于野生动物个体识别与空间表型分析的应用工作，虽涉及多智能体和可解释局部特征，但不涉及 VLM 推理状态、证据获取、停止决策或 latent 内容的使用，与 LVR 主线缺少实质联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** OpenStripe supports open-set identification and spatial stripe profiling in wild Amur tigers

**abstract/A1：** Non-invasive camera-trap monitoring of endangered wildlife requires open-set individual identification---grouping field images into identities without predefined classes or a known population size---yet pose deformation and cluttered backgrounds complicate image comparison. Here, we present OpenStripe, a multi-agent framework that reconstructs canonical two-dimensional (2D) tiger torso patterns and encodes four interpretable local stripe structures: breakpoints, bifurcations, irregular structures, and round structures. We evaluated OpenStripe on 100 camera-trap records from 28 wild Amur tigers (Panthera tigris altaica), representing nearly one-third of the documented population in the Northeast China Tiger and Leopard National Park. Combining all four complementary structures enabled automatic identity grouping with 0.90 matched accuracy and a 0.85 adjusted Rand index (ARI), outperforming 49 baseline configurations under the same backend (up to 0.43 accuracy and 0.14 ARI). In addition, results from OpenStripe indicate that stripe similarity in Amur tigers declines with geographic distance and suggest that traits such as fractal dimension and inter-stripe spacing are associated with geographic proximity. OpenStripe thus provides an interpretable representation that connects automated individual grouping with spatial phenotypic profiling in wild populations.

**comment/C1：** 14 pages, 4 figures. Code and nonspatial data: https://github.com/Yichun-Peng/OpenStripe

</details>

### 269. [Near-Optimal List Replicability from Littlestone Dimension](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32853v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32853

**作者：** Ari Blondal、Pooya Hatami、Chavdar Lalov、Sivan Tretiak

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32853v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32853v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文研究可实现 PAC 框架下的 list replicability number，利用基于差分隐私学习中「不可约性」概念的归纳框架，证明其上界为 2^{O(d·min{log d, v\*})}，接近下界并去除了对精度 ε 的依赖。

**排除原因：** 内容属于组合式学习理论中的可复制性与 Littlestone 维数上界，不涉及视觉或多模态推理状态、证据获取或训练监督，仅有的稳定学习器与多数投票线索也过于间接，缺乏可核查的具体联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Near-Optimal List Replicability from Littlestone Dimension

**abstract/A1：** We study the list replicability number in the realizable PAC learning framework and prove that every concept class $H$ over a countable domain with Littlestone dimension $d$ and dual VC dimension $v^\*$ satisfies \\\[ \\mathrm{LR}(H)\\leq 2^{O(d\\min\\{\\log d,v^\*\\})}. \\\] This matches the worst-case lower bound of $2^{Ω(d)}$ up to a logarithmic factor in the exponent and exponentially improves the previous best upper bound $\\mathrm{LR}(H)\\le 2^{2^{O(d)}}$ of Bun, Livni, and Moran \[FOCS 2020\]. Unlike earlier bounds of $2^{O\_ε(d^2)}$ for fixed accuracy $ε&gt;0$ implied by the work of Ghazi, Golowich, Kumar, and Manurangsi \[STOC 2021\] and Lyu \[STOC 2026\], our bound is independent of $ε$. The $2^{O(d\\log d)}$ bound follows from a new inductive framework for bounding list replicability that builds on the recent notion of irreducibility from differentially private learning. The $2^{O(dv^\*)}$ bound follows from a simple globally stable learner that takes the majority vote of multiple empirical risk minimizers, with stability established using tools from stable regularity.

**comment/C1：** 14 pages

</details>

### 270. [Sustainability of TSFM Deployment and Pretraining: From Carbon Footprint Analysis to Mitigation via Pruning](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32859v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32859

**作者：** Cécile Luc、Nathanaël Lemonnier、Ievgen Redko

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32859v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32859v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文分析时间序列基础模型预训练与部署的累计碳排放代价，并提出基于无梯度准则的编码器块剪枝以降低 FLOPs、CO2 与延迟。

**排除原因：** 主题为时间序列模型的碳成本核算与结构化剪枝，未涉及视觉或多模态推理状态、证据使用或停止决策，也无可迁移的 latent reasoning 线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Sustainability of TSFM Deployment and Pretraining: From Carbon Footprint Analysis to Mitigation via Pruning

**abstract/A1：** Time Series Foundation Models (TSFMs) offer strong zero-shot forecasting capabilities, but their high pretraining cost raises a practical sustainability question: does this upfront investment pay off over a full deployment lifecycle, compared to supervised standalone models that must be periodically retrained? We model the cumulative carbon cost of different approaches across a realistic industrial scenario: Chronos-2 amortizes its pretraining cost within months for large or frequently queried deployments, but a classical model remains cheaper indefinitely for small or rarely queried ones. For cases where reducing the TSFM's own cost matters, we show that removing a single encoder block (7.9% of parameters) via a gradient-free pruning criterion yields a consistent 6 to 9% reduction in FLOPs, CO2 emissions, and latency, at essentially no cost to zero-shot forecasting performance across a 25-task benchmark.

**comment/C1：** 26 pages, 6 figures, accepted at the NeurIPS 2026 Workshop TS-LIMITS

</details>

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文提出 PoKER，用机器学习在超视距空战中结合目标随机行为与脱靶量等因素，预测导弹杀伤概率，以替代基于静态距离的 WEZ 模型。

**排除原因：** 属于军事作战概率估计的应用建模，未涉及视觉/多模态内部表示、证据获取或推理轨迹，也未提供可迁移到 LVR 的状态或决策线索，故排除。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** PoKER: A Probability of Kill Estimation Rate Model for Air-to-Air Missiles Using Machine Learning on Stochastic Targets

**abstract/A1：** This work introduces PoKER, a novel probabilistic model engineered to optimize missile launch effectiveness in air-to-air scenarios, specifically within Beyond Visual Range (BVR) air combat. Unlike conventional Weapon Engagement Zone (WEZ) models that delineate zones based on static distances such as maximum, minimum, and no-escape ranges, PoKER applies machine learning to predict kill probabilities more accurately by integrating the stochastic behaviors of targets and missile miss distances. This model dynamically evaluates target behavior, greatly expanding the predictive capabilities of engagement analysis. By factoring in elements such as warhead lethality, target and shooter orientations, and the specific conditions of engagement, PoKER provides important insights into engagement dynamics and quantifies success probabilities. Consequently, it can potentially be an important tool for BVR air combat pilots, improving operational decision-making within this specialized combat domain.

**comment/C1：** Accepted manuscript. 18 pages, 10 figures, 5 tables. Code available at https://github.com/jpadantas/poker

</details>

<a id="paper-272"></a>

### 272. [Advancing SVD-based LLM Compression via Layer-Wise Error Model Search](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32878v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32878

**作者：** Moritz Thoma、Maximilian Groezinger、Maximilian Forstenhäusler、Emad Aghajanzadeh、Manoj Rohit Vemparala、Christos Anagnostopoulos、Pierpaolo Mori、Nael Fasfous、Alexander Frickenstein、Daniel Mueller-Gritschneder、Ulf Schlichtmann

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32878v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32878v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出基于 SVD 的 LLM 压缩改进：用逐层误差代理配合 ILP 做全局秩分配（LEMS），并用 token 级统计的 KFAC-SVD 缓解秩塌缩，提升零样本精度与困惑度。

**排除原因：** 逐层误差传播建模与 token 级统计服务于权重低秩分解的秩分配，关注的是压缩误差而非多模态推理中的 latent 状态、证据筛选或停止决策，未见可供核查的具体借鉴线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Advancing SVD-based LLM Compression via Layer-Wise Error Model Search

**abstract/A1：** Low-rank SVD-based compression offers a powerful strategy to reduce the computational costs of LLMs. However, existing methods face two key limitations: (i) global rank allocation, where uncalibrated error proxies fail to capture complex error propagation, and (ii) decomposition quality, where Fisher-based estimators suffer from severe rank collapse. In this work, we address these limitations by introducing Layer-wise Error Modeling Search (LEMS) and KFAC-SVD. LEMS advances rank allocation by introducing a layer-wise error surrogate that integrates local and global layer importance alongside a propagation bias, enabling effective global rank allocation via an ILP formulation. KFAC-SVD improves decomposition quality by utilizing token-wise statistics, mitigating the rank deficiency observed in prior Fisher-based SVD approaches. Across Mistral, Qwen3, and Llama 3 model families, we show that LEMS consistently outperforms existing search strategies, delivering significant zero-shot accuracy gains of up to 4.8 p.p. that generalize to model sizes of 70B parameters, while KFAC-SVD achieves an average perplexity improvement of 15%. Project page and code: https://lems-svd.github.io

**comment/C1：** Accepted at ICML 2026

</details>

### 273. [VTRL-Med: Progressive Visual Refinement with Topology-Guided Regularization for Medical Image Clustering](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32884v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32884

**作者：** Guangyu Meng、Pengfei Gu、Peixian Liang、John P. Lalor、Erin Wolf Chambers、Danny Z. Chen

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32884v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32884v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文提出 VTRL-Med：用 SAM 提取保留结构线索的特征，经原型选择与 kNN 一致性过滤生成伪标签，并引入持续同调拓扑描述子作为训练期辅助正则，用于医学图像无监督聚类。

**排除原因：** 属于医学图像聚类任务应用，缺少推理状态生成、证据获取或停止决策等与 LVR 主线的实质联系；拓扑分支只是聚类表示的训练正则，未涉及多模态推理中的 latent 使用或视觉证据筛选，可供借鉴线索不足。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** VTRL-Med: Progressive Visual Refinement with Topology-Guided Regularization for Medical Image Clustering

**abstract/A1：** Deep clustering offers a promising approach for analyzing large unlabeled medical image datasets, yet prevailing methods still face two limitations: they may neglect class-agnostic structural priors that capture object boundaries and regions, and they do not explicitly encode topological patterns (e.g., connectivity and holes). These sources of information play distinct and complementary roles: visual-structural features provide the primary discriminative representation for clustering, while topological features provide additional global geometric cues for residual visual ambiguities. We introduce VTRL-Med, a progressive visual-structural learning framework with topology-guided regularization for medical image clustering. VTRL-Med first adapts the Segment Anything Model (SAM) to extract image features that retain structural cues inherited from mask-supervised pretraining. It then generates reliable pseudo-labels through a two-step filtering process that combines prototype-based selection with k-nearest neighbor consistency validation. Finally, it progressively refines the visual branch while an auxiliary topology branch, constructed from persistent homology (PH) descriptors, provides additional geometric regularization during training. Across 13 medical image benchmarks, VTRL-Med achieves state-of-the-art clustering performance, improving average accuracy by 4.48% over existing methods. On three representative datasets, topology-guided regularization further improves accuracy by 1.3--4.3% over visual refinement alone.

</details>

### 274. [RobustRLlib: A Unified Library and Benchmark for Robust Reinforcement Learning Algorithms](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32971v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32971

**作者：** Xi Chen、Fengqi Hu、Zeyu Shen、Jiaying Xu、Shangding Gu、Pan Xu、Anqi Liu、Laixi Shi

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI、cs.RO

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32971v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32971v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文提出 RobustRLlib，将 17 种鲁棒在线/离线/安全 RL 算法统一到同一接口，并在含人形机器人与 VLA 任务的扰动配置上做系统评测，给出当前鲁棒 RL 的能力边界。

**排除原因：** 工作重心是 RL 算法复用与策略鲁棒性基准，VLA 任务仅作为评测环境，未涉及 latent 推理状态的表示、生成、使用或停止决策，与 LVR 主线缺少具体可借鉴线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** RobustRLlib: A Unified Library and Benchmark for Robust Reinforcement Learning Algorithms

**abstract/A1：** Robust reinforcement learning (RL) is critical for reliable real-world deployment, where policies may fail under diverse shifts and disruptions. Existing robust RL algorithms offer a rich set of mechanisms and principles, yet two major barriers limit their broader potential: limited algorithm reusability and an unclear empirical capability boundary. We introduce RobustRLlib, an algorithm-centric library and benchmark that addresses both challenges. It integrates 17 representative robust online, offline, and safe RL algorithms under a unified interface for substantially improved reusability, together with an enhanced evaluation platform spanning diverse real-world shifts and advanced humanoid and vision-language-action tasks. Systematic evaluation over 273 perturbation configurations provides an up-to-date capability map of current robust RL algorithms, revealing where they remain effective and where substantial robustness gaps persist. As an open-source, user-friendly platform, RobustRLlib offers a reproducible and extensible codebase for applying existing methods to new problems and developing new robust RL algorithms.

**comment/C1：** Project page, code, and tutorial: https://robustrl-lib.github.io/

</details>

### 275. [FlexGlue: Sparse-to-Dense Feature Matching and Visual Disambiguation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32976v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.32976

**作者：** Philipp Lindenberger、Paul-Edouard Sarlin、Ondrej Miksik、Marc Pollefeys

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32976v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.32976v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** FlexGlue 提出一种非对称稀疏到稠密的特征匹配网络：给定第一图的点坐标，在第二图的稠密像素中搜索有限对应点，以兼顾稀疏匹配的速度与稠密匹配的亚像素精度，并同时判别并拒绝错误配对。

**排除原因：** 该工作属几何视觉的跨图特征匹配与 SfM/定位应用，未涉及 VLM 内部推理状态、视觉证据筛选或停止决策，与 LVR 主线缺少实质联系，也无具体可迁移线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** FlexGlue: Sparse-to-Dense Feature Matching and Visual Disambiguation

**abstract/A1：** To estimate correspondences across images, existing approaches match either sparse interest points, which is highly efficient but limited by repeatability, or dense pixel grids, which is robust but expensive. Furthermore, these approaches are trained with only matching pairs and thus often fail to reject unrelated pairs that exhibit visual symmetries, which is equally important in applications like Structure-from-Motion (SfM). We bridge these gaps by introducing FlexGlue, a neural network for asymmetric sparse-to-dense matching and disambiguation. Given two images and coordinates of points in the first image, FlexGlue estimates the coordinates of the corresponding points in the second image while simultaneously rejecting the pair if non-matching. By searching for a limited set of correspondences among dense pixels, our approach is both fast and effective at both matching and disambiguation: it combines the sub-pixel accuracy of dense matching with the speed of sparse matching, while rejecting incorrect pairs with high precision. Experiments on multiple datasets show that FlexGlue pushes the Pareto frontier in relative pose estimation and significantly outperforms existing approaches for SfM and visual localization. FlexGlue is publicly available at https://github.com/cvg/flexglue .

</details>

</details>
